Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 先前的通用大语言模型在病理全切片图像任务中表现不佳，主要源于输入配置（如patch大小、数量、放大倍数）选择不当。通过系统优化这些参数，无需领域特化训练即可显著提升性能，在多个基准和模型上缩小了与专用模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通用大语言模型在病理WSI任务中常被用作基线，但因WSI超出上下文限制而采用非优化的patch采样策略（如小patch、独立处理、多数投票），导致其持续落后于专用模型。这强化了"需要领域特化训练或架构适配"的认知，但缺乏对输入设计因素的系统评估。

Method: 对四个输入设计因素（推理模式、patch大小、放大倍数、patch数量）进行系统析因分析。采用单一平衡配置（大patch、低放大倍数、联合处理），并在MultiPathQA基准上评估GPT-5；进一步进行每任务优化，并泛化到其他模型和完全保留的CPTAC队列。

Result: 单一平衡配置使GPT-5在TCGA（癌症分类）上从15.1%提升至39.5%，在GTEx（器官分类）上从38.1%提升至62.9%。每任务优化进一步分别达到43.9%和71.6%。该配置泛化到其他两个模型，且在CPTAC队列上使Gemini 3 Flash提升23.4个百分点，无需任务特定调优。

Conclusion: 先前研究因采用非最优输入配置而夸大了专用模型与通用LLM之间的性能差距。通过系统优化输入设计，通用LLM可在WSI病理任务中实现竞争力强的结果，无需领域特化训练或架构改动。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) are routinely used as baselines when evaluating specialized pathology models on whole-slide images (WSIs). Because WSIs exceed contemporary model context limits, LLM baselines routinely use small, high-magnification patches processed independently via majority voting, without systematic evaluation of seemingly inconsequential design choices such as patch size, patch count, and magnification. Generalist LLMs have consistently underperformed specialized systems, reinforcing the perception that domain-specific training or architectural adaptation is necessary for pathology tasks involving WSIs. Here, we conduct a systematic factorial analysis of four input design factors: inference mode, patch size, magnification, and patch count. We demonstrate that prior studies have overstated the gap between specialized models and general-purpose LLMs by choosing non-optimized input configurations. On the MultiPathQA benchmark, switching to a single balanced configuration (large patches at lower magnification, processed jointly) raises GPT-5 from 15.1% to 39.5% on cancer-type classification (TCGA) and from 38.1% to 62.9% on organ classification (GTEx). Per-task optimization yields further gains up to 43.9% (TCGA) and 71.6% (GTEx). The same configuration generalizes to two other models and to a fully held-out CPTAC cohort, where it improves Gemini 3 Flash by 23.4 percentage points without any task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [112] [Reroute, Don't Remove: Recoverable Visual Token Routing for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12412)
*Cheng-Yu Yang,Shao-Yuan Lo,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Reroute是一种无需训练的可恢复路由方法，替代传统视觉token的不可逆剪枝，允许被推迟的token在后续解码层重新参与候选，在保持计算预算的同时提升了视觉定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token缩减方法遵循"排序-丢弃"范式，将低分token永久丢弃。然而视觉token的重要性随解码器深度动态变化，早期排名低的token在后续层可能变得重要，这种不可逆操作对于需要精细定位的任务是脆弱的。

Method: Reroute在每个路由阶段将选中的视觉token送入解码器块，而被推迟的token则绕过当前阶段，在下一路由决策时重新进入候选池。该方法利用现有的注意力分数排序规则和阶段调度策略，无需训练即可集成到FastV、PDrop、Nüwa等剪枝方法中。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5和Qwen骨干网络上，Reroute增强了FastV、PDrop、Nüwa变体在激进token缩减下的定位能力，同时保持了一般的VQA性能。

Conclusion: VLM的token缩减不应仅被视为不可逆剪枝，而应看作可恢复的路由过程。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) project images into hundreds to thousands of visual tokens, making decoder inference expensive in both attention computation and KV-cache memory. Existing visual-token reduction methods largely follow a rank-and-remove paradigm: they score visual tokens, keep a compact subset, and permanently discard the rest. We show that this irreversible action is fragile because visual-token importance changes across decoder depth; tokens ranked low at one stage may become relevant in later layers, especially for grounding-sensitive queries. We propose Reroute, a training-free plug-in that replaces removal with recoverable routing. At each routing stage, selected vision tokens pass through decoder blocks, while deferred tokens bypass the stage and re-enter the candidate pool at the next routing decision. Reroute reuses existing attention-score ranking rules and stage-wise schedules, preserving the theoretical TFLOPs and KV-cache budget class of the pruning method it augments. Across FastV, PDrop, and Nüwa variants on LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen backbones, reroute improves grounding under aggressive token reduction while maintaining general VQA performance. These results suggest that VLM token reduction should not be viewed only as irreversible pruning, but also as recoverable routing. The code can be found here: https://github.com/elmma/mllm-reroute/

</details>


### [113] [Causal Clothes-Invariant Feature Learning for Cloth-Changing Person Re-ID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.06145)
*Xulin Li,Yan Lu,Bin Liu,Jiaze Li,Yating Liu,Qi Chu,Mang Ye,Wanli Ouyang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对换衣行人重识别中模型依赖衣物线索的伪相关性问题，提出基于因果干预的CCIL方法，通过干预学习P(Y|do(X))阻断衣物捷径，在PRCC和DeepChange上分别达到66.4%和59.2%的Rank-1准确率，超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReID方法因衣物所有权导致的伪相关性（衣物与身份在训练集中高度关联）而倾向于记忆衣物特征而非学习衣物不变特征，导致在换衣场景下泛化能力差。

Method: 将ReID从似然学习P(Y|X)转为因果干预学习P(Y|do(X))，通过三个模块实现：混杂字典（存储衣物风格等混淆变量）、干预模块（执行后门调整阻断衣物捷径）、解耦正则化（分离衣物相关与衣物不变特征）。

Result: 在PRCC上Rank-1为66.4%（超SOTA 1.4个百分点），DeepChange上Rank-1为59.2%（超SOTA 4.1个百分点）。

Conclusion: 基于因果关系的建模使模型天然具备衣物不变性，有效防止特征学习中的伪相关捕获，为CCReID提供了新的因果视角解决方案。

Abstract: In cloth-changing person re-identification (CCReID), it is critical to learn clothes-invariant feature, which can provide discriminative ID features that remain robust against clothing changes. However, a spurious correlation currently limits existing ReID methods from effectively extracting these clothing-invariant features. This spurious correlation arises from clothing ownership: clothing is rarely shared across different identities, so models tend to memorize clothing cues for identity recognition, and this strategy generalizes poorly to unseen clothing. In this paper, we propose Causal Clothes-Invariant Learning (CCIL), which explicitly shifts CC-ReID from likelihood learning P (Y|X) to causal intervention learning P (Y|do(X)) to block the clothing shortcut. CCIL realizes this intervention through three modules: a Confounder Dictionary, an Intervention Module, and Disentangle Regularization. The causality-based modeling makes the entire model naturally clothes-invariant, effectively preventing the capture of spurious correlations in feature learning. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of CCIL. On PRCC and DeepChange datasets, CCIL achieves Rank-1 accuracies of 66.4% and 59.2%, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 1.4 and 4.1 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [114] [Temporal2Seq: A Unified Framework for Temporal Video Understanding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18478)
*Min Yang,Zichen Zhang,Qian Dang,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal2Seq，一个统一框架，将时序视频理解任务（TAD、TAS、GEBD）的输出统一表示为离散token序列，通过单一架构和联合训练实现多任务通用模型，在多个任务上取得合理甚至优于单任务模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序视频理解任务（TAD、TAS、GEBD）通常各自为政，缺乏能够同时处理多个任务的统一框架，不利于下一代AI的通用化发展。

Method: 提出Temporal2Seq框架，将不同任务的输出统一建模为离散token序列；在单一架构下进行多任务联合训练；并构建了包含TAD、TAS、GEBD三个任务数据集的联合训练基准。

Result: 在TAD、TAS、GEBD三个任务的测试集上，Temporal2Seq能产生合理结果，且相比该框架下的单任务训练具有优势；在跨任务的新数据集上，通用模型优于特定任务模型。

Conclusion: Temporal2Seq证明通过统一的离散token序列表示，可以在单一架构下有效处理多种时序视频理解任务，为视频理解的通用模型提供了可行方向。

Abstract: With the development of video understanding, there is a proliferation of tasks for clip-level temporal video analysis, including temporal action detection (TAD), temporal action segmentation (TAS), and generic event boundary detection (GEBD). While task-specific video understanding models have exhibited outstanding performance in each task, there remains a dearth of a unified framework capable of simultaneously addressing multiple tasks, which is a promising direction for the next generation of AI. To this end, in this paper, we propose a single unified framework, coined as Temporal2Seq, to formulate the output of these temporal video understanding tasks as a sequence of discrete tokens. With this unified token representation, Temporal2Seq can train a generalist model within a single architecture on different video understanding tasks. In the absence of multi-task learning (MTL) benchmarks, we compile a comprehensive co-training dataset by borrowing the datasets from TAD, TAS, and GEBD tasks. We evaluate our Temporal2Seq generalist model on the corresponding test sets of three tasks, demonstrating that Temporal2Seq can produce reasonable results on various tasks and achieve advantages compared with single-task training on this framework. We also investigate the generalization performance of our generalist model on new datasets from different tasks, which yields superior performance to the specific model.

</details>


### [115] [STEAM: Squeeze and Transform Enhanced Attention Module](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09023)
*Rishabh Sabharwal,Ram Samarth B B,Parikshit Singh Rathore,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STEAM模块，一种基于图神经网络的轻量级通道-空间注意力机制，通过图多头变换器建模通道和空间关系，并引入输出引导池化(OGP)高效捕获空间上下文，在极小计算开销下提升CNN表示能力，在图像分类、目标检测和实例分割任务上超越ECA和GCT等主流注意力模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通道和空间注意力机制虽能增强CNN表示能力，但往往增加参数量和计算成本；近期工作虽聚焦于高效通道注意力建模，但缺乏对通道和空间注意力的联合高效建模方法。

Method: 提出STEAM模块：基于图多头变换器思想，以常参数方式同时建模通道和空间注意力；引入输出引导池化(OGP)高效捕获空间上下文信息以增强空间注意力。

Result: 在ResNet-50上仅以微小GFLOPs增加实现2%精度提升；在精度上超越ECA和GCT等领先模块，同时GFLOPs降低三倍。

Conclusion: 基于图的注意力建模方法能以极低计算成本同时实现通道和空间注意力的高效融合，为CNN注意力机制设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Channel and spatial attention mechanisms introduced in earlier work enhance the representational capabilities of deep convolutional neural networks (CNNs) but often increase parameter and computational costs. While recent approaches focus solely on efficient feature context modeling for channel attention, we aim to model both channel and spatial attention comprehensively with minimal parameters and reduced computation. Leveraging the principles of relational modeling in graphs, we introduce a constant-parameter module, \textit{STEAM: Squeeze and Transform Enhanced Attention Module}, which integrates channel and spatial attention to enhance the representation power of CNNs. To our knowledge, we are the first to propose a graph-based approach for modeling both channel and spatial attention, utilizing concepts from multi-head graph transformers. Additionally, we introduce \textit{Output Guided Pooling} (OGP), which efficiently captures spatial context to further enhance spatial attention. We extensively evaluate STEAM for large-scale image classification, object detection and instance segmentation on standard benchmark datasets. STEAM achieves a \(2\%\) increase in accuracy over the standard ResNet-50 model with only a meager increase in GFLOPs. Furthermore, STEAM outperforms the leading modules, ECA and GCT, in terms of accuracy while achieving a threefold reduction in GFLOPs. The code will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [116] [Contour Field based Elliptical Shape Prior for the Segment Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12556)
*Xinyu Zhao,Faqiang Wang,Li Cui,Yuping Duan,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种将椭圆形状先验融入SAM分割模型的方法，通过变分方法和参数化椭圆轮廓场约束分割结果为椭圆区域，实验表明相比原始SAM有性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习分割方法（包括SAM）难以高效生成符合椭圆形状的分割结果，而椭圆先验对医学和自然图像中的特定任务至关重要，因此需要一种能将椭圆形状先验有效集成到SAM中的方法。

Method: 提出参数化椭圆轮廓场约束分割结果；利用对偶算法融合图像特征、椭圆先验和空间正则化先验；将SAM分解为四个数学子问题，将变分椭圆先验嵌入设计新的SAM网络结构。

Result: 在特定图像数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法相比原始SAM有性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过变分方法将椭圆形状先验融入SAM可以有效提升特定场景下的分割准确性，为基于先验形状的深度学习分割提供了新思路。

Abstract: The elliptical shape prior information plays a vital role in improving the accuracy of image segmentation for specific tasks in medical and natural images. Existing deep learning-based segmentation methods, including the Segment Anything Model (SAM), often struggle to produce segmentation results with elliptical shapes efficiently. This paper proposes a new approach to integrate the prior of elliptical shapes into the deep learning-based SAM image segmentation techniques using variational methods. The proposed method establishes a parameterized elliptical contour field, which constrains the segmentation results to align with predefined elliptical contours. Utilizing the dual algorithm, the model seamlessly integrates image features with elliptical priors and spatial regularization priors, thereby greatly enhancing segmentation accuracy. By decomposing SAM into four mathematical sub-problems, we integrate the variational ellipse prior to design a new SAM network structure, ensuring that the segmentation output of SAM consists of elliptical regions. Experimental results on some specific image datasets demonstrate an improvement over the original SAM.

</details>


### [117] [Diffusion-based Cumulative Adversarial Purification for Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03933)
*Jia Fu,Yongtao Wu,Yihang Chen,Kunyu Peng,Xiao Zhang,Volkan Cevher,Sepideh Pashami,Anders Holst*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffCAP，一种基于扩散模型的对抗性净化策略，通过理论证明对抗效应在扩散过程中单调衰减，并利用噪声注入与VLM嵌入相似度阈值作为自适应准则，有效净化多模态大模型中的对抗扰动。在6个数据集、3种VLM和多种攻击强度下，DiffCAP显著优于现有防御方法，同时降低了超参数调优复杂度和扩散时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）在现实应用中面临对抗性扰动的严重威胁，这些扰动虽对人类不可见，却能显著改变模型输出，导致错误解释和决策。现有防御方法存在不足，亟需一种鲁棒且实用的净化策略。

Method: DiffCAP利用扩散模型的正向过程注入噪声，以VLM嵌入的相似度阈值作为自适应准则，在对抗效应单调衰减的理论指导下确定净化终止点，再通过反向扩散恢复干净的表示用于VLM推理。论文同时理论证明了前向扩散中的可恢复区域并量化了语义变化收敛率。

Result: 在6个数据集、3种VLM和多种攻击强度的三个任务场景下，DiffCAP显著优于现有防御技术，大幅降低超参数调优复杂度和所需扩散时间，加速了去噪过程。

Conclusion: DiffCAP结合理论证明与实验支持，为在对抗环境下安全部署VLM提供了一种鲁棒且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown remarkable capabilities in multimodal understanding, yet their susceptibility to adversarial perturbations poses a significant threat to their reliability in real-world applications. Despite often being imperceptible to humans, these perturbations can drastically alter model outputs, leading to erroneous interpretations and decisions. This paper introduces DiffCAP, a novel diffusion-based purification strategy that can effectively neutralize adversarial corruptions in VLMs. We theoretically establish a provable recovery region in the forward diffusion process and meanwhile quantify the convergence rate of semantic variation with respect to VLMs. These findings manifest that adversarial effects monotonically fade as diffusion unfolds. Guided by this principle, DiffCAP leverages noise injection with a similarity threshold of VLM embeddings as an adaptive criterion, before reverse diffusion restores a clean and reliable representation for VLM inference. Through extensive experiments across six datasets with three VLMs under varying attack strengths in three task scenarios, we show that DiffCAP outperforms existing defense techniques by a substantial margin. Notably, DiffCAP significantly reduces both hyperparameter tuning complexity and the required diffusion time, thereby accelerating the denoising process. Equipped with theorems and empirical support, DiffCAP provides a robust and practical solution for securely deploying VLMs in adversarial environments. The source code is available at https://github.com/JasonFu1998/DiffCAP.

</details>


### [118] [Towards Conditional Feature Alignment for Cross-Domain Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17137)
*Zhuonan Liang,Dongnan Liu,Jianan Fan,Yaxuan Song,Qiang Qu,Runnan Chen,Yu Yao,Peng Fu,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出条件特征对齐（CFA）框架，通过密度标签或伪密度构建条件（如前景/背景、密度等级），仅在匹配条件下进行特征对齐，以解决跨域目标计数中密度组成变化导致的标准域对齐方法失效问题。在UDA和DG场景下，人群和细胞计数基准上均取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准特征对齐方法追求全局域不变性，但跨域计数中密度组成（背景、稀疏前景、密集前景的比例）在不同域间存在差异且与任务相关；全局对齐会抑制这种有意义的密度分布变化，反而损害模型在目标域上的性能。

Method: 提出条件特征对齐（CFA）框架：（1）利用密度标注或伪密度预测构建条件（前景/背景二分条件或密度等级多分条件）；（2）仅在源域和目标域中匹配条件之间进行特征对齐，而非全边际分布对齐；（3）UDA场景：源域用真实标注、目标域用停止梯度的伪密度图，结合全图像一致性正则化进行条件级对抗对齐；（4）DG场景：结合MPCount，强制生成视图间的条件级记忆一致性。

Result: 在JHU-CROWD++ FH→SN上，CFA-DG将MAE/RMSE从MPCount的216.3/421.4降至90.5/169.9；在多种UDA和DG基准（人群计数、细胞计数）上表现竞争或更优，尤其在大天气和密度偏移场景下效果显著。

Conclusion: 条件级对齐是跨域计数中一种有前景的设计原则，能够在消除域间不匹配的同时保留密度组成变化这一任务相关信息。

Abstract: Object counting models often degrade under cross-domain deployment because density composition varies across domains and is itself task-relevant. Standard feature alignment methods tend to suppress such variation by encouraging global domain invariance, which can be harmful when source and target domains contain different proportions of background, sparse foreground, and dense foreground. We propose Conditional Feature Alignment (CFA), a cross-domain counting framework that aligns representations within label-induced conditions rather than across full marginal feature distributions. Given density annotations or pseudo-density predictions, CFA constructs foreground/background or density-level conditions and aligns only features belonging to matching conditions. We formalise this idea through a conditional divergence perspective, showing that conditional alignment removes within-condition discrepancy while preserving condition-marginal density shift. For unsupervised domain adaptation, CFA estimates source conditions from annotations and target conditions from detached pseudo-density maps, then performs condition-wise adversarial alignment with full-image consistency regularisation. For source-domain generalisation, we instantiate the same principle with MPCount by enforcing condition-wise memory-consistency between generated source-domain views. Experiments on crowd and cell counting benchmarks show competitive or improved performance across diverse UDA and DG settings. For example, on JHU-CROWD++ FH$\rightarrow$SN, CFA-DG reduces MAE/RMSE from MPCount's 216.3/421.4 to 90.5/169.9, indicating that condition-wise alignment is especially effective under large weather- and density-induced shifts. These results suggest that condition-wise alignment is a promising design principle for domain-adaptive counting.

</details>


### [119] [Periodic-MAE: Periodic Video Masked Autoencoder for rPPG Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21855)
*Jiho Choi,Sang Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Periodic-MAE，一种基于掩码自编码器的自监督框架，通过周期性感知帧掩码策略和生理带限约束，从无标签面部视频中学习泛化的时空表征，用于远程光电容积描记法(rPPG)估计。实验表明该方法在跨数据集和真实场景下显著提升了rPPG估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有rPPG方法依赖大量带标签数据且泛化能力有限，缺乏一种能够从无标签面部视频中学习通用且具有生理意义表征的自监督方法。

Method: 1) 采用掩码自编码器(MAE)框架，通过重构被掩盖的视频令牌学习高维面部表征；2) 引入周期性感知帧掩码策略，基于视频重采样使编码器学习准周期时间模式；3) 集成生理带限约束，利用脉冲信号在频域的稀疏性引导表征学习朝向生理有意义方向。

Result: 在PURE、UBFC-rPPG、MMPD、V4V四个基准数据集以及真实场景数据集上，Periodic-MAE持续提升了rPPG估计性能，尤其在跨数据集和真实场景评估中表现突出。

Conclusion: 周期性感知掩码策略和生理带限约束能够有效引导自监督表征学习对齐rPPG信号的周期性特征，从而提升模型在复杂场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Periodic-MAE, a self-supervised framework for learning generalizable spatio-temporal representations of periodic physiological signals from unlabeled facial videos. The proposed method leverages a masked autoencoder (MAE), which learns high-dimensional facial representations by reconstructing masked video tokens without relying on remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) specific supervision. To explicitly align representation learning with the characteristics of rPPG, we introduce a periodicity-aware frame masking strategy based on video resampling, enabling the encoder to learn representations that capture quasi-periodic temporal patterns relevant to pulse signal estimation. In addition, physiological bandlimit constraints are integrated into the MAE pre-training framework, exploiting the sparsity of pulse signals in the frequency domain to guide the learned representations toward physiologically meaningful patterns. After pre-training, the learned representations are transferred to downstream rPPG estimation, where the encoder serves as a generic feature extractor for recovering pulse-related signals from facial videos. We conduct extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets, including PURE, UBFC-rPPG, MMPD, and V4V. Moreover, we evaluate the proposed approach on a real-world rPPG dataset collected under unconstrained lighting conditions and subject motion. Experimental results demonstrate that Periodic-MAE consistently improves rPPG estimation performance, particularly in challenging cross-dataset and real-world evaluation settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/ziiho08/Periodic-MAE.

</details>


### [120] [RelayFormer: A Unified Local-Global Attention Framework for Scalable Image and Video Manipulation Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09459)
*Wen Huang,Jiarui Yang,Tao Dai,Jiawei Li,Shaoxiong Zhan,Bin Wang,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RelayFormer，一种统一框架，通过将输入划分为固定大小子图像并引入全局局部中继（GLR）令牌与中继注意力机制，有效应对视觉篡改定位任务中分辨率多样性和图像/视频统一建模两大挑战，在多个基准上实现优越性能与高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉篡改定位方法面临两个核心问题：一是分辨率多样性导致的重采样/填充会破坏微妙取证线索并引入额外计算开销；二是将图像空间模型扩展到视频时空输入的困难，往往需要为两种数据类型维护分离的架构。

Method: 提出RelayFormer统一框架，将输入划分为固定大小的子图像，并设计全局局部中继（GLR）令牌，通过中继注意力机制传播结构化上下文，实现全局线索（如语义或时序一致性）的高效交换，同时保留细粒度篡改伪影。

Result: 在多个不同基准上，RelayFormer展现出优于现有方法的性能与强效计算效率，实现了无需插值或过度填充的分辨率自适应性、图像与视频的统一处理，以及准确性与计算成本之间的良好平衡。

Conclusion: RelayFormer通过分辨率自适应与统一图像/视频建模，为视觉篡改定位任务提供了一种高效且准确的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Visual manipulation localization (VML) aims to identify tampered regions in images and videos, a task that has become increasingly challenging with the rise of advanced editing tools. Existing methods face two central issues. The first is resolution diversity. Resizing or padding can distort subtle forensic cues and introduce unnecessary computational cost. The second is the difficulty of extending spatial models for images to spatio-temporal inputs in videos, which often results in maintaining separate architectures for the two data types. To address these challenges, we propose RelayFormer, a unified framework that adapts to varying resolutions and naturally handles both static and temporal visual data. RelayFormer partitions inputs into fixed-size sub-images and introduces Global Local Relay (GLR) tokens that propagate structured context through a relay-based attention mechanism. This design enables efficient exchange of global cues, such as semantic or temporal consistency, while preserving fine-grained manipulation artifacts. Unlike prior approaches that depend on uniform resizing or sparse attention, RelayFormer scales to variable resolutions and video sequences with minimal overhead. Experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate superior performance and strong efficiency, combining resolution adaptivity without interpolation or excessive padding, unified processing for images and videos, and a favorable balance between accuracy and computational cost. Code is available at~\href{https://github.com/WenOOI/RelayFormer}{https://github.com/WenOOI/RelayFormer}.

</details>


### [121] [How Auxiliary Reasoning Unleashes GUI Grounding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11548)
*Weiming Li,Yan Shao,Jing Yang,Yujing Lu,Ling Zhong,Yuhan Wang,Min Yu,Tongxiao Ruan,Manni Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出三种零样本辅助推理方法（坐标轴、网格、标记交叉点），通过提供显式空间线索来激活VLM的隐含接地能力，无需微调即可显著提升GUI接地性能，在多个基准上达到接近最优微调方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLM在GUI接地任务上表现不佳，因为缺乏特定优化。虽然VLM在Pointing Game中展现出潜在的接地能力，但在输出显式坐标时却表现不佳。现有微调方法数据标注成本高，因此需要一种零样本的解决方案来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出三种零样本辅助推理方法：在输入图像中提供显式空间线索，包括坐标轴（Axes）、网格（Grid）和带标签的交叉点（Mark-Grid Scaffold），帮助VLM更好地表达其隐式空间理解能力。

Result: 在四个GUI接地基准上评估了七种开源和专有VLM。Mark-Grid Scaffold将Gemini-3.1-Pro从直接推理的11.72%提升到ScreenSpot-v2上的95.20%，在ScreenSpot上达到SOTA，在ScreenSpot-v2和UI-I2E-Bench上接近最强微调方法。

Conclusion: 通过注入显式空间线索的零样本方法可以有效激活VLM固有的空间理解潜力，无需微调即可在GUI接地任务上取得显著提升，为构建GUI Agent提供了低成本且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) grounding is a fundamental task for building GUI agents. However, general vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with this task due to a lack of specific optimization. We identify a key gap in this paper: while VLMs exhibit significant latent grounding potential, as demonstrated by their performance measured by Pointing Game, they underperform when tasked with outputting explicit coordinates. To address this discrepancy and bypass the high data and annotation costs of current fine-tuning approaches, we propose three zero-shot auxiliary reasoning methods. By providing explicit spatial cues such as axes, grids and labeled intersections as part of the input image, these methods enable VLMs to better articulate their implicit spatial understanding capabilities. We evaluate these methods on four GUI grounding benchmarks across seven open-source and proprietary VLMs. Experimental results show substantial gains from auxiliary reasoning. Mark-Grid Scaffold boosts Gemini-3.1-Pro from 11.72\% under direct inference to 95.20\% on ScreenSpot-v2, achieves state-of-the-art performance on ScreenSpot, and approaches the strongest fine-tuned methods on ScreenSpot-v2 and UI-I2E-Bench. Our code is available at https://github.com/liweim/AuxiliaryReasoning.

</details>


### [122] [MARIC: Multi-Agent Reasoning for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14860)
*Wonduk Seo,Minhyeong Yu,Hyunjin An,Seunghyun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MARIC框架，将图像分类重构为多智能体协作推理过程，通过提纲智能体分析全局主题、三个方面智能体提取细粒度特征、推理智能体综合反思，在4个基准数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像分类依赖参数密集的模型训练和大规模标注数据，而现有VLM虽缓解了部分限制，但受限于单次表征，无法捕捉视觉内容的互补方面。

Method: MARIC包含三个核心组件：1) Outliner Agent分析图像全局主题并生成目标提示；2) Aspect Agents基于提示沿不同视觉维度提取细粒度描述；3) Reasoning Agent通过集成反思步骤综合互补输出，生成统一分类表征。

Result: 在4个多样化图像分类基准数据集上，MARIC显著优于基线方法，验证了多智能体视觉推理在鲁棒且可解释的图像分类中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将任务显式分解为多视角并鼓励反思性综合，MARIC缓解了参数密集型训练和单体型VLM推理的缺陷，实现了更鲁棒和可解释的图像分类。

Abstract: Image classification has traditionally relied on parameter-intensive model training, requiring large-scale annotated datasets and extensive fine tuning to achieve competitive performance. While recent vision language models (VLMs) alleviate some of these constraints, they remain limited by their reliance on single pass representations, often failing to capture complementary aspects of visual content. In this paper, we introduce Multi Agent based Reasoning for Image Classification (MARIC), a multi agent framework that reformulates image classification as a collaborative reasoning process. MARIC first utilizes an Outliner Agent to analyze the global theme of the image and generate targeted prompts. Based on these prompts, three Aspect Agents extract fine grained descriptions along distinct visual dimensions. Finally, a Reasoning Agent synthesizes these complementary outputs through integrated reflection step, producing a unified representation for classification. By explicitly decomposing the task into multiple perspectives and encouraging reflective synthesis, MARIC mitigates the shortcomings of both parameter-heavy training and monolithic VLM reasoning. Experiments on 4 diverse image classification benchmark datasets demonstrate that MARIC significantly outperforms baselines, highlighting the effectiveness of multi-agent visual reasoning for robust and interpretable image classification.

</details>


### [123] [SDQM: Synthetic Data Quality Metric for Object Detection Dataset Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06596)
*Ayush Zenith,Arnold Zumbrun,Neel Raut,Jing Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出合成数据集质量度量（SDQM），用于评估目标检测任务中合成数据质量，无需模型训练收敛即可高效评估，与YOLO11的mAP高度相关，优于现有度量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型性能严重依赖训练数据，但大规模高质量标注数据集稀缺。合成数据虽能缓解这一问题，但缺乏有效的质量评估指标，导致难以高效生成和筛选高质量的合成数据集。

Method: 提出SDQM（Synthetic Dataset Quality Metric），无需模型训练收敛即可评估合成数据质量；通过实验验证SDQM与YOLO11的mAP得分之间的相关性，并与先前度量方法进行对比。

Result: SDQM与YOLO11目标检测模型的mAP得分呈现强相关性，而先前度量方法仅表现出中等或弱相关性；SDQM还能提供改进数据集质量的可行见解，减少昂贵的迭代训练需求。

Conclusion: SDQM是一种可扩展且高效的合成数据质量评估指标，为评估合成数据设立了新标准，特别适用于资源受限的目标检测任务。

Abstract: The performance of machine learning models depends heavily on training data. The scarcity of large-scale, well-annotated datasets poses significant challenges in creating robust models. To address this, synthetic data generated through simulations and generative models has emerged as a promising solution, enhancing dataset diversity and improving the performance, reliability, and resilience of models. However, evaluating the quality of this generated data requires an effective metric. We introduce the Synthetic Dataset Quality Metric (SDQM) to assess data quality for object detection tasks without requiring model training to converge. This metric enables more efficient generation and selection of synthetic datasets, addressing a key challenge in resource-constrained object detection tasks. In our experiments, SDQM demonstrated a strong correlation with the mean average precision (mAP) scores of YOLO11, a leading object detection model, whereas previous metrics only exhibited moderate or weak correlations. In addition, it provides actionable insights into improving dataset quality, minimizing the need for costly iterative training. This scalable and efficient metric sets a new standard for evaluating synthetic data. The code for SDQM is available at https://github.com/ayushzenith/SDQM

</details>


### [124] [Physics-Driven Spatiotemporal Modeling for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08073)
*Shuhai Zhang,ZiHao Lian,Jiahao Yang,Daiyuan Li,Guoxuan Pang,Feng Liu,Bo Han,Shutao Li,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于物理概率流守恒原理的AI生成视频检测范式，通过归一化时空梯度(NSG)统计量捕捉自然视频与生成视频的物理偏差，利用预训练扩散模型构建NSG估计器，并基于最大均值差异(MMD)设计检测方法NSG-VD，在召回率和F1分数上分别超越现有最优方法16.00%和10.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频（如Sora）已达到近乎完美的视觉真实性，迫切需要可靠的检测机制，但现有方法在建模高维时空动态和识别违反物理规律的细微异常方面面临重大挑战。

Method: 提出基于概率流守恒原理的物理驱动检测范式：1）定义归一化时空梯度(NSG)统计量，量化空间概率梯度与时间密度变化的比值；2）利用预训练扩散模型，通过空间梯度近似和运动感知时间建模构建NSG估计器；3）基于最大均值差异(MMD)计算测试视频与真实视频NSG特征的距离作为检测指标；4）理论推导了真实与生成视频NSG特征距离的上界。

Result: NSG-VD在召回率上超越现有最优方法16.00%，F1分数上提升10.75%，验证了方法的优越性能。

Conclusion: 基于物理概率流守恒的NSG统计量能有效捕捉AI生成视频的物理异常，为AI视频检测提供了新的理论范式，实验证明该方法显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: AI-generated videos have achieved near-perfect visual realism (e.g., Sora), urgently necessitating reliable detection mechanisms. However, detecting such videos faces significant challenges in modeling high-dimensional spatiotemporal dynamics and identifying subtle anomalies that violate physical laws. In this paper, we propose the first physics-driven AI-generated video detection paradigm based on probability flow conservation principles. Specifically, we propose a statistic called Normalized Spatiotemporal Gradient (NSG), which quantifies the ratio of spatial probability gradients to temporal density changes, explicitly capturing deviations from natural video dynamics. Leveraging pre-trained diffusion models, we develop an NSG estimator through spatial gradients approximation and motion-aware temporal modeling without complex motion decomposition while preserving physical constraints. Building on this, we propose an NSG-based video detection method (NSG-VD) that computes the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) between NSG features of the test and real videos as a detection metric. Last, we derive an upper bound of NSG feature distances between real and generated videos, proving that generated videos exhibit amplified discrepancies due to distributional shifts. Extensive experiments confirm that NSG-VD outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by 16.00% in Recall and 10.75% in F1-Score, validating the superior performance of NSG-VD. The source code is available at https://github.com/ZSHsh98/NSG-VD.

</details>


### [125] [Moving Beyond Diffusion: Hierarchy-to-Hierarchy Autoregression for fMRI-to-Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22335)
*Xu Zhang,Ruijie Quan,Wenguan Wang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MindHier框架，通过层级式fMRI编码器、层级对齐和尺度感知的粗到细神经引导策略，实现从fMRI信号到图像的快速、高质量重建，在NSD数据集上语义保真度更优、推理速度快4.67倍且结果更确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散方法将fMRI活动映射为单一神经嵌入作为静态引导，导致层级神经信息坍塌，且与图像重建的阶段依赖需求不匹配。

Method: 提出MindHier：1) 层级fMRI编码器提取多级神经嵌入；2) 层级间对齐机制强制与CLIP特征的逐层对应；3) 尺度感知的粗到细神经引导策略，在匹配尺度将嵌入注入自回归过程。

Result: 在NSD数据集上，MindHier在语义保真度上优于扩散基线，推理速度提升4.67倍，结果更具确定性。

Conclusion: MindHier通过模拟人类视觉感知的层级重建过程（先全局语义后局部细节），为基于扩散的方法提供了高效且认知对齐的替代方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing visual stimuli from fMRI signals is a central challenge bridging machine learning and neuroscience. Recent diffusion-based methods typically map fMRI activity to a single neural embedding, using it as static guidance throughout the entire generation process. However, this fixed guidance collapses hierarchical neural information and is misaligned with the stage-dependent demands of image reconstruction. In response, we propose MindHier, a coarse-to-fine fMRI-to-image reconstruction framework built on scale-wise autoregressive modeling. MindHier introduces three components: a Hierarchical fMRI Encoder to extract multi-level neural embeddings, a Hierarchy-to-Hierarchy Alignment scheme to enforce layer-wise correspondence with CLIP features, and a Scale-Aware Coarse-to-Fine Neural Guidance strategy to inject these embeddings into autoregression at matching scales. These designs make MindHier an efficient and cognitively aligned alternative to diffusion-based methods by enabling a hierarchical reconstruction process that synthesizes global semantics before refining local details, akin to human visual perception. Extensive experiments on the NSD dataset show that MindHier achieves superior semantic fidelity, 4.67$\times$ faster inference, and more deterministic results than the diffusion-based baselines.

</details>


### [126] [UI2Code^N: UI-to-Code Generation as Interactive Visual Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.08195)
*Zhen Yang,Wenyi Hong,Mingde Xu,Xinyue Fan,Weihan Wang,Jiale Cheng,Xiaotao Gu,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UI2Code^N，将UI截图转代码重构为基于视觉反馈的迭代优化问题，并提出相对视觉策略优化（RVPO）方法，在多项基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UI-to-code方法多采用单次生成范式，与实际UI开发中迭代、反馈驱动的过程不匹配。

Method: 提出RVPO（相对视觉策略优化），一种基于偏好的强化学习方法，在渲染候选间优化相对视觉排序；通过持续预训练、监督微调和强化学习训练9B开源模型UI2Code^N。

Result: 在UI草稿、UI润色和UI编辑基准上达到SOTA，超越更大模型，性能通过迭代视觉优化持续提升。

Conclusion: 将UI-to-code重构为交互式视觉优化问题，并通过RVPO有效利用渲染视觉反馈进行迭代改进，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: UI-to-code aims to translate UI screenshots into executable front-end code. Despite progress with vision-language models (VLMs), most existing methods formulate UI-to-code as a single-pass generation, which mismatches real-world UI development that is inherently iterative and feedback-driven. We reformulate UI-to-code as an interactive visual optimization problem, where code generation is embedded in a closed-loop process of execution, visual inspection, and iterative refinement driven by rendered visual feedback. To address the non-differentiability of visual objectives and the noise of absolute visual evaluators, we propose Relative Visual Policy Optimization (RVPO), a preference-based reinforcement learning method that optimizes relative visual rankings among rendered candidates under execution feedback. We instantiate this paradigm in UI2Code^N, an open-source 9B model trained via continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on UI drafting, UI polishing, and UI editing benchmarks, even outperforming larger models, with performance consistently improving through iterative visual optimization. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/zai-org/UI2Code_N.

</details>


### [127] [EvoLMM: Self-Evolving Large Multimodal Models with Continuous Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16672)
*Omkar Thawakar,Shravan Venkatraman,Ritesh Thawkar,Abdelrahman Shaker,Hisham Cholakkal,Rao Muhammad Anwer,Salman Khan,Fahad Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoLMM，一种无需人工标注或外部奖励模型的自演进框架，通过单个骨干模型实例化两个协作智能体（Proposer和Solver），在连续自我奖励过程中提升多模态大模型的推理能力，在多个数学推理基准上取得约3%的稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大模型训练流程大多依赖人工标注数据或外部验证的奖励模型，限制了模型的自主性和可扩展性。本文旨在以完全无监督的方式提升LMM的推理能力，无需任何标注数据或奖励蒸馏。

Method: 提出EvoLMM框架，从单个骨干模型中实例化两个协作智能体：Proposer负责生成多样化、基于图像的问题，Solver通过内部一致性解决这些问题；通过连续自我奖励过程驱动学习，不依赖真实标签或人工判断。

Result: 以Qwen2.5-VL为基础模型，在ChartQA、MathVista、MathVision等数学推理基准上，仅使用原始训练图像即可获得约3%的稳定提升。

Conclusion: 该简单而有效的方法将为未来完全无监督的自我改进LMM研究提供坚实的基线。

Abstract: Recent advances in large multimodal models (LMMs) have enabled impressive reasoning and perception abilities, yet most existing training pipelines still depend on human-curated data or externally verified reward models, limiting their autonomy and scalability. In this work, we strive to improve LMM reasoning capabilities in a purely unsupervised fashion (without any annotated data or reward distillation). To this end, we propose a self-evolving framework, named EvoLMM, that instantiates two cooperative agents from a single backbone model: a Proposer, which generates diverse, image-grounded questions, and a Solver, which solves them through internal consistency, where learning proceeds through a continuous self-rewarding process. This dynamic feedback encourages both the generation of informative queries and the refinement of structured reasoning without relying on ground-truth or human judgments. When using the popular Qwen2.5-VL as the base model, our EvoLMM yields consistent gains upto $\sim$3\% on multimodal math-reasoning benchmarks, including ChartQA, MathVista, and MathVision, using only raw training images. We hope our simple yet effective approach will serve as a solid baseline easing future research in self-improving LMMs in a fully-unsupervised fashion. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/mbzuai-oryx/EvoLMM.

</details>


### [128] [The N-Body Problem: Parallel Execution from Single-Person Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11393)
*Zhifan Zhu,Yifei Huang,Yoichi Sato,Dima Damen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出“N体问题”：从单人第一人称视频中，预测N个人如何并行完成相同任务集。通过结构化提示策略引导视觉语言模型推理3D环境、物体使用和时序依赖，在EPIC-Kitchens和HD-EPIC数据集上，相比基线提示，动作覆盖率提升45%，碰撞率、物体冲突和因果冲突分别降低51%、52%和55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能凭直觉并行化复杂活动，但现有模型无法仅从单人观察视频中预测多人如何协同执行任务。现有方法若简单将视频片段分配给多人，会违反物理约束（如两人使用同一物体或占据同一空间），因此需要一种能同时优化性能（加速比、任务覆盖率）和可行性（空间碰撞、物体冲突、因果约束）的预测方法。

Method: 形式化定义N体问题，并提出一套评估指标（加速比、任务覆盖率、空间碰撞、物体冲突、因果约束）。设计结构化提示策略，引导视觉语言模型（VLM）推理3D场景、物体使用状态及时序依赖关系，从而生成可行的并行执行方案。

Result: 在100段来自EPIC-Kitchens和HD-EPIC的视频上，针对N=2的设置，结构化提示相比基线提示使Gemini 2.5 Pro的动作覆盖率提升45%，碰撞率、物体冲突和因果冲突分别降低51%、52%和55%。

Conclusion: 结构化提示策略能有效引导VLM进行多人体感并行化推理，兼顾性能与可行性，为从单视角视频理解群体协同任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Humans can intuitively parallelise complex activities, but can a model predict this from observing a single person? Given one egocentric video, we introduce the N-Body Problem: predicting how N individuals, can hypothetically perform the same set of tasks. The goal is to maximise speed-up, but naive assignment of video segments to individuals often violates real-world constraints, leading to physically impossible scenarios like two people using the same object or occupying the same space. To quantify this, we formalise the N-Body Problem and propose a suite of metrics to evaluate both performance (speed-up, task coverage) and feasibility (spatial collisions, object conflicts and causal constraints). As a proof of concept, we introduce a structured prompting strategy that guides a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to reason about the 3D environment, object usage, and temporal dependencies, producing a viable parallel execution. On 100 videos from EPIC-Kitchens and HD-EPIC, for $N = 2$, our structured prompt improves action coverage by 45% over a baseline prompt for Gemini 2.5 Pro, while simultaneously slashing collision rates, object and causal conflicts by 51%, 52% and 55% respectively.

</details>


### [129] [RSTR: Reducing SpatioTemporal Redundancy in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14096)
*Ruitong Sun,Tianze Yang,Wei Niu,Jin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RSTR提出了一种联合减少扩散Transformer时空冗余的框架，通过进化搜索发现稀疏引导调度（阶段1）和自适应秩分配（阶段2），在DiT-XL/2、PixArt-α、FLUX和Qwen-Image上实现50%-70%的计算节省，同时保持或提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）在图像生成中表现出色，但高昂的计算成本阻碍了其部署。现有方法未联合考虑时间维度（CFG的双前向传播冗余）和空间维度（不同Transformer块在不同引导下的异质敏感性）的冗余问题。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段1通过进化搜索发现稀疏引导调度（仅在关键步骤使用引导，并采用可变引导尺度补偿跳过的步骤）；阶段2通过自适应秩分配，根据Transformer区域对引导的敏感度分配不同的校准容量。

Result: 在DiT-XL/2、PixArt-α、FLUX和Qwen-Image上实现50%-70%的计算节省且质量不变或提升。DiT-XL/2上节省57%计算并提升15% FID；Qwen-Image上实现3.43倍加速且质量不变。

Conclusion: RSTR是首个联合减少扩散Transformer时空冗余的框架，通过进化搜索和自适应秩分配有效降低计算成本，实验证明在多个模型上均能大幅节省计算并保持或提升质量。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have achieved remarkable success in image generation, yet their deployment is hindered by high computational costs. We identify two sources of redundancy. First, temporal redundancy: Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) applies costly dual forward passes at every timestep, yet guidance matters only at specific steps, and variable scales at critical steps can compensate for skipping others. Second, spatial redundancy: under variable guidance, different transformer blocks exhibit heterogeneous sensitivity, yet uniform calibration across all blocks wastes computation while failing to address their varying requirements. We present RSTR, the first framework to jointly reduce spatiotemporal redundancy in diffusion transformers. Stage-1 addresses temporal redundancy through evolutionary search, discovering sparse guidance schedules with variable scales. Stage-2 addresses spatial redundancy through adaptive rank allocation, assigning calibration capacities to transformer regions based on their sensitivity. Experiments on DiT-XL/2, PixArt-$α$, FLUX, and state-of-the-art Qwen-Image demonstrate 50%-70% compute savings while maintaining or improving quality. On DiT-XL/2, RSTR achieves 57% savings with 15% FID improvement; on Qwen-Image, 3.43$\times$ speedup with preserved quality.

</details>


### [130] [CountZES: Counting via Zero-Shot Exemplar Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16415)
*Muhammad Ibraheem Siddiqui,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CountZES提出了一种仅推理阶段的零样本目标计数方法，通过三个协同阶段（DAE、DGE、FCE）选择多样化的示例，有效解决了现有方法在密集场景中的语义噪声和示例质量差的问题，在多个数据集上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本计数方法要么依赖开集检测器（OVD）在密集场景中遭受语义噪声、外观多样性和多实例提议的困扰，要么采用随机图像块采样无法准确描绘目标实例，导致选取的示例代表性差，计数不准确。

Method: CountZES包含三个协同阶段：1) 检测锚定示例（DAE）——精炼OVD检测结果以分离精确的单实例示例；2) 密度引导示例（DGE）——引入密度驱动的自监督范式，选择统计一致且语义紧凑的示例；3) 特征共识示例（FCE）——通过特征空间聚类增强视觉一致性。三者共同形成互补的示例集，平衡文本定位、计数一致性和特征代表性。

Result: 在多个不同数据集上的实验表明，CountZES在零样本计数方法中表现优越，并能有效跨域泛化。

Conclusion: CountZES通过仅推理阶段的示例选择策略，显著提升了零样本目标计数的准确性和鲁棒性，为无需额外训练的零样本计数提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Object counting in complex scenes is particularly challenging in the zero-shot (ZS) setting, where instances of unseen categories are counted using only a class name. Existing ZS counting methods that infer exemplars from text often rely on off-the-shelf open-vocabulary detectors (OVDs), which in dense scenes suffer from semantic noise, appearance variability, and multi-instance proposals. Alternatively, random image-patch sampling is employed, which fails to accurately delineate object instances. Since counting is sensitive to exemplar quality, such selection strategies often yield poorly representative exemplars, leading to inaccurate count estimation. To address these issues, we propose CountZES, an inference-only approach for object counting via ZS exemplar selection. CountZES discovers diverse exemplars through three synergistic stages: Detection-Anchored Exemplar (DAE), Density-Guided Exemplar (DGE), and Feature-Consensus Exemplar (FCE). DAE refines OVD detections to isolate precise single-instance exemplars. DGE introduces a density-driven, self-supervised paradigm to identify statistically consistent and semantically compact exemplars, while FCE reinforces visual coherence through feature-space clustering. Together, these stages yield a complementary exemplar set that balances textual grounding, count consistency, and feature representativeness. Experiments on diverse datasets demonstrate CountZES superior performance among ZOC methods while generalizing effectively across domains.

</details>


### [131] [Higher order PCA-like rotation-invariant features for detailed shape descriptors modulo rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03326)
*Jarek Duda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用高阶张量（如三阶中心矩）和多项式乘高斯函数来扩展PCA，以构建更精确的旋转不变形状描述符，并应用于分子形状描述、物体识别和形状相似度度量等领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCA通过协方差矩阵将形状近似为椭球体，可获得旋转不变量（如迹的幂），但真实形状往往复杂得多，椭球近似精度不足，因此需要更强大的形状描述方法。

Method: 将PCA从二阶矩（协方差矩阵）扩展到高阶张量（如三阶中心矩\(p_{abc}\)），以及使用多项式乘高斯函数来生成可解码的、任意高精度的形状描述符，并推导其对应的旋转不变量。

Result: 获得了更高精度的旋转不变形状描述符，能够更准确地刻画复杂形状。

Conclusion: 该方法可用于旋转不变特征提取（如分子形状描述符）、2D图像/3D扫描中的物体识别（甚至3D场景理解），以及无需旋转优化的低成本形状相似度比较。

Abstract: PCA can be used for rotation invariant features, describing a shape with its $p_{ab}=E[(x_i-E[x_a])(x_b-E[x_b])]$ covariance matrix approximating shape by ellipsoid, allowing for rotation invariants like its traces of powers. However, real shapes are usually much more complicated, hence there is proposed its extension to e.g. $p_{abc}=E[(x_a-E[x_a])(x_b-E[x_b])(x_c-E[x_c])]$ order-3 or higher tensors describing central moments, or polynomial times Gaussian allowing decodable shape descriptors of arbitrarily high accuracy, and their analogous rotation invariants. Its practical applications could be rotation-invariant features to include shape modulo rotation e.g. for molecular shape descriptors, or for up to rotation object recognition in 2D images/3D scans maybe also for 3D scene understanding, or shape similarity metric allowing inexpensive comparison of objects modulo rotation avoiding costly optimization over rotations.

</details>


### [132] [OpenVTON-Bench: A Large-Scale High-Resolution Benchmark for Controllable Virtual Try-On Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22725)
*Jin Li,Tao Chen,Kai Wen,Siqi Yin,Shuai Jiang,Weijie Wang,Jingwen Luo,Chenhui Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenVTON-Bench 是一个大规模虚拟试穿评估基准，包含约10万对高分辨率图像（最高1536×1536），采用DINOv3层次聚类和Gemini密集描述构建，并提出多模态评估协议，从5个维度（背景一致性、身份保真度、纹理保真度、形状合理性、整体真实感）结合VLM语义推理与基于SAM3分割和形态学侵蚀的多尺度表示指标进行评测，在人类评判一致性上（Kendall's τ=0.833）显著优于传统指标SSIM（0.611）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟试穿系统视觉质量虽有提升，但缺乏可靠的评估指标和高质量基准数据集——传统指标难以量化细粒度纹理细节和语义一致性，现有数据集在规模和多样性上达不到商业标准。

Method: 1) 构建约10万对高分辨率图像（1536×1536），使用DINOv3层次聚类实现语义平衡采样，结合Gemini密集描述生成，覆盖20个细粒度服装类别；2) 提出多模态评估协议，沿5个可解释维度（背景一致性、身份保真度、纹理保真度、形状合理性、整体真实感）衡量VTON质量，集成VLM语义推理与新提出的基于SAM3分割和形态学侵蚀的多尺度表示指标，分离边界对齐误差与内部纹理伪影。

Result: 提出的评估协议与人类判断高度一致，Kendall's τ相关系数达到0.833，显著优于传统SSIM指标的0.611。

Conclusion: OpenVTON-Bench为VTON领域建立了一个可靠的基准评估框架，在评估指标的人类相关性上大幅超越传统方法，有望推动该领域评估标准化。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models have significantly elevated the visual fidelity of Virtual Try-On (VTON) systems, yet reliable evaluation remains a persistent bottleneck. Traditional metrics struggle to quantify fine-grained texture details and semantic consistency, while existing datasets fail to meet commercial standards in scale and diversity. We present OpenVTON-Bench, a large-scale benchmark comprising approximately 100K high-resolution image pairs (up to $1536 \times 1536$). The dataset is constructed using DINOv3-based hierarchical clustering for semantically balanced sampling and Gemini-powered dense captioning, ensuring a uniform distribution across 20 fine-grained garment categories. To support reliable evaluation, we propose a multi-modal protocol that measures VTON quality along five interpretable dimensions: background consistency, identity fidelity, texture fidelity, shape plausibility, and overall realism. The protocol integrates VLM-based semantic reasoning with a novel Multi-Scale Representation Metric based on SAM3 segmentation and morphological erosion, enabling the separation of boundary alignment errors from internal texture artifacts. Experimental results show strong agreement with human judgments (Kendall's $τ$ of 0.833 vs. 0.611 for SSIM), establishing a robust benchmark for VTON evaluation.

</details>


### [133] [Global Geometry Is Not Enough for Vision Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03282)
*Jiwan Chung,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 全局嵌入几何能反映“有什么”但不能反映“怎么组合”，而输入-输出Jacobian的功能敏感性可有效预测组合绑定能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索全局嵌入几何指标能否预测视觉编码器的组合绑定能力，揭示其局限性并寻找更好的度量。

Method: 在多种视觉编码器上，测试标准几何度量与组合绑定能力的相关性，并引入输入-输出Jacobian作为功能敏感性度量进行对比分析。

Result: 标准几何统计量与组合绑定能力近乎零相关；输入-输出Jacobian的功能敏感性可靠地追踪组合绑定能力。

Conclusion: 全局嵌入几何仅能捕捉表征能力的一部分，功能敏感性是建模复合结构的必要补充维度。

Abstract: A common assumption in representation learning is that globally well-distributed embeddings support robust and generalizable representations. This focus has shaped both training objectives and evaluation protocols, implicitly treating global geometry as a proxy for representational competence. While global geometry effectively encodes which elements are present, it is often insensitive to how they are composed. We investigate this limitation by testing the ability of geometric metrics to predict compositional binding across a diverse suite of vision encoders. We find that standard geometry-based statistics exhibit near-zero correlation with compositional binding. In contrast, functional sensitivity, as measured by the input--output Jacobian, reliably tracks this capability. We further provide an analytic account showing that this disparity arises from objective design, as existing losses explicitly constrain embedding geometry but leave the local input--output mapping unconstrained. These results suggest that global embedding geometry captures only a partial view of representational competence and establish functional sensitivity as a critical complementary axis for modeling composite structure.

</details>


### [134] [From Correspondence to Actions: Human-Like Multi-Image Spatial Reasoning in Multi-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08735)
*Masanari Oi,Koki Maeda,Ryuto Koike,Daisuke Oba,Nakamasa Inoue,Naoaki Okazaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多图像空间推理任务，本文提出HATCH训练框架，通过跨视图补丁级空间对齐和动作-答案推理两个目标，使模型在多个基准上超越同规模基线并接近更大模型，同时保持单图像推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在多图像空间推理中表现不足，而人类通过跨视图对应和视角逐步变换两种机制解决这类任务。已有研究仅部分或隐式地整合这些机制，缺乏显式监督。

Method: 提出HATCH框架，包含两个互补训练目标：(1) 补丁级空间对齐，鼓励跨视图对应空间区域的补丁表示对齐；(2) 动作-答案推理，要求模型在预测最终答案前生成显式的视角变换动作。

Result: 在三个基准上，HATCH一致性地以明显优势超越同规模基线，并取得与更大模型竞争的结果，同时保持单图像推理能力。

Conclusion: 显式建模跨视图对应和视角变换动作可以显著提升多图像空间推理性能，且不会损害单图像推理能力。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made substantial progress in single-image spatial reasoning, multi-image spatial reasoning, which requires integration of information from multiple viewpoints, remains challenging. Cognitive studies suggest that humans address such tasks through two mechanisms: cross-view correspondence, which identifies regions across different views that correspond to the same physical locations, and stepwise viewpoint transformation, which composes relative viewpoint changes sequentially. However, existing studies incorporate these mechanisms only partially and often implicitly, without explicit supervision for both. We propose Human-Aware Training for Cross-view correspondence and viewpoint cHange (HATCH), a training framework with two complementary objectives: (1) Patch-Level Spatial Alignment, which encourages patch representations to align across views for spatially corresponding regions, and (2) Action-then-Answer Reasoning, which requires the model to generate explicit viewpoint transition actions before predicting the final answer. Experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that HATCH consistently outperforms baselines of comparable size by a clear margin and achieves competitive results against much larger models, while preserving single-image reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [135] [ReMoT: Reinforcement Learning with Motion Contrast Triplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00461)
*Cong Wan,Zeyu Guo,Jiangyang Li,SongLin Dong,Yifan Bai,Lin Peng,Zhiheng Ma,Yihong Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReMoT是一种统一训练范式，通过规则生成的运动对比数据集（ReMoT-16K）和组相对策略优化（GRPO），显著提升视觉语言模型在时空一致性方面的表现，在相关任务上取得25.1%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在时空一致性方面存在根本性缺陷，这在导航、机器人和自动驾驶等场景中是关键的失败点。现有方法依赖昂贵的人工标注或模型生成，缺乏大规模、高质量的运动对比数据。

Method: (1) 基于规则的自动框架从视频元标注生成大规模运动对比数据集ReMoT-16K（16.5K三元组）；(2) 使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）来学习对比推理，验证其优于标准监督微调。同时构建了首个细粒度运动对比三元组基准。

Result: 在新基准和多个标准VLM基准上达到最先进性能，时空推理任务上实现25.1%的性能跃升。

Conclusion: ReMoT范式有效解决了VLM的时空一致性缺陷，规则化数据生成避免了昂贵成本，GRPO提供了最佳的数据效率和性能表现。

Abstract: We present ReMoT, a unified training paradigm to systematically address the fundamental shortcomings of VLMs in spatio-temporal consistency -- a critical failure point in navigation, robotics, and autonomous driving. ReMoT integrates two core components: (1) A rule-based automatic framework that generates ReMoT-16K, a large-scale (16.5K triplets) motion-contrast dataset derived from video meta-annotations, surpassing costly manual or model-based generation. (2) Group Relative Policy Optimization, which we empirically validate yields optimal performance and data efficiency for learning this contrastive reasoning, far exceeding standard Supervised Fine-Tuning. We also construct the first benchmark for fine-grained motion contrast triplets to measure a VLM's discrimination of subtle motion attributes (e.g., opposing directions). The resulting model achieves state-of-the-art performance on our new benchmark and multiple standard VLM benchmarks, culminating in a remarkable 25.1% performance leap on spatio-temporal reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [136] [CoVR-R:Reason-Aware Composed Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20190)
*Omkar Thawakar,Dmitry Demidov,Vaishnav Potlapalli,Sai Prasanna Teja Reddy Bogireddy,Viswanatha Reddy Gajjala,Alaa Mostafa Lasheen,Rao Muhammad Anwer,Fahad Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种推理优先的零样本组合视频检索方法，利用大型多模态模型推断编辑操作隐含的因果和时序后效，并构建了CoVR-Reason基准来评估检索中的推理能力。实验表明该方法在Recall@K上优于强基线，尤其在隐式后效子集上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合视频检索（CoVR）方法假设修改文本完全指定了视觉变化，忽略了编辑产生的后效和隐含后果（如运动、状态转换、视角或时长线索）。作者认为成功的CoVR需要对这类后效进行推理。

Method: 提出一种推理优先的零样本方法，利用大型多模态模型：(i)推断编辑隐含的因果和时间后果，(ii)将推理后的查询与候选视频对齐，无需任务特定微调。同时构建了CoVR-Reason基准，包含结构化推理轨迹和具有挑战性的干扰项。

Result: 零样本方法在Recall@K上优于强检索基线，尤其在隐式后效子集上表现优异。自动和人工分析验证了更高的步骤一致性和效果事实性。

Conclusion: 将推理融入通用多模态模型能够有效实现CoVR，通过显式建模因果和时间后效，减少了对任务特定监督的依赖，提升了对挑战性隐式后效案例的泛化能力，并增强了检索结果的可解释性。

Abstract: Composed Video Retrieval (CoVR) aims to find a target video given a reference video and a textual modification. Prior work assumes the modification text fully specifies the visual changes, overlooking after-effects and implicit consequences (e.g., motion, state transitions, viewpoint or duration cues) that emerge from the edit. We argue that successful CoVR requires reasoning about these after-effects. We introduce a reasoning-first, zero-shot approach that leverages large multimodal models to (i) infer causal and temporal consequences implied by the edit, and (ii) align the resulting reasoned queries to candidate videos without task-specific finetuning. To evaluate reasoning in CoVR, we also propose CoVR-Reason, a benchmark that pairs each (reference, edit, target) triplet with structured internal reasoning traces and challenging distractors that require predicting after-effects rather than keyword matching. Experiments show that our zero-shot method outperforms strong retrieval baselines on recall at K and particularly excels on implicit-effect subsets. Our automatic and human analysis confirm higher step consistency and effect factuality in our retrieved results. Our findings show that incorporating reasoning into general-purpose multimodal models enables effective CoVR by explicitly accounting for causal and temporal after-effects. This reduces dependence on task-specific supervision, improves generalization to challenging implicit-effect cases, and enhances interpretability of retrieval outcomes. These results point toward a scalable and principled framework for explainable video search. The model, code, and benchmark are available at https://github.com/mbzuai-oryx/CoVR-R.

</details>


### [137] [Auditing Demographic Bias in Facial Landmark Detection for Fair Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06961)
*Pablo Parte,Roberto Valle,José M. Buenaposada,Luis Baumela*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了人脸关键点检测任务中的年龄、性别和种族偏差，发现视觉混杂因素（头部姿态和面部分辨率）的影响远大于人口统计学属性。在控制混杂因素后，性别和种族的性能差异消失，但存在显著的年龄偏差（老年人定位误差更高），表明公平性问题会渗透到人机交互流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口统计学偏差在高级面部分析任务中已被广泛研究，但在人脸关键点检测这一低级视觉任务中的存在性尚未被探索，而该任务是人机交互感知管道的基础环节，其偏差会通过管道传播影响交互公平性。

Method: 引入一种受控的统计方法学，将人口统计学效应与视觉混杂因素（头部姿态、面部分辨率）解耦，系统审计年龄、性别和种族偏差。

Result: 视觉混杂因素（头部姿态和面部分辨率）的影响远超人口统计学属性；控制混杂因素后，性别和种族的性能差异消失；但存在显著的年龄相关偏差，老年人定位误差更高。

Conclusion: 公平性问题在低级视觉组件中也可能出现并通过HRI管道传播，审计和纠正此类偏差是实现可信、公平的机器人感知系统的必要步骤。

Abstract: Fairness in human-robot interaction critically depends on the reliability of the perceptual models that enable robots to interpret human behavior. While demographic biases have been widely studied in high-level facial analysis tasks, their presence in facial landmark detection remains unexplored. In this paper, we conduct a systematic audit of demographic bias in this task, analyzing the age, gender, and race biases. To this end, we introduce a controlled statistical methodology to disentangle demographic effects from confounding visual factors. Our analysis demonstrates that visual confounders, particularly head pose and face resolution, heavily outweigh the impact of demographic attributes. Notably, after accounting for these confounders, performance disparities across gender and race vanish. However, we identify a statistically significant age-related bias, with higher localization errors for older individuals. This shows that fairness issues can emerge even in low-level vision components and can propagate through the HRI pipeline. We argue that auditing and correcting such biases is a necessary step toward trustworthy and equitable robot perception systems.

</details>


### [138] [MedVeriSeg: Teaching LISA-Like Medical Segmentation Models to Verify Query Validity Without Extra Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10242)
*Qinyue Tong,Xiaozhen Wang,Ziqian Lu,Jun Liu,Yunlong Yu,Zheming Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedVeriSeg是一个无需训练的查询验证框架，通过相似度响应质量评分模块和轻量级路由多智能体验证模块，帮助LISA-like医疗分割模型拒绝虚假分割查询，减少幻觉分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LISA-like多模态大模型方法在医疗图像分割中，无论查询目标是否存在都会生成掩码，导致幻觉分割问题。

Method: 提出MedVeriSeg框架，包含：(1) 相似度响应质量评分模块，量化[SEG]令牌与图像特征之间的响应质量；(2) 轻量级路由多智能体验证模块，融合定量评分证据与定性智能体证据，综合验证查询有效性；(3) 构建MedVeriSeg-Bench基准用于评估。

Result: MedVeriSeg能够有效识别虚假分割查询并减少幻觉分割，同时保持对有效查询的高接受率，从而保留了LISA-like医疗分割模型的分割效用。

Conclusion: MedVeriSeg是一种无需训练的框架，可有效增强现有医疗分割模型的查询验证能力，减少幻觉输出。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in text-prompt-based medical image segmentation, existing LISA-like MLLM-based methods typically generate masks regardless of whether the target specified in the query is present, leading to hallucinated segmentation. In this work, we propose MedVeriSeg, a training-free query verification framework that enables LISA-like medical segmentation models to reject false segmentation queries. MedVeriSeg first quantifies the response quality between the [SEG] token and image features through a Similarity Response Quality Scoring Module. To further improve robustness, it employs a Lightweight Routed Multi-Agent Verification Module, which fuses quantitative score evidence with qualitative agent evidence to comprehensively verify the validity of the query. To support systematic evaluation, we construct MedVeriSeg-Bench, a benchmark designed for query verification in medical image segmentation. Experimental results demonstrate that MedVeriSeg effectively identifies false segmentation queries and reduces hallucinated segmentation, while maintaining a high acceptance rate for valid queries, thereby largely preserving the segmentation utility of LISA-like medical segmentation models.

</details>


### [139] [Right Regions, Wrong Labels: Semantic Label Flips in Segmentation under Correlation Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13326)
*Akshit Achara,Yovin Yahathugoda,Nick Byrne,Michela Antonelli,Esther Puyol Anton,Alexander Hammers,Andrew P. King*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了语义分割模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性问题，重点揭示了一种特定失败模式：模型可能在不同场景类别相关训练后，将前景像素错误地分配为另一个合理的前景类别（而非背景），即"语义标签翻转"。作者提出了简单诊断指标Flip来量化该行为，并基于熵提出无需真实标签的flip-risk分数以在推理时检测易翻转样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类任务中针对虚假相关性的鲁棒性测试已有充分研究，但在语义分割任务中，具体的失败模式尚不明确。需要理解模型在训练数据中类别与场景存在相关性时，测试分布偏移下会出现何种语义错误。

Method: 定义Flip指标：统计真实前景像素被预测为前景但类别身份错误的像素比例。在训练中人为控制类别与场景的相关性，观察常见与罕见测试条件下的性能差距。提出基于预测熵的flip-risk分数，无需真实标签即可在推理时评估翻转风险。

Result: 增加训练时类别-场景相关性，会持续扩大常见与罕见测试条件间的性能差距，并增加对象内部标签翻转的次数。这表明标准交并比（IoU）指标无法充分反映此类语义错误。

Conclusion: 语义分割的鲁棒性评估应超越简单的重叠度量，需要将前景错误分解为正确像素、身份翻转像素和遗漏到背景的像素。熵基flip-risk分数可以在推理时有效标记高风险区域。

Abstract: The robustness of machine learning models can be compromised by spurious correlations between non-causal features in the input data and target labels. A common way to test for such correlations is to train on data where the label is strongly tied to some non-causal cue, then evaluate on examples where that tie no longer holds. This idea is well established for classification tasks, but for semantic segmentation the specific failure modes are not well understood. We show that a model may achieve reasonable overlap while assigning the wrong semantic label, swapping one plausible foreground class for another, even when object boundaries are largely correct. We focus on this semantic label-flip behaviour and quantify it with a simple diagnostic (Flip) that counts how often ground truth foreground pixels are assigned the wrong foreground identity while remaining predicted as foreground. In a setting where category and scene are correlated during training, increasing the correlation consistently widens the gap between common and rare test conditions and increases these within-object label swaps on counterfactual groups. Overall, our results motivate assessing segmentation robustness under distribution shift beyond overlap by decomposing foreground errors into correct pixels, flipped-identity pixels, and missed-to-background pixels. We also propose an entropy-based, ground truth label-free `flip-risk' score, which is computed from foreground identity uncertainty, and show that it can flag flip-prone cases at inference time. Code is available at https://github.com/acharaakshit/label-flips.

</details>


### [140] [TopoHR: Hierarchical Centerline Representation for Cyclic Topology Reasoning in Driving Scenes with Point-to-Instance Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24119)
*Yifeng Bai,Zhirong Chen,Bo Song,Erkang Cheng,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoHR提出了一个端到端的拓扑层次表示框架，通过层次化中心线表示和拓扑推理模块，实现中心线检测与拓扑推理的循环互动，在OpenLane-V2基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多采用实例级中心线检测后接简化的MLP层进行拓扑推理，且忽略了点-实例关系在拓扑推理中的重要性。

Method: 提出层次化中心线表示（点查询、实例查询、语义表示）和层次化拓扑推理模块（捕获细粒度P2I关系和全局I2I关系），构建中心线检测与拓扑推理之间的循环交互机制。

Result: 在OpenLane-V2的subset_A上DET_l提升3.8、TOP_ll提升5.4；subset_B上DET_l提升11.0、TOP_ll提升7.9。

Conclusion: 层次化表示与循环交互机制能有效提升拓扑推理的准确性和鲁棒性，验证了所提出组件的有效性。

Abstract: Topology reasoning is crucial for autonomous driving. Current methods primarily focus on instance-level learning for centerline detection, followed by a sequential module for topology reasoning that relies on simplified MLP layers. Moreover, they often neglect the importance of \textit{point-to-instance} (P2I) relationships in topology reasoning. To address these limitations, we present TopoHR (Topological Hierarchical Representation), a novel end-to-end framework that establishes cyclic interaction between centerline detection and topology reasoning, allowing them to iteratively enhance each other. Specifically, we introduce a hierarchical centerline representation including point queries, instance queries, and semantic representations. These multi-level features are seamlessly integrated and fused within a hierarchical centerline decoder. Furthermore, we design a hierarchical topology reasoning module that captures both fine-grained P2I relationships and global instance-to-instance (I2I) connections within a unified architecture. With these novel components, TopoHR ensures accurate and robust topology reasoning. On the OpenLane-V2 benchmark, TopoHR refreshes state-of-the-art performance with significant improvements. Notably, compared with previous best results, TopoHR achieves +3.8 in $\mathrm{DET}_{\text{l}}$, +5.4 in $\mathrm{TOP}_{\text{ll}}$ on $\text{subset_A}$ and +11.0 in $\mathrm{DET}_{\text{l}}$, +7.9 in $\mathrm{TOP}_{\text{ll}}$ on $\text{subset_B}$, validating the effectiveness of the proposed components. The code will be shared publicly at https://github.com/Yifeng-Bai/TopoHR.git.

</details>


### [141] [Active Sampling for Ultra-Low-Bit-Rate Video Compression via Conditional Controlled Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02849)
*Amirhosein Javadi,Shirin Saeedi Bidokhti,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActDiff-VC提出了一种基于扩散模型的超低比特率视频压缩框架，通过内容自适应关键帧选择和预算感知稀疏轨迹选择来生成紧凑的条件信号，实验结果表明在UVG和MCL-JCV基准上相比强基线方法实现了显著比特率节约和感知质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型为超低比特率下的感知重建提供了强大生成先验，但有效的视频压缩需要使用高度紧凑的条件信号来控制生成过程，现有方法在此方面存在不足。

Method: 将视频划分为可变长度片段，仅在需要时传输关键帧，并用紧凑的跟踪点轨迹集概括时间动态；设计内容自适应关键帧选择和预算感知稀疏轨迹选择两个机制来生成紧凑有效的条件信号；利用条件扩散解码器合成剩余帧。

Result: 在UVG和MCL-JCV基准上，相比强学习编解码器，NIQE匹配下比特率降低高达64.6%，KID提升64.6%，FID提升37.7%，在超低比特率下实现了优于学习型和扩散型基线的感知率失真权衡。

Conclusion: 通过紧凑的稀疏条件信号结合扩散生成先验，ActDiff-VC在超低比特率视频压缩中实现了高感知质量重建，验证了内容自适应和预算感知策略的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide a powerful generative prior for perceptual reconstruction at ultra-low bitrates, but effective video compression requires controlling the generative process using highly compact conditioning signals. In this work, we present ActDiff-VC, a diffusion-based video compression framework for the ultra-low-bitrate regime. Our method partitions videos into variable-length segments, transmits keyframes only when needed, and summarizes temporal dynamics using a compact set of tracked point trajectories. Conditioned on these sparse signals, a conditional diffusion decoder synthesizes the remaining frames, enabling perceptually realistic reconstruction under severe rate constraints. To support this design, we introduce two mechanisms: content-adaptive keyframe selection and budget-aware sparse trajectory selection, which together enable compact yet effective conditioning for generative reconstruction. Experiments on the UVG and MCL-JCV benchmarks show that ActDiff-VC achieves up to 64.6\% bitrate reduction at matched NIQE, improves KID by up to 64.6\% and FID by up to 37.7\% at comparable bitrates against strong learned codecs, and delivers favorable perceptual rate--distortion trade-offs relative to learned and diffusion-based baselines in the ultra-low-bitrate regime.

</details>


### [142] [Weakly Supervised Segmentation as Semantic-Based Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13674)
*Stefano Colamonaco,Andrei-Bogdan Florea,Jaron Maene*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号方法，将可微模糊逻辑与SAM结合，通过连续逻辑约束统一弱标注和领域先验知识，在弱监督下微调SAM生成更高质量的伪标签，进而训练无提示第二阶段分割模型，在Pascal VOC 2012和REFUGE2上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSSS方法利用SAM时依赖启发式提示选择，且缺乏有效方式融合先验知识和异质标注，无法充分利用弱监督信息。

Method: 将可微模糊逻辑与深度分割模型整合，把弱标注和领域先验统一为连续逻辑约束，在弱监督下微调SAM；再用改进的伪标签训练无提示的第二阶段分割模型。

Result: 在Pascal VOC 2012和REFUGE2数据集上，逻辑引导的微调生成的伪标签质量更高，分割精度达到SOTA，甚至超过密集监督基线。

Conclusion: 神经符号视角下的逻辑约束微调有效提升了SAM在弱监督下的伪标签质量，为WSSS提供了一种灵活、可解释的范式。

Abstract: Weakly supervised semantic segmentation (WSSS) trains dense pixel-level segmentation models from partial or coarse annotations such as bounding boxes, scribbles, or image-level tags. While recent work leverages foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) to generate pseudo-labels, these approaches typically depend on heuristic prompt choices and offer limited ways to incorporate prior knowledge or heterogeneous labels. We address this gap by taking a neurosymbolic perspective: integrating differentiable fuzzy logic with deep segmentation models. Weak annotations and domain-specific priors are unified as continuous logical constraints that fine-tune SAM under weak supervision. The refined foundation model then produces improved pseudo-labels, from which we train a second-stage prompt-free segmentation model. Experiments on Pascal VOC 2012 and the REFUGE2 optic disc/cup segmentation dataset show that our logic-guided fine-tuning yields higher-quality pseudo-labels, leading to state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy that often exceeds densely supervised baselines.

</details>


### [143] [Right Predictions, Misleading Explanations: On the Vulnerability of Vision-Language Model Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16651)
*Narges Babadi,Hadis Karimipour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）的解释热力图存在脆弱性，并提出X-Shift攻击方法，能在不改变模型预测的情况下将解释热力图定向转移到语义无关区域，揭示了当前解释机制在对抗条件下无法忠实反映模型推理过程的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然解释机制被广泛用于提升视觉-语言模型（VLM）的透明度和可信度，但这些解释在对抗条件下的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。作者旨在探究VLM（尤其是基于CLIP的模型）的解释热力图是否能在对抗条件下忠实反映模型推理过程。

Method: 提出X-Shift，一种新型灰盒攻击方法，通过扰动 patch 级视觉表征，在不修改模型参数且不改变预测输出的前提下，将解释热力图定向重定向到语义无关区域。该方法可泛化到多种CLIP架构和解释方法。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k、MS-COCO和Flickr30K数据集上，X-Shift在保持预测稳定性的同时，在不可感知的扰动下持续降低了解释对齐度。此外，传统的面向预测的对抗攻击即使在更大的扰动预算下也无法复现相同的解释偏移行为。

Conclusion: 当前视觉-语言模型中的解释机制存在根本性局限，其作为高影响力应用中模型可信度可靠指标的做法令人担忧。

Abstract: Explanation mechanisms are increasingly used to support transparency and trust in vision-language models (VLMs), particularly in settings where model decisions require human oversight. However, the robustness of these explanations remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate whether explanation heatmaps in VLMs, particularly CLIP-based models, faithfully reflect model reasoning under adversarial conditions. We show that explanation maps can be systematically manipulated while preserving the model's original prediction, revealing a disconnect between predictive behavior and explanation faithfulness. To study this vulnerability, we introduce X-Shift, a novel grey-box attack that perturbs patch-level visual representations to redirect explanation heatmaps toward semantically irrelevant regions without altering the predicted output. Unlike conventional adversarial attacks that aim to induce misclassification, X-Shift specifically targets the integrity of the explanation process itself. The attack operates without modifying model parameters and generalizes across multiple CLIP architectures and explanation methods. We evaluate the proposed approach on ImageNet-1k, MS-COCO, and Flickr30K, demonstrating consistent degradation in explanation alignment under imperceptible perturbations while maintaining prediction stability. Furthermore, standard prediction-oriented adversarial attacks fail to reproduce the same explanation-shifting behavior even under substantially larger perturbation budgets. Our findings highlight a fundamental limitation of current explanation mechanisms in VLMs and raise concerns about their use as reliable indicators of model trustworthiness in high-impact applications.

</details>


### [144] [Lighting-aware Unified Model for Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20436)
*Qisai Liu,Alloy Das,Zhanhong Jiang,Joshua R. Waite,Aditya Balu,Adarsh Krishnamurthy,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出照明卷积注意力（LCA）适配器模块，通过双分支架构处理RGB特征与对比度图，结合成对训练策略和针对性损失项，在不微调重量级骨干网络的情况下，增强SAM在多样化真实光照条件下的实例分割鲁棒性，并基于Unity构建了复杂光照合成数据集进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM等基础模型在实例分割任务中，面对真实世界的多样化光照条件时性能显著下降，现有方法缺乏对光照鲁棒性的针对性改进。

Method: 提出LCA适配器模块，采用双分支架构分别处理RGB特征和对比度图，基于物理启发的设计使其对结构变化敏感而非光照伪影；通过成对训练策略，引入针对性损失项显式惩罚干净图像与其光照变体之间的分割差异。

Result: 在多个现有基准以及自建的Unity合成光照数据集上，实验结果表明该方法有效弥合了领域差距，实现了更优的光照鲁棒分割性能。

Conclusion: LCA适配器在不微调骨干网络的前提下，通过轻量级适配有效提升了SAM在复杂光照条件下的分割鲁棒性，验证了基于物理感知的对比度建模对光照不变分割的有效性。

Abstract: Foundation models like the Segment Anything Model (SAM) demonstrate impressive zero-shot generalization but frequently degrade under diverse real-world illumination, particularly for instance segmentation. In this work, we address this limitation by developing \textit{Lighting Convolutional-Attention (\lca{})}, an adapter module that enhances segmentation robustness without fine-tuning the heavy backbone. \lca{} employs a dual-branch architecture to process RGB features alongside contrast maps, enabling physically motivated sensitivity to structural changes rather than illumination artifacts. We optimize \lca{} through a pairwise training strategy, introducing a targeted loss term that explicitly penalizes discrepancies between clean images and their corresponding illumination variants. To evaluate and support this architecture, we conduct a comprehensive empirical study across multiple existing benchmarks and present a novel Unity-based synthetic dataset specifically designed to accurately replicate complex real-world lighting conditions. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that our approach successfully bridges the domain gap, delivering superior lighting-robust segmentation.

</details>


### [145] [What Semantics Survive the Connector? Diagnosing VLM-to-DiT Alignment in Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20795)
*Hangyu Lin,Chao Wen,Chengming Xu,Jianxiong Gao,Jiangning Zhang,Xiaobin Hu,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE-Edit诊断数据集和系统分析协议，揭示了基于Flow Matching的视频生成模型中VLM到DiT的对齐过程会导致细粒度结构语义严重退化，证明该对齐是语义瓶颈而非无损传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑模型普遍假设VLM与DiT之间的连接器模块能够无损对齐多模态推理与文本嵌入空间，但这一假设缺乏验证。由于端到端评估混淆了对齐失败与生成错误，且自然数据集缺乏解耦标注，难以直接研究对齐过程对细粒度结构变量的影响。

Method: 提出基于视频合成的可控数据处理流水线，构建TRACE-Edit诊断数据集（聚焦关系型编辑）；设计综合诊断协议，分别分析meta-query和connector两种关键设计；在四种代表性模型案例上进行系统评估。

Result: 发现细粒度结构语义在对齐过程中会严重退化，四种代表性模型均表现出此问题。推翻了"无损语义传输"的假设，证实VLM到DiT的对齐是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: VLM-to-DiT对齐是当前指令驱动视频编辑模型中的关键语义瓶颈，该发现为未来多模态对齐架构的设计提供了新的诊断基础。

Abstract: Flow matching based video generative models have been increasingly relying on prepended Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to handle complex, instruction-based video editing. The prevailing assumption underlying this paradigm is that a connector module can seamlessly align the VLM's rich multi-modal reasoning with the original text embedding space of DiTs. However, we hypothesize that this alignment acts as a severe semantic bottleneck, degrading fine-grained structural variables. Verifying this is challenging, as end-to-end evaluations conflate alignment failures with generation errors, and natural datasets lack disentangled annotations. To rigorously investigate this, we propose a controlled data processing pipeline based on video composition that results in TRACE-Edit, a diagnostic dataset focusing on relation-based editing. Leveraging this dataset, we propose a comprehensive diagnostic protocol to analyze two important designs of meta-query and connector in the existing video editing models. Systematic evaluation of four representative model cases reveals that fine-grained structural semantics can be severely degraded during alignment. Our findings overturn the assumption of lossless semantic transfer, identifying the VLM-to-DiT alignment as a major bottleneck and providing a new diagnostic foundation for future multi-modal alignment architectures.

</details>


### [146] [Mitigating Content Shift and Hallucination in GenAI Image Editing via Structural Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30437)
*Luxi Zhao,Michael S. Brown*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种后处理框架，用于将输入图像与其GenAI增强版本融合，在保留感知增强效果的同时，保持对原始图像的像素级结构忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI图像编辑器在图像修描中会产生空间错位、纹理扭曲和内容幻觉等问题，这些对需要像素级保真度的下游工作流有害。

Method: 提出"结构保持GenAI融合"后处理框架：首先建立粗略的空间和光度对应关系，然后进行融合阶段，在抑制幻觉内容的同时传递所需的增强效果。

Result: 与照片级风格迁移和图像融合的代表性方法相比，该方法在保持像素级结构一致性和输入分辨率的同时，更好地保留了美学质量。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效解决GenAI图像修描中的结构一致性问题，在感知增强与像素保真度之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) image editors, such as Nano Banana, produce visually compelling results for retouching tasks, enabling non-experts to edit images through text prompts alone. However, the generative nature of these models often introduces spatial misalignment, texture distortion, and content hallucination, all of which are detrimental to downstream workflows that require pixel-level fidelity. We identify a problem setting we call "structure-preserving GenAI fusion" for black-box GenAI image retouching: retain the perceptual enhancements of a GenAI output while enforcing structural faithfulness to the original input image. To address this problem, we propose a post-processing framework that fuses an input image with its GenAI-enhanced counterpart by first establishing coarse spatial and photometric correspondences, then performing a fusion stage that transfers desired enhancements while suppressing hallucinated content. In the absence of direct prior work in this setting, we evaluate our framework against representative methods from photorealistic style transfer and image fusion. Our experiments demonstrate that our method better preserves aesthetic quality while maintaining pixel-level structural consistency and the input resolution.

</details>


### [147] [Latent Geometric Chords for Query-Efficient Decision-Based Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31219)
*Ei Hmue Khine,Yao Li,Jiebao Sun,Shengzhu Shi,Zhichang Guo,Boying Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Geometric Chords (LGC)方法，通过在压缩语义流形上进行曲率感知几何搜索，结合残差对抗生成机制(RAG)解决像素级攻击产生高频伪影和潜在空间框架搜索受限的问题，在极低扰动下实现高视觉保真度(SSIM>0.99, LPIPS<0.01)和强攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策级黑盒对抗攻击存在根本性局限：像素级攻击引入不自然的高频视觉伪影，而潜在空间框架受限于低维流形的有限搜索空间和固有的重建缺陷。亟需一种既能保持高视觉保真度又能突破维度瓶颈的查询高效攻击方法。

Method: 提出Latent Geometric Chords (LGC)方法及其变体LGC-H。核心思想是在压缩语义流形上执行曲率感知几何搜索以导航决策边界；引入残差对抗生成(RAG)机制，将语义扰动隔离为几何和弦并直接叠加到原始图像上，解决重建缺陷并有效将搜索空间维度翻倍。

Result: LGC实现跨数据集的鲁棒迁移性，在5000次查询下SSIM超过0.99、LPIPS低于0.01，达到最先进视觉保真度，同时维持高攻击成功率，成功突破对抗训练鲁棒模型。

Conclusion: LGC通过几何和弦与残差生成机制有效解决了决策级黑盒攻击中的视觉伪影和搜索空间受限问题，在查询效率、扰动幅度和视觉保真度方面全面超越现有基线方法。

Abstract: While decision-based black-box adversarial attacks present a severe security threat, current methodologies suffer from fundamental limitations. Pixel-wise attacks frequently introduce unnatural, high-frequency visual artifacts, while latent-space frameworks are confined by the limited search space of low-dimensional manifolds and inherent reconstruction flaws. To resolve these limitations, we propose Latent Geometric Chords (LGC) for Query-Efficient Decision-Based Adversarial Attacks alongside a variant, LGC-H. At its core, LGC navigates decision boundaries by executing a curvature-aware geometric search within a compressed semantic manifold. To guarantee high visual fidelity and circumvent dimensionality bottlenecks, we introduce a Residual-based Adversarial Generation (RAG) mechanism. RAG isolates semantic perturbations as geometric chords and superimposes them directly onto the original source image. RAG substantially resolves baseline reconstruction flaws and effectively doubles the permissible search space dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that LGC achieves robust cross-dataset transferability and substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Notably, our method, LGC, minimizes perturbation magnitudes while achieving state-of-the-art visual fidelity--with a Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) exceeding 0.99 and a Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity (LPIPS) below 0.01 at 5000 queries--and sustaining high attack success rates under stringent perceptual constraints, successfully compromising adversarially trained robust models. The source code is available at: https://github.com/eihmuekhine/Latent-Geometric-Chords.

</details>


### [148] [Frames2LoRA: Parametric Video Internalization for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04351)
*Manan Suri,Sarvesh Baskar,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Frames2LoRA方法，通过感知器超网络将视频内部化为LoRA适配器，无需视觉token即可让冻结的VLM回答视频查询，在保持性能的同时减少高达1500倍视觉token负载和6-80倍TTFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频在视觉-语言模型中处理成本高昂：每帧占用数百个token，推理成本随帧数和查询次数线性增长。
