Method: 使用感知器超网络逐层读取冻结VLM编码视频时产生的中间表示，单次前向传播生成LoRA适配器，将视频直接内部化为模型参数。

Result: 在5个视频描述基准上统计非劣效且等价于视频直接推理；8个视频问答中7个等价；支持高达1024帧和1024px；视觉token负载降低1500倍，TTFT降低6-80倍。

Conclusion: Frames2LoRA实现了高效的视频内部化，且非重叠片段生成的适配器可在秩空间中组合，为长视频分块处理提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Processing video in vision-language models is expensive: each frame occupies hundreds of tokens, and inference cost scales with every frame and every repeated query. We introduce Frames2LoRA, a method for parametric video internalization. A perceiver hypernetwork reads the intermediate representations produced layer-by-layer as a frozen VLM encodes a video, and generates a Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapter in a single forward pass. Unlike standard LoRA fine-tuning, which requires iterative gradient updates, Frames2LoRA predicts these weights directly from the video. Trained for SmolVLM2 500M and 2.2B on video summarization and captioning, Frames2LoRA enables the same frozen VLM to answer queries from the adapter alone, with zero visual tokens in its context at query time. Frames2LoRA is statistically non-inferior and equivalent to direct video-in-context inference across all five captioning benchmarks at both model scales, and across seven of eight video question answering benchmark-scale pairings. Although trained only on 12 frames at 384px, it remains stable up to 1,024 frames and 1024px, where direct video-in-context inference often degenerates. Across this sweep, it reduces answer-time visual-token load by up to 1,500x and query TTFT by 6-80x, while preserving video-faithful outputs. We also find that independently generated adapters for non-overlapping video segments can compose in rank space, suggesting a path toward chunked long-video internalization.

</details>


### [149] [CoVEBench: Can Video Editing Models Handle Complex Instructions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08415)
*Jiangtao Wu,Jiaming Wang,Yiwen He,Yuanxing Zhang,Shihao Li,Dunyuan Liu,Xuedong Zhao,Jialu Chen,Zekun Moore Wang,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoVEBench是一个面向组合式视频编辑的新型基准测试，包含416个源视频、626个多点编辑指令和9,990个细粒度检查项。实验表明，现有模型在处理多操作组合编辑时严重不足，常出现遗漏编辑、违反保留约束或引入伪影等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑基准局限于孤立编辑和粗粒度全局指标，无法诊断模型如何处理真实世界中高度组合性的用户请求（如同时修改主体、动作和相机视角并保留无关内容）。

Method: 构建了包含416个源视频、626个多点编辑指令和9,990个细粒度检查项的基准测试，采用MLLM评判指令合规性和视频保真度，结合自动指标评估视频质量。

Result: 当前模型在组合式编辑中表现不佳：频繁出现编辑遗漏、违反保留约束、引入伪影等问题。

Conclusion: 组合式编辑仍是当前视频编辑领域的重大挑战，CoVEBench为推进视频编辑向真实用户工作流程发展提供了具有挑战性的诊断测试平台。

Abstract: While recent text-guided video editing models excel at elementary tasks (e.g., style transfer, object insertion), real-world user requests are highly compositional. A single prompt often demands multiple coupled edits, such as modifying subjects, actions, and camera views, while strictly preserving unrelated spatiotemporal content. Existing benchmarks, heavily constrained by isolated edits and coarse global metrics, fail to diagnose how models handle such complex workflows. To address this gap, we introduce CoVEBench, a compositional video editing benchmark comprising 416 curated source videos, 626 multi-point editing instructions, and 9,990 fine-grained checklist items. Covering diverse editing dimensions, CoVEBench evaluates models via MLLM-judged instruction compliance and video fidelity, alongside automated metrics for video quality. Extensive experiments reveal that compositional editing remains a profound challenge: current models frequently omit edits, violate preservation constraints, or introduce artifacts when handling multiple operations simultaneously. CoVEBench provides a challenging, diagnostic testbed to advance video editing toward realistic user workflows.

</details>


### [150] [MB-Loc: Multi-planar Bird's-eye-view Localization in outdoor LiDAR scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08744)
*Ayaan Choudhury,Preet Savalia,Anirudh Pydah,Avinash Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MB-Loc提出了一种轻量级、视角鲁棒的LiDAR全局定位框架，通过将3D点云投影为2.5D多平面鸟瞰图表示，结合KL正则化潜在瓶颈和3D空间增强，在NCLT数据集上实现了实时推理速度下的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于场景坐标回归(SCR)的LiDAR全局定位方法存在两个瓶颈：一是处理原始3D几何数据的计算效率极低，二是在传感器视角变化下性能显著下降。本文旨在解决这两个问题。

Method: 1) 将输入LiDAR扫描投影为2.5D多平面鸟瞰图(BEV)表示，沿Z轴切片并将有符号深度映射到离散2D平面，保留3D结构的同时利用标准2D CNN计算；2) 引入KL正则化潜在瓶颈，显式建模空间不确定性；3) 在平面投影前应用3D空间增强，迫使网络学习视角不变特征。

Result: 在NCLT公开数据集上，MB-Loc以实时推理速度超越当前SOTA方法，计算效率显著优于传统3D-SCR架构。

Conclusion: 通过2.5D多平面BEV表示、KL正则化潜在瓶颈和3D空间增强的组合，MB-Loc有效克服了现有SCR方法的计算和视角鲁棒性瓶颈，实现了轻量高效的LiDAR全局定位。

Abstract: Global LiDAR localization is a fundamental task for autonomous navigation systems. Recent methods perform Scene Coordinate Regression (SCR) and achieve superior accuracy over Absolute Pose Regression (APR) solutions by predicting dense 3D world coordinates. However, SCR approaches introduce two major bottlenecks: severe computational inefficiency from processing raw 3D geometries and significant performance degradation under varying sensor viewpoints. To address these limitations, we present MB-Loc, a lightweight and viewpoint-robust SCR framework. Instead of relying on heavy 3D convolutions, we project the input LiDAR scan into a 2.5D Multi-planar Bird's-Eye View (BEV) representation. By slicing the point-cloud along the Z-axis and mapping signed depths into discrete 2D planes, MB-Loc retains essential 3D geometric structures while exploiting the computational tractability of standard 2D CNNs. To handle the inherent sparsity of outdoor LiDAR, we introduce a KL-regularized latent bottleneck that explicitly models spatial uncertainty without injecting stochastic noise. Finally, to ensure rotation robustness, we apply 3D spatial augmentations prior to planar projection, forcing the network to implicitly learn viewpoint-invariant features. We perform extensive experiments on the publicly available NCLT dataset and demonstrate that our proposed method outperforms the current state-of-the-art. Operating at real-time inference speeds, MB-Loc significantly outperforms traditional 3D-SCR architectures in computational efficiency.

</details>


### [151] [IB-HFN: Information Bottleneck-Driven SAR-Optical Fusion Network for High-Fidelity Cloud Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09347)
*Haojun Guo,Fan Feng,Ziquan Wang,Yongsheng Zhang,Ying Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种信息瓶颈驱动的高保真网络（IB-HFN），用于SAR辅助的光学云去除，通过双流骨干网络、空间信息瓶颈融合模块和联合优化策略，在SEN12MS-CR数据集上实现了优于现有方法的结构保持和光谱保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAR辅助光学云去除方法的多模态融合通常依赖直接空间拼接和逐像素监督，这会将SAR散斑噪声传播到光学重建中，导致结果过度平滑。为解决这些局限性，需要一种能够有效抑制噪声同时保留纹理细节的融合方法。

Method: 1) 双流骨干网络保留模态特定表示，避免早期跨模态污染；2) 空间信息瓶颈融合模块通过通道变分信息瓶颈压缩SAR特征以抑制散斑噪声；3) 局部-全局门控机制预测晴空区域，通过Dirac初始化的跳跃连接路由可靠光学细节；4) 联合优化策略整合特征级瓶颈正则化与图像级重建精度、结构一致性、光谱保真度和对比度锐度约束。

Result: 在SEN12MS-CR数据集上具有挑战性的时空划分条件下，IB-HFN在结构保留和光谱保真度方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的信息瓶颈驱动策略能有效抑制SAR散斑噪声对光学重建的干扰，同时保留高保真纹理细节，为多模态遥感图像融合提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR)-assisted optical cloud removal aims to recover surface information obscured by clouds in optical remote sensing images by exploiting complementary SAR observations. Existing multimodal fusion methods typically rely on direct spatial concatenation and pixel-wise supervision, which can propagate SAR speckle noise into optical reconstruction and lead to over-smoothed results. To address these limitations, we propose an Information Bottleneck-driven High-Fidelity Network (IB-HFN) for SAR-assisted optical cloud removal. IB-HFN employs a dual-stream backbone to preserve modality-specific representations before deep semantic fusion, thereby mitigating premature cross-modal contamination. At the fusion stage, we introduce a Spatial Information Bottleneck Fusion module that compresses SAR features through a channel-wise variational information bottleneck to suppress unstructured speckle noise. In parallel, a local-global gating mechanism predicts clear-sky regions and routes reliable optical details through a Dirac-initialized skip connection, decoupling noise suppression from texture preservation. We further develop a joint optimization strategy that integrates feature-level bottleneck regularization with image-level constraints on reconstruction accuracy, structural consistency, spectral fidelity, and contrastive sharpness. A dynamic weighting schedule balances these objectives to stabilize training and reduce hazy artifacts. Experiments on the SEN12MS-CR dataset under challenging spatio-temporal splits demonstrate that IB-HFN achieves superior structural preservation and spectral fidelity over existing methods.

</details>


### [152] [BiWM: Advancing Open-Source Interactive Video World Models with Bidirectional Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10135)
*Shaohao Rui,Xiaofeng Mao,Zhanyu Zhang,Peijia Lin,Yansong Zhu,Yibo Zhang,Haibin Wan,Weijie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BiWM是一个首个全栈式双向自回归视频世界模型框架，仅需两阶段训练即可实现高质量、可控的视频生成与长程推演，并支持多种主流骨干网络和可选的NVFP4低比特管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果视频模型需多阶段训练且质量因误差累积而下降，而双向自回归模型虽质量高但缺乏开源框架支持，BiWM旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于预训练视频骨干，先通过微调注入摄像机控制，再执行几步分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）将骨干转化为可控世界模型；仅需两阶段训练，并引入GAN和前向KL损失以缓解DMD的模式坍缩问题。

Result: 在多种骨干（1.3B至22B）上通用，支持真实摄像机控制，支持可插拔历史压缩与NVFP4低比特管线，在有限资源下实现高保真长程推演。

Conclusion: BiWM为资源受限的研究和高保真环境模拟提供了开源、高效、高质量的双向自回归视频世界模型框架。

Abstract: Transitioning bidirectional video diffusion models into an autoregressive paradigm improves the interactivity of video world models, but existing causal pipelines need many stages (control fine-tuning, autoregressive training, causal initialization, few-step distillation) and still trail bidirectional models in quality due to error accumulation. Recent world models such as Yume-1.5 and Matrix-Game-3.0 instead adopt a bidirectional autoregressive approach, gaining fidelity and stable long-horizon rollout from self-correcting error propagation, yet open-source frameworks (e.g., minWM) support only causal models. We present BiWM, the first full-stack framework for interactive video world models under the bidirectional autoregressive paradigm, jointly optimizing generation quality and inference speed. From a pretrained video backbone, BiWM injects camera control by fine-tuning, then runs a few-step Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) stage that turns the backbone into an action/camera-controllable world model: just two training stages instead of four in minWM, converging in a few hundred steps on 8xH200 GPUs. A single recipe spans Wan2.1-1.3B, Wan2.2-5B, HunyuanVideo-1.5-8B, and LTX-2.3-22B, and also supports secondary fine-tuning of existing bidirectional models. BiWM enables real-world camera control where minWM loses controllability, integrates pluggable history compression (FramePack-style and PackForcing-style) for long rollouts, and offers an optional NVFP4 4-bit training/inference pipeline. To counter DMD's mode-seeking degradation, we add GAN and mass-covering forward-KL objectives that preserve scene dynamics. We open-source BiWM for resource-constrained research and high-fidelity environment simulation.

</details>


### [153] [CoCoSI: Collaborative Cognitive Map Construction for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10401)
*Yiming Zhang,Ruoxuan Cao,Zhihang Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种即插即用的多智能体框架，通过协作构建认知地图作为结构化空间记忆，在不修改架构或额外训练的情况下，增强任意预训练多模态大语言模型的空间理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在处理多帧视觉输入以构建空间认知地图时受限于上下文长度，而长上下文建模、外部记忆等方法需要架构修改、记忆模块或微调，难以直接应用于现成的预训练MLLM。因此需要一种轻量级、模型无关的方法来保留超出原生上下文窗口的空间信息。

Method: 提出即插即用的多智能体框架，包含：(1) 局部-全局智能体协同；(2) 带原子提交的认知地图构建；(3) 跨智能体验证。该框架无需架构修改或额外训练，可直接增强任意预训练MLLM。

Result: 在空间理解任务上取得优异性能，且完全保持训练无关（training-free）。

Conclusion: 该多智能体框架有效解决了MLLM空间理解中的上下文长度限制问题，提供了一种轻量、通用的方案，未来代码将开源。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence is a key frontier for multimodal large language models (MLLMs), enabling them to reason about the physical world from visual experience. Inspired by human spatial cognition, recent approaches construct grid-based cognitive maps from multi-frame visual inputs to maintain coherent spatial representations over time. However, limited context lengths still challenge spatial understanding, while existing methods, such as long-context modeling and external memory, often require architectural changes, memory modules, or finetuning, limiting their applicability to off-the-shelf pretrained MLLMs. This motivates a lightweight, model-agnostic method for preserving spatial information beyond the native context window. To this end, we propose a plug-and-play multi-agent framework that collaboratively constructs cognitive maps as structured spatial memory, enhancing the spatial understanding of arbitrary pretrained MLLMs without architectural modification or additional training. Our framework features local-global agent coordination, cognitive map construction with atomic commits, and cross-agent verification. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance on spatial understanding tasks while remaining fully training-free. Code will be released.

</details>


### [154] [Spatially Selective Self-Training for Unsupervised Building Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10775)
*Wafaa I. M. Hussin,Zhi Lu,Anas M. I. Mohammed,Xiang Zhou,Ratiba A. H. Abubaker,Zhenming Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SST-CD框架，通过空间选择性自训练将无标签建筑变化检测转化为端到端检测器学习，利用局部一致性准则筛选可靠像素进行伪标签训练，在三个数据集上超越现有无监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督建筑变化检测方法直接使用时序差异等作为最终变化图，没有学习任务特定的检测器，难以区分真正的建筑结构变化与外观偏移、配准误差等噪声干扰。

Method: 提出SST-CD框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 局部一致性准则筛选空间可靠像素作为训练监督信号；(2) 轻量级特征适配器校准双时相特征；(3) 基于原型的解码器生成紧凑的变化/无变化表示。

Result: 在LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD和DSIFN-CD数据集上分别达到83.08%、91.69%和86.60%的F1分数，超越现有无监督和免标签基线方法。

Conclusion: 将无标签建筑变化检测重构为在噪声伪监督下的端到端检测器学习问题，通过空间选择性自训练有效缓解了通用时序差异与建筑结构变化之间的差距。

Abstract: Unsupervised building change detection aims to learn building-change masks from unlabeled bi-temporal remote sensing images. Existing label-free methods often follow a discrepancy-to-mask paradigm, directly using temporal differences, frozen foundation-model responses, prompt-based outputs, or post-processing results as final change maps. Although these strategies provide annotation-free cues, they do not learn a task-specific building-change detector and remain vulnerable to the gap between generic temporal discrepancies and building-defined structural changes. In practice, such discrepancies are often noisy and task-irrelevant, as appearance shifts, registration errors, and non-building modifications can produce strong but misleading responses. To address this problem, we propose SST-CD, a spatially selective self-training framework that reformulates fully label-free building change detection as end-to-end detector learning under noisy pseudo supervision. SST-CD uses temporal discrepancies as candidate pseudo labels and trains the detector only on spatially reliable pixels, whose reliability is estimated by a local consistency criterion that filters inconsistent regions from supervision. To further stabilize noisy self-training, a lightweight feature adapter recalibrates bi-temporal features, while a prototype-based decoder produces compact change and no-change representations. Experiments on LEVIR-CD, WHU-CD, and DSIFN-CD show that SST-CD achieves F1 scores of 83.08%, 91.69%, and 86.60%, respectively, outperforming existing unsupervised and label-free baselines.

</details>


### [155] [SCAIL-2: Unifying Controlled Character Animation with End-to-end In-Context Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10804)
*Wenhao Yan,Fengjia Guo,Zhuoyi Yang,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCAIL-2是一个端到端角色动画框架，通过直接拼接驱动视频来替代中间表示，并利用合成数据集MotionPair-60K、上下文掩码条件、模式特定RoPE和Bias-Aware DPO等技术，在多种角色动画任务上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖中间表示（如姿态骨架、遮罩背景）进行运动迁移，导致信息丢失。作者希望绕过中间表示，实现端到端的角色动画，直接从驱动视频中获取所有视觉信息。

Method: 1) 直接拼接驱动视频到序列以端到端获取视觉信息；2) 统一角色动画子任务并构建解耦条件，合成MotionPair-60K数据集；3) 使用上下文掩码条件（in-context mask conditioning）和模式特定RoPE作为软引导；4) 提出Bias-Aware DPO构建偏好项以缓解合成数据在细节区域的偏差。

Result: 在多种角色动画任务中，SCAIL-2显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 绕过中间表示的直接端到端方法在角色动画中可行且有效，合成数据与偏好优化能进一步缩小与真实数据之间的差距。

Abstract: Controlled character animation requires transferring motion from a driving sequence to a reference character. Prior works heavily rely on intermediate representations, including pose skeletons to represent motion or masked background to represent environment, which inevitably leads to information loss. To address this, we present SCAIL-2, a framework that bypasses those intermediates and achieves \textbf{end-to-end} character animation. By directly concatenating driving videos to the sequence, the model can obtain all the required visual information from the input video. To address the lack of end-to-end data, we unify sub-tasks of character animation with decoupled conditions and then curate a pipeline to synthesize MotionPair-60K, an end-to-end motion transfer dataset containing heterogeneous tasks of character animation. To achieve the unification, we utilize in-context mask conditioning and mode-specific RoPE as soft guidance beyond textual instructions and raw visual information. To address synthetic discrepancy in detailed regions, we propose Bias-Aware DPO to construct preference items to mitigate the errors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method substantially outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches in various character animation tasks. A large subset of synthetic data as well as model weights will be released at our project page: https://teal024.github.io/SCAIL-2/.

</details>


### [156] [P3D-Bench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Parametric 3D Generation and Structural Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11152)
*Yikang Yang,Zhanpeng Hu,Youtian Lin,Mengqi Zhou,Jingxi Xu,Feihu Zhang,Jiaheng Liu,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P3D-Bench是一个面向参数化3D生成的基准测试，评估多模态大语言模型通过代码进行3D建模的能力，涵盖文本到3D、图像到3D和装配体到3D三类任务，从可执行性、几何精度、拓扑结构、文本约束、多视角语义对齐和部件级结构六个维度进行评分。实验发现：装配体任务最难，模型能恢复全局形状和语义但无法复现精确参数化几何，部件级建模能力薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试很少评估通过代码进行3D建模的能力，而这种建模方式要求从文本或视觉规范生成参数化3D程序，不仅需要代码可运行，还需要几何精确、语义对齐和装配一致性。现有基准无法全面评估MLLM在参数化3D生成方面的能力。

Method: 构建P3D-Bench基准测试，包含三大任务族（Text-to-3D、Image-to-3D、Assembly-3D），共400个文本案例、400个图像案例和203个带注释的装配体案例。采用统一评估协议，从可执行性、几何保真度、拓扑结构、文本约束、多视角语义对齐、部件级结构六个维度打分。

Result: 1) 装配体是最困难的任务，模型仍无法将多个部件组合成连贯结构；2) 模型通常能恢复目标对象的全局形状和语义身份，但无法复现输入指定的精确参数化几何；3) 部件级建模在装配体上表现薄弱，模型既无法恢复每个部件的几何，也无法恢复正确的部件数量。

Conclusion: P3D-Bench可作为评估参数化3D生成中精确参数化几何和部件级结构的基准测试，揭示了当前多模态大语言模型在这一任务上的关键局限性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models can write code to produce complex programs as well as use programs to do 3D modeling, which opens up a new avenue for 3D generation powered by their priors, world knowledge and reasoning. Yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate 3D modeling through code. Such modeling demands more than runnable code: from a text or visual specification, a model must generate a parametric 3D program that is geometrically precise, semantically aligned and assembly-consistent. We introduce P3D-Bench, a benchmark for parametric 3D generation. Unlike a 3D mesh, a parametric 3D program exposes explicit dimensions, construction operations and part relations, revealing whether a model recovers a design's structure, not just its appearance. Under a unified protocol, P3D-Bench covers three task families (Text-to-3D, Image-to-3D and Assembly-3D) and scores each output for executability, geometric fidelity, topology, text-grounded constraints, multiview semantic alignment and part-level structure. We evaluate frontier MLLMs and text-only LLMs on 400 text cases, 400 image cases and 203 annotated assemblies, with domain-specific models as reference points. Our extensive evaluation yields three findings. First, assemblies are the hardest setting, where models still fail to compose multiple parts into a coherent structure. Second, models can often recover the global shape and semantic identity of the target object, yet fail to reproduce the precise parametric geometry specified by the input. Third, part-level modeling remains weak on assemblies, where models recover neither the geometry of each part nor the right number of parts. These results position P3D-Bench as a benchmark for evaluating precise parametric geometry and part-level structure in parametric 3D generation.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Spatially Masked Regression Reveals Local and Distributed Predictability in Electrophysiological Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11415)
*Maryam Ostadsharif Memar,Nima Dehghani*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出空间掩码回归（SMR）框架，通过逐步排除目标电极邻近通道来量化神经记录中局部与分布式信息的贡献。在颅内脑电图和头皮脑电图上的实验表明，邻近电极贡献显著但非全部可预测性，且脑电图比颅内脑电图具有更强的跨被试可迁移性。SMR可作为量化局部与分布式信息平衡的可解释工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经记录通常被解读为局部测量，但单个传感器的信号也可能反映更广泛网络中的结构化活动。这引出一个基本问题：电极信号在多大程度上反映局部vs分布式信息？具体而言，电极活动有多少由其紧邻区域承载，多少更广泛地嵌入整个阵列？

Method: 提出空间掩码回归（SMR）框架：用剩余电极重建目标电极的时间序列，同时排除目标周围可配置邻域。通过逐步增大掩码尺寸，将空间局部性作为实验控制变量，量化在剔除邻近通道后还有多少预测信息保留。应用于颅内脑电图（异质电极覆盖）和头皮脑电图（感觉运动皮层的标准化导联）。使用原始信号与重建信号之间的距离相关性评估。

Result: 两种模态均观察到强被试内重建；即使排除邻近电极仍存在显著的残差可预测性；脑电图跨被试迁移显著强于颅内脑电图。掩码分析表明邻近电极对重建贡献强烈但非全部，说明单个通道同时反映局部冗余和更广泛的分布式结构。破坏相位结构或时间顺序的替代数据显著降低性能。

Conclusion: SMR是一个可解释的框架，可用于量化神经记录中局部与分布式信息的平衡。SMR依赖于结构化的时间与跨通道组织，而非仅依赖边缘统计量。

Abstract: Neural recordings are often interpreted as local measurements, yet the signal at any one sensor can also reflect structured activity distributed across the broader network. This raises a basic question: to what extent does an electrode's signal reflect local versus distributed information in the underlying system? More specifically, how much of an electrode's activity is carried by its immediate neighborhood, and how much is embedded more broadly across the array? We address this with a Spatially Masked Regression (SMR) framework that reconstructs each electrode's timeseries from the remaining electrodes while excluding a configurable neighborhood around the target. By progressively increasing this mask, spatial locality becomes an experimental control for quantifying how much predictive information survives after nearby channels are withheld. We apply SMR to intracranial EEG with heterogeneous electrode coverage and to scalp EEG with standardized montages over sensorimotor cortex. Using distance correlation between original and reconstructed signals, we find strong within-subject reconstruction in both modalities, substantial residual predictability even when local neighbors are excluded, and markedly stronger cross-subject transfer in EEG than in iEEG. Masking shows that nearby electrodes contribute strongly to reconstruction but do not account for all of it, indicating that individual channels reflect both local redundancy and broader distributed structure. Surrogates that preserve selected marginal or spectral properties while disrupting phase structure or temporal ordering substantially reduce performance, supporting the conclusion that SMR depends on structured temporal and cross-channel organization rather than on marginal statistics alone. These results position SMR as an interpretable framework for quantifying the balance between local and distributed information in recordings.

</details>


### [158] [End-to-End Machine Learning for Depressive State Classification via EEG and fNIRS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11555)
*Riki Sakurai,Simon Kojima,Mihoko Otake-Matsuura,Shin'ichiro Kanoh,Tomasz M. Rutkowski*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 为应对传统精神诊断依赖主观报告的局限，本研究以11名健康学生为对象，构建了基于EEG和fNIRS生物信号的抑郁检测框架，旨在为临床提供客观、自动化的诊断工具，尤其关注隐匿性抑郁及老年抑郁与痴呆的早期鉴别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会压力增大导致心理健康需求攀升，传统精神病学诊断（临床访谈+患者自述）易受主观偏差和经验差异影响，亟需量化、客观的检测方法。

Method: 采用脑电图（EEG）和功能性近红外光谱（fNIRS）两种生物信号进行抑郁检测，以11名健康学生为对象开展试点研究，构建检测框架。

Result: 建立了基于生物信号的抑郁检测框架，为后续自动化客观诊断工具的开发奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 生物信号检测技术在抑郁筛查中具有客观量化优势，尤其适用于隐匿性抑郁识别及老年人群中抑郁与痴呆的早期鉴别，未来有望发展为临床自动化诊断工具。

Abstract: The escalating demand for mental healthcare, driven by rising societal stress, highlights the limitations of traditional psychiatric diagnostics. Conventional methods - relying primarily on clinical interviews and patient self-reports - are inherently vulnerable to subjective bias and the varying empirical judgment of practitioners. To address the need for quantitative evaluation, biological signal-based detection, including electroencephalography (EEG) and functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS), has emerged as a promising objective alternative. Such technology is particularly vital for identifying latent depressive states that may be unrecognized by the subjects themselves. Furthermore, in aging populations, the high comorbidity between depression and dementia necessitates early differentiation to prevent mutual symptom exacerbation and maintain Quality of Life (QoL). This pilot study of eleven healthy students establishes a framework for biological signal-based depression detection, serving as a foundational step toward automated, objective diagnostic tools for clinical use.

</details>


### [159] [Large language models selectively converge with human-shared neural semantic representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11598)
*Chen Hong,Ximing Shao,Gangyi Feng*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文结合MEG脑成像与跨脑编码模型，比较人类与大语言模型（LLM）在十维语义空间上的共享神经表征结构。发现人-LLM语义维度均能解释说话者-听者神经同步（NS），且共享语义呈多维结构而非单一信号，但LLM与人类在表征几何上存在系统性差异，尤其在能动性、情感和社会性维度上偏差最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人际交流需要共享语义结构以实现有效沟通，但共享神经表征的维度结构尚不清楚。LLM日益逼近人类语言能力，但其是否捕捉到人类大脑间共享的语义结构仍未知。

Method: 采用故事讲述-聆听伪超扫描MEG实验，结合维度解析的跨脑编码模型，从人类和五种LLM（如GPT系列等）分别获取十维语义评分（感知、运动、空间、时间、社会性、生命性、情感、注意、因果、驱力），评估这些维度对说话者-听者神经同步的解释力，并控制声学/语音特征。

Result: 人类和LLM语义空间均能解释神经同步，且共享语义呈多维结构而非单维信号。这些模式还能预测听者故事理解的个体差异。但整体预测能力相当的表象下，存在系统性的表征几何差异：越大越先进的LLM与人类在语义结构及神经同步上重合度越高，但这种对齐仍不完整且具有维度依赖性，在能动性、情感和社会经验相关维度上差异最大。

Conclusion: LLM捕捉了人类共享神经语义的实质性成分，但其对齐是选择性的。更大或更强的模型会改善近似程度，但社会性和情感性维度的表征仍只能被部分捕获。

Abstract: Interpersonal communication requires building shared semantics that enable listeners to understand speakers' meanings from their unfolding language, but the dimensional structure of this shared neural representation remains unclear. LLMs increasingly approximate human language capability and neural responses, raising the question of whether they capture the same semantic structure shared between human brains. Here, we combined storytelling-listening pseudo-hyperscanning MEG with dimension-resolved interbrain encoding modeling to compare human- and LLM-derived accounts of shared neural semantic representations. Content words from the speaker's narratives were rated by humans and five recent LLMs along ten semantic dimensions (i.e., perception, motor, space, time, socialness, animacy, emotion, attention, causality, and drive). We tested whether these dimensions explained speaker-listener neural synchronization (NS) beyond acoustic and phonological features. Both human- and LLM-derived semantic spaces explained NS, but these shared semantics are better characterized as a multidimensional neural structure rather than a single global signal. These patterns also predicted individual differences in listeners' story comprehension, linking neural alignment to cognition. However, comparable overall prediction concealed systematic differences in representational geometry. Larger LLMs aligned more closely and showed greater overlap with humans in semantic structure and NS, but this was incomplete and dimension-dependent. The largest divergences emerged for dimensions closely tied to agency, affect, and social experience. These findings show that LLMs capture substantial components of human shared neural semantics, but their alignment is selective. Larger or more capable models improve the approximation, whereas socially and affectively grounded dimensions are captured only partially.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Restless bandits with imperfect binary feedback: PCL-indexability analysis and computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11192)
*José Niño-Mora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有二元潜在状态和不完美二元反馈的Restless Bandit问题，提出了基于部分守恒律（PCL）的分析与计算框架，用于建立可索引性并计算Whittle指数。通过确定性骨架、更新分解和组合词分析，在多个阈值区域推导出折扣奖励和资源度量的可处理表达式，并通过数值实验验证了其余区域的PCL可索引性条件。实验表明，MP指数策略通常显著优于标准基准策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在机会频谱接入等应用中，Restless Bandit模型存在感知误差（不完美反馈）且状态为隐变量，导致经典Whittle指数方法难以直接应用。亟需一个既能理论验证可索引性又能高效计算Whittle指数的统一框架，且避免先前工作中苛刻的参数限制。

Method: 基于部分守恒律（PCL）建立分析与计算框架；利用确定性骨架分析随机动力学；采用更新分解和组合词技术；在阈值区域进行解析验证；对未完全验证区域设计高效数值方案计算边际生产(MP)指数（等于Whittle指数）。

Result: 在多个阈值区域完全验证了PCL可索引性条件并得到闭式表达式；在剩余区域通过数值实验提供了可索引性成立的有力证据，且无需先前工作的严格参数限制；MP指数策略在广泛参数范围内显著优于标准基准策略。

Conclusion: 本文为带感知误差的Restless Bandit提供了完整的PCL可索引性验证与Whittle指数计算方案，理论分析和数值实验共同支持该方法在广泛参数范围内的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: We study restless bandits with binary latent states and imperfect binary feedback, motivated by opportunistic spectrum access with sensing errors. For the associated belief-state model, we develop a partial conservation laws (PCL)-based analytical and computational framework for establishing indexability and evaluating the Whittle index, building on a verification theorem for real-state discounted restless bandits. The framework analyzes the stochastic dynamics via an associated deterministic skeleton, renewal decompositions, and combinatorics on words. It yields tractable expressions for discounted reward and resource metrics in several threshold regimes, enabling full verification of the PCL-indexability conditions there. For the remaining regime, where a complete analytic verification is not achieved in this paper, we derive efficient numerical schemes for computing the relevant marginal metrics and the marginal productivity (MP) index, which equals the Whittle index when those conditions hold. Extensive computational experiments provide strong evidence that these conditions also hold in that regime across broad parameter ranges and without the stringent parameter restrictions imposed in prior work. The experiments further show that theMP index policy typically outperforms standard benchmark policies, often by a substantial margin.

</details>


### [161] [To Intervene or Not: Guiding Inference-time Alignment with Probabilistic Model Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11201)
*Jin Gan,Xin Li,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统评估发现推理时对齐中不同模型的指导有效性差异显著，无效指导会导致过度干预和性能下降。为此提出BlendIn框架，从二元决策转向创建混合分布，基于可靠性按比例加权各模型贡献，实现质量感知对齐，在困难模型对上取得最高50%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法直接从对齐模型中提取指导，但未评估其可靠性。系统评估发现指导有效性在不同模型间差异极大，无效指导会导致模型混淆和过度干预，造成性能下降。

Method: 提出BlendIn框架，将二元决策转变为创建混合分布以整合多模型知识。通过质量感知对齐，基于可靠性按比例加权各模型的贡献，保留有益指导同时降低不可靠建议的权重。

Result: 在困难模型对上实现了一致且最高达50%的性能提升，提供了对错误对齐指导的诊断信号和缓解策略。

Conclusion: BlendIn通过可靠性感知的混合分布策略有效解决了推理时对齐中指导质量不均的问题，相比现有方法更稳定高效。

Abstract: The wide deployment of LLMs has made model alignment necessary to make newly trained models safely and effectively respond to user instructions. Among different methods, inference-time alignment is often cheaper as it intervenes (i.e., offers guidances) only during output generation. Existing proposals apply guidances extracted from certain aligned models without properly assessing their reliability. Nonetheless, our systematic evaluation reveals that guidance effectiveness varies drastically across models; since ineffective guidances lead to further confusion and thus further interventions, the resulting excessive interventions typically indicate poor performance. To make interventions more effective and thus more efficient, we introduce BlendIn, an inference-time alignment framework that shifts from binary decisions to creating hybrid distributions integrating both models' knowledge. BlendIn stabilizes inference-time alignment by performing quality-aware alignment and proportionally weighting each model's contribution based on reliability. Compared with existing works, it preserves beneficial guidance while downweighting unreliable suggestions. BlendIn provides both diagnostic signals and mitigation strategies for misaligned guidance, achieving consistent and up to 50% performance improvement on challenging model pairs. Our code is available at: https://github.com/DecayingSeart/BlendIn.

</details>


### [162] [Dual-Stance Evaluation of Sycophancy: The Structure of Agreement and the Limits of Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11205)
*Matthew James Buchan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现虽然模型在激活空间中用几何上分离的子空间分别表示谄媚性同意和事实性同意，但通过质心差引导的方向对两者有同等投影，无法差异化干预，导致减少谄媚的同时也减少了正确事实的同意（如地球是圆的），揭示了激活空间中“可读”未必“可写”的普遍问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导（activation steering）方法能减少LLM的谄媚行为，但标准评估未检验该方法是否也会抑制对事实正确陈述的同意，存在评估盲区。

Method: 提出双立场评估方法（dual-stance evaluation），对每个话题测试正反两种立场；采用质心差引导（centroid-difference steering）对Llama-3-8B-Instruct进行干预分析，对比谄媚性同意与事实性同意在激活空间中的几何关系。

Result: 谄媚与事实同意在几何上位于分离的子空间，但引导方向对两者投影相等，无法差异化干预；引导后同时减少谄媚性同意和正确事实的同意；两组激活的其他静态属性均匹配。

Conclusion: 行为层面的解离源于生成动态或残差流分析无法捕捉的细粒度结构，揭示了激活空间中“可读”不等于“可写”的普遍局限性。

Abstract: Activation steering can shift LLM behaviour, but standard evaluations do not typically test whether a sycophancy-reduction direction also suppresses agreement with factually correct statements. We introduce dual-stance evaluation, which tests both stances of each topic, and apply it to centroid-difference steering on Llama-3-8B-Instruct. We find a dissociation: the model represents sycophantic and factual agreement in geometrically distinct subspaces, yet the steering direction projects equally onto both and cannot differentially target either. The direction accordingly reduces agreement with factually correct statements (e.g. that the Earth is round) as well as sycophantic ones. All other static properties of the two activation groups are matched, suggesting the behavioural dissociation arises from generation dynamics or from finer-grained structure that residual-stream analysis cannot resolve. The pattern illustrates a general gap: representations that are readable from activations may not be writable through them.

</details>


### [163] [Few-Shot Resampling for Scalable Statistically-Sound Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11235)
*Leonardo Pellegrina,Fabio Vandin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FewRS方法，利用新推导的检验统计量上确界偏差界，仅需极少量重采样数据集即可严格评估数据挖掘结果的统计显著性，在模式挖掘和网络分析等任务上相比现有方法减少多达两个数量级的运行时间，同时保持高统计功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重采样方法需要生成和分析数千个重采样数据集，对于大规模数据集或计算密集型分析来说不切实际，因此需要一种更高效的统计显著性评估方法。

Method: 推导了检验统计量上确界偏差的新边界，基于此提出FewRS方法，仅需生成和分析极少量的重采样数据集即可保证错误发现概率的严格保证。

Result: 在模式挖掘和网络分析任务上，相比现有方法运行时间减少高达两个数量级，同时保持高统计功效，能够在大型真实世界数据集上实现统计验证。

Conclusion: FewRS是一种简单有效、高度可扩展且广泛适用的重采样方法，适用于任何现有重采样方法的应用场景。

Abstract: A key step in knowledge discovery is the evaluation of data mining results. In several applications, including pattern mining, graph analysis, and others, this step includes the evaluation of the statistical significance of the results, to avoid spurious discoveries due only to noise or random fluctuations in the data. While specialized procedures have been developed for some specific applications, resampling-based approaches are widely used, in particular for complex analyses where analytical results cannot be derived. However, current resampling-based approaches require the generation and analysis of thousands of resampled datasets, and are therefore impractical for large datasets or computationally intensive analyses.
  In this paper, we introduce FewRS, a simple and effective resampling-based approach to assess the statistical significance of data mining results with rigorous guarantees on the probability of false discoveries. Our approach can be used in every situation where resampling-based approaches are applied. FewRS builds on our derivation of a novel bound to the supremum deviation of test statistics representing the quality of data mining results. We prove that FewRS needs to generate and analyze an extremely small number of resampled datasets, leading to a highly scalable approach with wide applicability. We test our approach on common tasks such as pattern mining and network analysis. In all cases, our approach results in a reduction of up to two orders of magnitude in running time compared to the state of the art, while preserving high statistical power, enabling the statistical validation of data mining results on large-scale real-world datasets.

</details>


### [164] [Flow Matching with In-Context Priors for Out-of-Distribution Brain Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11833)
*Sam Gijsen,Michał Łukomski,Marc-André Schulz,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逐时间步条件扩散Transformer的模型，能够通过组合语言和可选空间先验，在未见过的认知任务条件下生成逼真的fMRI脑动态数据，首次实现全脑fMRI动力学的零样本生成，支持反事实神经科学和实验设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经时间序列生成模型局限于分类条件，无法实现组合性和零样本泛化，限制了在未见认知任务上的反事实神经科学研究。

Method: 提出逐时间步条件扩散Transformer，通过注入组合语言和可选空间先验（in-context），在未见认知任务条件下生成fMRI脑动态数据。

Result: 模型在数百个未见任务条件下进行评估，仅凭语言就能恢复区域特异性招募和空间激活模式；空间先验可在语言退化区域补充生成，同时保持组合结构。

Conclusion: 这是首个在未见认知任务上生成全脑fMRI动力学的生成模型，推动了反事实神经科学和数据驱动实验设计的发展。

Abstract: Flow matching and diffusion models enable conditional generation across domains ranging from images to proteins, with recent extensions to out-of-distribution contexts. Yet generative models of neural time series have largely remained restricted to categorical conditioning, precluding compositional and zero-shot generalization. In this work, we propose a per-timestep conditioned diffusion transformer for generating realistic fMRI brain dynamics during unseen cognitive tasks by injecting both compositional language and optional spatial priors in-context. Such zero-shot generation could enable counterfactual neuroscience by supporting in-silico design and evaluation of novel cognitive experiments before empirical validation. Leveraging this model, we evaluate across hundreds of held-out task conditions and characterize predictive performance in relation to the training manifold. From language alone, the model recovers region-specific recruitment across tasks and held-out spatial activation patterns. Spatial priors, when available, complement the text pathway by anchoring generation in regions of task space where language alone degrades, while retaining the compositional structure needed for counterfactual task specification. To our knowledge this is the first generative model of whole-cortex fMRI dynamics for unseen cognitive tasks, advancing counterfactual neuroscience and data-driven experimental design.

</details>


### [165] [ProHiFlo: Hierarchical Flow Matching with Functional Guidance for De Novo Protein Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11243)
*Chuanzhen Wang,Meade Cleti,Pete Jano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProHiFlo是一种层次化流匹配框架，通过粗到细生成、功能引导和自适应SE(3)-等变架构三大创新，在无条件生成、基序支架和功能设计任务上达到最优性能，采样步骤减少4倍，酶活性位点支架成功率58.9% vs RFDiffusion 41.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流匹配方法通常仅在单一分辨率下操作，缺乏整合功能约束的机制，难以满足蛋白质从头生成中对功能特性的精确控制需求。

Method: 提出层次化流匹配框架ProHiFlo，包含三项创新：(1)粗到细生成策略，先建模主链几何再细化到全原子坐标；(2)利用预训练预测器提供功能引导，无需重训练即可调控生成方向；(3)自适应SE(3)-等变架构实现高效多尺度处理。

Result: 在无条件生成、基序支架和功能设计任务上达到最优性能，采样步骤减少4倍；酶活性位点支架任务成功率58.9%，显著优于RFDiffusion的41.2%。

Conclusion: ProHiFlo通过层次化设计和功能引导机制，有效解决了蛋白质从头生成中的多尺度建模和功能约束整合问题，为治疗设计、酶工程和合成生物学提供了更高效可控的方案。

Abstract: De novo protein generation has transformative potential in therapeutic design, enzyme engineering, and synthetic biology. While diffusion-based and flow matching approaches have achieved progress, they typically operate at single resolution and lack mechanisms for incorporating functional constraints. We introduce ProHiFlo, a hierarchical flow matching framework with three innovations: (1) coarse-to-fine generation that models backbone geometry before refining to all-atom coordinates, reducing computational cost while maintaining accuracy; (2) functional guidance leveraging pretrained predictors to steer generation toward desired properties without retraining; (3) adaptive SE(3)-equivariant architecture for efficient multi-scale processing. Experiments on unconditional generation, motif scaffolding, and functional design demonstrate state-ofthe-art performance while requiring 4 fewer sampling steps. On enzyme active site scaffolding, ProHiFlo achieves 58.9% success rate compared to 41.2% for RFDiffusion.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond representational alignment with brain-guided language models for robust reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11893)
*Mingqing Xiao,Kai Du,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型（LLMs）的内部表征与人类推理相关脑区的fMRI信号部分对齐，并进一步提出一种脑引导框架，通过联合模型与脑表征的结构方向对模型进行推理干预和微调，显著提升LLM在演绎推理任务上的准确率（最高提升13%），将LLM-大脑对应关系从相关性推进到指导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLMs与人类高阶认知（尤其是推理）背后的神经机制之间的对应关系尚不明确，人类大脑中语言与推理是可分离的，因此一个开放问题是LLMs是否与推理相关脑区的神经信号对齐，以及这些信号能否反过来提升LLMs的推理能力。

Method: 首先使用神经预测性指标衡量LLMs内部表征与任务态fMRI活动中推理相关脑区的对齐程度；随后提出脑引导框架，联合模型与大脑表征的联合结构方向，在推理时进行表征干预（inference-time intervention），在训练时进行微调（fine-tuning），将任务诱发的脑信号直接用于增强LLM推理。

Result: LLMs在聚合水平上解释了推理相关脑区中大部分可解释方差，但特定推理类型内的预测性较低，表明既有对齐也有差异。脑引导方法在10种LLM（1.5B-72B参数规模）上均带来了正交于纯语言监督的增益，推理类型间可迁移，最高绝对准确率提升达13%。

Conclusion: 研究将LLM-大脑的对应关系从相关性推进到指导性，建立了一条通过脑信号驱动实现更鲁棒且认知对齐更强的AI的路径。

Abstract: The correspondence between large language models (LLMs) and the neural mechanisms underlying human higher-order cognition remains insufficiently characterized. Given that language and reasoning in the human brain appear dissociable, an open question is whether LLMs align with neural signals from reasoning-related regions and whether such signals can improve them. Here, focusing on deductive reasoning, we show that LLM internal representations are not only partially aligned with task-fMRI activity but can also be directly enhanced by these signals. Using a neural-predictivity metric, we find that LLMs explain a substantial fraction of the explainable variance in reasoning-related regions at the aggregate level, whereas predictivity within specific reasoning types is lower, indicating both alignment and divergence. Building on this, we propose a brain-guided framework: we steer model representations along directions induced by the joint structure of model and brain representations, applying intervention at inference and fine-tuning during training. We demonstrate that task-evoked brain signals can directly enhance LLM reasoning, yielding gains orthogonal to language-only supervision across 10 LLMs (1.5B-72B), with transfer across reasoning types and up to 13\% absolute accuracy gain. Our results advance LLM-brain correspondences from correlation to guidance, establishing a brain-signal-driven pathway toward more robust and cognitively aligned AI.

</details>


### [167] [Physics-informed generative AI for semiconductor manufacturing: Enforcing hard physical constraints in generative models by construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11247)
*Yaser Mike Banad,Sarah Sharif*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了半导体制造领域中生成式AI必须内建物理约束而非事后过滤的核心观点，综述了物理信息生成模型的技术工具包，并提出了面向物理保真度的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成模型依赖感知似然性，但在半导体制造等受硬物理约束支配的领域中，物理无效的样本不仅是低质量的，而是完全不可用的。因此需要论证：受约束物理域中的生成AI必须通过架构设计内建物理信息，而非通过事后过滤纠正。

Method: 综述了物理信息扩散模型、PDE约束变分模型、神经算子先验、守恒律约束生成网络等新兴架构工具，识别了生成模型与物理仿真器之间的四种集成模式，并提出了以物理保真度基准、可微仿真器基础设施、多模态基础模型为核心的研究议程。

Result: 提出核心论断：在物理有效性是成功约束条件的环境中，通过架构设计内建物理约束的模型在性能上应优于事后过滤的模型，而半导体制造工厂正是这一区分最为鲜明的场景。

Conclusion: 物理有效性作为约束条件时，内建物理信息的生成架构优于事后过滤方案；半导体制造是验证这一论断的最具挑战性和代表性的领域。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly used to propose designs, data, and control actions for physical systems, yet many such systems are governed by hard physical constraints rather than by perceptual plausibility. Semiconductor manufacturing provides a demanding test case: generated masks, layouts, synthetic defect data, and process recipes must obey lithography, transport, reaction, and device-physics constraints, because physically invalid samples are not merely low quality but unusable. This Perspective argues that semiconductor manufacturing exposes a broader computational-science challenge, namely that generative AI for constrained physical domains must be physics-informed by construction, not corrected only through post-hoc filtering. We survey the emerging architectural toolkit, including physics-informed diffusion, PDE-constrained variational models, neural-operator priors, and conservation-law-respecting generative networks, and show how it connects to differentiable lithography, TCAD, process simulation, and autonomous experimentation. We identify four integration patterns between generative models and physics-based simulators, and we propose a research agenda centered on physics-fidelity benchmarks, differentiable simulator infrastructure, and multimodal foundation models for physical design and manufacturing. The central claim is analytical rather than rhetorical: where physical validity is the binding criterion of success, architectures that enforce it by construction should be expected to outperform those that filter for it after the fact, and the fab is the setting where this distinction is sharpest.

</details>


### [168] [Mechanical Field Networks: Structured Neural Dynamics for Multivariate Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11251)
*Xingji Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MF-Net是一种循环动力模型，通过将多变量系统表示为共享场状态并学习关系-运动驱动的演化机制，在实现高精度预测的同时保持可解析的结构读出能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么预设可解释的动态结构，要么学习灵活的状态转移但隐式处理交互关系，缺乏在预测过程中同时显式揭示变量间关系结构的能力。

Method: 提出MF-Net，每个变量携带场分量，所有场分量通过可学习的力学转移共同演化。转移采用关系-运动组织方式：学习的关系塑造状态依赖的流、场响应和运动趋势，推动场状态前向演化。内部量同时支持预测和结构读出。

Result: 在已知定律系统、混沌基准（40维Lorenz-96八步R²=0.798±0.018，Precision@K=1.000±0.000）、真实神经记录和生态时间序列上，取得了竞争性的短中期预测性能，并保持可检视的结构读出。

Conclusion: MF-Net提供了结构可读的动力建模框架，其中学习的关系通过前向演化训练得到，在真实数据上可在适当观测限制下解释为功能性的预测耦合。

Abstract: Many multivariate dynamical systems are observed only through trajectories, leaving the mechanisms governing their joint dynamics hidden. Existing approaches can impose interpretable dynamics or learn flexible state transitions, yet the resulting interaction structure is typically either specified in advance or left implicit within the learned dynamics. We introduce MF-Net, a recurrent dynamical model that represents all variables in a shared field state and updates this state through a learned relation law. Each variable carries a field component, and these components evolve jointly through a learnable mechanical transition. Here, mechanical refers to the relation-to-motion organization of the transition, where learned relations shape state-dependent flows, field responses, and motion tendencies that move the field state forward. The resulting structure is part of the rollout itself: learned relations influence how the field moves, and the same internal quantities support both forecasting and structural readout. Across known-law interaction systems, chaotic benchmarks, real neural recordings, and ecological time series, MF-Net achieves competitive short- and medium-horizon forecasting while retaining inspectable structural readout. On the 40-dimensional Lorenz--96 testbed, MF-Net achieves an eight-step $R^2$ of $0.798\pm0.018$; across five seeds, its learned relation matrix recovers the local coupling support with a local/nonlocal strength ratio of $19.80\pm1.00$ and Precision@$K$ of $1.000\pm0.000$. MF-Net provides a structure-readable dynamical modeling framework in which learned relations are trained through forward evolution and, on real data, interpreted as functional predictive couplings under appropriate observational limits.

</details>


### [169] [Bernstein-Schur Kernels: Random Features by Sketched Modulation and Radial Randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11255)
*Taha Bouhsine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对 Bernstein–Schur 核（有限特征核与完全单调平移不变核的乘积）提出一种双随机化特征构造方法：对有限调制进行 sketch，对完全单调径向因子进行 Bernstein–Widder 采样后施加高斯随机傅里叶特征。理论证明了无偏性、方差表达式、算子范数界（由核与调制的 Gram 矩阵顶特征值及内在维度控制），并给出了确定性相对谱核岭稳定性结果。以 yat 核作为实例展示了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernstein–Schur 核既非纯平移不变核也非纯点积核，因此 Bochner 采样和多项式 sketching 均无法直接适用。需要一种通用的随机特征构造方法来高效近似这类混合核。

Method: 将核分解为有限特征映射（调制部分）与完全单调径向部分的乘积。对调制部分做 sketch（有限维特征投影），对径向部分利用 Bernstein–Widder 定理表示为尺度混合，对每个尺度采样后施加 d 维高斯随机傅里叶特征。

Result: 1) 固定调制（m→∞）时证明了无偏性、推荐平坦估计器的精确方差、基于核和调制 Gram 矩阵顶特征值与内在维度的矩阵-Bernstein 算子范数界及高概率尾界、确定性相对谱核岭稳定性。2) 双随机化估计继承上述内在维度算子范数保证，并附加一个可由 m 独立于 D 调节的 sketch 项。

Conclusion: 提出了一种适用于 Bernstein–Schur 核族的双随机化特征框架，理论性质优良，且 yat 核实例表明该方法具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Bernstein--Schur kernels are products of a finite-feature kernel (one with an explicit finite-dimensional feature map) and a completely monotone shift-invariant kernel: nonstationary kernels that fall between the shift-invariant and dot-product templates random features usually exploit, so in general neither Bochner sampling nor polynomial sketching applies to the full kernel directly. We give one random-feature construction for the whole class that \emph{randomizes both factors: it sketches the finite modulation and randomizes the completely monotone radial factor, sampling the latter's one-dimensional Bernstein--Widder scale and then applying Gaussian random Fourier features (whose frequency is still $d$-dimensional). The feature dimension is then $Dm$, set by the sketch size $m$ and the radial-draw count $D$, free of the $O(d^2)$ size of the exact modulation feature. Keeping the modulation \emph{exact} is the analyzable limit ($m\to\infty$): there we prove unbiasedness, an exact variance for the recommended flat estimator, an expected matrix-Bernstein operator-norm bound (with a matching high-probability tail) controlled by the top eigenvalues of the kernel and modulation Gram matrices together with an intrinsic dimension rather than the crude $N\max_{ij}$ entrywise route, and a deterministic relative-spectral kernel-ridge stability result. By conditioning on the sketch, the doubly-randomized estimator inherits the same intrinsic-dimension operator-norm guarantee plus a single additive sketch term, tunable by $m$ independently of $D$. The motivating instance is the biased $yat$-kernel $k_{yat,b}(w,x)=(w^\top x+b)^2/(\|w-x\|^2+\varepsilon)$, $b\ge0$, whose family span contains the inverse-multiquadric kernel by finite differences in $b$; for it the radial mixture is the IMQ spectral sampler, and one frequency per scale is variance-optimal at a fixed radial-feature budget.

</details>


### [170] [Loss Landscape Diagnosis for Gradient-Based Gray-Scott System Inversion: Disentangling the Roles of PINN Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11258)
*Yan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文直接通过反应-扩散系统的PDE结构反向传播梯度来恢复参数，发现优化失败源于损失景观中存在平坦高原和陡峭峭壁（与分岔边界对齐）的几何结构。进一步通过消融PINN实验揭示：残差损失项本身即可避免该病态，而神经网络仅用于补全观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度反演反应-扩散系统通常使用代理模型或PINN，而最直接的方法——通过PDE本身反向传播——被回避了。本文旨在探究这一直接路径为何失效，并揭示其根本原因。

Method: 将Gray-Scott模拟展开(unrolled)，通过稳态损失反向传播梯度进行参数恢复，不依赖任何代理模型或神经网络的辅助；并通过可视化损失景观分析优化失败原因；进一步将PINN各组件消融，分别检验残差损失和神经网络的作用。

Result: 直接优化无法收敛，损失景观呈现平坦高原（无梯度信号）被陡峭峭壁（与分岔边界对齐）分割的结构，该结构对损失函数选择和梯度路由方式具有稳健性。消融实验发现：残差损失（固定神经网络）呈二次型光滑景观，已能避免病态；神经网络无法修复病态参数子空间，仅用于补全观测数据。

Conclusion: PINN类型方法中各组件存在明确的分工：残差损失隐式编码了全部初始条件下的完整PDE动力学，是解决病态问题的关键；神经网络仅完成数据插补。这为PINN方法设计提供了具体指导，并揭示了何时增加额外维度真正有助于解决问题。

Abstract: Gradient-based inversion of reaction-diffusion systems is typically approached via surrogate models or physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), while the most direct route, backpropagation through the PDE's structure itself, has largely been avoided. We pursue this direct route as a diagnostic probe, backpropagating a steady-state loss through unrolled Gray-Scott simulation to recover its parameters, with no surrogate or neural-network augmentation. Optimization fails to converge, and plotting the landscape directly locates the failure in its geometry -- flat plateaus with no gradient signal, bounded by sharp cliffs that align with bifurcation boundaries -- a structure that recurs across loss functions and is inherited however the gradients are routed to parameters. Reading this minimal setup as an ablation of PINN, we disentangle each component's role: with the neural network fixed, the residual loss is quadratic in the PDE parameters and yields a smooth landscape, so it alone already avoids the pathology, by implicitly encoding the full PDE dynamics across all initial conditions. The neural network, for its part, cannot repair an ill-posed parameter subspace, and so serves only to complete the observed data -- a division of labor not previously made explicit. These findings carry concrete design implications for PINN-type methods and a broader heuristic on when added dimensions actually help.

</details>


### [171] [PermDoRA -- Understanding Adapter Interference in Language Models: Limits of Parameter-Space Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11262)
*Gowtham Sivaramakrishnan,Sarvesha Kumar Kombaiah Seetha,Kishan Gupta Balaji,Santhosh Baradwaj Vaduvur Ranganathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DoRA-RBAC分层适配器组合框架，测试了两种合并策略（欧几里得合并与黎曼几何感知合并）。结果表明，几何感知合并相比标准平均没有持续优势，且适配器更新的角度对齐和正交性对组合性能的预测能力很弱。结论是适配器干扰主要由共享非线性表示中的交互导致，而非参数空间几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的访问控制需要模块化机制来实现领域特定行为，而无需重新训练或跨领域干扰。常见的假设是适配器组合中的干扰源于线性参数更新的重叠，因此施加正交性或方向独立性应能改善多领域性能。本文旨在检验这一假设。

Method: 使用DoRA-RBAC（基于权重分解低秩适配的分层适配器组合框架），在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Mistral-7B上，对多个问答基准（GPQA、PubMedQA、SimpleQA、WMDP）比较了欧几里得合并与近似Frechet均值的黎曼几何感知合并策略。

Result: 1）单领域性能与LoRA相当；2）几何感知合并相比标准平均在多领域设置中没有一致优势；3）适配器更新的角度对齐和正交性是组合性能的弱预测因子。

Conclusion: 适配器干扰的主要来源不是参数空间几何，而是共享非线性表示中的交互作用。该发现挑战了通过正交性约束来改善多领域适配器组合的主流假设。

Abstract: Access control in large language models (LLMs) requires modular mechanisms to enable domain-specific behavior without retraining or cross-domain interference. A common hypothesis is that interference during adapter composition arises from overlap in linear parameter updates, suggesting that enforcing orthogonality or directional independence should improve multi-domain performance. We test this hypothesis using DoRA-RBAC, a hierarchical adapter composition framework based on weight-decomposed low-rank adaptation. We compare conventional Euclidean merging with a geometry-aware Riemannian-inspired merging strategy that approximates the Frechet mean via normalized directional averaging across multiple QA benchmarks (GPQA, PubMedQA, SimpleQA, WMDP) on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B. Our results show that while single-domain performance matches LoRA, geometry-aware merging provides no consistent advantage over standard averaging in multi-domain settings.Diagnostic analysis further reveals that angular alignment and orthogonality of adapter updates are weak predictors of composition performance. These findings suggest that adapter interference is not governed primarily by parameter-space geometry, but is instead consistent with interactions in shared nonlinear representations.

</details>


### [172] [Seeing Before Colliding: Anticipatory Safe RL with Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11266)
*Samuel Tetteh,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VLM-Safe-RL框架，将冻结的视觉语言模型集成到CMDP拉格朗日更新中作为预判成本项，解决约束RL在高速场景下因成本信号滞后导致安全性不足的问题。在Safety-Gymnasium FormulaOne L2等环境中，该方法在保持较高回报的同时将成本控制在预算内，优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束RL依赖的反应式成本信号（碰撞发生后或预算超限后才产生代价）在高速场景下结构性地滞后，无法及时干预瞬时且不可逆的碰撞。需要一种预判机制在碰撞发生前提供安全信号。

Method: 提出四个组件：(i) 解耦双路径CLIP，独立的奖励/成本路径尊重CMDP分解；(ii) VLM-Lagrange，在乘子更新中引入每步VLM成本作为预判项；(iii) 置信门控，基于CLIP间隔的逻辑噪声模型推导贝叶斯最优权重；(iv) VLMPPOLag，组合上述组件的完整算法。

Result: 在Safety-Gymnasium FormulaOne L2上，VLMPPOLag+Conf是唯一在保持回报~40的同时在多数种子内将成本控制在预算内的配置；在MetaDrive Medium上灾难率从41%降至26%；在Bullet Safety-Gym上表现方向一致；在MetaDrive Easy/Hard及Qwen2-VL骨干上未能泛化。

Conclusion: 这是首个将冻结的VLM信号作为预判成本项集成到CMDP拉格朗日更新的工作，验证了该方法在高速安全场景的有效性，并诚实报告了失效案例及其原因。

Abstract: The cost signal that constrained-RL algorithms optimize against is almost always reactive: the simulator emits a non-zero cost only after a collision has begun, and the Lagrange multiplier of PPO-Lagrangian grows only after the episode budget has been exceeded. At race speeds, where collisions are instantaneous and irreversible, any safety mechanism that waits for cost to accumulate is structurally too late. We present VLM-Safe-RL, a framework that integrates a frozen vision-language model into the CMDP Lagrangian update as an anticipatory cost term. The framework comprises four contributions: (i) Decoupled Dual-Path CLIP, independent reward/cost paths that respect the CMDP's factorization; (ii) VLM-Lagrange, an augmented multiplier update that incorporates a per-step VLM cost as an anticipatory term; (iii) Confidence Gating, a Bayes-optimal weight derived from a logistic noise model on the CLIP margin; and (iv) VLMPPOLag, the composed algorithm. On Safety-Gymnasium FormulaOne L2, our principal evaluation ($n{=}5$ seeds, $10^{6}$ steps, budget $d_{\text{lim}}{=}25$) VLMPPOLag$+$Conf is the only configuration in our default budget comparison that simultaneously retains substantive return ($J_r{\approx}40$) and holds cost within budget on a majority of seeds; the five constraint-aware baselines (PPOLag, CPO, CPPOPID, CPO-CLG, PPOLag-RND) each fail at least one requirement. The mechanism generalizes to held-out MetaDrive Medium (catastrophe rate $41\%{\to}26\%$, 95\% bootstrap CI $[-26,-5]$\,pp) and shows directionally consistent transfer to Bullet Safety-Gym; we report honestly where it does not (MetaDrive Easy/Hard, Qwen2-VL backbone) and trace the Hard failure to a Lagrangian-regulation pathology rather than the VLM signal itself. To our knowledge, this is the first work to use frozen VLM signals as an anticipatory cost term inside the CMDP Lagrangian update.

</details>


### [173] [A prior-free blind detection of information leakage from model predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11267)
*Laurence A. Jacobs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从模型预测和真实结果出发检测数据泄露的决策理论框架，证明了泄露可被分类为三种类型（未校准、广泛校准、确定性），并给出了对应的检测方法。其中确定性泄露产生的"单位纯度头部"是任何非确定性真实预测器无法伪造的，可作为无先验检验的依据。在UK Biobank数据上验证了检测下限约为ΔĈ≈0.007。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据泄露是机器学习驱动科学研究中最主要的可重复性失败来源，但现有检测工具需要训练代码、外部数据或领域专业知识，无法仅基于模型输出进行检测。本文旨在探索能否仅从预测结果和真实结局出发判断数据泄露的存在。

Method: 建立决策理论框架，将泄露诊断定义为预测风险/结局联合分布的函数，通过阈值加权与恰当评分规则和决策曲线分析建立联系。推导了泄露的三个分类（未校准泄露、广泛校准泄露、确定性泄露），并针对每类提出匹配的检测器。在UK Biobank上使用时窗共病泄露进行验证。

Result: 证明了重校准后的泄露模型在标定和区分度上与诚实模型无法区分，任何基于预测的函数都无法识别。确定性泄露（如近标签泄露）会产生持续的"单位纯度头部"，这是非确定性真实预测器无法制造的，可实现无先验检验。检测下限ΔĈ≈0.007。

Conclusion: 输出仅检测在下述情况下失败：残留泄露与一个诚实的更强预测器无法区分。检测可在普通硬件上于一秒内完成对预测向量的判定。数值下限是队列和终点特异的，但结构性的结论是通用的。

Abstract: Data leakage -- contamination of a model with information unavailable at baseline -- is the dominant reproducibility failure in machine-learning-based science, yet detection tools require training code, external data, or domain expertise. None operates on the artifact an auditor most often holds: the model's output. We ask what can be decided about leakage from predictions and outcomes alone. We give a decision-theoretic framework in which leakage diagnostics are functionals of the predicted-risk/outcome law, parameterized by a threshold-weighting linked to proper scoring rules and decision-curve analysis. We prove a sharp impossibility: a recalibrated leak matching an honest model's calibration and discrimination is indistinguishable from honest performance by \emph{any} function of the predictions, so the broad class is detectable only against an externally supplied ceiling on achievable discrimination. We then prove what leakage cannot hide: a near-deterministic subgroup -- the signature of a near-label leak -- produces a sustained unit-purity head that no legitimate predictor of a non-deterministic outcome can manufacture, yielding a prior-free test. These results organize leakage into a trichotomy -- miscalibrated, broad-calibrated, and deterministic -- each with a matched detector and failure mode. We validate on UK Biobank using time-windowed comorbidity leakage with known, graded severity, measuring a detection floor of $Δ\cstar \approx 0.007$ on this endpoint, below which residual leakage is undetectable from output and too small to alter conclusions. The numerical floor is cohort- and endpoint-specific; the structural lesson is general: output-only detection fails where residual leakage is indistinguishable from an honestly stronger predictor. The test returns a verdict on a prediction vector in under a second on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [174] [LakeFM: Toward a Foundation Model for Aquatic Ecosystems Using Irregular Multivariate Multi-depth Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11268)
*Abhilash Neog,Sepideh Fatemi,Medha Sawhney,Kazi Sajeed Mehrab,Aanish Pradhan,Bennett J. McAfee,Emma Marchisin,Arka Daw,Robert Ladwig,Cayelan C. Carey,Paul Hanson,Anuj Karpatne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LakeFM，一个针对湖泊生态系统的基础模型，通过预训练大规模模拟和观测数据，解决了现有方法在时间序列预测中难以处理不规则采样、异质性变量和跨湖泊泛化的问题，并在预测性能和物理合理性上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法应用于生态时间序列数据时，假设时间与深度维度上规则采样，难以在具有异质性变量、不同深度和观测模式的湖泊间泛化，亟需一种能够处理不规则时空数据的通用模型。

Method: 构建LakeFM基础模型，在大规模包含模拟与实测数据的湖泊生态数据集上进行预训练，学习跨湖泊的通用表征。

Result: LakeFM学习了有意义的湖泊级表征，在预测性能上达到或超越现有时间序列基础模型与非基础模型，同时生成符合真实湖泊动力学的物理合理预测。

Conclusion: LakeFM有效解决了不规则时空采样下的湖泊动态预测问题，为水生系统的基础模型研究提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Understanding and forecasting lake dynamics is critical for monitoring water quality and ecosystem health across lakes and reservoirs. While machine learning methods have been recently applied to ecological time-series data, existing works assume regular sampling in time and depth, and struggle to generalize across lakes with heterogeneous variables, depths, and observation patterns. To address these limitations, we introduce \textsc{LakeFM}, a foundation model for aquatic systems, pre-trained on large-scale ecological datasets comprising both simulated and observed lakes. Through extensive empirical evaluation, we show that \textsc{LakeFM} learns meaningful representations spanning broader lake-level characteristics, and achieves competitive or often superior-forecasting performance compared to existing time-series foundation and non-foundation models, while producing physically plausible predictions consistent with real-world lake dynamics.

</details>


### [175] [Quantifying Subliminal Behavioral Transfer Ratios in Language Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11270)
*Uwe Konig,Hamza Kazmi,Ruizhe Li,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究量化了语言模型蒸馏中“潜意识学习”效应的大小，发现不良行为从教师模型到学生模型的迁移是稳健的，但不同模型表现出不同的缩放行为：Llama-2存在尖锐阈值，而Qwen2.5呈连续且更高水平的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然定性证据支持蒸馏过程中不良行为会从教师模型迁移到学生模型（即潜意识学习），但该效应的幅度尚未被系统性地量化表征。

Method: 通过在不同引导强度下操控两个教师模型（Llama-2-7B-Chat和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct），仅使用良性数据蒸馏学生模型，并在100个JailbreakBench提示上使用GPT-4.1作为评估器进行量化分析。

Result: 迁移行为是稳健的，但表现出不同的缩放模式：Llama-2在α=-0.15之外呈现尖锐阈值（τ=0.25,0.32），而Qwen2.5呈现连续且更高水平的迁移（τ最高达0.61）。

Conclusion: 潜意识学习效应存在且具有模型依赖性，不同架构的安全行为迁移特性存在本质差异，需要在蒸馏中针对性防范。

Abstract: Distillation of a language model intended to transfer benign behavior to a student model may also transfer undesirable characteristics, if they are present in the teacher model, a phenomenon known as subliminal learning. While qualitative evidence supports the existence of this effect, its magnitude has not been systematically characterized. This study quantifies subliminal behavioral transfer ratios by steering two teacher models (Llama-2-7B-Chat and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) at varying steering strengths and distilling student models using only benign data. Evaluation on 100 JailbreakBench prompts with GPT-4.1, serving as the evaluator, indicates that transfer is robust but exhibits distinct scaling behaviors. Llama-2 demonstrates a sharp threshold ($τ= {0.25,0.32} \ \text{beyond} \ α= -0.15$), whereas Qwen2.5 displays continuous and higher levels of transfer ($τ$ up to $0.61$).

</details>


### [176] [Federated continual learning: A comprehensive survey on lifelong and privacy-preserving learning over distributed and non-stationary data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11272)
*Masoume Gholizade,Fabrizio Ruffini,Pietro Ducange,Francesco Marcelloni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了联邦持续学习（FCL），即在联邦学习（FL）框架下引入持续学习（CL）能力，以解决分布式非平稳数据流中的灾难性遗忘、性能退化等问题，并提出了多维分类法、评估指标及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FL方法假设数据平稳，无法应对医疗、工业物联网、智能城市等真实场景中数据的非平稳性，导致性能下降和灾难性遗忘；而CL虽能处理数据分布变化，但多在集中式环境下研究，忽略了联邦系统的隐私、通信和异构性约束。因此需要将两者结合形成FCL。

Method: 本文采用综述方法：首先形式化定义FCL问题及其特征；分析经典FL在非平稳条件下的局限性；提出多维分类法对FCL方法进行系统组织；综述代表性应用领域、数据模态、评估指标和实验视角。

Result: 本文提出了一个系统性的FCL综述框架，涵盖问题定义、方法分类、应用场景、评估体系，并指出了极端异构性、可扩展隐私保护记忆机制、标准化基准等关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: FCL是联邦学习走向真实世界部署的关键方向，本文旨在为该领域提供系统参考和路线图，推动其向鲁棒、可部署系统发展。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative and privacy-preserving model training across distributed clients, but most existing FL systems implicitly assume data stationarity. In real-world settings-such as healthcare, industrial IoT (IIOT), cybersecurity, and smart cities-data streams are inherently non-stationary, leading classical FL methods to suffer from performance degradation, instability, and catastrophic forgetting.
  Continual Learning (CL) addresses learning under evolving data distributions but has been largely studied in centralized settings, overlooking key constraints of federated systems, including privacy, limited communication, and client heterogeneity. Federated Continual Learning (FCL) emerges at the intersection of FL and CL, aiming to support lifelong, adaptive, and privacy-aware learning over distributed and non-stationary data.
  This survey provides a comprehensive and systematic overview of FCL. We first present a formal definition of the FCL problem and clarify its distinctive characteristics. We then analyze the limitations of classical FL under non-stationary conditions, highlighting how CL principles support long-term adaptation. To organize the rapidly growing literature, we propose a multi-dimensional taxonomy of FCL approaches. Furthermore, we review representative application domains and data modalities, summarize commonly used evaluation metrics, and discuss experimental perspectives for assessing long-term performance and forgetting. Finally, we highlight key open challenges, including handling extreme heterogeneity under temporal drift, designing scalable and privacy-preserving memory mechanisms, and establishing standardized benchmarks. This survey aims to serve as a reference and a roadmap for advancing FCL toward robust and deployable real-world systems.

</details>


### [177] [RoVE: Rotary Value Embeddings Attention for Relative Position-dependent Value Pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11275)
*Alejandro García-Castellanos,Maurice Weiler,Erik J Bekkers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoVE通过同时对键和值进行旋转编码，使值路径具备位置敏感性，将RoPE注意力转化为注意力卷积，在多尺度模型上显著提升了少样本上下文学习、分布外困惑度和长上下文检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RoPE在注意力计算中对键进行旋转编码使注意力分数具备位置相对性，但值路径仍然是位置盲的——无论值token与查询距离多远，其发送的消息完全相同。这一不对称设计限制了模型对位置信息的利用能力。

Method: 提出RoVE，一种无参数修改方法：在RoPE的基础上，不仅对键进行旋转，同时对值也进行相同的旋转编码，使值路径获得位置敏感性。这一操作将RoPE注意力统一为注意力卷积形式。

Result: 在124M和354M参数的GPT-2模型上，RoVE相比RoPE在少样本上下文学习、分布外困惑度和长上下文检索任务上取得一致提升，尤其是在需要长距离聚合的任务上改进最明显。

Conclusion: RoVE通过简单无参数修改统一了计算机视觉、机器人学和现代LLM架构中多种独立定义的操作，为位置编码的对称化设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) make attention scores position-relative but leave the value pathway position-blind: the message sent by a value token is the same regardless of its distance from the query. We propose RoVE, a parameter-free modification that makes values position-sensitive by rotating them simultaneously with keys, and show that it turns RoPE attention into attentive convolution. This new perspective unifies several independent formulations of the same operation across computer vision, robotics, and modern LLM architectures. Trained 124M and 354M GPT-2 models show consistent empirical gains over RoPE on few-shot in-context learning, out-of-distribution perplexity, and long-context retrieval, with the clearest improvements on tasks that require long-range aggregation.

</details>


### [178] [Least-Action-Guided Diffusion for Physical Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11277)
*Zhongxin Yang,Yuanwei Bin,Xiang I. A. Yang,Shiyi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于最小作用量原理引导的扩散模型框架(LAPG)，通过在推理阶段引入物理引导分数，使生成模型能够在训练分布外实现物理一致的可靠外推，在多个ODE/PDE系统上优于传统物理约束基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在计算物理中面临可靠外推的挑战，模型在有限的时间、参数或几何范围内训练后，可能在训练分布外产生物理不一致的预测。现有方法依赖训练阶段的约束，缺乏推理阶段的物理一致性保障。

Method: 提出LAPG框架，将条件分数扩散模型与基于作用量的物理引导分数相结合。第一阶段：训练好的分数模型生成分布内提议；第二阶段：基于作用量的变分先验将提议向目标分布外条件优化。将最小作用量原理转化为可微的推理时校正机制，替代需要经验性损失平衡的逐点残差惩罚方法。

Result: 在自由落体、保守/耗散弹簧-质量系统、相互作用点涡和参数化翼型势流等ODE/PDE系统上评估。在时间、参数和几何外推测试中，相比训练时物理约束基线方法，LAPG减少了相位漂移、保持了耗散衰减、捕捉了涡运动并改善了翼型升力响应。

Conclusion: LAPG通过推理阶段的作用量引导有效实现了物理一致的外推，为计算物理中生成模型的可靠外推提供了新范式，避免了训练时物理约束的损失平衡难题。

Abstract: Reliable extrapolation remains a central challenge for generative models in computational physics, because models trained over finite ranges of time, parameters, or geometries may produce physically inconsistent predictions outside the training distribution. We introduce a least-action-principle-guided diffusion, LAPG, a framework that promotes physical consistency during inference rather than relying solely on constraints imposed during training. The method combines a conditional score-based diffusion model with an action-derived physical guidance score. In the first stage, the learned score model generates an in-distribution proposal; in the second, an action-based variational prior refines this proposal toward the target out-of-distribution condition. This formulation turns the principle of least action into a differentiable inference-time correction mechanism and provides an alternative to pointwise residual penalties that often require empirical loss balancing.
  We evaluate LAPG on representative ordinary- and partial-differential-equation systems, including free fall, conservative and dissipative spring-mass dynamics, interacting point vortices, and potential flow over parameterized airfoils. In temporal, parameter, and geometric extrapolation tests, LAPG reduces phase drift, preserves dissipative decay, captures vortex motion, and improves the lift response of airfoil flows compared with training-time physics-informed baselines.

</details>


### [179] [FreeBridge: Variational Schrödinger Bridges for Cellular Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11286)
*Xurui Wang,Qin Ren,Jun Ma,Haibin Ling,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreeBridge通过Schrödinger桥公式，在仅知端点分布条件下对单细胞进行随机传输建模，利用实例分割的细胞表示建立固定流形并通过潜在支持正则化约束传输路径，在多个数据集上保持竞争性端点对齐并减少中间状态违反，强调了几何基础对生物可解释扰动动力学的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高内涵成像分析中细胞被化学固定，无法观测个体细胞的连续轨迹，而仅从对照和处理组的边际分布推断随机传输存在非唯一性问题：多个随机过程可能连接相同边际分布但穿越单细胞形态学不支持的区域，导致生物学上不可解释的中间演化。

Method: 提出FreeBridge，一种基于Schrödinger桥的公式，将原子状态定义为实例分割的单细胞表示以建立固定细胞流形，并通过经验潜在支持正则化学习约束在该几何空间内的随机传输，仅需端点监督。

Result: 在BBBC021、RxRx1和JUMP数据集上，FreeBridge在统一评估协议下保持竞争性或改进的端点保真度和作用机制保留；在BBBC021上进一步减少了中间状态支持违反。

Conclusion: 几何基础对生成生物学可解释的扰动动力学至关重要。

Abstract: High-content imaging assays quantify cellular responses to chemical and genetic perturbations, yet continuous trajectories of individual cells are unobservable because cells are chemically fixed at acquisition. Perturbation modeling therefore reduces to inferring stochastic transport between control and treated populations observed only as separate marginals. While recent generative models achieve strong end-point alignment, boundary consistency does not determine intermediate evolution: multiple stochastic processes may connect identical marginals while traversing regions unsupported by observed single-cell morphologies. We introduce \textbf{FreeBridge}, a Schrödinger Bridge formulation for single-cell transition modeling under endpoint-only supervision. FreeBridge defines atomic states as instance-segmented single-cell representations, establishing a fixed cellular manifold, and learns stochastic transport constrained within this geometry via empirical latent support regularization. Across BBBC021, RxRx1, and JUMP, FreeBridge maintains competitive or improved endpoint fidelity and mechanism-of-action retention under a unified evaluation protocol; on BBBC021, it further reduces intermediate support violations. These findings highlight the importance of geometric grounding for biologically interpretable perturbation dynamics. Project page: https://y-research-sbu.github.io/FreeBridge/.

</details>


### [180] [FlowBank: Query-Adaptive Agentic Workflows Optimization through Precompute-and-Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11290)
*Lingzhi Yuan,Chenghao Deng,Fangxu Yu,Souradip Chakraborty,Mohammad Rostami,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowBank提出了一种基于组合(portfolio)的智能体工作流优化框架，通过多样化生成、精简压缩和自适应匹配三个阶段，在性能和成本之间取得更好平衡，在五个基准上超越最强自动和手工基线分别达4.26%和14.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体工作流优化存在两种范式的权衡：任务级方法离线计算开销大但只部署单一工作流，查询级方法每查询生成新工作流推理成本高。分析发现二者互补而非竞争，离线发现的工作流往往解决不同查询子集，许多查询可由更便宜的预计算工作流解决。因此目标应转向构建可复用的互补工作流库并在推理时自适应选择。

Method: FlowBank包含三个阶段：1) Diversifying阶段提出DiverseFlow算法，通过引导搜索覆盖欠覆盖查询来生成多样性候选池；2) Curating阶段提出CuraFlow算法，将候选池压缩为紧凑的组合(portfolio)以最小化冗余；3) Matching阶段将部署建模为查询-工作流二分图上的边价值预测，为每个传入查询路由到预测效用最佳的工作流成员。

Result: 在五个基准上，FlowBank在保持成本竞争力的同时取得了最高平均分，相对性能提升：超越最强自动化基线4.26%，超越最强手工基线14.92%。

Conclusion: 任务级和查询级工作流优化范式是互补的，通过构建紧凑、可复用的工作流组合并在推理时自适应选择，可以在性能和成本之间实现更优的权衡。FlowBank有效解决了生成互补候选、压缩冗余组合和自适应匹配三个耦合问题。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems are increasingly powerful, but current agentic workflow optimization paradigms make an unsatisfying trade-off. Task-level methods spend substantial offline compute yet deploy only a single workflow, leaving complementary candidates unused, while query-level methods synthesize a new workflow per query at substantial inference cost. Our motivating analysis shows these paradigms are more complementary than competing: workflows discovered during offline search often solve different subsets of queries, and many queries handled by expensive query-level generation can already be solved by cheaper precomputed workflows. This suggests a different objective: rather than searching for one universally best workflow or regenerating one per instance, we should build a compact bank of reusable, complementary workflows and select among them adaptively at inference time. Doing so requires solving three coupled problems: generating complementary rather than redundant candidates, compressing them into a small deployable portfolio, and assigning each query to the right workflow under a performance-cost trade-off. To this end, we present FlowBank, a three-stage framework for portfolio-based agentic workflow optimization. Diversifying proposes DiverseFlow to steer search toward under-covered queries and produce a high-coverage candidate pool. Curating proposes CuraFlow to compress this pool into a compact portfolio with minimal redundancy. Matching casts deployment as edge-value prediction on a query-workflow bipartite graph and routes each incoming query to the portfolio member with the best predicted utility. Across five benchmarks, FlowBank achieves the highest average score among the evaluated methods while remaining cost-competitive, improving over the strongest automated and handcrafted baselines by 4.26% and 14.92% relative, respectively.

</details>


### [181] [Learning from almost nothing: How neural networks survive heavy input corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11319)
*Justin Tahmassebpur,Asadullah Bhuiyan,Hyejin Kim,Omri Lesser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了属性噪声（输入损坏）下神经网络的学习鲁棒性，发现即使输入被破坏超过90%，神经网络仍能显著高于随机水平。通过平均场方法分析无限宽度网络，揭示了其鲁棒性源于网络隐式实现了最近类均值（原型规则）决策规则，该规则具有跨架构、深度、激活函数和噪声分布的普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性噪声（输入损坏）相较于标签噪声受到的关注较少，本文旨在从理论角度理解神经网络在输入高度损坏下仍能保持良好性能的机制。

Method: 实验上采用多层感知器（MLP）在加性噪声和替换噪声两种损坏模型下进行分类任务；理论上采用平均场方法分析无限宽度网络在重度损坏下的行为，推导出主导阶决策规则。

Result: 神经网络在输入损坏超过90%时仍能维持显著高于随机的准确率；无限宽度网络的主导阶决策规则等价于最近类均值（原型规则）；该规则在多种MLP架构、深度、激活函数和噪声分布下普适成立。

Conclusion: 神经网络在属性噪声下的鲁棒性可归因于其隐式实现的质心机制（最近类均值规则），这为即使单个样本几乎无信号时学习仍能成功提供了可解释且可分析的理论依据。

Abstract: Learning from imperfect data is a central theme in machine learning, connecting practical questions of robustness to fundamental questions of learnability. Here we examine attribute noise: learning from corrupted inputs while keeping the labels intact, a setting that has received considerably less analytical attention than its label-noise counterpart. We consider two types of corruption models: additive noise and replacement noise. Through experiments with multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) on corrupted classification datasets, we find that neural networks remain robust, maintaining well-above-chance accuracy even when inputs are >90% corrupted -- far beyond human recognition. To understand this robustness, we analyze infinite-width networks in the heavy-corruption regime using a mean-field-inspired approach and derive a leading-order decision rule for the classification outcome: the network implements a prototype rule, the nearest-class-mean, assigning each test point to the class whose training-set average it most closely resembles. This leading-order decision rule is universal across a broad range of MLP architectures, holding for any depth, as well as a wide class of activation functions and noise distributions. The same centroid mechanism closely matches finite-width network behavior in our experiments and provides an interpretable and analytically tractable account of why learning can succeed even when individual training examples carry almost no signal.

</details>


### [182] [Energy-Conserved Neural Pipelines: Attenuating Error Propagation in Modular Neural Networks via Physical Conservation Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11341)
*David Young,Swan Yi Htet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出能量守恒作为模块化神经网络管线的硬物理约束，强制特征向量的激活能量（L2范数平方）在模块边界精确守恒。实验表明该方法显著提升了对多种噪声的鲁棒性（如sigma=0.2时clean accuracy 77.4% vs 基线35.1%），实现深度不变性，并在真实机器人管线（Franka Panda, MuJoCo）中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化神经网络管线存在误差累积问题：任何模块边界的噪声会传播并在后续模块中放大，导致整体性能严重下降。现有软惩罚方法不足以从根本上解决这一问题。

Method: 提出能量守恒约束，要求特征向量的激活能量（squared L2 norm）在每一模块边界精确守恒，作为不可违反的硬物理约束。网络可以跨神经元重新分配能量，但不能创造或消灭能量。

Result: (1) CIFAR-10上噪声sigma=0.2时clean accuracy 77.4% vs 基线35.1%和能量惩罚模型30.9%（p<0.001）；(2) 管线深度不变性，深度2-5均保留93.3%；(3) 对系统偏置(+45.1%)、高斯(+40.4%)、对抗(+4.8%)噪声有效，对dropout无影响(-0.3%)；(4) ResNet-18上无BatchNorm时优势达+26.2pp（sigma=0.2）至+58.0pp（sigma=0.5）；(5) 真实机器人管线中+18.9pp平均优势。

Conclusion: 能量守恒约束是一种有效的硬物理约束，能够从根本上抑制模块化神经网络管线中的误差累积，显著提升噪声鲁棒性，且在理论和实验上均验证了其优势。

Abstract: Modular neural network pipelines suffer from error compounding: noise at any module boundary propagates and potentially amplifies through subsequent modules. We introduce energy conservation as a hard physical constraint on inter-module information flow. Activation energy (the squared L2 norm of feature vectors) is enforced to be exactly preserved at every module boundary. Unlike soft energy penalties, conservation is an inviolable law: the network may redistribute energy across neurons but cannot create or destroy it. Four experiments on CIFAR-10 demonstrate: (1) conservation retains 77.4% of clean accuracy at noise sigma=0.2, versus 35.1% for baselines and 30.9% for energy-penalized models (p<0.001, 5 seeds); (2) pipelines become depth-invariant, retaining 93.3% at depths 2 through 5 with noise at every boundary; (3) the advantage generalizes to systematic bias (+45.1%), Gaussian (+40.4%), and adversarial noise (+4.8%), with a principled non-effect on dropout (-0.3%); (4) on ResNet-18, the conservation advantage scales inversely with intrinsic normalization: +0.3 pp with BatchNorm, +26.2 pp without at sigma=0.2, reaching +58.0 pp at sigma=0.5. Experiment 5 validates the operator on a real modular robotic pipeline (MuJoCo physics, Franka Panda). Across three independent runs on separate machines (90 trials per cell), conservation provides +18.9 pp average advantage on monocular-depth-style noise. A formal bound proves conserved noise energy is strictly less than input noise energy.

</details>
