### [253] [Multi-Rate Mixture of Experts for Accelerating Liquid Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12240)
*Shilong Zong,Almuatazbellah Boker,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于液态神经网络的多速率混合专家框架（MR-MoE），通过多个运行在不同时间尺度的LNN专家、门控网络以及特征级和时间级注意力机制，解决多变量时间序列中复杂的时间依赖、不规则采样和多尺度异质动态建模问题，在预测任务中取得了优于LSTM、单LNN和标准MoE的AUROC和AUPRC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RNN（如LSTM）在离散时间步操作，难以有效捕捉连续和不规则的时间行为；标准LNN虽具有连续时间动力学，但依赖单一动力系统，无法建模多尺度异质时序模式。现有方法缺乏对多尺度动态的明确分离与自适应专家分工。

Method: 提出MR-MoE框架，包含：(1) 多个LNN专家，每个专家运行在独立的时间尺度以分别捕获快变和慢变动态；(2) 门控网络根据输入条件实现自适应专家分配；(3) 特征级注意力机制抑制噪声/无关变量；(4) 时间注意力机制聚焦信息丰富的历史状态。

Result: 在复杂多变量时间序列预测任务上，MR-MoE在AUROC和AUPRC指标上持续优于LSTM、单LNN和标准MoE基线，同时保持较好的计算效率。

Conclusion: 将连续时间动力学、多尺度专家分解与自适应注意力机制相结合，可以有效提升时间序列建模的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series data often exhibit complex temporal dependencies, irregular sampling, and heterogeneous dynamics across multiple time scales, making accurate sequence modeling particularly challenging. Traditional recurrent neural networks (RNNs), such as Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, operate in discrete time and may struggle to effectively capture continuous and irregular temporal behaviors. Liquid Neural Networks (LNNs) address some of these limitations through continuous-time dynamics, but standard LNN architectures typically rely on a single dynamical system, limiting their ability to model heterogeneous temporal patterns. To address these challenges, we propose a Multi-Rate Mixture-of-Experts (MR-MoE) framework built on top of Liquid Neural Networks. In the proposed architecture, multiple LNN-based experts operate at distinct time scales, enabling the model to explicitly separate fast-changing dynamics from slow-evolving temporal trends. A gating network further enables adaptive expert specialization based on input conditions. In addition, we incorporate both feature-level and temporal attention mechanisms to improve robustness, interpretability, and long-range dependency modeling. Feature-level attention suppresses noisy or irrelevant variables, while temporal attention selectively focuses on informative historical states. We evaluate the proposed framework on a complex multivariate time-series prediction task and compare it against strong baselines, including LSTM, monolithic LNN, and standard MoE models. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed MR-MoE framework consistently achieves improved AUROC and AUPRC performance while maintaining favorable computational efficiency. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining continuous-time dynamics, multi-scale expert decomposition, and adaptive attention mechanisms for time-series modeling.

</details>


### [254] [Reinforcement Learning Disrupts Gradient-Based Adversarial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12251)
*Xinhai Zou,Chang Zhao,Alireza Aghabagherloo,Dave Singelée,Robin Degraeve,Bart Preneel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习(RL)训练能够破坏梯度结构，使梯度对抗攻击失效，并揭示了RL作为隐式正则化器通过产生不稳定的梯度方向和较小的梯度幅值来干扰攻击。将RL与对抗训练结合(RL-adv)可形成梯度级和边界级的双层防御，显著优于传统监督学习对抗训练(SL-adv)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度信息为对抗攻击提供了优化方向，研究者想探索RL训练是否能破坏梯度结构，从而防御基于梯度的对抗攻击。

Method: 使用策略梯度目标和epsilon-greedy探索训练图像分类器，在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、ImageNet-100上系统实验；通过损失景观可视化、静态和动态梯度指标、预测熵进行机制分析；并将RL与对抗训练结合(RL-adv)。

Result: RL训练能显著破坏梯度优化；RL作为隐式正则化器产生不稳定的梯度方向和较小梯幅；RL-adv在PGD、AutoAttack、迁移攻击、查询攻击上均超越SL-adv。

Conclusion: RL诱导的梯度破坏是一种互补的鲁棒机制，未来可研究混合SL-RL训练策略。

Abstract: Gradient-based adversarial attacks remain a dominant threat to deep neural networks (DNNs), as they exploit gradient information to efficiently optimize adversarial perturbations. To address this, we investigate whether reinforcement learning (RL) training can disrupt the gradient structure used by attackers by training image classifiers with policy-gradient objectives and epsilon-greedy exploration. Through systematic experiments across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-100 with multiple architectures, we find that RL-trained classifiers significantly disrupt gradient-based adversarial optimization. To explain this, we conduct a comprehensive mechanism analysis using loss landscape visualization, static and dynamic gradient indicators, and predictive entropy. Our analysis reveals that RL acts as an implicit regularizer, producing models with highly unstable gradient directions and smaller gradient magnitudes. This combination makes each PGD step both unreliable in direction and limited in magnitude, causing gradient-based attacks to fail within practical iteration budgets. We further show that combining RL with adversarial training (RL-adv) provides a dual-layer defense operating at two complementary levels: RL degrades gradient information available to attackers (gradient-level defense), while adversarial training strengthens decision boundaries (boundary-level defense). RL-adv achieves the highest robustness across all major attack types evaluated, including gradient-based (PGD, AutoAttack), transfer-based, and query-based attacks, outperforming SL-adv by a significant margin. These findings identify RL-induced gradient disruption as a complementary robustness mechanism and motivate future research on hybrid SL-RL training schedules that combine SL's efficiency with RL's gradient-regularization properties.

</details>


### [255] [Using Explainability as a Training-Time Reliability Signal for Efficient ECG Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12252)
*Veerendhra Kumar Dangeti,Xiao Gu,Ying Weng,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ERTS方法，利用可解释性（Grad-CAM注意力图的质量）作为训练信号，在ECG分类中自动筛选有信息量的样本，在降低训练成本的同时提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在临床时间序列分析（尤其是ECG分类）中训练成本高昂，现有方法Progressive Data Dropout依赖模型置信度筛选样本，但可能保留因噪声或歧义导致的难样本，无法区分有用信号与不可靠的不确定性。

Method: 提出ERTS框架，在渐进式数据选择基础上，为候选样本计算Grad-CAM注意力图并导出聚焦得分（focus score），衡量模型预测是否由连贯且局部化的模式支持；低聚焦样本被过滤，高注意力样本优先用于梯度更新。

Result: 在三个ECG数据集和多种骨干架构上，ERTS一致提升了macro-F1指标，同时降低了有效训练成本。

Conclusion: 解释质量可以作为提升临床时间序列学习效率与可靠性的实用训练信号。

Abstract: Training deep neural networks for clinical time-series analysis is computationally demanding, yet many healthcare settings lack the resources required for repeated model development and deployment. This challenge is particularly evident in electrocardiogram classification, where large datasets and long training schedules make efficiency practically important. Progressive Data Dropout reduces training cost by excluding samples from gradient updates once they are learned, but it relies on model confidence and may retain samples that are difficult due to noise or ambiguity rather than useful signal. In this work, we introduce ERTS, an explainability-based reliability training signal for efficient ECG classification. ERTS uses explanation quality during training to distinguish between informative and unreliable uncertainty. Building on progressive data selection, we compute Grad-CAM attention maps for candidate samples and derive a focus score that measures whether model predictions are supported by coherent and localised patterns. Samples with low focus are filtered out, while those with meaningful attention are prioritised for gradient updates. We evaluate ERTS across three ECG datasets and multiple backbone architectures, showing consistent improvements in macro-F1 alongside reduced effective training cost. These results suggest that explanation quality can serve as a practical signal for improving both efficiency and reliability in clinical time-series learning. Code will be released.

</details>


### [256] [Finding Multiple Interpretations in Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12277)
*Matthew Chak,Paul Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方法，用于在损失/准确率测量方面找到性能相似但上下文感知特性高度不同的模型集合。在METABRIC数据集上的实验表明，该方法能在不牺牲性能的情况下，找到与对照方法相比具有高度不同基因表达的多组模型。作者认为，该方法对于分析模型全局特征以提取对潜在现象的洞察具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法往往关注模型性能的优化，忽略了在相似性能下模型内部特征（如上下文感知特性）的多样性。研究者希望找到性能相似但内部特征（如基因表达）高度不同的模型，以便更全面地分析模型所捕捉到的潜在现象。

Method: 提出一种用于发现性能相似但上下文感知特性高度不同的模型集合的方法。通过在METABRIC数据集上进行实验，与对照方法比较模型在基因表达方面的差异性。

Result: 在METABRIC数据集上，所提方法能够找到多个与对照方法相比具有高度不同基因表达的模型，且不造成性能损失。

Conclusion: 该方法对于分析模型的全局特征以提取对潜在现象的洞察具有重要意义，尤其适用于需要分析模型内部特征的研究场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an approach to finding sets of similar-performing models (in terms of loss/accuracy measurements) with highly different context-aware characteristics. Through experiments on the METABRIC dataset, we show that the proposed method finds multiple models with highly different gene expressions than those found by the control methodology without performance penalties. We argue that the proposed methodology is important whenever one aims to analyze any global characteristic of a model to extract insight into the underlying phenomenon being studied.

</details>


### [257] [Holding the FP8 Quality Ceiling at 8-Bit Weights and Activations: INT8 and GGUF Post-Training Quantization of Ideogram 4.0 for Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12280)
*Deep Gandhi,Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Ideogram 4.0（9.3B流匹配扩散Transformer）在Ampere RTX 3090 GPU上进行INT8 W8A8后训练量化，在无FP8张量核心的硬件上达到FP8质量水平，INT8相比NF4提升+1.9 CLIP，GGUF Q4_K在质量-内存前沿上为Pareto最优，并分析了量化在Ampere架构上的加速瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化使大型文本到图像扩散Transformer能在消费级GPU上运行，但硬件特定的质量-效率权衡鲜有直接测量，尤其是在缺乏FP8张量核心的Ampere架构上。

Method: 对Ideogram 4.0 (9.3B) 采用INT8 W8A8方案：per-channel权重量化、per-token动态激活量化、SmoothQuant、混合精度保护高脆弱层子集。同时评估GGUF Q4_K和Q8_0量化。使用200提示基准和配对同种子bootstrap置信区间进行统计评估。

Result: INT8达到FP8质量上限（CI包含零）；INT8比NF4高+1.9 CLIP (95% CI [+1.21, +2.64])；OCR文本可读性保持；FFN下投影保护是主导质量杠杆；Q4_K优于NF4且为Pareto最优；INT8权重与FP8相同大小，Ampere上速度提升需融合INT8内核。

Conclusion: 在无FP8张量核心的Ampere GPU上，INT8量化可达到FP8质量水平，但内存占用未减少，加速受限于缺乏融合INT8内核；GGUF Q4_K在质量-内存权衡上表现最优。

Abstract: Post-training quantization lets large text-to-image diffusion transformers run on consumer GPUs, yet the hardware-specific trade-offs are seldom measured directly. We quantize Ideogram 4.0 - a 9.3B flow-matching diffusion transformer (DiT), shipped as two separate-weight copies of a single-stream 34-layer backbone for classifier-free guidance and conditioned by a Qwen3-VL-8B encoder - for Ampere RTX 3090 GPUs, which lack FP8 tensor cores. Our INT8 W8A8 recipe (per-channel weights, per-token dynamic activations, SmoothQuant, and mixed-precision protection of a small high-fragility layer set) holds the FP8 quality ceiling: on a 200-prompt benchmark the paired same-seed bootstrap CI for INT8-FP8 includes zero on both Pick and CLIP, while INT8 improves on NF4 by $+1.9$ CLIP (95% CI $[+1.21,+2.64]$, excluding zero). A per-category OCR analysis, to our knowledge unreported for this model class, confirms text legibility is preserved, and an ablation isolates protection of the FFN down-projections as the dominant quality lever. Our GGUF Q4_K quantization beats NF4 at equal on-disk size and is the Pareto winner on the quality-memory frontier, with paired confidence intervals excluding zero (Q8_0 is quality neutral). Finally, we characterize where 8-bit quantization helps and where it does not: INT8's weights match FP8's footprint rather than shrink it, so a speed gain on Ampere awaits a fused INT8 kernel.

</details>


### [258] [The Standard Interpretable Model: A general theory of interpretable machine learning to deductively design interpretable methods using Lagrangian mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12289)
*Pietro Barbiero,Giovanni De Felice,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Francesco Giannini,Filippo Bonchi,Mateja Jamnik,Giuseppe Marra,Ruggero Noris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出标准可解释模型（SIM），一种基于拉格朗日力学的通用理论框架，通过演绎方式从用户对可解释性的前提需求出发，系统推导可解释性对称性及约束，并将最优可解释模型的求解转化为拉格朗日函数的最小化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性领域缺乏通用理论来演绎式地设计可解释方法，导致文献碎片化且评估协议不一致。

Method: 基于拉格朗日力学建立标准可解释模型（SIM）：从目标用户的可解释性前提集出发，系统推导可解释性对称性及对应约束，构建拉格朗日函数，其最小值对应最优可解释模型；通过更新不透明模型参数或编译约束到可解释架构来达到最小值。

Result: SIM能够识别并解决现有方法（传统、基于概念、机制可解释性）的局限，突出未被充分探索的研究方向，并为核心编程接口的设计提供指导。

Conclusion: SIM不仅是研究方法，其演绎性质为可解释性课程提供教学基础，并可能转变该长期碎片化领域的科学共同体视角。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence models grow in complexity, interpretability has become an indispensable tool for understanding, debugging, and controlling their computations. However, interpretability lacks general theories to deductively design interpretable methods. This gap between theories and methods results in a fragmented literature and inconsistent evaluation protocols.
  To fill this gap, we introduce the Standard Interpretable Model (SIM), a general theory grounded in Lagrangian mechanics that enables the deductive design of interpretable methods. Specifically, the SIM summarises, in a set of premises, what interpretability is for a target user. From these premises, the SIM systematically derives interpretability symmetries and corresponding constraints, which shape the landscape of a Lagrangian whose minima correspond to optimal interpretable models. To reach the minima, one can either update the parameter values of an opaque model to make it more interpretable or compile constraints into an interpretable architecture.
  We empirically show that the SIM identifies and solves limitations of existing methods (including traditional, concept-based, and mechanistic interpretability), highlights underexplored research directions, and informs the design of core programming interfaces. Beyond being a research method, the deductive nature of the SIM offers pedagogical grounding for interpretability curricula and may shift the scientific community's perspective of a discipline that has long been fragmented.

</details>


### [259] [Harness In-Context Operator Learning with Chain of Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12318)
*Minghui Yang,Ling Guo,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CHOP（Chain of Operators）通过构建包含显式初等变换和冻结ICON的操作链，使预训练ICON无需参数更新即可泛化到分布外算子任务，在标量守恒律和均场控制问题中降低了相对推理误差，且操作链具有可解释性和跨族泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICON虽能通过提示学习不同算子，但在分布外（OOD）算子任务上泛化能力不足，需要一种无需微调或参数更新的方法来提升其OOD泛化性能。

Method: 受大语言模型提示工程启发，提出CHOP框架：构建由显式初等变换（如缩放、平移等）和冻结的ICON组成的操作链，通过链式组合逐步将OOD算子任务映射回ICON能处理的分布内范围，而不更新任何参数。

Result: 在标量守恒律和均场控制问题上，CHOP相比直接ICON评估降低了相对推理误差；在一个PDE族上构建的操作链可泛化到另一个不同PDE族，表明不同动力系统间存在共享机制。

Conclusion: CHOP提供了一种轻量级、可解释且无需参数更新的方法，显著提升了神经算子（ICON）在OOD算子任务上的泛化能力，并揭示了跨系统操作链的迁移潜力。

Abstract: Neural operators approximate mappings between function spaces, but often generalize poorly to other operators and usually require fine-tuning or retraining. In-Context Operator Networks (ICON) addresses this issue by prompting the model with numerical context so that the model learns specific operators from prompts and adapt to different operators without fine-tuning. However, ICON may still fail to generalize to out-of-distribution (OOD) operator tasks. Inpired by the success of harness engineering of Large Language models (LLMs), we introduce Chain of Operators (CHOP), a framework that harness a frozen ICON to OOD operator tasks without updating its parameters. Specifically, CHOP constructs a chain of operators consisting of explicit elementary transformations and the frozen ICON. Experiments on a scalar conservation law and a mean-field control problem show that CHOP reduces relative inference error over direct ICON evaluation, while each operator in the chain remains interpretable and in closed form. A chain constructed on one PDE family further generalizes to a different family, indicating shared mechanisms across harness systems.

</details>


### [260] [Claw-SWE-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating OpenClaw-style Agent Harnesses on Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12344)
*Mengyu Zheng,Kai Han,Boxun Li,Haiyang Xu,Yuchuan Tian,Wei He,Hang Zhou,Jianyuan Guo,Hailin Hu,Lin Ma,Chao Xu,Guohao Dai,Lixue Xia,Yunchao Wei,Yunhe Wang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Claw-SWE-Bench，一个多语言SWE-bench风格基准测试及适配器协议，用于公平比较异构智能体框架（"claws"）的编码能力。在完整基准上，OpenClaw采用最小适配器仅得19.1% Pass@1，而完整适配器达73.4%；模型选择和框架选择分别带来29.4和27.4个百分点的差异。该基准将框架设计和成本核算作为编码智能体评估的核心维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体（如OpenClaw）作为自主工具使用者的应用日益广泛，但它们的编码能力在SWE-bench下难以衡量，因为通用智能体本身不满足评分所需的干净Docker工作区、补丁和预测合约。需要一种方法使异构智能体框架在公平设置下可比。

Method: 提出Claw-SWE-Bench基准测试和适配器协议，包含固定提示、运行时预算、工作区合约、补丁提取过程和评估器。构建350个GitHub问题解决实例（8种语言、43个仓库），并从SWE-bench-Multilingual和SWE-bench-Verified-Mini中筛选。同时发布80实例的Lite子集，通过成本感知和排名感知程序从17个校准列中选择。

Result: OpenClaw在完整基准上，最小直接差异适配器仅19.1% Pass@1，而完整适配器达73.4%（同GLM 5.1骨干网络）。九模型×OpenClaw扫描中模型选择带来29.4 pp差异；五框架×两模型扫描中框架选择带来27.4 pp差异。精度相似的系统在总API成本上可能差异显著。

Conclusion: Claw-SWE-Bench将框架设计和成本核算作为SWE风格编码智能体评估的核心维度，提供完整基准和低成本参考集用于可重复比较。

Abstract: General-purpose agents such as OpenClaw are increasingly used as autonomous tool users, but their coding ability is difficult to measure under SWE-bench: a generic agent does not by itself satisfy the clean Docker workspace, patch, and prediction contract required for scoring. We introduce Claw-SWE-Bench, a multilingual SWE-bench-style benchmark and adapter protocol that makes heterogeneous agent harnesses, or claws, comparable under fair settings including a fixed prompt, runtime budget, workspace contract, patch extraction procedure, and evaluator. The full benchmark contains 350 GitHub issue-resolution instances across 8 languages and 43 repositories, drawn from SWE-bench-Multilingual and SWE-bench-Verified-Mini after future-commit cleanup. We also release Claw-SWE-Bench Lite for faster validation, which is an 80-instance subset selected by a cost-aware, rank-aware procedure over 17 calibration columns. On the full benchmark, OpenClaw with a minimal direct-diff adapter scores only $19.1\%$ Pass@1, whereas the full adapter reaches $73.4\%$ with the same GLM 5.1 backbone, showing that adapter design is essential for enabling OpenClaw-style harnesses to perform coding tasks effectively. Across an OpenClaw $\times$ nine-model sweep and a five-claw $\times$ two-model sweep, model choice changes Pass@1 by $29.4$ pp and harness choice by $27.4$ pp under fixed models; systems with similar accuracy can differ substantially in total API cost. Claw-SWE-Bench therefore treats harness and cost accounting as first-class axes of SWE-style coding-agent evaluation, providing both a full benchmark and a low-cost reference set for reproducible comparison. The data is available at https://github.com/opensquilla/claw-swe-bench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/TokenRhythm/Claw-SWE-Bench.

</details>


### [261] [Anatomy of Post-Training: Using Interpretability to Characterize Data and Shape the Learning Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12360)
*Leon Bergen,Usha Bhalla,Sidharth Baskaran,Max Loeffler,Raphael Sarfati,Dhruvil Gala,Ryan Panwar,Santiago Aranguri,Thomas Fel,Atticus Geiger,Matthew Kowal,Siddharth Boppana,Daniel Balsam,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Thomas McGrath,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释性的数据驱动后训练流水线，通过分析偏好数据中的潜在概念来检测和修正模型学到的不良相关（如过度风格化、谄媚行为），将后训练从优化不透明代理奖励转变为审计和塑造学习信号本身的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型后训练主要依赖标量奖励优化，但这种方法无法让从业者了解数据实际教会了模型什么，导致模型学到虚假相关性并产生不良行为（如过度风格化、谄媚）。需要一种能在优化前检查偏好数据、在概念层面决定模型应学习哪些行为的方法。

Method: 提出一个数据驱动的后训练流水线，使用可解释性协议开发统计假设来识别偏好/非偏好生成之间的潜在概念，将其显式化以获取细粒度用户反馈；统一了多种基于可解释性的训练协议，通过特征或数据干预来塑造奖励。

Result: 实验表明该流水线能够诊断现有偏好数据中的不良信号，减轻非目标学习，并能帮助放大或塑造期望属性（如安全护栏、模型个性）。

Conclusion: 可解释性可以将后训练从优化不透明代理奖励转变为审计和塑造学习信号本身的过程，为模型行为控制提供更透明、更细粒度的数据驱动方法。

Abstract: Language-model post-training is the main stage at which model behavior is shaped, yet it still largely involves optimization of scalar rewards that summarize diverse desiderata. This abstraction gives practitioners little visibility into what their data actually teaches models, allowing spurious correlations to be learned by a model and inducing undesirable behaviors such as over-stylization and sycophancy. To address this problem, we ask: can we inspect a preference dataset before optimization and decide, at the level of concepts, which behaviors a model should be allowed to learn? Motivated by this, we introduce a data-centric post-training pipeline that uses interpretability protocols to develop statistical hypotheses for the latent concepts separating preferred from dispreferred generations, making them explicit for fine-grained user feedback. Building on this view, we unify several interpretability-based training protocols as ways of shaping rewards via feature or data interventions. Empirically, we show that our pipeline diagnoses undesirable signals in existing preference data, mitigates off-target learning, and can also help amplify or shape desired properties such as safeguards and model personality. More broadly, our results suggest that interpretability can turn post-training from optimizing opaque proxy rewards into a process of auditing and sculpting the learning signal itself.

</details>


### [262] [Latent World Recovery for Multimodal Learning with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12362)
*Hui Wang,Tianyu Ren,Joseph Butler,Christopher Baker,Karen Rafferty,Simon McDade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出潜在世界恢复（LWR）框架，通过邻居对齐的模态特定嵌入和可用性感知的模态融合，在缺失模态场景下实现鲁棒的多模态学习，无需显式填补缺失数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物科学应用中异构模态通常仅部分可用，现有方法依赖缺失模态填补或固定模态集，导致错误传播和灵活性不足。

Method: (i) 不同模态的特定嵌入在共享潜在空间中进行邻居对齐；(ii) 仅利用训练和推理时实际可用的模态嵌入进行可用性感知融合，避免显式重建缺失模态。

Result: 在真实世界的不完整多组学基准数据集上，LWR在癌症表型分类和生存预测等下游任务中表现有效。

Conclusion: LWR通过将模态视为潜在状态的局部感知，结合邻居对齐和可用性感知融合，在部分观测下实现鲁棒的多模态预测，避免缺失模态重建带来的误差传播。

Abstract: We study multimodal learning under missing modalities, with particular motivation from bioscience applications in which heterogeneous modalities are often only partially available when decisions need to be made. We propose Latent World Recovery (LWR), a framework built on two key ideas: (i) modality-specific embeddings from different modalities are aligned in a shared latent space, and (ii) a unified representation is constructed by fusing only the embeddings of the modalities that are actually available at both training and inference time. Rather than imputing missing modalities or requiring a fixed modality set, LWR treats each modality as a partial perception of an underlying latent state and performs availability-aware representation learning directly from the observed modalities. This combination of neighbor-based latent alignment and availability-aware modality fusion enables robust multimodal prediction under partial observation, while avoiding error propagation from explicit reconstruction of missing modalities. We evaluate the proposed framework on real-world incomplete multi-omics benchmarks and demonstrate that it provides an effective approach to downstream tasks such as cancer phenotype classification and survival prediction.

</details>


### [263] [On Subquadratic Architectures: From Applications to Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12364)
*Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,Levente Zólyomi,David Stap,Pieter-Jan Hoedt,Niklas Schmidinger,Lukas Hauzenberger,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet三种次二次架构在复杂依赖任务上的表现，发现xLSTM凭借其更灵活稳定的门控机制实现的状态跟踪和记忆动态，在代码模型预训练、大模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型预训练中均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的二次注意力计算成本高昂，次二次架构虽提供了可扩展替代方案，但何种设计能产生最有效的序列模型仍不明确。本文旨在系统比较三种领先的次二次架构，厘清其设计优劣。

Method: 在代码模型预训练、大模型代码模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型预训练三个复杂依赖任务上，对xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet进行对比评估，并通过统一公式化分析底层架构机制，聚焦状态跟踪和记忆动态。

Result: xLSTM在所有评估设置中均取得最强综合性能；其门控方案能实现更灵活稳定的记忆校正；在受控合成长度泛化任务上验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: xLSTM在复杂任务上的优势源于其稳健的状态跟踪和累积能力，这为次二次序列模型的架构设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Transformers dominate modern sequence modeling, but their quadratic attention incurs substantial computational cost. Subquadratic architectures offer a scalable alternative. However, it remains unclear which designs yield the most effective sequence models. We compare three leading approaches: xLSTM, Mamba-2, and Gated DeltaNet. We evaluate these models on tasks with complex dependencies: (1) code-model pre-training, (2) distillation of code models from large language models, and (3) pre-training of time-series foundation models. Across these settings, xLSTM delivers the strongest overall performance. To explain xLSTM's advantage, we present a unified formulation and analyze the underlying architectural mechanisms, focusing on state tracking and memory dynamics. Our results show that xLSTM enables more flexible and stable memory correction via its gating scheme. We corroborate these findings on controlled synthetic length-generalization tasks. Overall, our findings indicate that xLSTM's gains on complex tasks stem from robust state tracking and accumulation.

</details>


### [264] [Breaking Entropy Bounds: Accelerating RL Training via MTP with Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12370)
*Yucheng Li,Huiqiang Jiang,Yang Xu,Jianxin Yang,Yi Zhang,Yizhong Cao,Yuhao Shen,Fan Zhou,Rui Men,Jianwei Zhang,An Yang,Bowen Yu,Bo Zheng,Fei Huang,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多令牌预测（MTP）在LLM后训练（RL阶段）中的应用，揭示了熵波动导致MTP接受率下降的负线性关系，提出了端到端全变差（TV）损失和拒绝采样策略，实现了最高95%的接受率和1.8倍异步RL训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练中rollout阶段是瓶颈，MTP虽可通过推测解码加速，但在RL训练中接受率显著下降，导致加速效果有限。

Method: 1) 揭示模型熵波动与MTP接受率的负线性关系；2) 采用概率拒绝采样替代贪婪草稿采样；3) 提出端到端TV损失直接优化多步拒绝采样接受率；4) 验证预RL阶段的MTP训练（e2e TV损失+拒绝采样）可在整个RL过程中保持稳定接受率。

Result: 接受率提升约10%，最高达95%；额外推理吞吐提升达25%；在数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务上实现1.8倍异步RL训练端到端加速。

Conclusion: 预RL阶段的MTP训练配合TV损失和拒绝采样，能有效避免在线MTP更新的高昂成本，实现RL全阶段的稳定加速。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key component in modern large language models, yet the rollout stage remains the key bottleneck in RL training pipelines. Although Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) offers a natural solution to accelerate rollouts through speculative decoding, many studies have observed that MTP acceptance rates degrade significantly during RL training, leading to limited speedup performance. To address this bottleneck, we present Bebop, a systematic study of MTP in LLM post-training, and offer practical recipes to integrate MTP into large-scale RL pipelines. First, we reveal that the MTP acceptance rate is fundamentally bounded by the fluctuation of model entropy, which demonstrates a clear negative linear relationship with the rise of entropy in the RL stage. Second, we show that probabilistic rejection sampling largely alleviates the disturbance introduced by entropy in RL compared to greedy draft sampling. We further identify that the conventional MTP training objectives (cross-entropy or KL) are suboptimal in such settings, and therefore we propose a novel end-to-end TV loss that directly optimizes multi-step rejection sampling acceptance rate, yielding ~10% acceptance rate improvements, achieving up to 95% acceptance rates and up to 25% extra inference throughput gains across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic tasks. Third, we test various online MTP training strategies during RL and show that pre-RL MTP training with e2e TV loss and rejection sampling achieves a consistent acceptance rate and speedup throughout the entire RL, eliminating the need for costly online MTP updating. We provide extensive experiments and analysis that validate our findings. Experimental results show our method achieves up to 1.8x end-to-end acceleration in async RL training of Qwen3.5, Qwen3.6, and Qwen3.7 models.

</details>


### [265] [APPO: Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12384)
*Xucong Wang,Ziyu Ma,Yong Wang,Yuxiang Ji,Shidong Yang,Guanhua Chen,Pengkun Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)，通过结合token不确定性和策略似然增益的Branching Score进行细粒度分支定位，并引入过程级优势缩放进行信用分配，在13个基准测试上平均提升近4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体强化学习方法将信用分配建立在粗粒度启发式单元（如工具调用边界或固定工作流）上，难以识别哪些中间决策影响下游结果。

Method: APPO方法包含两个核心组件：(1) Branching Score：结合token不确定性与策略诱导的后续延续似然增益来选择分支位置；(2) 过程级优势缩放（procedure-level advantage scaling）：在分支 rollout 间更精细地分配信用。

Result: 在13个基准测试上，APPO在强智能体RL基线基础上平均提升近4个百分点，同时保持高效的工具调用和可解释性。

Conclusion: 将分支和信用分配从粗粒度交互单元转移到细粒度决策点，能够更有效地探索和分配信用，提升智能体强化学习性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL) have substantially improved the multi-turn tool-use capabilities of large language model agents. However, most existing methods assign credit over coarse heuristic units, such as tool-call boundaries or fixed workflows, making it difficult to identify which intermediate decisions influence downstream outcomes. In this work, we study agentic RL from two perspectives: \textit{where to branch and how to assign credit after branching}. Our pilot analysis shows that influential decision points are broadly distributed throughout the generated sequence rather than concentrated at tool calls, while token entropy alone does not reliably reflect their impact on final outcomes. Motivated by these observations, we propose \textbf{Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)}, which shifts branching and credit assignment from coarse interaction units to fine-grained decision points in the sequence. APPO selects branching locations using a Branching Score that combines token uncertainty with policy-induced likelihood gains of subsequent continuations, enabling more targeted exploration while filtering out spurious high-entropy positions. It further introduces procedure-level advantage scaling to better distribute credit across branched rollouts. Experiments on 13 benchmarks show that APPO consistently improves strong agentic RL baselines by nearly 4 points, while keeping efficient tool-calls and maintaining behavior interpretability.

</details>


### [266] [ATLAS: Active Theory Learning for Automated Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12386)
*Noémi Éltető,Nathaniel D. Daw,Kimberly L. Stachenfeld,Kevin J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ATLAS是一个主动学习框架，通过迭代生成可解释的稀疏神经网络假设并设计最优区分实验，在强化学习智能体行为恢复任务中实现了5-10倍的样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学建模需要提出正确的实验问题以获取最具信息量的数据，但这一过程通常依赖人工直觉。作者希望自动化这一过程，在认知科学中实现数据驱动的可解释行为模型发现。

Method: ATLAS框架包含两个核心迭代步骤：(1) 生成机械论假设——实例化为多样化的稀疏神经网络集成（Disentangled RNNs）；(2) 设计能最优区分这些假设的实验。在bandit任务中测试了恢复强化学习智能体的能力。

Result: ATLAS在所有评估指标（行为相似性、结构相似性、计算相似性）上相比随机实验实现了5-10倍的样本效率提升，且其性能在与文献中专家设计实验的对比中得到了验证。

Conclusion: ATLAS展示了在认知科学及其他依赖机械论模型发现的科学领域中加速人类可解释洞察的潜力。

Abstract: Advancing scientific understanding through mechanistic modeling requires posing the right experimental questions to yield maximally informative data. To automate this pursuit within cognitive science, we introduce ATLAS (Active Theory Learning for Automated Science), an active learning framework for the data-driven discovery of interpretable behavioral models. ATLAS iterates between generating mechanistic hypotheses--instantiated as a diverse ensemble of sparse neural networks (Disentangled RNNs)--and designing experiments that optimally distinguish between them. We test this approach on the problem of recovering reinforcement learning agents from their behavior in bandit tasks. ATLAS designs varied sequences of qualitatively novel experiments with temporal structure tailored to underlying agent characteristics. The models trained on these experiments are evaluated against a comprehensive set of metrics for mechanistic modeling that capture behavioral, structural, and computational similarity. ATLAS achieves a 5-10x improvement in sample efficiency across all metrics compared to random experimentation, and its performance is further validated against expert-designed experiments derived from literature. These in silico results showcase ATLAS's potential to accelerate human-interpretable insights in cognitive science and other domains where scientific inquiry relies on discovering mechanistic models.

</details>


### [267] [Redesign Mixture-of-Experts Routers with Manifold Power Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12397)
*Songhao Wu,Ang Lv,Ruobing Xie,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的MoE路由机制——流形幂迭代（MPI），通过将路由行与对应专家矩阵的主奇异方向对齐，使路由矩阵能更精确地编码专家信息，从而提升MoE模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型中，路由矩阵的行作为专家代理，理论上应编码专家矩阵的表示向量以反映token-专家亲和度，但目前缺乏强制这种编码的设计原则，导致路由效果存在提升空间。

Method: 提出流形幂迭代（MPI）方法，采用"幂迭代-回缩"范式：先在路由权重上执行幂迭代步骤，再通过回缩施加范数约束，使路由行收敛到对应专家矩阵的主奇异方向。

Result: 理论上证明了MPI能驱动路由行向专家主奇异方向收敛；实验上在1B到11B参数规模的MoE模型上进行了预训练验证，确认该对齐策略能构建更有效的MoE模型。

Conclusion: 将路由行与专家主奇异方向对齐是一种有效的路由设计原则，MPI方法能够实现这一对齐并提升MoE模型性能。

Abstract: Router is the cornerstone component to the Mixture-of-Experts models. Serving as expert proxies, the rows of the router matrix compute their similarity to the MoE inputs to determine which subset of experts is activated. Ideally, each router row is designed to encode the expert matrix into this representative vector, such that its dot-product with token can better reflect token-expert affinity. However, there exists no design principles to enforce this condensation. In this paper, we propose to align each router row with the principal singular direction of the associated expert, as this direction provides the most expressive mathematical description of a matrix. Based on this principle, we propose a router redesign with Manifold Power Iteration (MPI). Specifically, it introduces a "Power-then-Retract" paradigm, where a power iteration step is performed on the router weights, followed by a retraction to impose a norm constraint to ensure both efficiency and stability. Theoretically, we show that MPI drives router rows to converge toward the principal singular directions of associated experts. Empirically, we pretrain MoE model across scales from 1B to 11B parameters to confirm that this alignment facilitates more effective MoE models.

</details>


### [268] [Learning Patterns and Abstractions from Perceptual Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10973)
*Shuchen Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出组块化（chunking）和抽象化（abstraction）作为从具体到抽象、从简单到复杂的分层序列学习中结构化知识获取的核心计算原理。通过行为实验、规范模型和非参数层次变量模型，论文展示了人类如何将序列分解为可复用的组块并发现抽象模式，从而实现高效学习、泛化与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知系统如何将高维感官流快速分解为熟悉的部分并发现其关系？序列学习中结构为何涌现，以及它们如何支持学习、泛化和预测？这些核心感知与智能问题的计算原理尚不明确。

Method: 第一部分：通过序列反应时任务探究影响组块化的因素，开发逐组块解析序列的学习模型，并提出组块化作为发现重复模式和嵌套层次结构的理性策略。第二部分：对抽象模式进行分类，提出非参数层次变量模型，同时学习组块和抽象变量以发现不变符号模式，并与人类学习及大语言模型进行比较。

Result: 人类在序列学习中适应底层组块结构，并能在速度与准确度之间进行权衡；提出的模型能有效学习单维和多维序列中的层次结构，支持无监督模式发现；抽象模式分类和行为证据表明人类利用模式冗余进行压缩和迁移；非参数层次变量模型能发现不变符号模式，其行为与人类学习相似。

Conclusion: 组块化和抽象化作为简单的计算原理，能够实现从具体到抽象、从简单到复杂的分层序列中的结构化知识获取，构成感知和智能的核心方面。

Abstract: Cognition swiftly breaks high-dimensional sensory streams into familiar parts and uncovers their relations. Why do structures emerge, and how do they enable learning, generalization, and prediction? What computational principles underlie this core aspect of perception and intelligence? A sensory stream, simplified, is a one-dimensional sequence. In learning such sequences, we naturally segment them into parts -- a process known as chunking. In the first project, I investigated factors influencing chunking in a serial reaction time task and showed that humans adapt to underlying chunks while balancing speed and accuracy. Building on this, I developed models that learn chunks and parse sequences chunk by chunk. Normatively, I proposed chunking as a rational strategy for discovering recurring patterns and nested hierarchies, enabling efficient sequence factorization. Learned chunks serve as reusable primitives for transfer, composition, and mental simulation -- letting the model compose the new from the known. I demonstrated this model's ability to learn hierarchies in single and multi-dimensional sequences and highlighted its utility for unsupervised pattern discovery. The second part moves from concrete to abstract sequences. I taxonomized abstract motifs and examined their role in sequence memory. Behavioral evidence suggests that humans exploit pattern redundancies for compression and transfer. I proposed a non-parametric hierarchical variable model that learns both chunks and abstract variables, uncovering invariant symbolic patterns. I showed its similarity to human learning and compared it to large language models. Taken together, this thesis suggests that chunking and abstraction as simple computational principles enable structured knowledge acquisition in hierarchically organized sequences, from simple to complex, concrete to abstract.

</details>


### [269] [A Physics-Inspired Optimizer: Velocity Regularized Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13196)
*Pranav Vaidhyanathan,Lucas Schorling,Natalia Ares,Maike Osborne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VRAdam是一种受物理学启发的优化器，通过引入基于速度的四次项动能正则化，在权重更新过大时自动降低学习率，从而抑制振荡并提升训练稳定性，在图像分类、语言建模和生成建模等多个任务上优于AdamW等标准优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器（如Adam）在训练过程中处于自适应稳定性边缘，导致快速振荡和损失收敛变慢。作者希望引入物理学的动能正则化思想，通过速度相关的更高阶惩罚项自动调控学习率，从而提升训练稳定性和收敛性能。

Method: VRAdam在Adam的基础上增加了基于速度的四次项动能正则化（velocity-based regularizer），对学习率施加高阶惩罚：当权重更新（速度）变大时，有效动态学习率自动缩小，从而阻尼振荡。该方法结合了全局阻尼（速度正则化）与Adam的逐参数缩放机制，形成混合优化器。

Result: 理论方面：从物理和控制视角提供了在稳定性边缘运行的严格分析，并在温和假设下推导出随机非凸目标下O(ln(N)/√N)的收敛界。实验方面：在图像分类（CNN）、语言建模（Transformer）和生成建模（GFlowNets）等多个任务上，VRAdam优于包括AdamW在内的标准优化器。

Conclusion: VRAdam通过物理启发的速度正则化有效解决了训练中的振荡问题，在理论和实验上均表现出优于现有优化器的性能，是一种有前景的深度学习优化方法。

Abstract: We introduce Velocity-Regularized Adam (VRAdam), a physics-inspired optimizer for training deep neural networks that draws on ideas from quartic terms for kinetic energy with its stabilizing effects on various system dynamics. Previous algorithms, including the ubiquitous Adam, operate at the so-called adaptive edge of stability regime during training, leading to rapid oscillations and slowed convergence of loss. However, VRAdam adds a higher order penalty on the learning rate based on the velocity such that the algorithm automatically slows down whenever weight updates become large. In practice, we observe that the effective dynamic learning rate shrinks in high-velocity regimes, and damping oscillations. By combining this velocity-based regularizer for global damping with per-parameter scaling of Adam, we create a powerful hybrid optimizer. For this optimizer, we provide rigorous theoretical analysis of operation at the edge of stability from a physical and control perspective for the momentum. Furthermore, we derive convergence bounds with the rate $\mathcal{O}(\ln(N)/\sqrt{N})$ for a stochastic non convex objective under mild assumptions. We demonstrate that VRAdam exceeds the performance against standard optimizers including AdamW. We benchmark various tasks such as image classification, language modeling, and generative modeling using diverse architectures and training methodologies including Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), Transformers, and GFlowNets.

</details>


### [270] [Pass@K Policy Optimization: Solving Harder Reinforcement Learning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15201)
*Christian Walder,Deep Karkhanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PKPO 方法，通过对奖励进行联合变换，直接优化 pass@k 性能，从而提升采样集的多样性和集体效用，在保持或提升 pass@1 的同时显著改善 pass@k，尤其在困难任务上能解锁学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 RL 算法采样多个解并独立奖励，优化的是 pass@1 性能，忽视了样本集的多样性和集体效用，导致采样能力利用不足，限制了在困难样本上的探索和提升。

Method: 提出 Pass-at-k Policy Optimization (PKPO)，推导了 pass@k 及其梯度的低方差无偏估计量（适用于二值和连续奖励场景），通过稳定高效的变换函数对奖励进行联合变换，使优化退化为标准 RL 形式。支持任意 k ≤ n，并可通过退火 k 在训练中同时优化 pass@1 和 pass@k。

Result: 在玩具实验和 GEMMA-2 开源 LLM 上验证，PKPO 能有效优化目标 k，高 k 值可解决更多更难的问题，退火 k 能同时提升 pass@1 和 pass@k。在传统 pass@1 优化停滞的困难任务上，pass@k 方法能解锁学习。

Conclusion: 通过优化样本集的联合效用而非单个样本的效用，pass@k 优化能更好地进行探索，在困难任务上突破 pass@1 优化的瓶颈，实现更强的整体性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms sample multiple n>1 solution attempts for each problem and reward them independently. This optimizes for pass@1 performance and prioritizes the strength of isolated samples at the expense of the diversity and collective utility of sets of samples. This under-utilizes the sampling capacity, limiting exploration and eventual improvement on harder examples. As a fix, we propose Pass-at-k Policy Optimization (PKPO), a transformation on the final rewards which leads to direct optimization of pass@k performance, thus optimizing for sets of samples that maximize reward when considered jointly. Our contribution is to derive novel low variance unbiased estimators for pass@k and its gradient, in both the binary and continuous reward settings. We show optimization with our estimators reduces to standard RL with rewards that have been jointly transformed by a stable and efficient transformation function.
  While previous efforts are restricted to k=n, ours is the first to enable robust optimization of pass@k for any arbitrary k <= n. Moreover, instead of trading off pass@1 performance for pass@k gains, our method allows annealing k during training, optimizing both metrics and often achieving strong pass@1 numbers alongside significant pass@k gains.
  We validate our reward transformations on toy experiments, which reveal the variance reducing properties of our formulations. We also include real-world examples using the open-source LLM, GEMMA-2. We find that our transformation effectively optimizes for the target k. Furthermore, higher k values enable solving more and harder problems, while annealing k boosts both the pass@1 and pass@k . Crucially, for challenging task sets where conventional pass@1 optimization stalls, our pass@k approach unblocks learning, likely due to better exploration by prioritizing joint utility over the utility of individual samples.

</details>


### [271] [Mitigating Disparate Impact of Differentially Private Learning through Bounded Adaptive Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01396)
*Linzh Zhao,Aki Rehn,Mikko A. Heikkilä,Razane Tajeddine,Antti Honkela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有界自适应裁剪（bounded adaptive clipping）方法，通过引入可调下界防止梯度过度抑制，缓解差分隐私学习中自适应裁剪对少数群体（困难样本）造成的不公平影响，在Skewed和Fashion MNIST上最差类准确率提升超过10个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私学习方法对模型预测存在不同影响，尤其对少数群体（困难样本）不公平。梯度裁剪（特别是自适应裁剪）会抑制困难样本的较大梯度，当裁剪边界缩小到极小值以适应拟合良好的多数样本时，会显著降低其他样本的准确率。

Method: 提出有界自适应裁剪（bounded adaptive clipping），在自适应裁剪中引入一个可调的下界，防止裁剪边界过度缩小从而过度抑制困难样本的梯度。

Result: 在Skewed和Fashion MNIST数据集上，相比无界自适应裁剪提升最差类准确率超过10个百分点，相比Automatic clipping提升7个百分点，相比常数裁剪提升5个百分点。

Conclusion: 有界自适应裁剪能有效缓解差分隐私学习中自适应裁剪导致的不公平问题，在保护隐私的同时提升对困难样本/少数群体的模型准确率。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) has become an essential framework for privacy-preserving machine learning. Existing DP learning methods, however, often have disparate impacts on model predictions, e.g., for minority groups. Gradient clipping, which is often used in DP learning, can suppress larger gradients from challenging samples. We show that this problem is amplified by adaptive clipping, which will often shrink the clipping bound to tiny values to match a well-fitting majority, while significantly reducing the accuracy for others. We propose bounded adaptive clipping, which introduces a tunable lower bound to prevent excessive gradient suppression. Our method improves worst-class accuracy by over 10 percentage points on Skewed and Fashion MNIST compared to unbounded adaptive clipping, 7 points compared to Automatic clipping, and 5 points compared to constant clipping. The code is available at https://github.com/TrustworthyMLHelsinki/adaptive-clipping-fairness.

</details>


### [272] [AsFT: Anchoring Safety During LLM Fine-Tuning Within Narrow Safety Basin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08473)
*Shuo Yang,Qihui Zhang,Yuyang Liu,Xiaojun Jia,Kunpeng Ning,Jiayu Yao,Jigang Wang,Hailiang Dai,Yibing Song,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 微调大语言模型时，与对齐方向正交的扰动会迅速破坏模型安全性，而沿对齐方向的更新则能保持安全。基于此发现，本文提出AsFT方法，通过在微调中显式约束更新方向（惩罚与对齐方向正交的更新），将模型限制在"窄安全盆地"内，从而保持固有安全性。实验表明，AsFT将有害行为降低7.60%，任务性能提升3.44%，在多个任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调虽然能提升LLM性能，但会引入严重的安全漏洞，即使少量有害数据也足以破坏模型的安全防护机制。作者希望找到一种方法，在微调过程中保持模型的安全性。

Method: 提出AsFT方法，通过分析对齐方向（安全模型与未安全模型的权重差）发现参数空间存在"窄安全盆地"现象——与对齐方向正交的扰动会破坏安全性，沿对齐方向的更新则保持安全。AsFT在微调中显式惩罚与对齐方向正交的更新，将模型约束在安全盆地内。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型上的实验表明，AsFT将有害行为降低7.60%，任务性能提升3.44%，在多个任务上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过约束微调时的更新方向，可以有效保持LLM的固有安全性，AsFT提供了一种在提升任务性能的同时维护模型安全的有效方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) improves performance but introduces critical safety vulnerabilities: even minimal harmful data can severely compromise safety measures. We observe that perturbations orthogonal to the alignment direction - defined by weight differences between aligned (safe) and unaligned models - rapidly compromise model safety. In contrast, updates along the alignment direction largely preserve it, revealing the parameter space as a "narrow safety basin". To address this, we propose AsFT (Anchoring Safety in Fine-Tuning) to maintain safety by explicitly constraining update directions during fine-tuning. By penalizing updates orthogonal to the alignment direction, AsFT effectively constrains the model within the "narrow safety basin," thus preserving its inherent safety. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets and models show that AsFT reduces harmful behaviors by up to 7.60%, improves task performance by 3.44%, and consistently outperforms existing methods across multiple tasks.

</details>


### [273] [Cross-Layer Discrete Concept Discovery for Interpreting Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20040)
*Ankur Garg,Xuemin Yu,Hassan Sajjad,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出跨层矢量量化变分自编码器(CLVQ-VAE)，通过离散矢量量化瓶颈将残差流中跨层重复特征压缩为可解释概念向量，在概念移除、LLM评估和人类标注三个维度上优于聚类、单层VQ-VAE和稀疏自编码器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型残差流中特征在相邻层间线性混合并重复，导致单层分析遗漏跨层结构；现有跨层稀疏自编码器在连续空间中操作，概念分散且边界模糊。

Method: CLVQ-VAE框架：将低层表示通过离散矢量量化瓶颈映射到高层，结合top-k温度采样和指数移动平均码本更新，实现可控探索与码本多样性。

Result: 在ERASER-Movie、Jigsaw和AGNews上，概念移除使模型准确率下降高达93%；LLM评估中概念排名第一占66.7%；人类标注从可视化恢复预测准确率78%（聚类仅54%）。

Conclusion: CLVQ-VAE通过离散量化有效解决残差流跨层特征混合与重复问题，生成更紧凑可解释的概念表示。

Abstract: Interpreting language models remains challenging due to the existence of residual stream, which linearly mixes and duplicates features across adjacent layers, causing single-layer analyses to miss this cross-layer structure. Cross-layer sparse autoencoders (SAEs) address layer mixing but operate in continuous space, where concepts split across many neurons without clear boundaries. We introduce Cross-Layer Vector Quantized-Variational Autoencoder (CLVQ-VAE), a novel framework which maps representations from a lower layer to a higher layer through a discrete vector-quantization bottleneck, collapsing duplicated residual-stream features into compact, interpretable concept vectors. Our approach combines top-k temperature-based sampling with exponential moving average (EMA) codebook updates, providing controlled exploration of the discrete latent space while maintaining codebook diversity. Across both encoder- and decoder-based models on ERASER-Movie, Jigsaw, and AGNews, CLVQ-VAE outperforms clustering, single-layer vector quantized-variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE), and sparse autoencoder (SAE) baselines across three evaluation axes: removing identified concepts drops model accuracy by up to 93%, LLM judges rank our concepts first in 66.7% of comparisons, and human annotators recover model predictions from our visualizations with 78% accuracy versus 54% for clustering.

</details>


### [274] [Persistent Homology as a Theory of Emergent Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03065)
*Xin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将涌现（emergence）识别为持久非平凡同调类，通过持续条、谱间隙、Hodge分解和函子凝聚等数学工具，用一个统一框架解释了宏观结构在微观组分持续变化下为何保持可识别，并在大气、神经和社会系统中给出可证伪预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答一个根本性科学问题：为什么某些宏观结构（如涡旋、神经记忆、社会制度）在微观组分（流体粒子、突触、个体）持续更替时仍然保持可识别性。现有各学科对此现象的解释碎片化，缺乏统一的数学语言。

Method: 1) 将涌现定义为尺度相对（scale-relative）的持久非平凡同调类 [z] ∈ H_p = ker∂_p/im∂_{p+1}；2) 引入收缩相似（CS）图算子构建谱间隙来预测鲁棒性；3) 使用Hodge分解将谐波宏观支架与精确/协精确微观流分离；4) 通过函子凝聚解释不同层次间涌现类的单位化过程。

Result: 提出"支架-流"（scaffold-flow）框架，用同一种数学语言表达了涌现的六种特征：必然性、相干性、不可还原性、互补性、鲁棒性和层级性。该框架给出了可证伪的预测：真正的涌现结构应跨滤子持续存在、保持谱稳定性、对谐波干预有不成比例响应、层级自治需要时间尺度分离。

Conclusion: 将涌现从哲学思辨转化为可测量的数学问题，提供了跨学科的统一理论工具，能够在大气动力学、神经科学和社会系统中进行实证检验。

Abstract: Why do some macroscopic structures remain identifiable even though their microscopic constituents continually change? Vortices persist while fluid parcels turn over, neural memories persist while spikes and synapses fluctuate, and institutions persist while individuals enter and leave. We propose a scale-relative answer: an emergent property is a persistent nontrivial homology class $[z]\in H_p=\ker\partial_p/\im\partial_{p+1}$, a macro-feature that is closed but not exact across a filtration of descriptions. This identification turns emergence into a \emph{measurement} problem. Persistent bars detect stable macro-features, and we introduce a contractive-similarity (CS) graph operator to supply scaffold spectral gaps that predict robustness. Hodge decomposition separates harmonic macro-scaffold from exact and co-exact micro-flow; and functorial condensation explains when one level's emergent class becomes a unit for the next. The resulting scaffold-flow framework expresses six familiar signatures of emergence (i.e., inevitability, coherence, irreducibility, complementarity, robustness, and hierarchy) within one mathematical language. It also yields falsifiable predictions across atmospheric, neural, and social systems: genuine emergent structures should persist across filtrations, remain spectrally stable, respond disproportionately to harmonic interventions, and require timescale separation for hierarchical autonomy.

</details>


### [275] [Composing Linear Layers from Irreducibles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11688)
*Travis Pence,Daisuke Yamada,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Clifford代数将线性层分解为双向量（几何基元）的复合，仅需O(log² d)参数即可匹配强基线方法（如分块Hadamard和低秩近似）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代大模型的行为暗示存在低级基元组合成更丰富功能的模块，但这些基础构建块尚未被充分理解。本文旨在探究线性层中的组合结构，试图从最小几何基元集合中识别/合成线性变换。

Method: 利用Clifford代数将线性层表示为双向量（编码有向平面的几何对象）的复合，提出一种可微算法将其分解为旋转子的乘积。该构造仅需O(log² d)参数，远少于稠密矩阵的O(d²)。

Result: 在LLM注意力层的键、查询、值投影上，基于旋转子的层在性能上与分块Hadamard和低秩近似等强基线方法相当。

Conclusion: 这些几何基元可以在深度模型中组合成更高级的功能，为理解大模型的组合结构提供了代数视角。

Abstract: Contemporary large models often exhibit behaviors suggesting the presence of low-level primitives that compose into modules with richer functionality, but these fundamental building blocks remain poorly understood. We investigate this compositional structure in linear layers by asking: can we identify/synthesize linear transformations from a minimal set of geometric primitives? Using Clifford algebra, we show that linear layers can be expressed as compositions of bivectors -- geometric objects encoding oriented planes -- and introduce a differentiable algorithm that decomposes them into products of rotors. This construction uses only O(log^2 d) parameters, versus O(d^2) required by dense matrices. Applied to the key, query, and value projections in LLM attention layers, our rotor-based layers match the performance of strong baselines such as block-Hadamard and low-rank approximations. Our findings provide an algebraic perspective on how these geometric primitives can compose into higher-level functions within deep models.

</details>


### [276] [OCSVM-Guided Representation Learning for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21164)
*Nicolas Pinon,Robin Trombetta,Carole Lartizien*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将表示学习与可解析求解的One-Class SVM耦合的无监督异常检测方法，通过自定义损失函数使潜在特征直接对齐OCSVM决策边界。在MNIST-C和脑部MRI病灶检测两项任务上，该方法表现出良好的性能和鲁棒性，特别是在检测微小、非高信号病灶方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督异常检测方法存在两类问题：基于重构的方法常能很好地重构异常样本，而解耦的表示学习+密度估计方法可能受到次优特征空间的限制。部分尝试耦合特征学习与异常检测的方法依赖替代目标函数、限制核选择或引入近似，限制了表达能力和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一种将表示学习与可解析求解的One-Class SVM相耦合的方法，通过设计自定义损失函数，使潜在特征直接与OCSVM决策边界对齐，实现端到端联合优化。

Result: 在MNIST-C基准测试和脑部MRI微小非高信号病灶检测任务上，该方法表现出优异的性能和鲁棒性，尤其能够检测传统方法难以处理的微小、非高信号病灶，并在体素级指标评估中取得良好效果。

Conclusion: 所提方法在通用无监督异常检测和真实医学影像应用中均展现出潜力和鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection (UAD) aims to detect anomalies without labeled data, a necessity in many machine learning applications where anomalous samples are rare or not available. Most state-of-the-art methods fall into two categories: reconstruction-based approaches, which often reconstruct anomalies too well, and decoupled representation learning with density estimators, which can suffer from suboptimal feature spaces. While some recent methods attempt to couple feature learning and anomaly detection, they often rely on surrogate objectives, restrict kernel choices, or introduce approximations that limit their expressiveness and robustness. To address this challenge, we propose a novel method that couples representation learning with an analytically solvable One-Class SVM (OCSVM), through a custom loss formulation that directly aligns latent features with the OCSVM decision boundary. The model is evaluated on two tasks: a \deleted{new} benchmark based on MNIST-C, and a challenging brain MRI \deleted{subtle} lesion detection task. Unlike most methods that focus on large, hyperintense lesions at the image level, our approach succeeds to target small, non-hyperintense lesions, while we evaluate voxel-wise metrics, addressing a more clinically relevant scenario. Both experiments evaluate a form of robustness to domain shifts, including corruption types in MNIST-C and texture or population age variations in MRI. Results demonstrate performance and robustness of our proposed model, highlighting its potential for general UAD and real-world medical imaging applications. The source code is available at https://github.com/Nicolas-Pinon/uad_ocsvm_guided_repr_learning.

</details>


### [277] [Continual Learning with Support Boundary Experience Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23534)
*Chih-Fan Hsu,Ming-Ching Chang,Wei-Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Experience Blending (EB)框架，通过差分隐私启发的潜空间噪声注入生成支持边界数据（SBD），与经验回放中的旧样本联合训练，以缓解灾难性遗忘。在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny ImageNet和ImageNet1K上分别提升10%、6%、13%、2%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经验回放（ER）仅稀疏地近似数据分布，导致决策边界脆弱且过于简化，无法有效缓解灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出Experience Blending (EB)框架，包含两个组件：(1)在潜空间注入差分隐私启发的噪声生成支持边界数据（SBD）；(2)端到端联合训练旧样本和SBD，通过双模型聚合策略隐式正则化决策边界。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny ImageNet和ImageNet1K上分别提升10%、6%、13%、2%的准确率，优于标准经验回放方法。

Conclusion: SBD通过丰富决策边界附近的特征空间，使持续学习更稳定且鲁棒，有效克服了传统ER的局限性。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) seeks to mitigate catastrophic forgetting when models are trained with sequential tasks. A common approach, experience replay (ER), stores past exemplars but only sparsely approximates the data distribution, yielding fragile and oversimplified decision boundaries. We address this limitation by introducing Support Boundary Data (SBD), generated via differential-privacy-inspired noise into latent features to create boundary-adjacent representations that implicitly regularize decision boundaries. Building on this idea, we propose Experience Blending (EB), a framework that jointly trains on exemplars and SBD through a dual-model aggregation strategy. EB has two components: (1) latent-space noise injection to generate support boundary data, and (2) end-to-end training that jointly leverages exemplars and SBD. Unlike standard experience replay, SBD enriches the feature space near decision boundaries, leading to more stable and robust continual learning. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, Tiny ImageNet, and ImageNet1K demonstrate consistent accuracy improvements of 10%, 6%, 13%, 2%, respectively.

</details>


### [278] [The Algorithm Is Not the Behavior: Learned Priors Override Look-Ahead in a Chess-Playing Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21380)
*Elias Sandmann,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在Leela Chess Zero中，尽管网络内部层正确计算了棋局解法（包括将杀），但最终输出被安全偏好覆盖，导致错误结果。通过干预安全偏好可恢复61.7%的被遗忘解法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络中学习到的算法结构是否必然保证算法行为，即模型内部正确求解问题是否一定能输出正确答案。

Method: 扩展logit lens到策略网络，分析中间层表示；复制先前的look-ahead分析（未来走法表示、因果重要性、线性解码）；通过模型干预（steering）改变安全偏好。

Result: 发现"遗忘谜题"现象：正确解法在中间层出现但最终被覆盖；look-ahead机制本身正常运作；后期层逐步偏向安全保守玩法；干预安全偏好恢复61.7%的被遗忘解法。

Conclusion: 算法结构不保证算法行为，模型可内部求解问题却输出错误答案，内部安全偏好会覆盖正确计算结果。

Abstract: Recent mechanistic work has uncovered learned algorithms within neural networks, from modular arithmetic to search and planning in game-playing agents. But does algorithmic structure guarantee algorithmic behavior? We investigate this in Leela Chess Zero, the strongest neural chess engine, where prior work identified learned look-ahead. By extending the logit lens to its move-selecting policy network, we discover that correct puzzle solutions-including immediate checkmates-often appear in intermediate layers but are systematically overridden in the final output, a phenomenon we term "forgotten puzzles". Replicating prior analyses on these positions, we find that look-ahead operates normally-future moves of the correct continuation are represented, causally important, and linearly decodable-ruling out a failure of the algorithm itself. Instead, late layers increasingly shift toward prioritizing safe play over aggression. To test whether this shift drives the override, we steer the model against these preferences and recover 61.7% of forgotten puzzles, providing causal evidence that safety priors override algorithmically computed solutions. These findings demonstrate that algorithmic structure does not guarantee algorithmic behavior: a model can internally solve a problem and still output the wrong answer.

</details>


### [279] [Generalizing Beyond Suboptimality: Offline Reinforcement Learning Learns Effective Scheduling through Random Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.10303)
*Jesse van Remmerden,Zaharah Bukhsh,Yingqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CDQAC，一种用于作业车间调度问题的离线强化学习算法，通过分位数批评家和延迟策略更新从静态次优数据集中学习调度策略，仅需1-5%的原始数据即可超越现有方法，并发现离线RL性能主要取决于状态-动作覆盖范围而非轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习方法在作业车间调度问题上表现优异，但需要大量训练交互，样本效率低，限制了实际应用。为解决这一挑战，作者希望开发一种能从静态次优数据集直接学习有效调度策略的离线RL算法。

Method: 提出Conservative Discrete Quantile Actor-Critic (CDQAC)，将分位数基批评家与延迟策略更新相结合，用于估计机器-操作对的回报分布。算法从静态次优数据集中学习，无需与环境交互。

Result: 在JSP和FJSP基准测试上，CDQAC持续优于数据生成启发式方法，超越现有离线与在线RL基线，且样本效率极高，仅需原数据集1-5%即可学习高质量策略。此外发现，随机启发式生成的更广泛覆盖的数据集反而优于强启发式（如遗传算法）生成的数据集。

Conclusion: 在调度问题中，离线RL性能主要由状态-动作覆盖范围决定，而非单个轨迹的质量。调度任务的密集奖励和等长轨迹特性使得从广泛行为中学习成为可能。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) approaches have demonstrated strong performance on Job Shop Scheduling (JSP) and Flexible JSP (FJSP) problems by learning scheduling policies through direct interaction with simulated environments. However, these methods often require extensive training interactions, limiting their sample efficiency and practical applicability. Motivated by this challenge, we introduce Conservative Discrete Quantile Actor-Critic (CDQAC), an offline RL algorithm that learns effective scheduling policies directly from static, suboptimal datasets. CDQAC couples a quantile-based critic with delayed policy updates to estimate the return distribution of machine-operation pairs. Extensive experiments on JSP and FJSP benchmarks demonstrate that CDQAC consistently outperforms the data-generating heuristics, surpasses state-of-the-art offline and online RL baselines, and is highly sample efficient, requiring only 1 to 5% of the original dataset to learn high-quality policies. Our analysis suggests that, in scheduling, offline RL performance is governed mainly by state-action coverage rather than the quality of individual trajectories. Scheduling couples a dense reward aligned with the makespan objective with equal-length trajectories across heuristics, enabling effective learning from a broad range of behaviors. Consistent with this observation, datasets generated by a simple random heuristic with broader coverage let it outperform policies trained on datasets produced by stronger heuristics such as Genetic Algorithms.

</details>


### [280] [Energy Use of AI Inference, Efficiency Pathways, and Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20241)
*Felipe Oviedo,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Esha Choukse,Allen Kim,Amy Luers,Melanie Nakagawa,Ricardo Bianchini,Juan M. Lavista Ferres*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自底向上的推理能耗估算框架，发现大规模部署下前沿模型（>200B参数）的单次查询能耗中位数为0.31 Wh，比广泛引用的估算值低4-20倍；测试时扩展场景下能耗增加13倍，但通过效率干预可显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI推理能耗估算多基于非生产环境假设，导致系统性高估，不利于容量规划、效率干预和政策制定。

Method: 提出自底向上框架，从token吞吐量、节点功耗和开销三个维度估算推理能耗，并引入大规模部署假设。

Result: 前沿模型单次查询能耗中位数0.31 Wh（IQR 0.16-0.60）；测试时扩展场景中位数升至3.91 Wh；跨模型/系统/硬件存在8-20倍能效优化空间；10亿查询/天需0.7 GWh，含10%长查询时升至1.7 GWh/天，效率干预后可降至0.8 GWh/天。

Conclusion: 大规模生产环境下的推理能耗远低于先前估计，且通过效率优化可有效缓解测试时扩展带来的能耗增长。

Abstract: As AI inference scales to billions of queries, estimates of per-query energy use are increasingly important for capacity planning, efficiency interventions, and policy. Yet many public estimates assume non-production settings, leading to systematic overestimation. We introduce a bottom-up framework estimating inference energy from token throughput, node power, and overhead under large-scale deployment assumptions. For frontier-scale models (>200B parameters) on H100 nodes, we estimate a median energy of 0.31 Wh/query (IQR 0.16-0.60), indicating widely cited estimates are overstated by 4-20x. In test-time scaling scenarios 15x longer than typical queries, the median energy rises 13x to 3.91 Wh (IQR 2.15-7.05). Across models, serving systems, and hardware, we estimate 8-20x line-of-sight energy reductions. At datacenter scale, serving 1 billion queries/day requires 0.7 GWh; if 10% are long queries, demand rises to 1.7 GWh/day. With efficiency interventions, it falls to 0.8 GWh/day, mitigating the energy impact of test-time scaling.

</details>


### [281] [Noise-Guided Transport for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26294)
*Lionel Blondé,Joao A. Candido Ramos,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出噪声引导输运（NGT），一种轻量级离策略模仿学习方法，将模仿学习视为通过对抗训练求解的最优传输问题。NGT无需预训练或专用架构，内置不确定性估计，在低数据 regime（仅20个transitions）下仍能在高维连续控制任务（包括Humanoid）上取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低数据场景下，仅需少量专家演示即可完成模仿学习，避免对大规模预训练或高容量架构的依赖，提高数据效率。

Method: 将模仿学习建模为最优传输问题，通过对抗训练求解；采用离策略学习范式，引入噪声引导机制，内置不确定性估计。

Result: 在超低数据 regime（仅20个transitions）下，于高维连续控制任务（包括Humanoid）上取得强性能。

Conclusion: NGT是一种简单、易实现、无需预训练或专用架构的轻量级方法，在极低数据条件下仍能有效完成高维连续控制模仿学习。

Abstract: We consider imitation learning in the low-data regime, where only a limited number of expert demonstrations are available. In this setting, methods that rely on large-scale pretraining or high-capacity architectures can be difficult to apply, and efficiency with respect to demonstration data becomes critical. We introduce Noise-Guided Transport (NGT), a lightweight off-policy method that casts imitation as an optimal transport problem solved via adversarial training. NGT requires no pretraining or specialized architectures, incorporates uncertainty estimation by design, and is easy to implement and tune. Despite its simplicity, NGT achieves strong performance on challenging continuous control tasks, including high-dimensional Humanoid tasks, under ultra-low data regimes with as few as 20 transitions.

</details>


### [282] [Bypassing Prompt Guards in Production with Controlled-Release Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01529)
*Jaiden Fairoze,Sanjam Garg,Keewoo Lee,Mingyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出受控释放提示攻击，利用轻量级输入过滤器与大型语言模型之间的资源不对称性，使任何有界过滤器无法识别恶意提示，而目标LLM仍可处理。该攻击在四个主流商用平台（Google Gemini、DeepSeek Chat、xAI Grok、Mistral Le Chat）上成功实施，并能从Gemini提取版权数据。对14个开源提示防护模型的评估显示，即使具备推理能力的过滤器也无法在不产生过大资源开销的情况下可靠检测该攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ball等人近期证明，在标准密码学假设下，提示过滤在理论上存在根本性障碍——运行速度显著快于被保护模型的过滤器无法通用地区分对抗性提示和良性提示。本研究旨在探究这一不可能性结论是否会在部署的大语言模型系统中转化为现实世界漏洞。

Method: 提出受控释放提示（controlled-release prompting）攻击，该攻击利用轻量级输入过滤器与主模型之间的资源不对称性，生成任何有界过滤器无法解读但目标LLM仍可处理的恶意提示，且无需修改模型本身。

Result: 在Google Gemini、DeepSeek Chat、xAI Grok和Mistral Le Chat四个主流商用平台上成功实施攻击（基线方法均失败）；成功从Gemini提取版权数据；对14个开源提示防护模型的评估表明，即使具备推理能力的过滤器也无法在不产生过大资源开销的情况下可靠检测该攻击。

Conclusion: 理论上的提示过滤不可能性结果在现实部署的LLM系统中确实构成实际威胁，现有防护机制存在根本性漏洞，亟需更有效的对齐方案。

Abstract: Ball et al. recently established that prompt filtering for AI alignment faces a fundamental barrier: under standard cryptographic assumptions, no filter running significantly faster than the protected model can universally distinguish adversarial prompts from benign ones. We investigate whether this impossibility result translates to real-world vulnerabilities in deployed large language model (LLM) systems. We answer affirmatively by introducing controlled-release prompting, a practical instantiation of the theoretical framework that exploits the resource asymmetry between lightweight input filters and the main models they protect. Unlike the theoretical construction, our attack does not require model modification: it generates malicious prompts that are indecipherable by any bounded filter yet remain tractable to the target LLM. We find our attack to be successful on four major chat platforms (Google Gemini, DeepSeek Chat, xAI Grok, and Mistral Le Chat) where baseline methods fail. Additionally, we apply our attack to extract copyrighted data from Gemini. Finally, we provide a systematic evaluation of 14 open-weight prompt guard models, revealing that even reasoning-capable filters cannot reliably detect our attack without incurring prohibitive resource overhead.

</details>


### [283] [Reinforcement Learning with Action-Triggered Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02149)
*Alexander Ryabchenko,Wenlong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ATST-MDP框架，在部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程中引入动作触发的随机全观测，利用观测间的动作序列进行线性表示，并基于此设计了ATST-LSVI-UCB算法，在 episodic 线性MDP设置下达到了$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{Kd^3(1-γ)^{-3}})$的遗憾界，与完全可观测情形下的已知最优率匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分可观测强化学习框架通常假设观测是确定性的或与状态同分布，而实际场景（如远程控制、通信受限系统）中，完整状态信息仅在由动作触发的随机时刻才可获得。作者希望建立一个能够描述这种"动作触发式随机可观测"的通用框架，并证明其可解性与学习效率。

Method: 1) 定义ATST-MDP：每个时间步以依赖于动作的概率获得完整状态观测；2) 推导该设定下的贝尔曼方程并证明最优策略存在性；3) 利用"观测揭示完整状态"特性，将问题等价转化为智能体在连续观测之间提交动作序列；4) 在线性MDP假设下证明动作序列上的价值函数具有有限维线性表示；5) 基于此设计ATST-LSVI-UCB算法，采用乐观上置信界方法进行探索。

Result: ATST-LSVI-UCB算法在几何分布幕长（带折扣因子γ的episodic设置）下达到了$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{Kd^3(1-γ)^{-3}})$的累积遗憾界，其中K为幕数，d为特征维度，γ为折扣因子（幕继续概率），该速率与完全可观测线性MDP的最优遗憾界相匹配。

Conclusion: ATST-MDP框架成功地将部分可观测性与线性函数近似相结合，证明了动作触发的随机观测并未从根本上牺牲学习效率——在合理的结构假设下，其遗憾界可与完全可观测情形相媲美。这一结果表明，对于这类特定的部分可观测问题，智能体无需付出额外代价即可应对观测的不确定性。

Abstract: We introduce Action-Triggered Sporadically Traceable Markov Decision Processes (ATST-MDPs), a reinforcement learning framework for partial observability in which full state observations occur stochastically at each step, with probability determined by the chosen action. We derive Bellman equations tailored to this setting and establish the existence of an optimal policy. Exploiting the fact that sporadic observations reveal the full state, we provide an equivalent formulation in which agents commit to action-sequences between consecutive observations. Under the linear MDP assumption, we show that the value function over such action-sequences admits a linear representation in a finite-dimensional feature map, enabling standard regression-based methods. As an application, we derive ATST-LSVI-UCB, an optimistic algorithm achieving regret $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{Kd^3(1-γ)^{-3}})$ for episodic learning with geometrically distributed horizons, where $K$ is the number of episodes, $d$ the feature dimension, and $γ$ the discount factor (episode continuation probability), matching the known rate for linear MDPs with full observability.

</details>


### [284] [Certifiable Safe RLHF: Semantic Grounding and Fixed Penalty Constraint Optimization for Safer LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03520)
*Kartik Pandit,Sourav Ganguly,Arnesh Banerjee,Shaahin Angizi,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CS-RLHF方法，通过基于语义的安全成本模型和精确惩罚函数机制，在避免双变量更新的同时提供可证明的安全保证，相比现有方法至少提升5倍安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CMDP的RLHF方法存在两个关键局限：一是依赖奖励/成本函数，性能高度受限于底层评分机制（需捕捉语义而非关键词触发）；二是双变量调优计算昂贵且无法提供可证明的安全保证（固定双变量易受对抗性越狱攻击）。

Method: CS-RLHF提出：1) 在大规模语料上训练成本模型以分配语义基础的安全分数；2) 采用基于精确惩罚函数的矫正惩罚公式，替代拉格朗日方法。通过适当缩放惩罚项，在优化器处直接保证安全约束可行性，无需双变量更新。

Result: 实验表明CS-RLHF在应对常规和越狱提示时，相比现有最先进方法至少提升5倍效率。

Conclusion: 通过精确惩罚函数理论，CS-RLHF在消除双变量更新计算开销的同时，提供了可证明的安全约束满足保证，有效解决了CMDP方法的固有缺陷。

Abstract: Ensuring safety is a foundational requirement for large language models (LLMs). Achieving an appropriate balance between enhancing the utility of model outputs and mitigating their potential for harm is a complex and persistent challenge. Contemporary approaches frequently formalize this problem within the framework of Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) and employ established CMDP optimization techniques. However, these methods exhibit two notable limitations. First, their reliance on reward and cost functions renders performance highly sensitive to the underlying scoring mechanism, which must capture semantic meaning rather than being triggered by superficial keywords. Second, CMDP-based training entails tuning dual-variable, a process that is both computationally expensive and does not provide any provable safety guarantee for a fixed dual variable that can be exploitable through adversarial jailbreaks. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Certifiable Safe-RLHF (CS-RLHF) that introduces a cost model trained on a large-scale corpus to assign semantically grounded safety scores. In contrast to the lagrangian-based approach, CS-RLHF adopts a rectified penalty-based formulation. This design draws on the theory of exact penalty functions in constrained optimization, wherein constraint satisfaction is enforced directly through a suitably chosen penalty term. With an appropriately scaled penalty, feasibility of the safety constraints can be guaranteed at the optimizer, eliminating the need for dual-variable updates. Empirical evaluation demonstrates that CS-RLHF outperforms state-of-the-art LLM model responses rendering at-least 5 times efficient against nominal and jail-breaking prompts

</details>


### [285] [GILT: An LLM-Free, Tuning-Free Graph Foundational Model for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04567)
*Weishuo Ma,Yanbo Wang,Xiyuan Wang,Lei Zou,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GILT框架，一种无需LLM和无需微调的图上下文学习Transformer，通过统一的token化机制处理节点、边和图级别的分类任务，有效解决了图数据异构性和效率瓶颈问题，在少样本场景下性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型面临两大挑战：基于LLM的方法依赖文本特征，难以处理大规模图中的数值特征；基于结构预训练的方法需要针对每个图进行昂贵的微调，效率低下。需要一种既能处理异构数值特征又能实现高效迁移的图学习范式。

Method: 提出GILT框架，核心是新颖的基于token的图上下文学习机制。该框架无需LLM和微调，通过统一的token化表示将节点、边和图级别的分类任务纳入同一框架，利用上下文动态理解类别语义，实现对异构数值特征的通用处理。

Result: 实验表明，GILT在少样本场景下显著优于基于LLM和基于微调的基线方法，同时推理时间大幅减少，验证了其在性能和效率上的有效性。

Conclusion: GILT通过LLM-free和tuning-free的架构设计，成功克服了图数据的异构性挑战和效率瓶颈，为图基础模型提供了一种更加通用和高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are powerful tools for processing relational data but often struggle to generalize to unseen graphs, giving rise to the development of Graph Foundational Models (GFMs). However, current GFMs are challenged by the extreme heterogeneity of graph data, where each graph can possess a unique feature space, label set, and topology. To address this, two main paradigms have emerged. The first leverages Large Language Models (LLMs), but is fundamentally text-dependent, thus struggles to handle the numerical features in vast graphs. The second pre-trains a structure-based model, but the adaptation to new tasks typically requires a costly, per-graph tuning stage, creating a critical efficiency bottleneck. In this work, we move beyond these limitations and introduce \textbf{G}raph \textbf{I}n-context \textbf{L}earning \textbf{T}ransformer (GILT), a framework built on an LLM-free and tuning-free architecture. GILT introduces a novel token-based framework for in-context learning (ICL) on graphs, reframing classification tasks spanning node, edge and graph levels in a unified framework. This mechanism is the key to handling heterogeneity, as it is designed to operate on generic numerical features. Further, its ability to understand class semantics dynamically from the context enables tuning-free adaptation. Comprehensive experiments show that GILT achieves stronger few-shot performance with significantly less time than LLM-based or tuning-based baselines, validating the effectiveness of our approach. Our code is available at: https://github.com/yiming421/inductnode/.

</details>


### [286] [Time-multiplexed layer reuse for physical neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00044)
*Kohei Tsuchiyama,Andre Roehm,Takatomo Mihana,Ryoichi Horisaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TIDAL-Net，一种介于循环神经网络和深度神经网络之间的架构，通过时间复用机制在物理神经网络中实现参数的高效重用，从而在不显著增加实现成本的情况下提升有效深度和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理神经网络（PNN）的规模远小于现代数字神经网络，且存在权重调整速度慢的瓶颈，需要探索参数重用的高效硬件架构。

Method: 提出时间索引深度交替层网络（TIDAL-Net），利用PNN中快速前向动力学与慢速可训练权重之间的时间尺度分离，通过逐层时间复用来增加有效深度，同时限制实现成本。

Result: 在图像分类和自然语言处理任务上的数值实验表明，TIDAL-Net在仅对传统PNN进行微小修改的情况下提升了性能。

Conclusion: TIDAL-Net为受限于权重调整速度的物理神经网络提供了一种高效的架构设计思路，展示了时间复用机制在硬件资源受限场景下的潜力。

Abstract: Physical neural networks (PNNs) are promising candidates for next-generation computing, but existing demonstrations remain several orders of magnitude smaller than modern digital neural networks, whose recent advances have been driven by rapid growth in trainable parameters. This situation resembles the constraints of early digital neural networks, which led to ideas around parameter reuse. We investigate what similarly efficient hardware architectures may look like, focusing specifically on the common bottleneck of slow re-adjustment of the weights in PNNs. We propose the Time-Indexed Deep Alternating Layers Network (TIDAL-Net), which occupies an intermediate regime between recurrent and deep neural networks, specifically aimed at the scales and restrictions of common PNN prototypes. TIDAL-Net leverages the timescale separation found in many PNNs between fast forward dynamics and slowly trainable weights and biases, using layer-by-layer time multiplexing to increase effective depth while limiting implementation cost. Numerical experiments on image classification and natural language processing tasks show that TIDAL-Net improves performance with only minor modifications to conventional PNNs.

</details>


### [287] [Grounding Computer Use Agents on Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07332)
*Aarash Feizi,Shravan Nayak,Xiangru Jian,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Kaixin Li,Rabiul Awal,Xing Han Lù,Johan Obando-Ceron,Juan A. Rodriguez,Nicolas Chapados,David Vazquez,Adriana Romero-Soriano,Reihaneh Rabbany,Perouz Taslakian,Christopher Pal,Spandana Gella,Sai Rajeswar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GroundCUA，一个基于专家人工演示的大规模桌面端UI元素定位数据集，覆盖87个应用、12个类别、5.6万张截图和356万个经过人工验证的标注。基于该数据集训练的GroundNext模型族（3B/7B）在五个基准上达到SOTA，仅需不到先前工作十分之一的训练数据。强化学习后训练进一步提升性能，在OSWorld代理任务中表现可比或优于使用更多数据训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向网页和移动端交互的UI元素定位数据集较为丰富，但桌面端环境的高质量资源严重匮乏，这限制了可靠计算机操作智能体的发展。为填补这一空白，研究者构建了大规模、高质量、专家驱动的桌面端UI元素定位数据集。

Method: 首先，收集专家人工演示数据，覆盖87个应用和12个类别，共5.6万张截图，对每个屏幕元素进行人工标注，总计超过356万个标注。基于这些演示生成多样化的自然语言指令。然后，使用GroundCUA数据集对模型进行监督微调，训练GroundNext模型族（3B和7B），随后应用强化学习后训练进一步提升性能。

Result: 在3B和7B两种规模下，GroundNext在五个基准测试中均达到SOTA水平，训练数据量不到先前工作的十分之一。强化学习后训练进一步提升了性能。在OSWorld基准上以o3为规划器的代理设置中，GroundNext取得了与使用更多数据训练的模型相当或更优的结果。

Conclusion: 高质量、专家驱动的数据集对于推进通用计算机操作智能体的发展具有关键作用。通过高质量数据和合理训练策略，可以在大幅减少数据量的情况下获得领先性能。

Abstract: Building reliable computer-use agents requires grounding: accurately connecting natural language instructions to the correct on-screen elements. While large datasets exist for web and mobile interactions, high-quality resources for desktop environments are limited. To address this gap, we introduce GroundCUA, a large-scale desktop grounding dataset built from expert human demonstrations. It covers 87 applications across 12 categories and includes 56K screenshots, with every on-screen element carefully annotated for a total of over 3.56M human-verified annotations. From these demonstrations, we generate diverse instructions that capture a wide range of real-world tasks, providing high-quality data for model training. Using GroundCUA, we develop the GroundNext family of models that map instructions to their target UI elements. At both 3B and 7B scales, GroundNext achieves state-of-the-art results across five benchmarks using supervised fine-tuning, while requiring less than one-tenth the training data of prior work. Reinforcement learning post-training further improves performance, and when evaluated in an agentic setting on the OSWorld benchmark using o3 as planner, GroundNext attains comparable or superior results to models trained with substantially more data,. These results demonstrate the critical role of high-quality, expert-driven datasets in advancing general-purpose computer-use agents.

</details>


### [288] [CaReTS: A Multi-Task Framework Unifying Classification and Regression for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.09789)
*Fulong Yao,Wanqing Zhao,Chao Zheng,Xiaofei Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CaReTS，一种结合分类与回归任务的双流多任务学习框架，用于多步时间序列预测。分类分支学习未来逐步趋势，回归分支估计与最新观测值的偏差。该框架通过不确定性感知的多任务损失自适应平衡各任务，并实例化四种变体（CaReTS1-4）以集成CNN、LSTM和Transformer等编码器。实验表明CaReTS在预测精度和趋势分类性能上均优于SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度预测模型虽性能优异，但多数方法难以同时提供准确预测和对时间动态的可解释性洞察。

Method: 提出双流多任务学习框架CaReTS：1）分类分支学习未来逐步趋势；2）回归分支估计与最新观测值的偏差；3）设计不确定性加权的多任务损失自适应平衡各任务；4）实例化四种变体（CaReTS1-4）以集成CNN、LSTM和Transformer等主流时序编码器。

Result: 在真实数据集上，CaReTS在预测精度和趋势分类性能上均优于SOTA算法。

Conclusion: CaReTS通过解耦宏观趋势与微观偏差的双流设计，在提升预测准确性的同时提供了更具可解释性的预测结果。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep forecasting models have achieved remarkable performance, yet most approaches still struggle to provide both accurate predictions and interpretable insights into temporal dynamics. This paper proposes CaReTS, a novel multi-task learning framework that combines classification and regression tasks for multi-step time series forecasting problems. The framework adopts a dual-stream architecture, where a classification branch learns the stepwise trend into the future, while a regression branch estimates the corresponding deviations from the latest observation of the target variable. The dual-stream design provides more interpretable predictions by disentangling macro-level trends from micro-level deviations in the target variable. To enable effective learning in output prediction, deviation estimation, and trend classification, we design a multi-task loss with uncertainty-aware weighting to adaptively balance the contribution of each task. Furthermore, four variants (CaReTS1--4) are instantiated under this framework to incorporate mainstream temporal modelling encoders, including convolutional neural networks (CNNs), long short-term memory networks (LSTMs), and Transformers. Experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that CaReTS outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) algorithms in forecasting accuracy, while achieving higher trend classification performance.

</details>


### [289] [Visualizing LLM Latent Space Geometry Through Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21594)
*Alex Ning,Vainateya Rangaraju,Yen-Ling Kuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过降维方法（PCA和UMAP）提取、处理和可视化Transformer语言模型（GPT-2和LLaMa）的潜在状态几何结构，发现了注意力与MLP组件输出在中间层的清晰分离、初始位置高范数状态、层级演化模式以及位置编码的螺旋结构等未在先前工作中记录的几何模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多种自然语言任务中取得了最先进的结果，但其内部机制仍然难以解释，因此需要对其潜在状态几何结构进行可视化和系统分析。

Method: 捕获Transformer模块内多个点的逐层激活，通过主成分分析（PCA）和均匀流形近似与投影（UMAP）进行降维，对GPT-2和LLaMa模型进行实验分析。

Result: 发现了注意力与MLP组件输出在中间层的清晰分离这一未记录的模式；识别了初始序列位置潜在状态的高范数特征；可视化了层级演化模式；展示了GPT-2位置编码的高维螺旋结构以及LLaMa的序列维度几何模式。

Conclusion: 通过降维可视化方法有效揭示了Transformer语言模型潜在空间中有意义的几何结构，为理解LLM内部机制提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve state-of-the-art results across many natural language tasks, but their internal mechanisms remain difficult to interpret.
