In this work, we extract, process, and visualize latent state geometries in Transformer-based language models through dimensionality reduction. We capture layerwise activations at multiple points within Transformer blocks and enable systematic analysis through Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection (UMAP). We demonstrate experiments on GPT-2 and LLaMa models, where we uncover interesting geometric patterns in latent space. Notably, we identify a clear separation between attention and MLP component outputs across intermediate layers, a pattern not documented in prior work to our knowledge. We also characterize the high norm of latent states at the initial sequence position and visualize the layerwise evolution of latent states. Additionally, we demonstrate the high-dimensional helical structure of GPT-2's positional embeddings and the sequence-wise geometric patterns in LLaMa. We make our code available at https://github.com/Vainateya/Feature_Geometry_Visualization. A better formatted blog-post with identical content is available at https://iclr-blogposts.github.io/2026/blog/2026/vis-llm-latent-geometry/.

</details>


### [290] [MobileFineTuner: A Mobile-Native Framework for On-Device LLM Fine-Tuning in Real-World Embedded AI Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08211)
*Jiaxiang Geng,Lunyu Zhao,Yiyi Lu,Bing Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MobileFineTuner 是一个面向移动端、基于 C++ 实现的开源 LLM 微调框架，通过内存高效注意力、激活检查点、梯度累积、参数分片和能量感知调度等资源感知优化，在商用手机上实现了 GPT-2、Gemma 3、Qwen2.5 等模型的全参数和 LoRA 微调，并通过校园健康助手应用验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 微调框架多基于 Python 且面向服务器端，难以部署到移动应用中；而移动端拥有用户私密的纵向感知数据，亟需一种能在商用手机上实现本地微调的移动原生框架。

Method: 使用 C++ 实现移动原生训练栈，集成资源感知训练运行时，包括内存高效注意力、激活检查点、梯度累积、参数分片和能量感知调度等技术以应对移动端资源约束。

Result: 在真实手机上验证了 GPT-2、Gemma 3、Qwen2.5 的微调，成功复现标准 Full-FT 和 LoRA 行为，显著降低内存压力并提升在受限手机上的可执行性；校园健康助手应用展示了本地微调个性化响应能力。

Conclusion: MobileFineTuner 作为实用工具包，为嵌入式 AI 和感知系统中研究和构建设备端 LLM 微调应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are moving from cloud-centric services toward on-device embedded AI, where models interact with private, longitudinal signals sensed from users and their physical environments. Mobile phones are a natural platform for such applications because they are continuously carried by users, connected to wearable sensors, and deeply integrated with daily mobile applications. However, practical LLM fine-tuning on commodity phones remains difficult. Existing fine-tuning frameworks are largely Python-based and server-oriented, making them hard to deploy inside mobile applications. We present MobileFineTuner, a mobile-native open-source framework for end-to-end LLM fine-tuning on commodity mobile phones. MobileFineTuner is implemented in C++ and provides a reusable training stack. To make fine-tuning feasible under mobile resource constraints, MobileFineTuner integrates a resource-aware training runtime with memory-efficient attention, activation checkpointing, gradient accumulation, parameter sharding, and energy-aware scheduling. We evaluate MobileFineTuner on real mobile phones using GPT-2, Gemma 3, and Qwen2.5 models across multiple fine-tuning tasks. The results show that MobileFineTuner reproduces standard Full-FT and LoRA fine-tuning behavior, substantially reduces memory pressure and improves executability on memory-constrained phones. We further demonstrate MobileFineTuner through a private campus health-agent application, where a local LLM is fine-tuned on user-specific wearable-sensing records to provide more personalized responses while keeping raw records on the phone. These results establish MobileFineTuner as a practical toolkit for studying and building on-device LLM fine-tuning applications in embedded AI and sensing systems.

</details>


### [291] [Unifying Learning Dynamics and Generalization in Transformers Scaling Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22088)
*Chiwun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了基于Transformer的语言模型学习动力学为ODE系统，通过核方法近似，证明了泛化误差在计算资源扩展下存在相变：指数衰减阶段后进入幂律衰减阶段（Θ(C^{-1/7})），并给出了匹配的上下界以及独立的数据规模、模型大小和训练时间的缩放律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然缩放定律在大语言模型开发中被广泛验证，但其理论基础仍不充分。现有工作多基于玩具模型分析，缺乏对实际条件下多层Transformer训练的严格理论刻画。

Method: 将Transformer学习动力学形式化为ODE系统，并近似为核行为；对任意数据分布上的序列到序列数据，严格分析SGD训练过程；通过信息论两点归约和一阶预言机论证分别给出统计侧和优化侧的下界。

Result: 建立了过量风险（excess risk）的匹配上下界，揭示了两个阶段的相变：初始阶段指数衰减；资源分配阈值后进入统计阶段，泛化误差以Θ(C^{-1/7})幂律衰减。上界与下界在常数、对数因子和条件数间隙下紧致匹配。同时推导了模型大小、训练时间和数据集规模的独立缩放律。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM缩放定律提供了严格的理论基础，统一了不同资源维度下的泛化行为刻画，并揭示了资源分配中的相变现象。

Abstract: The scaling law, a cornerstone of Large Language Model (LLM) development, predicts improvements in model performance with increasing computational resources. Yet, while empirically validated, its theoretical underpinnings remain poorly understood. This work formalizes the learning dynamics of transformer-based language models as an ordinary differential equation (ODE) system, then approximates this process to kernel behaviors. Departing from prior toy-model analyses, we rigorously analyze stochastic gradient descent (SGD) training for multi-layer transformers on sequence-to-sequence data with arbitrary data distribution, closely mirroring real-world conditions. Our analysis characterizes the convergence of generalization error to the irreducible risk as computational resources scale with data, especially during the optimization process.
  We establish matching upper and lower bounds on the excess risk, characterized by a distinct phase transition. In the initial optimization phase, the excess risk decays exponentially relative to the computational cost ${\sf C}$. However, once a specific resource allocation threshold is crossed, the system enters a statistical phase, where the generalization error follows a power-law decay of $Θ(\mathsf{C}^{-1/7})$. These rates are certified by complementary lower bounds -- statistical, via an information-theoretic two-point reduction, and optimization-side, via a first-order oracle argument -- rendering the two-stage law tight up to constants, logarithmic factors, and a condition-number gap. Beyond this unified framework, our theory derives isolated scaling laws for model size, training time, and dataset size, elucidating how each variable independently governs the bounds of generalization.

</details>


### [292] [Geometry of Reason: Spectral Signatures of Valid Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00791)
*Valentin Noël*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的频谱分析方法，通过提取Transformer注意力矩阵的四个频谱诊断指标（Fiedler值、高频能量比、频谱熵和平滑度），有效区分语言模型的真实推理与模式匹配，在七个模型上达到85-96%的分类准确率，效果量高达Cohen's d=3.30。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证语言模型是否真正推理而非模式匹配是一个开放问题：学习型验证器成本高昂，基于输出的启发式方法脆弱。需要一种无需训练、原理清晰且架构感知的推理验证方法。

Method: 将每个注意力矩阵视为加权token图，提取四个频谱诊断指标（Fiedler值、高频能量比HFER、频谱熵、平滑度）；跨七个模型（四种架构家族）进行实验验证；通过因果消融追踪归纳头电路；进行柏拉图有效性验证和架构确定性分析。

Result: 分类准确率85-96%，效果量高达d=3.30 (p<10^{-116})；柏拉图有效性：信号追踪逻辑连贯性而非编译器接受度；架构确定性：滑动窗口注意力将判别特征从HFER转变为平滑度(d=2.09)；因果消融确认信号追踪归纳头电路；泛化至非正式思维链(d=0.78)；HFER重排序提升证明搜索Best-of-16 Pass@1 4.4-6.6%。

Conclusion: 频谱图分析是一种原理性、架构感知的推理验证原始方法，无需标签即可匹配98%全监督探针的AUC，可作为推理验证的基础工具。

Abstract: Verifying whether a language model is genuinely reasoning or pattern-matching remains an open problem: learned verifiers are expensive, and output-based heuristics are brittle. We show that valid mathematical reasoning induces a measurable, training-free spectral signature in transformer attention. By treating each attention matrix as a weighted token graph, we extract four diagnostics: Fiedler value, High-Frequency Energy Ratio (HFER), spectral entropy, and smoothness, that require no learned parameters. Experiments across seven models from four architectural families yield effect sizes up to Cohen's $d = 3.30$ ($p < 10^{-116}$), enabling $85$--$96\%$ single-threshold classification accuracy. Two findings sharpen the interpretation. First, \emph{Platonic validity}: the spectral signal tracks logical coherence rather than compiler acceptance, proofs rejected for timeouts or missing imports are correctly classified as valid, a distinction confirmed by a manual audit ($κ= 0.82$, $n = 51$). Second, \emph{architectural determinism}: Sliding Window Attention shifts the discriminative feature from HFER to smoothness ($d = 2.09$, $p < 10^{-48}$), showing that attention design governs which spectral channel encodes reasoning quality. Causal ablation confirms the signature traces induction-head circuits. The method generalises to informal chain-of-thought ($d = 0.78$, $p < 10^{-3}$), and in proof search, HFER reranking improves Best-of-16 Pass@1 by $+4.4$--$6.6$\%, matching $98\%$ of the AUC of fully supervised probes with zero labels. Spectral graph analysis is a principled, architecture-aware primitive for reasoning verification.

</details>


### [293] [Reverse Flow Matching: A Unified Framework for Online Reinforcement Learning with Diffusion and Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08136)
*Zeyang Li,Sunbochen Tang,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架RFM（反向流匹配），通过Langevin Stein算子构建零均值控制变量，将现有噪声期望和梯度期望两类扩散策略训练方法统一为更一般形式的估计器，并扩展到流策略，提高了在线强化学习中策略训练的效率和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习中训练扩散策略和流策略面临一个根本困难：缺乏来自Q函数定义的玻尔兹曼目标分布的直接样本。现有两类方法（噪声期望族和梯度期望族）之间的关系不明确，缺乏统一的数学形式化。

Method: 提出反向流匹配（RFM）框架，采用反向推断视角将训练目标形式化为给定中间噪声样本的后验均值估计问题；引入Langevin Stein算子构造零均值控制变量，导出一类具有相同期望的通用估计器族。

Result: 证明了现有噪声期望和梯度期望方法只是该通用框架的两个特例；将能力从扩散策略扩展到流策略；实现了Q值和Q梯度信息的有机结合，提升了训练效率与稳定性；在连续控制基准上改进扩散策略基线。

Conclusion: RFM为在线RL中训练扩散和流模型提供了统一的理论框架，通过控制变量技术实现了更高效的估计器设计，推动了从扩散到流策略的拓展。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow policies are gaining prominence in online reinforcement learning (RL) due to their expressive power, yet training them efficiently remains a critical challenge. A fundamental difficulty that distinguishes online RL from standard generative modeling is the lack of direct samples from the target Boltzmann distribution defined by the Q-function. To address this, two seemingly distinct families of methods have been proposed for diffusion policies: a noise-expectation family, which uses a weighted average of noise as the training target, and a gradient-expectation family, which employs a weighted average of Q-function gradients. However, it remains unclear how these objectives are formally related, or whether they can be synthesized into a more general formulation. In this paper, we propose a unified framework, reverse flow matching (RFM), which rigorously addresses the problem of training diffusion and flow models without direct target samples. By adopting a reverse inferential perspective, we formulate the training target as a posterior mean estimation problem given an intermediate noisy sample. Crucially, we introduce Langevin Stein operators to construct zero-mean control variates, deriving a general class of estimators that share the same expectation. We show that existing noise-expectation and gradient-expectation methods are simply two specific instances within this broader class. This unified view yields two key advancements: it extends the capability of targeting Boltzmann distributions from diffusion to flow policies, and it enables the principled combination of Q-value and Q-gradient information to form an effective estimator, thereby improving training efficiency and stability. We instantiate RFM to train a flow policy in online RL and demonstrate improved performance on continuous-control benchmarks compared to diffusion policy baselines.

</details>


### [294] [Analytic Bijections for Smooth and Interpretable Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10774)
*Mathis Gerdes,Miranda C. N. Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三类全局光滑、解析可逆的标量双射（C∞，定义域ℝ），结合仿射变换、单调样条和残差流的优势。在耦合流中作为即插即用模块与样条性能相当；同时提出径向流架构，利用径向坐标变换保持角度方向，在径向结构目标上以千倍参数缩减达到耦合流质量，并在φ⁴格点场论中优于仿射基线并解决模式崩塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化流中缺少兼具表达力、全局光滑性、解析可逆性和全定义域ℝ性质的标量双射，现有方法（仿射变换、单调样条、残差流）各自存在表达力不足、仅分段光滑、有界定义域或需数值求逆等局限。

Method: (1) 提出三类全局C∞、解析可逆、定义于ℝ的解析双射族；(2) 开发径向流架构，直接参数化径向坐标变换并保持角度方向不变。

Result: (1) 在耦合流中作为即插即用模块，性能匹配或超越样条方法；(2) 径向流训练极稳定，生成几何可解释变换，在径向结构目标上以千倍参数缩减达到耦合流质量；(3) 在φ⁴格点场论中优于仿射基线，并可通过问题特定设计解决模式崩塌。

Conclusion: 提出的解析双射族和径向流架构有效结合了多种现有方法的优势，在多个基准测试和物理问题中表现出色，尤其适用于具有径向对称性的目标分布。

Abstract: A key challenge in normalizing flows is finding expressive invertible scalar bijections. Existing approaches face trade-offs: affine transformations are smooth and analytically invertible but lack expressivity; monotonic splines offer local control but are only piecewise smooth and act on bounded domains; residual flows achieve smoothness but need numerical inversion. We introduce three families of analytic bijections that are globally smooth ($C^\infty$), defined on all of $\mathbb{R}$, and analytically invertible in closed form, combining the favorable properties of prior approaches. Beyond serving as drop-in replacements in coupling flows, where they match or exceed spline performance, we develop radial flows: a novel architecture using direct parametrization that transforms the radial coordinate while preserving angular direction. Radial flows exhibit exceptional training stability, produce geometrically interpretable transformations, and on targets with radial structure can achieve comparable quality to coupling flows with $1000\times$ fewer parameters. We provide comprehensive evaluation on 1D and 2D benchmarks, and demonstrate applicability to higher-dimensional physics problems through experiments on $φ^4$ lattice field theory, where our bijections outperform affine baselines and enable problem-specific designs that address mode collapse.

</details>


### [295] [CoVar: Confidence-Variance-Guided Pseudo-Label Selection for Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11670)
*Jinshi Liu,Lei He,Pan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoVar 提出置信度-方差联合评估框架，通过最大置信度和残差类方差两个维度衡量伪标签可靠性，利用SVD谱松弛实现无阈值可靠/不可靠预测分离，并生成训练权重以改进半监督分割与分类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半监督学习中基于最大置信度阈值的伪标签选择在模型过自信和类别不平衡时不可靠，需要更鲁棒的可靠性评估标准。

Method: 从熵最小化出发推导二阶交叉熵近似，揭示低损失伪标签需满足高置信度且低残差类方差；将预测嵌入二维置信度-方差空间，用SVD谱松弛无阈值分离可靠/不可靠预测，再通过聚类高斯加权生成样本权重。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC 2012、Cityscapes、CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、STL-10上验证，分割任务在相同骨干下取得明显提升，分类任务达到有竞争力或更低的错误率。

Conclusion: 残差类离散度作为置信度的互补信号能有效提升伪标签选择的鲁棒性，方法可无缝集成现有半监督流程且无推理额外开销。

Abstract: Pseudo-label selection in semi-supervised learning is commonly driven by maximum-confidence thresholds, yet confidence alone can be unreliable under model overconfidence and class imbalance. We propose CoVar, a confidence--variance framework that assesses pseudo-label reliability by jointly modeling Maximum Confidence (MC) and Residual-Class Variance (RCV). Starting from entropy minimization, we derive a second-order cross-entropy approximation showing that low-loss pseudo-labels are favored when MC is high and RCV is low, with a confidence-dependent penalty that becomes stronger for near-certain predictions. Based on this criterion, CoVar embeds predictions into a two-dimensional confidence--variance space and uses SVD-based spectral relaxation to separate reliable and unreliable predictions without hand-tuned confidence thresholds. Cluster-wise Gaussian weighting then converts this separation into per-sample training weights. The resulting weights can be integrated into existing semi-supervised segmentation and classification pipelines during training and introduce no inference-time overhead. Experiments on PASCAL VOC 2012, Cityscapes, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, SVHN, and STL-10 show clear gains on VOC and Cityscapes under matched backbones, as well as competitive or improved error rates on standard classification benchmarks. These results indicate that residual-class dispersion provides a useful signal complementary to confidence for robust pseudo-label selection.

</details>


### [296] [Robustness of Mixtures of Experts to Feature Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14792)
*Dong Sun,Rahul Nittala,Rebekka Burkholz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究混合专家模型（MoE）在等参数条件下优于密集网络的原因，发现稀疏专家激活能作为噪声滤波器，在特征噪声存在时实现更低的泛化误差、更强的鲁棒性和更快的收敛速度，并通过合成数据和真实语言任务验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MoE在实践中取得了成功，但其为何在参数规模相同时能超越密集网络的内在机制尚不明确，本文旨在从理论上解释这一现象。

Method: 在等参数条件下，构建输入具有潜在模块化结构但被特征噪声破坏的理论框架，对比MoE与密集估计器的泛化误差、鲁棒性和收敛速度。

Result: MoE的稀疏专家激活机制能有效过滤噪声，相比密集模型在特征噪声下具有更低的泛化误差、更强的扰动鲁棒性和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 稀疏模块化计算是MoE在等参数条件下优于密集网络的核心原因，带来了鲁棒性和效率的双重提升。

Abstract: Despite their practical success, it remains unclear why Mixture of Experts (MoE) models can outperform dense networks beyond sheer parameter scaling. We study an iso-parameter regime where inputs exhibit latent modular structure but are corrupted by feature noise, a proxy for noisy internal activations. We show that sparse expert activation acts as a noise filter: compared to a dense estimator, MoEs achieve lower generalization error under feature noise, improved robustness to perturbations, and faster convergence speed. Empirical results on synthetic data and real-world language tasks corroborate the theoretical insights, demonstrating consistent robustness and efficiency gains from sparse modular computation.

</details>


### [297] [Robust Privacy: Inference-Stage Privacy through Certified Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17360)
*Jiankai Jin,Xiangzheng Zhang,Zhao Liu,Wenzhuo Xu,Dongdong Yang,Deyue Zhang,Quanchen Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出鲁棒隐私（RP）概念，基于认证鲁棒性在推理阶段保护隐私，通过确保预测在输入邻域内不变性来限制攻击者优势，并在属性推断、模型逆向攻击等场景中优于DP-SGD等传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者可以通过观察模型输出泄露敏感属性或重构训练数据，推理接口成为隐私泄露的侧信道。现有训练阶段防御（如DP-SGD）存在严重的隐私-效用权衡问题，亟需直接针对推理阶段泄漏通道的防御机制。

Method: 基于认证鲁棒性思想，定义(R,α)-鲁棒隐私：若模型预测在输入x的半径R邻域内以至少1-α置信度不变，则攻击者区分x与邻域内任意输入的优势不超过α/2。进一步形式化鲁棒属性隐私（RAP），刻画与预测兼容的敏感属性值集合。通过随机平滑等技术实现推理阶段的隐私保护。

Result: 分类任务上，RP将RAP兼容推断区间中位数从23.50提升至29.96，降低属性推断精度；黑盒模型逆向攻击成功率从73%降至4%。在隐私-效用权衡上，RP以98.4%准确率实现21%攻击成功率，而DP-SGD需降至61.7%准确率才能达到相近ASR。增大平滑样本量N可同时增强隐私和提升效用。

Conclusion: RP能有效缓解属性级和实例级的推理阶段隐私泄漏，但无法防御通过模型蒸馏进行的函数级提取。直接针对泄漏通道的防御方式使RP在隐私-效用权衡上显著优于DP-SGD和随机响应等传统方法。

Abstract: An adversary observing a model's released prediction can infer sensitive attributes of the queried input, or even reconstruct representatives of the model's training data. The inference interface thus acts as a side channel for privacy leakage. We introduce Robust Privacy (RP), an inference-stage privacy notion inspired by certified robustness: if a model's prediction is provably invariant within a radius-R neighborhood around an input x with confidence at least $1-α$, then x enjoys $(R,α)$-Robust Privacy, under which we prove that any adversary observing the released prediction has at most $α/2$ advantage in distinguishing x from any input within distance R of x. Building on RP, we formalize Robust Attribute Privacy (RAP), an attribute-level privacy notion that characterizes the set of sensitive-attribute values that remain compatible with a released prediction. On a classification task, RP increases the median length of the RAP-compatible inference interval from 23.50 to 29.96, reducing attribute-inference precision. Model inversion attacks, often treated as a training-stage threat, in fact rely on fine-grained signals leaked through the inference interface; RP masks these signals at the inference stage, reducing attack success rate (ASR) from 73% to 4% on a black-box inversion attack. This direct targeting of the leakage channel enables RP to dominate DP-SGD and randomized response in the privacy-utility tradeoff space: RP retains 98.4% accuracy at 21% ASR, whereas DP-SGD must drop accuracy to 61.7% to reach a comparable ASR. Across both experiments, increasing the smoothing sample size N strengthens privacy and improves utility together. Finally, we examine model distillation as a scope boundary and show that RP mitigates attribute-level and instance-level inference-stage privacy leakage, but not function-level extraction through model distillation.

</details>


### [298] [Reliability-Calibrated Edge-IoT Early Fault Warning for Rotating Machinery with a Physics-Guided Tiny-Mamba Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21293)
*Changyu Li,Huabei Nie,Xiaoya Ni,Lu Wang,Lijuan Shen,Kaishun Wu,Fei Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向工业物联网预测性维护的可靠性校准边缘早期预警框架，核心是紧凑型物理引导Tiny-Mamba Transformer（PG-TMT）结合极值理论校准层，在有限计算资源下实现可解释、低误报的分布式振动监测与早期故障预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网分布式振动传感系统中，原始信号上传成本高，报警决策需在本地有限计算、变化工况和严格误报预算约束下实时完成，现有方法难以兼顾校准性、可解释性和轻量化部署需求。

Method: 提出PG-TMT（深度可分离卷积主干+Tiny-Mamba状态空间分支+轻量局部Transformer）+ EVT校准层（双阈值迟滞、修剪尾拟合），时域注意力投影至频域并软对齐轴承故障阶次带，实现单样本推理。

Result: 在CWRU、Paderborn、XJTU-SY及工业试点数据集上，PR-AUC提升，检测延迟降低，误报可控；模型<1MB，Jetson p99延迟<7ms，对结构化干扰、元数据不确定性、复合故障与域迁移具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架在边缘端实现了校准可解释的早期预警，适合工业物联网预测性维护的实际部署需求。

Abstract: Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) systems increasingly rely on distributed vibration sensing to support predictive maintenance of rotating machinery. In practical deployments, however, raw signal upload is costly and alarm decisions must be made locally under limited computation, changing operating conditions, and strict nuisance-alarm budgets. This paper presents a reliability-calibrated edge-IoT early-warning framework, in which a compact Physics-Guided Tiny-Mamba Transformer (PG-TMT) acts as the representation module and an extreme value theory (EVT) layer converts streaming anomaly scores into event-level alarm episodes. PG-TMT combines a depthwise-separable convolutional stem, a Tiny-Mamba state-space branch, and a lightweight local Transformer to capture transient, long-horizon, and multichannel degradation cues under batch-size-one inference. To improve auditability, temporal attention is projected to the frequency domain and softly aligned with analytical bearing fault-order bands. EVT calibration, dual-threshold hysteresis, and trimmed-tail fitting provide controllable false-alarm intensity even when healthy calibration data are imperfect. Experiments on CWRU, Paderborn, XJTU-SY, and an industrial pilot demonstrate that the proposed framework improves PR-AUC, reduces detection delay under a controlled nuisance-alarm budget, and remains robust to structured interference, metadata uncertainty, compound fault mixtures, and domain transfer. With a sub-1 MB footprint and Jetson p99 latency below 7 ms, the framework supports calibrated and interpretable early warnings for IIoT predictive maintenance.

</details>


### [299] [FOCUS: DLLMs Know How to Tame Their Compute Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23278)
*Kaihua Liang,Xin Tan,An Zhong,Hong Xu,Marco Canini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散大语言模型（DLLM）解码过程中大量计算浪费在不可解码token上的问题，本文提出FOCUS推理系统，通过动态聚焦可解码token并驱逐不可解码token，有效提升批量大小和吞吐量，在保持生成质量前提下实现最高3.52倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（DLLM）虽然比自回归模型更有优势，但其解码成本高昂。现有DLLM解码中，计算虽在token块上并行化，但每个扩散步骤仅有少量token可解码，大部分计算浪费在不可解码token上，导致效率低下。

Method: 观察到注意力导出的token重要性与逐token解码概率之间存在强相关性，基于此提出FOCUS推理系统：动态聚焦计算于可解码token，并实时驱逐（evict）不可解码token，从而扩大有效批量大小，缓解计算瓶颈，实现可扩展的吞吐量。

Result: 在大批量设置下，相较于生产级引擎LMDeploy，FOCUS实现了最高3.52倍的吞吐量提升，同时在多个基准测试中保持或提升了生成质量。

Conclusion: FOCUS有效解决了DLLM解码中的计算浪费问题，通过动态聚焦策略显著提升推理效率，是一种实用的DLLM推理加速方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) offer a compelling alternative to Auto-Regressive models, but their deployment is constrained by high decoding cost. In this work, we identify a key inefficiency in DLLM decoding: while computation is parallelized over token blocks, only a small subset of tokens is decodable at each diffusion step, causing most compute to be wasted on non-decodable tokens. We further observe a strong correlation between attention-derived token importance and token-wise decoding probability. Based on this insight, we propose FOCUS, an inference system designed for DLLMs. By dynamically focusing computation on decodable tokens and evicting non-decodable ones on-the-fly, FOCUS increases the effective batch size, alleviating compute limitations and enabling scalable throughput. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that FOCUS achieves up to 3.52$\times$ throughput improvement over the production-grade engine LMDeploy in large-batch settings, while preserving or improving generation quality across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [300] [Open Materials Generation with Inference-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00424)
*Philipp Hoellmer,Stefano Martiniani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OMatG-IRL，一种基于策略梯度的强化学习框架，可直接作用于连续时间生成模型的速度场，无需显式计算分数，首次将RL应用于晶体结构预测，实现了基于能量的目标优化，并通过学习时间依赖的速度退火调度将采样效率提升了一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间晶体材料生成模型能够学习预测稳定晶体结构，但将显式目标属性融入生成过程仍具挑战性。策略梯度RL需访问分数函数，而基于流的模型仅学习速度场，无法直接应用。

Method: OMatG-IRL通过对底层生成动力学施加随机扰动，在保持预训练生成模型基线性能的同时，在推理时实现探索和策略梯度估计，无需显式计算分数。该方法可直接作用于学习到的速度场，并支持学习时间依赖的速度退火调度。

Result: 首次将RL应用于晶体结构预测（CSP），在强化基于能量的目标的同时通过成分条件化保持多样性，性能与基于分数的RL方法相当；学习时间依赖速度退火调度后，采样效率提升一个数量级，生成时间显著减少。

Conclusion: OMatG-IRL为连续时间生成模型提供了一种无需分数函数的策略梯度RL框架，有效实现了晶体材料生成中的目标导向优化，并大幅提升采样效率。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative models for crystalline materials enable inverse materials design by learning to predict stable crystal structures, but incorporating explicit target properties into the generative process remains challenging. Policy-gradient reinforcement learning (RL) provides a principled mechanism for aligning generative models with downstream objectives but typically requires access to the score, which has prevented its application to flow-based models that learn only velocity fields. We introduce Open Materials Generation with Inference-time Reinforcement Learning (OMatG-IRL), a policy-gradient RL framework that operates directly on the learned velocity fields and eliminates the need for the explicit computation of the score. OMatG-IRL leverages stochastic perturbations of the underlying generation dynamics preserving the baseline performance of the pretrained generative model while enabling exploration and policy-gradient estimation at inference time. Using OMatG-IRL, we present the first application of RL to crystal structure prediction (CSP). Our method enables effective reinforcement of an energy-based objective while preserving diversity through composition conditioning, and it achieves performance competitive with score-based RL approaches. Finally, we show that OMatG-IRL can learn time-dependent velocity-annealing schedules, enabling accurate CSP with order-of-magnitude improvements in sampling efficiency and, correspondingly, reduction in generation time. The OMatG-IRL code is included in a new release of the Open Materials Generation (OMatG) framework available at https://github.com/FERMat-ML/OMatG.

</details>


### [301] [Prediction-Powered Risk Monitoring of Deployed Models for Detecting Harmful Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02229)
*Guangyi Zhang,Yunlong Cai,Guanding Yu,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出预测驱动风险监控（PPRM）方法，在标注数据有限且环境动态变化的情况下，通过半监督方式结合合成标签与少量真实标签，构建任意有效风险下界，实现对模型性能变化的实时监控，并保证有限样本下的类型I错误控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决动态环境中标注数据有限时模型性能难以持续监控的问题。

Method: 基于预测驱动推断（PPI）的半监督风险监控方法，将合成标签与少量真实标签结合，构建运行风险的任意有效下界，通过与标称风险上界的阈值比较来检测有害漂移，并保证无假设的有限样本类型I错误控制保证。

Result: 在图像分类、大型语言模型（LLM）和电信监控任务上的大量实验验证了PPRM的有效性。

Conclusion: PPRM是一种适用于动态环境下标注数据稀缺场景的、具有理论保证的模型性能监控方法。

Abstract: We study the problem of monitoring model performance in dynamic environments where labeled data are limited. To this end, we propose prediction-powered risk monitoring (PPRM), a semi-supervised risk-monitoring approach based on prediction-powered inference (PPI). PPRM constructs anytime-valid lower bounds on the running risk by combining synthetic labels with a small set of true labels. Harmful shifts are detected via a threshold-based comparison with an upper bound on the nominal risk, satisfying assumption-free finite-sample guarantees on the type-I error. We demonstrate the effectiveness of PPRM through extensive experiments on image classification, large language model (LLM), and telecommunications monitoring tasks.

</details>


### [302] [The Latent Color Subspace: Emergent Order in High-Dimensional Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12261)
*Mateusz Pach,Jessica Bader,Quentin Bouniot,Serge Belongie,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现FLUX.1模型的VAE潜空间中存在一个反映色相、饱和度和明度的隐式颜色子空间(LCS)，并提出一种无需训练、仅基于闭式潜空间操作的颜色控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型虽发展迅速，但细粒度控制仍困难，主要因为对语义信息编码方式的理解有限。

Method: 通过解释FLUX.1中VAE潜空间的颜色表征，揭示其反映HSL（色相、饱和度、明度）的结构，并基于此提出LCS（隐式颜色子空间）解释，通过闭式潜空间操作实现无需训练的颜色预测与显式控制。

Result: 验证了LCS解释能够预测和显式控制颜色，实现了FLUX中完全无需训练的颜色控制方法。

Conclusion: 揭示VAE潜空间颜色表征的HSL结构，并提出一种无需训练的闭式潜空间操作方法，实现了对生成图像颜色的细粒度控制。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation models have advanced rapidly, yet achieving fine-grained control over generated images remains difficult, largely due to limited understanding of how semantic information is encoded. We develop an interpretation of the color representation in the Variational Autoencoder latent space of FLUX.1 [Dev], revealing a structure reflecting Hue, Saturation, and Lightness. We verify our Latent Color Subspace (LCS) interpretation by demonstrating that it can both predict and explicitly control color, introducing a fully training-free method in FLUX based solely on closed-form latent-space manipulation. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/LCS.

</details>


### [303] [AI4SLT: Empirical Processes in Lean 4 for Formal Statistical Learning Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02285)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Jason D. Lee,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在Lean 4中完成了基于经验过程理论的统计学习理论的完整形式化。项目包含高斯Lipschitz浓度、Dudley熵积分类定理以及对最小二乘（稀疏）回归的应用，采用人机协作工作流，建立了一个可复用的形式化基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补Lean 4库中统计学习理论形式化的空白，通过形式化过程揭示并解决标准教材中的隐含假设和缺失细节，建立可复用的机器学习理论基础。

Method: 采用人类设计证明策略、AI智能体执行战术性证明构建的人机协作工作流，在Lean 4中实现了高斯Lipschitz浓度、Dudley熵积分类定理等关键定理的形式化，并应用于最小二乘（稀疏）回归。

Result: 完成了Lean 4中统计学习理论的端到端形式化基础设施，包括高斯Lipschitz浓度、Dudley熵积分类定理，以及最小二乘稀疏回归的锐利速率应用。

Conclusion: 本研究为机器学习理论建立了可复用的形式化基础，形式化过程强制逐行理解理论、暴露了教材中的隐含假设，为未来机器学习理论的形式化发展打开了大门。

Abstract: We present the first comprehensive Lean 4 formalization of statistical learning theory (SLT) grounded in empirical process theory. Our en-to-end formal infrastructure implement the missing contents in latest Lean library, including a complete development of Gaussian Lipschitz concentration, Dudley's entropy integral theorem for sub-Gaussian processes, and an application to least-squares (sparse) regression with a sharp rate. The project was carried out using a human-AI collaborative workflow, in which humans design proof strategies and AI agents execute tactical proof construction, leading to the human-verified Lean 4 toolbox for SLT. Beyond implementation, the formalization process exposes and resolves implicit assumptions and missing details in standard SLT textbooks, enforcing a granular, line-by-line understanding of the theory. This work establishes a reusable formal foundation and opens the door for future developments in machine learning theory. The code is provided in https://github.com/YuanheZ/lean-stat-learning-theory.

</details>


### [304] [Vector Quantized Latent Concepts: A Scalable Alternative to Clustering-Based Concept Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02726)
*Xuemin Yu,Ankur Garg,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VQLC框架，通过向量量化的离散概念学习，在冻结的隐藏状态上学习潜在概念码本，在计算效率和语义连贯性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类方法存在权衡：层次聚类产生连贯概念但二次内存成本限制了数据集规模，K-Means可扩展但语义连贯性较差。需要一种既能扩展又能保持概念语义连贯性的方法。

Method: 提出向量量化潜在概念（VQLC）框架，在冻结的LLM隐藏状态上学习离散概念码本，通过向量量化实现高效的概念学习。

Result: 在12个数据集-模型设置中，VQLC计算成本接近K-Means，可扩展性优于层次聚类，在忠实性方面具有竞争力，在仅解码器模型上表现最佳。

Conclusion: VQLC框架学习的潜在概念是可解释且与任务相关的，通过LLM评估、定性分析和稀疏自编码器比较得到验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) encode rich semantic information in their hidden states, yet it remains difficult to understand what information these internal representations capture. Latent concepts extracted from hidden states offer a promising direction for interpreting LLMs, but existing clustering-based methods face a trade-off: hierarchical clustering produces coherent concepts but is limited to small datasets due to its quadratic memory cost, while K-Means scales efficiently but may yield less semantically coherent concepts. We propose Vector Quantized Latent Concept (VQLC), a discrete concept learning framework that learns a codebook of latent concepts on frozen hidden states. Across 12 dataset-model settings, VQLC stays close to K-Means in computational cost, scales better than hierarchical clustering, and remains competitive in faithfulness, with the clearest gains on decoder-only models. LLMs-based evaluation, qualitative analysis, and a Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) comparison demonstrate that the learned concepts are interpretable and task-relevant.

</details>


### [305] [Density Ridge Selective Prediction for LLM and VLM Hallucination Detection under Calibration Label Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10198)
*Nina I. Shamsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于响应流形密度脊（Response Manifold Density Ridge）的无监督幻觉检测方法，通过核密度估计在六维运动学特征图上构建LLM输出分布的几何骨架，距离度量作为置信度分数。在标注匮乏场景下，相较语义熵、拓扑方法和对数概率等方法，AUROC提升5-20个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督采样检测器（如语义熵）质量停滞，有监督探针在标定标签稀缺时性能急剧下降，需要一种既避免大量标注依赖又能保持鲁棒性的幻觉检测方法。

Method: 将LLM的隐状态生成轨迹映射为六维运动学特征图，在其上构建核密度估计，提取密度脊作为响应流形；测试生成通过其投影特征点到最近脊顶点的负欧氏距离进行评分，形成低维几何骨架。

Result: 在6个QA基准（HaluEval-QA、TriviaQA、GSM8K、POPE、ScienceQA、A-OKVQA）上使用8种文本和视觉LLM，在标定样本仅200条、N=5次生成的标注匮乏协议下，AUROC超越语义熵、拓扑方法和对数概率5-20个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于密度脊的无监督几何方法在标注稀缺条件下显著优于现有基线，且性能退化幅度受控，验证了响应流形结构作为幻觉检测特征的有效性。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language and vision-language models is increasingly framed as selective prediction, where a detector assigns a confidence score and abstains when confidence is low. Unsupervised sampling detectors (Semantic Entropy) avoid labels but plateau in quality, while supervised probes attain stronger in-distribution scores yet degrade sharply when calibration labels are scarce. We recover the response manifold of an LLM as the density ridge of a kernel density estimate built on a six-dimensional kinematic feature map of hidden state generation trajectories. A test generation is scored by the negated Euclidean distance from its projected feature point to the nearest ridge vertex, yielding a low-dimensional geometric skeleton of the stochastic output distribution. We evaluate against Semantic Entropy, topological methods, and log-probability on six QA benchmarks (HaluEval-QA, TriviaQA, GSM8K, POPE, ScienceQA, A-OKVQA) using eight text and vision LLMs in a deliberately label-scarce protocol ($n_{\text{cal}}{=}200$ queries, $N{=}5$ generations). Our ridge-based score beats on AUROC with 5-20 points gain, while demonstrating tempered degradation under calibration-label scarcity.

</details>


### [306] [Learning to Inject: Automated Prompt Injection via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05746)
*Xin Chen,Jie Zhang,Florian Tramèr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoInject提出了一种基于黑盒强化学习的框架，能够自动学习对抗性后缀用于LLM代理的提示注入攻击，在AgentDojo上显著优于模板攻击、GCG、TAP等方法，并能攻破专门防御提示注入的Meta-SecAlign-70B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入攻击严重依赖人工红队和手工制作提示，而自动化越狱优化器无法直接迁移到提示注入场景，因为注入需要生成特定工具调用且成功信号是二元的，标准优化器缺乏梯度信息。

Method: 提出AutoInject框架，核心创新包括：(1) 基于比较的奖励函数，将二元成功信号转化为稠密奖励；(2) 支持在线查询攻击和离线可迁移后缀训练；(3) 在有任务完成反馈时可融入效用目标。

Result: 在AgentDojo上，AutoInject在多项生产模型上显著优于模板攻击、GCG、TAP和自适应攻击（McNemar检验p<0.05）。自动学习的后缀能够攻破专门为抵抗提示注入而微调的Meta-SecAlign-70B模型，而模板攻击完全失效。

Conclusion: AutoInject建立了提示注入攻击的自动化基线，揭示了基于偏好的防御与自适应优化攻击者之间的差距，表明当前防御手段不足以应对自动化优化攻击。

Abstract: Prompt injection is a critical vulnerability in LLM agents, yet the strongest methods still rely on human red-teamers and hand-crafted prompts. Adapting automated jailbreak optimizers does not close this gap: jailbreaks shape models toward generic compliance, while prompt injection requires emitting specific tool calls with correct parameters. The success signal is binary, and randomly sampled suffixes almost never trigger it, so standard optimizers have no gradient to follow. We present AutoInject, a black-box reinforcement learning (RL) framework that learns adversarial suffixes for prompt injection. A learned comparison-based reward scores each candidate against the best suffix seen so far, turning the binary signal into a dense reward suitable for RL optimization. The framework supports both online query-based attacks and offline-trained transferable suffixes that need no utility access at deployment, and incorporates a utility objective when task-completion feedback is available. On AgentDojo, AutoInject outperforms template attacks, GCG, TAP, and adaptive attack across production models, with statistically significant improvements under McNemar's test with p<0.05. Suffixes learned by AutoInject also break Meta-SecAlign-70B, a model fine-tuned specifically to resist prompt injection, where template attacks fail outright. The results establish an automated baseline for prompt injection and expose a gap between preference-based defenses and adaptive optimization-based attackers.

</details>


### [307] [Improving Detection of Rare Nodes in Hierarchical Multi-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08986)
*Isaac Xu,Martin Gillis,Ayushi Sharma,Benjamin Misiuk,Craig J. Brown,Thomas Trappenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合节点级不平衡加权和集成不确定性聚焦加权的损失函数，通过关注稀有节点而非稀有样本，并利用模型输出分布中的不确定性信息，在层级多标签分类中显著提升召回率（最高5倍）和F1分数，尤其在数据有限或编码器次优的场景下效果明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级多标签分类中，模型难以预测到层次结构的深层（即更细粒度的分类），主要原因是某些类别（层级节点）自然稀少，且层级约束导致子节点几乎总是比父节点出现频率更低，现有方法难以有效处理这种节点级的不平衡问题。

Method: 提出一种加权损失函数，结合两个组件：（1）节点级不平衡加权（node-wise imbalance weighting），对稀有节点赋予更高权重；（2）利用集成不确定性（ensemble uncertainties）的聚焦加权（focal weighting）组件，在训练过程中关注每个模型输出分布中不确定性高的节点。该方法强调稀有节点而非稀有数据点。

Result: 在基准数据集上，召回率最高提升5倍，F1分数取得统计显著的提升；在编码器次优或数据有限的挑战性任务中，该方法对卷积网络也有帮助。

Conclusion: 通过直接对稀有节点加权并引入基于不确定性的聚焦机制，能够有效缓解层级多标签分类中的深层节点预测困难问题，是一种简单而有效的改进方案。

Abstract: In hierarchical multi-label classification, a persistent challenge is enabling model predictions to reach deeper levels of the hierarchy for more detailed or fine-grained classifications. This difficulty partly arises from the natural rarity of certain classes (or hierarchical nodes) and the hierarchical constraint that ensures child nodes are almost always less frequent than their parents. To address this, we propose a weighted loss objective for neural networks that combines node-wise imbalance weighting with focal weighting components, the latter leveraging modern quantification of ensemble uncertainties. By emphasizing rare nodes rather than rare observations (data points), and focusing on uncertain nodes for each model output distribution during training, we observe improvements in recall by up to a factor of five on benchmark datasets, along with statistically significant gains in $F_{1}$ score. We also show our approach aids convolutional networks on challenging tasks, as in situations with suboptimal encoders or limited data.

</details>


### [308] [Tensor Methods: A Unified and Interpretable Approach for Material Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10392)
*Shaan Pakala,Aldair E. Gongora,Brian Giera,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用张量补全方法作为材料设计的替代模型，相比传统机器学习方法，在保持可解释性的同时，在非均匀采样场景下预测性能提升高达5% R²，误差减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统材料设计面临设计空间指数级增长带来的搜索难题，有限元分析等计算方法计算量过大，而现有机器学习替代模型存在可解释性差和在非均匀采样数据下性能不佳的问题。

Method: 采用张量补全方法作为统一的材料设计替代模型，利用其可解释的张量因子进行预测，并与传统机器学习方法在均匀和非均匀采样场景下进行对比实验。

Result: 经典张量方法在预测能力上可与传统机器学习相抗衡；张量因子能够重新发现物理现象，表明预测与真实物理一致；在非均匀采样场景下，专用张量方法泛化能力更强，整体R²提升最高5%，部分分布外区域误差减半。

Conclusion: 张量补全方法作为材料设计的替代模型，兼具可解释性和强预测能力，尤其在非均匀采样场景下优于传统机器学习，其张量因子可帮助实验人员发现潜在的新模式。

Abstract: When designing new materials, it is often necessary to tailor the material design to have some desired properties. As the set of design parameters grow, the search space grows exponentially, making the actual synthesis and evaluation of all material combinations virtually impossible. Even using traditional computational methods such as Finite Element Analysis becomes too computationally heavy to search the design space. Recent methods use machine learning (ML) surrogate models to more efficiently determine optimal material designs; unfortunately, these methods often (i) are notoriously difficult to interpret and (ii) under perform when the training data comes from a non-uniform sampling of the design space. We suggest the use of tensor completion methods as an all-in-one approach for interpretability and predictions. We observe classical tensor methods are able to compete with traditional ML in predictions, with the added benefit of their interpretable tensor factors (which are given completely for free, as a result of the prediction). In our experiments, we are able to rediscover physical phenomena via the tensor factors, indicating that our predictions are aligned with the true underlying physics of the problem. This also means these tensor factors could be used by experimentalists to identify potentially novel patterns, given we are able to rediscover existing ones. We also study the effects of both types of surrogate models when we encounter training data from a non-uniform sampling of the design space. We observe more specialized tensor methods that can give better generalization in these non-uniforms sampling scenarios. We find the best generalization comes from a tensor model, which is able to improve upon the baseline ML methods by up to 5% on aggregate $R^2$, and halve the error in some out of distribution regions.

</details>


### [309] [Kalman Linear Attention: Parallel Bayesian Filtering For Efficient Language Modelling and State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10743)
*Vaisakh Shaj,Cameron Barker,Aidan Scannell,Andras Szecsenyi,Elliot J. Crowley,Amos Storkey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出卡尔曼线性注意力（KLA）层，通过将卡尔曼滤波重参数化为信息形式，将序列混合转化为可并行前缀扫描的精确贝叶斯滤波，在保持线性复杂度并行计算的同时，实现了比线性SSM和门控线性注意力更强的表达能力，并在状态跟踪任务和零样本常识基准上达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的状态空间语言模型（如Mamba、GLA）虽然提供了线性复杂度和并行化的替代方案，但其线性状态更新限制了表达能力和鲁棒的状态跟踪能力。作者希望从概率角度弥合这一差距，将序列混合建模为精确的贝叶斯滤波。

Method: 将经典卡尔曼滤波重参数化为信息形式（information form），使其更新转化为可结合的扫描操作（associative scan）。尽管每个token的循环更新是非线性的（莫比乌斯/精度递归），但整体仍然保持时间维度的并行性。由此得到的KLA层作为一个即插即用的序列混合器，进行时间并行的概率推理。

Result: KLA在置换组合（$A_5$）任务上解决了线性SSM和注意力无法解决的问题，同时在保持扫描并行的同时，在合成token操作和零样本常识基准测试中达到或超越了现代SSM和GLA。

Conclusion: KLA是首批在十亿token规模下训练的堆叠贝叶斯滤波原语之一，证明了将概率推断融入序列建模架构的有效性，在表达能力与计算效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: State-space language models such as Mamba and gated linear attention (GLA) offer linear-complexity, parallelisable alternatives to transformers, but their linear state updates limit expressivity and robust state tracking. We close this gap from a probabilistic angle, casting sequence mixing as exact Bayesian filtering with the Kalman filter as the core primitive. Classical Kalman filters give principled state and uncertainty estimates but are viewed as inherently sequential; we show that reparameterising them in information form turns their updates into an associative scan - so the per-token recurrent update is non-linear (a Möbius/precision recursion) yet remains temporally parallel. The resulting Kalman Linear Attention (KLA) layer is a drop-in sequence mixer that performs time-parallel probabilistic inference, carries an explicit belief-state uncertainty, and is strictly more expressive than GLA-style linear updates at the same computational cost. This expressivity translates directly into stronger state tracking: KLA solves permutation-composition ($A_5$) tasks that linear SSMs and attention cannot, while staying scan-parallel. As a drop-in primitive it also matches or improves on modern SSMs and GLAs across synthetic token-manipulation and zero-shot commonsense benchmarks, and is among the first stacked Bayesian-filtering primitives trained at the billion-token scale.

</details>


### [310] [SpaTeoGL: Spatiotemporal Graph Learning for Interpretable Seizure Onset Zone Analysis from Intracranial EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11801)
*Elham Rostami,Aref Einizade,Taous-Meriem Laleg-Kirati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpaTeoGL，一种时空图学习框架，用于从颅内脑电（iEEG）中定位癫痫发作起始区（SOZ）。该方法联合学习窗口级空间图（捕捉电极间交互）和时间图（基于空间结构相似性链接时间窗口），在平滑图信号处理框架下求解，并在多中心数据集上展示了竞争性性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从iEEG中准确定位SOZ是癫痫手术的关键，但复杂的时空发作动态使得这一任务极具挑战性，现有方法难以兼具准确性和可解释性。

Method: 提出SpaTeoGL，一种时空图学习框架：1）联合学习窗口级空间图（刻画电极交互）和时序图（基于空间结构相似性链接窗口）；2）基于平滑图信号处理框架建模；3）采用交替块坐标下降算法求解，具有收敛保证。

Result: 在多中心iEEG数据集（成功手术结局）上，SpaTeoGL与基于水平可见性图+逻辑回归的基线方法表现相当，同时在非SOZ识别上表现更优，并能提供关于发作起始和传播动态的可解释性洞察。

Conclusion: SpaTeoGL在保持SOZ定位竞争力的同时，提升了非SOZ识别能力，并为癫痫网络分析提供了可解释的时空结构信息，具有临床辅助价值。

Abstract: Accurate localization of the seizure onset zone (SOZ) from intracranial EEG (iEEG) is essential for epilepsy surgery but is challenged by complex spatiotemporal seizure dynamics. We propose SpaTeoGL, a spatiotemporal graph learning framework for interpretable seizure network analysis. SpaTeoGL jointly learns window-level spatial graphs capturing interactions among iEEG electrodes and a temporal graph linking time windows based on similarity of their spatial structure. The method is formulated within a smooth graph signal processing framework and solved via an alternating block coordinate descent algorithm with convergence guarantees. Experiments on a multicenter iEEG dataset with successful surgical outcomes show that SpaTeoGL is competitive with a baseline based on horizontal visibility graphs and logistic regression, while improving non-SOZ identification and providing interpretable insights into seizure onset and propagation dynamics.

</details>


### [311] [Momentum LMS Theory beyond Stationarity: Stability, Tracking, and Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11995)
*Yifei Jin,Xin Zheng,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了动量最小均方（MLMS）算法在时变随机线性系统中的自适应跟踪性能，推导了其在非平稳数据流下的跟踪误差和遗憾界，并通过实验验证了其快速适应和鲁棒跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数据处理中，数据流常呈现分布漂移和时变系统参数的非平稳特性，违反了经典i.i.d.假设，亟需能实时更新且无需昂贵重训练的在线算法。MLMS算法因其计算简单性和在线处理能力而被关注。

Method: 理论推导了MLMS在时变随机线性系统中的跟踪性能和遗憾界；与经典LMS的一阶随机向量差分方程不同，MLMS因动量引入导致二阶时变随机向量差分方程，其稳定性分析依赖于更复杂的随机矩阵乘积。实验在合成和真实数据流上验证。

Result: MLMS在非平稳环境下实现快速适应和鲁棒跟踪，实验结果与理论推导一致，证明了其在现代流式和在线学习应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: MLMS作为自适应识别工具，凭借其计算简单性和在线处理能力，能有效应对非平稳数据流挑战，理论分析与实验共同验证了其优越性。

Abstract: In large-scale data processing scenarios, data often arrive in sequential streams generated by complex systems that exhibit drifting distributions and time-varying system parameters. This nonstationarity challenges theoretical analysis, as it violates classical assumptions of i.i.d. (independent and identically distributed) samples, necessitating algorithms capable of real-time updates without expensive retraining. An effective approach should process each sample in a single pass, while maintaining computational and memory complexities independent of the data stream length. Motivated by these challenges, this paper investigates the Momentum Least Mean Squares (MLMS) algorithm as an adaptive identification tool, leveraging its computational simplicity and online processing capabilities. Theoretically, we derive tracking performance and regret bounds for the MLMS in time-varying stochastic linear systems under various practical conditions. Unlike classical LMS, whose stability can be characterized by first-order random vector difference equations, MLMS introduces an additional dynamical state due to momentum, leading to second-order time-varying random vector difference equations whose stability analysis hinges on more complicated products of random matrices, which poses a substantially challenging problem to resolve. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data streams demonstrate that MLMS achieves rapid adaptation and robust tracking, in agreement with our theoretical results especially in nonstationary settings, highlighting its promise for modern streaming and online learning applications.

</details>


### [312] [Coverage Guarantees for Pseudo-Calibrated Conformal Prediction under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14913)
*Farbod Siahkali,Ashwin Verma,Vijay Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在标签条件协变量偏移下，利用伪校准（pseudo-calibration）来提升共形预测（CP）的覆盖保证，提出了一种通过松弛参数膨胀共形阈值的理论方法，并设计了基于分类器不确定性的源调优伪校准算法，实验验证了该方法能有效缓解分布偏移下的覆盖退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测在数据分布发生偏移时，其边际覆盖保证会失效，导致预测性能下降。本文旨在利用伪校准技术来抵消这种性能损失。

Method: 在标签条件协变量偏移模型下，利用领域自适应工具推导出目标覆盖的下界（与源域分类器损失和Wasserstein偏移度量相关）；据此设计通过松弛参数膨胀共形阈值的伪校准集构建方法；提出源调优伪校准算法，根据分类器不确定性在硬伪标签和随机标签之间插值。

Result: 实验结果表明，理论下界能够定性追踪伪校准行为，所提出的源调优方案能在保持预测集大小合理的前提下，有效缓解分布偏移下的覆盖退化。

Conclusion: 伪校准配合松弛阈值策略是一种应对分布偏移的有效工具，源调优算法在不显著增加预测集大小的前提下提升了目标域覆盖鲁棒性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) offers distribution-free marginal coverage guarantees under an exchangeability assumption, but these guarantees can fail if the data distribution shifts. We analyze the use of pseudo-calibration as a tool to counter this performance loss under a bounded label-conditional covariate shift model. Using tools from domain adaptation, we derive a lower bound on target coverage in terms of the source-domain loss of the classifier and a Wasserstein measure of the shift. Using this result, we provide a method to design pseudo-calibrated sets that inflate the conformal threshold by a slack parameter to keep target coverage above a prescribed level. Finally, we propose a source-tuned pseudo-calibration algorithm that interpolates between hard pseudo-labels and randomized labels as a function of classifier uncertainty. Numerical experiments show that our bounds qualitatively track pseudo-calibration behavior and that the source-tuned scheme mitigates coverage degradation under distribution shift while maintaining nontrivial prediction set sizes.

</details>


### [313] [Scaling Laws of Global Weather Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22962)
*Yuejiang Yu,Langwen Huang,Alexandru Calotoiu,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了天气预报领域中数据驱动模型的缩放定律，发现Aurora具有最强的数据缩放能力（数据量增加10倍，验证损失降低3.2倍），GraphCast参数效率最高但硬件利用率有限，计算最优分析表明固定预算下应优先增加数据量而非模型规模，且天气模型始终偏好更宽而非更深的架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化天气预报领域数据驱动模型的训练效率和模型性能，通过分析经验缩放定律来指导资源分配和架构设计。

Method: 系统分析验证损失与模型规模(N)、数据集规模(D)和计算预算(C)三者之间的关系；对比不同模型（Aurora、GraphCast等）的缩放行为；进行计算最优分析和模型形状（宽度vs深度）缩放分析。

Result: Aurora数据缩放最强（10x数据→3.2x损失降低）；GraphCast参数效率最高但硬件利用率受限；固定计算预算下增加数据量比增大模型更有效；天气模型始终偏好增加宽度而非深度。

Conclusion: 未来天气预报模型应优先采用更宽的架构和更大的有效训练数据集，以最大化预测性能。

Abstract: Data-driven models are revolutionizing weather forecasting. To optimize training efficiency and model performance, this paper analyzes empirical scaling laws within this domain. We investigate the relationship between model performance (validation loss) and three key factors: model size ($N$), dataset size ($D$), and compute budget ($C$). Across a range of models, we find that Aurora exhibits the strongest data-scaling behavior: increasing the training dataset by 10x reduces validation loss by up to 3.2x. GraphCast demonstrates the highest parameter efficiency, yet suffers from limited hardware utilization. Our compute-optimal analysis indicates that, under fixed compute budgets, allocating resources to more total training data yields greater performance gains than increasing model size. Furthermore, we analyze model shape and uncover scaling behaviors that differ fundamentally from those observed in language models: weather forecasting models consistently favor increased width over depth. These findings suggest that future weather models should prioritize wider architectures and larger effective training datasets to maximize predictive performance.

</details>


### [314] [Why Depth Matters in Parallelizable Sequence Models: A Lie Algebraic View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05573)
*Gyuryang Heo,Timothy Ngotiaoco,Kazuki Irie,Samuel J. Gershman,Bernardo L. Sabatini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从李代数控制论视角出发，研究了可扩展序列模型（如Transformer变体和结构化状态空间模型）在超出其表达能力范围时的误差边界与缩放规律，建立了模型深度与李代数扩展塔之间的对应关系，并推导出误差随深度增加呈指数衰减的结论，实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展序列模型（如Transformer和状态空间模型）通常为了序列级并行训练效率而牺牲表达能力，这导致模型在超出其表达能力范围的场景下表现未知。作者希望从理论角度量化这种权衡，解释为何深度增加能有效提升模型性能。

Method: 采用李代数控制理论视角，建立序列模型深度与李代数扩展塔之间的形式化对应关系；刻画常深度序列模型的李代数类别及其表达能力边界；推导近似误差的解析界，证明误差随深度增加呈指数衰减。

Result: 1) 建立了深度与李代数扩展塔的对应关系；2) 刻画了常深度序列模型的表达能力边界；3) 推导出近似误差随深度增加呈指数衰减的理论界；4) 在符号词和连续值状态追踪任务上验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 深度增加可以指数级降低序列模型的近似误差，这解释了可扩展序列模型在实际中表现优异的原因，为理解和改进此类模型提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Scalable sequence models, such as Transformer variants and structured state-space models, often trade expressivity power for sequence-level parallelism, which enables efficient training. Here we examine the bounds on error and how error scales when models operate outside of their expressivity regimes using a Lie-algebraic control perspective. Our theory formulates a correspondence between the depth of a sequence model and the tower of Lie algebra extensions. Echoing recent theoretical studies, we characterize the Lie-algebraic class of constant-depth sequence models and their corresponding expressivity bounds. Furthermore, we analytically derive an approximation error bound and show that error diminishes exponentially as the depth increases, consistent with the strong empirical performance of these models. We validate our theoretical predictions using experiments on symbolic word and continuous-valued state-tracking problems.

</details>


### [315] [Impact of Connectivity on Laplacian Representations in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08558)
*Tommaso Giorgi,Pierriccardo Olivieri,Keyue Jiang,Laura Toni,Matteo Papini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在强化学习中利用谱特征进行线性价值函数近似的误差上界，揭示了误差与状态图代数连通性的关系，并给出了特征向量估计误差的边界，最终建立了表示学习管道的端到端误差分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在马尔可夫决策过程中学习紧致状态表示对于解决大规模强化学习中的维度灾难至关重要。现有方法利用图拉普拉斯特征向量构造状态表示，但如何在转移图未知或状态空间过大的情况下保证近似质量仍缺乏理论保障。

Method: 通过理论分析，推导了基于学习到的谱特征进行线性价值函数近似的误差上界，展示了该误差如何随状态图的代数连通性变化，并进一步界定了特征向量估计本身引入的误差，形成了完整的端到端误差分解。同时给出了强化学习场景下拉普拉斯算子的表达式，澄清了文献中的常见误解。

Result: 证明了近似误差与状态图代数连通性之间的定量关系，建立了特征向量估计误差的边界，实现了表示学习管道的端到端误差分解。结果适用于一般（非均匀）策略，不要求诱导转移核的对称性假设。

Conclusion: 本文的理论结果为基于谱特征的表示学习方法在强化学习中的应用提供了严格的误差分析基础，并通过网格世界环境的数值模拟验证了理论发现。

Abstract: Learning compact state representations in Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) has proven crucial for addressing the curse of dimensionality in large-scale reinforcement learning (RL) problems. Existing principled approaches leverage structural priors on the MDP by constructing state representations as linear combinations of the state-graph Laplacian eigenvectors. When the transition graph is unknown or the state space is prohibitively large, the graph spectral features can be estimated directly via sample trajectories. In this work, we prove an upper bound on the approximation error of linear value function approximation under the learned spectral features. We show how this error scales with the algebraic connectivity of the state-graph, grounding the approximation quality in the topological structure of the MDP. We further bound the error introduced by the eigenvector estimation itself, leading to an end-to-end error decomposition across the representation learning pipeline. Additionally, our expression of the Laplacian operator for the RL setting, although equivalent to existing ones, prevents some common misunderstandings, of which we show some examples from the literature. Our results hold for general (non-uniform) policies without any assumptions on the symmetry of the induced transition kernel. We validate our theoretical findings with numerical simulations on gridworld environments.

</details>


### [316] [Compiler-First State Space Duality and Portable $O(1)$ Autoregressive Caching for Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09555)
*Cosmo Santoni,Anmol Thapar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于纯JAX原语的Mamba-2推理实现，无需自定义CUDA/Triton内核，通过在TPU和GPU上利用SSD递推的编译器友好结构实现了高效推理，解码速度相比全前缀重计算加速27-36倍，且精度与Triton参考实现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Mamba-2高效推理依赖于融合的CUDA和Triton自定义内核，这限制了模型在不同加速器后端之间的可移植性。作者希望实现一种无需自定义内核、可跨平台移植的高效Mamba-2推理方案。

Method: 利用状态空间对偶(SSD)递推的编译器友好结构（对角每头动力学、固定大小分块、以einsum为主的计算和静态控制流），使用标准JAX原语表达，结合注册的JAX PyTree缓存和编译的片上自回归循环，完全避免自定义内核。

Result: 在单块Google Cloud TPU v6e上，batch-1预填充达到约140 TFLOPS（15% MFU，达该场景roofline上限），缓存解码达64% HBU；4096 token上下文下，缓存解码比全前缀重计算快27-36倍（130M-2.7B参数五个检查点）；相同代码无需修改即可在NVIDIA L40S上运行；WikiText-103验证困惑度与Triton参考实现mamba_ssm v2.2.2相差±0.0005以内，隐藏状态在float32舍入误差内一致。

Conclusion: 通过利用SSD递推的编译器友好结构，可以在纯JAX原语下实现高效、可移植的Mamba-2推理，在不牺牲性能的前提下消除了对自定义内核的依赖，实现了跨TPU/GPU后端的无缝部署。

Abstract: High-throughput Mamba-2 inference is usually tied to fused CUDA and Triton kernels, limiting portability across accelerator backends. We show that the state space duality (SSD) recurrence has a compiler-friendly structure: diagonal per-head dynamics, fixed-size chunking, einsum-dominated compute, and static control flow. Expressing this structure in standard JAX primitives gives a single-source inference path with no custom kernels, a registered JAX PyTree cache, and a compiled on-device autoregressive loop. On a single Google Cloud TPU v6e, batch-1 prefill reaches approximately 140 TFLOPS, or 15% model FLOP utilisation (MFU), the roofline ceiling for this regime, and cached decode reaches up to 64% hardware bandwidth utilisation (HBU). At a 4096-token context, cached decode is 27x--36x faster than full-prefix recomputation across five Mamba-2 checkpoints from 130M to 2.7B parameters. The same source runs unmodified on NVIDIA L40S, where cached decode remains sequence-length independent across all model scales. WikiText-103 validation perplexity matches the Triton reference mamba_ssm v2.2.2 within +/-0.0005 points, and hidden states agree to float32 rounding tolerance. Code is available at https://github.com/CosmoNaught/mamba2-jax.

</details>


### [317] [Sample-Efficient Hypergradient Estimation for Decentralized Bi-Level Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14867)
*Mikoto Kudo,Takumi Tanabe,Akifumi Wachi,Youhei Akimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对双层强化学习中的去中心化设置（领导者无法干预跟随者的优化过程，仅能观察优化结果），提出基于玻尔兹曼协方差技巧的替代超梯度公式，实现从交互样本中高效估计超梯度，尤其适用于高维领导者决策空间，并首次将该方法扩展至双人马尔可夫博弈的去中心化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多战略决策问题（如仓库机器人环境设计）中，问题可自然建模为双层强化学习，但领导者无法干预跟随者的优化过程，只能观察优化结果。现有超梯度方法需要大量重复状态访问数据或复杂度随领导者决策空间维度急剧增长，亟需一种更高效的方法。

Method: 利用玻尔兹曼协方差技巧（Boltzmann covariance trick）推导出替代的超梯度公式，使得仅从交互样本中即可高效估计超梯度，无需大量重复状态访问，且能应对高维领导者决策空间。该方法还可扩展至双人马尔可夫博弈的去中心化设置。

Result: 实验验证了超梯度更新的有效性，并在离散和连续状态任务中展示了该方法的优越性能。

Conclusion: 该方法首次实现了去中心化设置下双人马尔可夫博弈的超梯度优化，为双层强化学习在环境设计等实际应用中提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Many strategic decision-making problems, such as environment design for warehouse robots, can be naturally formulated as bi-level reinforcement learning (RL), where a leader agent optimizes its objective while a follower solves a Markov decision process (MDP) conditioned on the leader's decisions. In many situations, a fundamental challenge arises when the leader cannot intervene in the follower's optimization process; it can only observe the optimization outcome. We address this decentralized setting by deriving the hypergradient of the leader's objective, i.e., the gradient of the leader's strategy that accounts for changes in the follower's optimal policy. Unlike prior hypergradient-based methods that require extensive data for repeated state visits or rely on gradient estimators whose complexity can increase substantially with the high-dimensional leader's decision space, we leverage the Boltzmann covariance trick to derive an alternative hypergradient formulation. This enables efficient hypergradient estimation solely from interaction samples, even when the leader's decision space is high-dimensional. Additionally, to our knowledge, this is the first method that enables hypergradient-based optimization for 2-player Markov games in decentralized settings. Experiments highlight the impact of hypergradient updates and demonstrate our method's effectiveness in both discrete and continuous state tasks.

</details>


### [318] [Point-Identification of a Robust Predictor Under Latent Shift with Imperfect Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15158)
*Zahra Rahiminasab,Reza Soumi,Arto Klami,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜变量等价类（LECs）概念，用更弱的跨域秩条件替代传统完备性假设，在存在不完美代理变量的域适应问题中实现鲁棒预测器的点识别，并据此设计主动学习框架PQAL来查询满足条件的多样化域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代理变量的方法在处理域分布偏移时依赖强完备性假设（即代理变量必须充分捕获潜混杂变量的所有变化）。当代理变量不完美时，该假设被破坏，导致只能识别出集合而非单一预测器。需要一种更弱假设的方法来实现点识别。

Method: 1) 提出潜变量等价类（LECs），将产生相同条件代理分布的潜混杂变量归为一类；2) 证明当多个域在混合LECs形成鲁棒预测器的方式上有足够差异时，即使不满足完备性假设，点识别依然可达；3) 将此域多样性条件形式化为跨域混合权重的秩条件；4) 提出PQAL主动学习框架，通过主动查询满足秩条件的小规模目标域集合来恢复点识别预测器。

Result: 在合成数据、半合成dSprites、IHDP和ACS Folktables数据集上，PQAL在存在域偏移时展现出鲁棒性，性能优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 域多样性条件（跨域秩条件）比传统完备性假设更弱，使得在不完美代理变量场景下也能实现点识别；PQAL框架能高效选择满足该条件的域，实现鲁棒的域适应。

Abstract: Addressing the domain adaptation problem becomes more challenging when distribution shifts across domains stem from latent confounders that affect both covariates and outcomes. Existing proxy-based approaches that address latent shift rely on a strong completeness assumption to uniquely determine (point-identify) a robust predictor. Completeness requires that proxies have sufficient information about variations in latent confounders. For imperfect proxies the mapping from confounders to the space of proxy distributions is non-injective, and multiple latent confounder values can generate the same proxy distribution. This breaks the completeness assumption and observed data are consistent with multiple potential predictors (set-identified). To address this, we introduce latent equivalent classes (LECs). LECs are defined as groups of latent confounders that induce the same conditional proxy distribution. We show that point-identification for the robust predictor remains achievable as long as multiple domains differ sufficiently in how they mix proxy-induced LECs to form the robust predictor. This domain diversity condition is formalized as a cross-domain rank condition on the mixture weights, which is substantially weaker assumption than completeness.
  We introduce the Proximal Quasi-Bayesian Active learning (PQAL) framework, which actively queries
  a small, targeted set of diverse domains that satisfy this rank condition. PQAL can recover the point-identified predictor, demonstrates robustness to varying degrees of shift and outperforms previous methods on synthetic data and semi-synthetic dSprites, IHDP, ACS Folktables datasets.

</details>


### [319] [Mechanisms of Introspective Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21396)
*Uzay Macar,Li Yang,Atticus Wang,Peter Wallich,Emmanuel Ameisen,Jack Lindsey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM对注入残差流的操控向量具有"内省意识"（即能检测到注入并识别注入概念）的机制。研究发现：该能力行为鲁棒、零误报，且仅出现在后训练阶段（偏好优化可诱发，SFT不行）；检测机制是一个两阶段电路（早期层的"证据载体"特征检测扰动，抑制下游"门控"特征的默认否定响应）；识别机制与检测部分重叠；该能力被严重欠激发（消融拒绝方向可提升53%检测率，训练偏置向量可提升75%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM为何能检测到注入残差流的操控向量并识别注入概念，揭示其背后的机制原理。

Method: 通过行为实验验证鲁棒性；对比不同训练范式（DPO vs SFT）；使用电路分析追踪检测和识别的神经机制；通过消融实验和偏置向量训练测试能力增强效果。

Result: 检测能力行为鲁棒、零误报；仅后训练阶段（偏好优化）可诱发；检测由两阶段电路实现（证据载体→门控特征）；识别机制与检测部分重叠；该能力被严重欠激发（消融拒绝方向+53%，偏置向量+75%）。

Conclusion: LLM对注入操控向量的内省意识是鲁棒且机制上非平凡的，该能力可在未来模型中被显著放大。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that LLMs can sometimes detect when steering vectors are injected into their residual stream and identify the injected concept -- a phenomenon termed "introspective awareness." We investigate the mechanisms underlying this capability in open-weights models. First, we find that it is behaviorally robust: models detect injected steering vectors at moderate rates with 0% false positives across diverse prompts and dialogue formats. Notably, this capability emerges specifically from post-training; we show that preference optimization algorithms like DPO can elicit it, but standard supervised finetuning does not. We provide evidence that detection cannot be explained by simple linear association between certain steering vectors and directions promoting affirmative responses. We trace the detection mechanism to a two-stage circuit in which "evidence carrier" features in early post-injection layers detect perturbations monotonically along diverse directions, suppressing downstream "gate" features that implement a default negative response. This circuit is absent in base models and robust to refusal ablation. Identification of injected concepts relies on largely distinct later-layer mechanisms that only weakly overlap with those involved in detection. Finally, we show that introspective capability is substantially underelicited: ablating refusal directions improves detection by +53%, and a trained bias vector improves it by +75% on held-out concepts, both without meaningfully increasing false positives. Our results suggest that this introspective awareness of injected concepts is robust and mechanistically nontrivial, and could be substantially amplified in future models. Code: https://github.com/safety-research/introspection-mechanisms.

</details>


### [320] [Estimating Tail Risks in Language Model Output Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22167)
*Rico Angell,Raghav Singhal,Zachary Horvitz,Zhou Yu,Rajesh Ranganath,Kathleen McKeown,He He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重要性采样的方法，通过构建目标模型的不安全版本来高效估计语言模型在特定输入下产生有害输出的极低概率（如10^-4量级），从而量化模型的尾部风险，弥补了现有安全评估忽略概率性质和尾部行为的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型被大规模部署，即使经过对齐训练，在数十亿次查询中仍然会出现罕见的有害输出。现有安全评估仅关注产生有害输出的输入分布，忽视了模型的概率本质和尾部输出行为，无法有效衡量这类小概率高风险事件。

Method: 提出基于重要性采样的稀有事件估计方法。不直接从目标模型中暴力采样（因有害输出极其稀少），而是通过构建目标模型的"不安全版本"（使有害输出概率增大），在这些版本上高效采样，再通过重要性权重校正回原始分布，从而用更少的样本准确估计有害输出概率。

Result: 在滥用和失调基准测试上，该方法使用500个样本即可估计10^-4量级的有害概率，且估计结果与使用10-20倍样本的暴力蒙特卡洛估计相匹配。此外，该估计能揭示模型对输入扰动的敏感性，并可预测部署风险。

Conclusion: 准确估计稀有事件对于安全评估既关键又可行，尾部风险应成为语言模型安全评估的重要组成部分。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly capable and are being rapidly deployed on a population-level scale. As a result, the safety of these models is increasingly high-stakes. Fortunately, advances in alignment have significantly reduced the likelihood of harmful model outputs. However, when models are queried billions of times in a day, even rare worst-case behaviors will occur. Current safety evaluations focus on capturing the distribution of inputs that yield harmful outputs. These evaluations disregard the probabilistic nature of models and their tail output behavior. To measure this tail risk, we propose a method to efficiently estimate the probability of harmful outputs for any input query. Instead of naive brute-force sampling from the target model, where harmful outputs could be rare, we operationalize importance sampling by creating unsafe versions of the target model. These unsafe versions enable sample-efficient estimation by making harmful outputs more probable. On benchmarks measuring misuse and misalignment, these estimates match brute-force Monte Carlo estimates using 10-20x fewer samples. For example, we can estimate probability of harmful outputs on the order of 10^-4 with just 500 samples. Additionally, we find that these harmfulness estimates can reveal the sensitivity of models to perturbations in model input and predict deployment risks. Our work demonstrates that accurate rare-event estimation is both critical and feasible for safety evaluations. Code is available at https://github.com/rangell/LMTailRisk

</details>


### [321] [Beyond Continuity: Simulation-free Reconstruction of Discrete Branching Dynamics from Single-cell Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00545)
*Junda Ying,Yuxuan Wang,Bowen Yang,Peijie Zhou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unbalanced Schrödinger Bridge (USB)，一种无需模拟的细胞轨迹推断框架，能够同时处理随机性和不平衡质量动态（如增殖与凋亡），并以单细胞分辨率建模离散的出生-死亡跳跃过程，在理论和实证上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非平衡最优传输方法将质量视为连续流体，在群体层面进行推断，无法捕捉单细胞分辨率下出生-死亡事件的离散跳跃特性，而这对于理解谱系分支和命运决定至关重要。

Method: 提出USB框架：理论上为分支薛定谔桥问题提供易处理的解，赋予单细胞同时进行布朗运动和离散出生-死亡跳跃的微观解释；技术上引入免模拟训练目标，可高效扩展至高维组学数据。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据上，USB的轨迹重建性能达到或优于确定性基线，并且唯一能够实现单细胞分辨率下出生-死亡动态的离散模拟。

Conclusion: USB为单细胞轨迹推断提供了一种兼顾随机性与非平衡动力学的有效新范式，兼具理论严谨性与实际可扩展性。

Abstract: Inferring cellular trajectories from destructive snapshots is complicated by the challenges of stochasticity and non-conservative mass dynamics such as cell proliferation and apoptosis. Existing unbalanced Optimal Transport (OT) methods treat mass as a continuous fluid, performing inference at the population level. However, this macroscopic view often fails to capture the discrete, jump-like nature of birth-death events at single-cell resolution, which is essential for understanding lineage branching and fate decisions. We present Unbalanced Schrödinger Bridge (USB), a simulation-free framework for learning underlying dynamics that effectively integrates both stochastic and unbalanced effects which also models the discrete, jump-like birth-death dynamics at single-cell resolution. Theoretically, USB provides a tractable solution to the Branching Schrödinger Bridge (BSB) problem, offering a rigorous microscopic interpretation where individual cells undergo both Brownian motion and discrete birth-death jumps. Technically, the method implements an efficient solver by introducing a simulation-free training objective that effectively scales to high-dimensional omics data. Empirically, we demonstrate on both simulated and real-world datasets that USB not only achieves trajectory reconstruction performance better than or comparable to deterministic baselines but also uniquely enables realistic discrete simulation of birth-death dynamics at single-cell resolution.

</details>


### [322] [Hybrid Iterative Neural Low-Regularity Integrator for Nonlinear Dispersive Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04853)
*Zhangyong Liang,Huanhuan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HIN-LRI混合框架，用神经网络校正低正则性数值求解器的截断误差，在保持稳定性（Lipschitz贡献为O(τ)）的同时提升精度，实验证明在色散PDE上优于传统和神经方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数值方法对非线性色散PDE的截断误差难以解析闭合，而纯神经PDE替代方法缺乏稳定性与泛化保证，因此需要一种既能利用解析结构又能通过学习补偿缺陷的混合方法。

Method: 将低正则性积分器（提供一致一阶近似）与轻量神经网络结合；网络在低维潜流形上学习残差缺陷；显式时间步缩放使神经修正的Lipschitz贡献为O(τ)；通过solver-in-the-loop目标（展开完整迭代、在Bourgain型范数下惩罚轨迹误差）进行端到端训练。

Result: 全局误差满足C(ε_net+δ)τ^γ ln(1/τ)；在三个粗糙数据色散基准上，HIN-LRI在精度上超越解析积分器、分裂方法和神经PDE代理，且具有稳定的空间细化、有效的分布外迁移和适中的在线开销。

Conclusion: HIN-LRI成功融合数值分析与学习范式，以可证明的稳定性和误差界实现了对经典方法的系统性改进，为非线性色散PDE的高精度求解提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We propose HIN-LRI, a hybrid framework that augments a classical numerical solver with a neural operator trained to correct the solver's structured truncation error. A base low-regularity integrator provides a consistent first-order approximation to nonlinear dispersive PDEs, while a lightweight neural network, operating on a low-dimensional latent manifold, learns the residual defect that analytical methods cannot close. An explicit time-step scaling on the neural correction ensures that its Lipschitz contribution remains $\mathcal{O}(τ)$, yielding a Gronwall stability factor bounded uniformly in the step size and independent of the spatial resolution. The network is trained end-to-end through a solver-in-the-loop objective that unrolls the full iteration and penalises trajectory error in a Bourgain-type norm, aligning learning with multi-step solver dynamics rather than isolated one-step targets. Under stated assumptions, the global error satisfies $C(\varepsilon_{net}+δ)\,τ^γ\ln(1/τ)$, where $\varepsilon_{net}$ measures the network approximation quality and $δ$ the training shortfall. Experiments on three dispersive benchmarks with rough data show that HIN-LRI improves accuracy over analytical integrators, splitting methods, and neural PDE surrogates, with stable spatial refinement, effective out-of-distribution transfer, and modest online overhead.

</details>


### [323] [Self-Attention as Transport: Limits of Symmetric Spectral Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04893)
*Dominik Dahlem,Diego Maniloff,Mac Misiura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于注意力路由形状（过度集中或过度分散）的幻觉诊断方法，证明谱诊断的转置不变性导致方向盲视，建立因果注意力的二分Cheeger下界，并利用容量φ与方向G双轴诊断实现极性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在生成幻觉时，注意力路由呈现两种失败模式（过度集中或过度分散），这些形状包含诊断信号。现有谱方法分析对称成分时存在方向盲视缺陷，需建立更完整的诊断框架。

Method: 1) 从强迫评分下的注意力矩阵提取诊断特征；2) 证明所有转置不变的谱诊断是方向盲视的，并用不对称系数G界定敏感性；3) 推导因果注意力的闭式二分Cheeger景观，建立容量下界；4) 构建φ-G双轴诊断空间。

Result: 1) 统一因果注意力满足n无关下界φ≥1/5，窗口注意力下界为O(w/n)；2) 双轴诊断在长度控制评估中达到0.62-0.84 LC-AUROC；3) HaluEval与MedHallu的极性反转得到验证。

Conclusion: 注意力路由的失败形状不仅是数值差异，更是结构差异；基于容量和方向的诊断框架能有效区分不同类型幻觉基准，具有可证伪的极性预测能力。

Abstract: When a language model processes a hallucinated response, its attention routing tends to fail in one of two shapes: over-concentrating on a narrow set of positions, or spreading so diffusely that relevance is diluted, and the shape of the failure carries diagnostic signal. We study these shapes as a diagnostic characterization, computed from attention matrices under \emph{forced scoring} of benchmark-labeled responses rather than during live generation. A widely used family of spectral methods analyzes the symmetric component of the degree-normalized attention operator, which governs transport \emph{capacity}; we prove that every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic of this operator is structurally \emph{orientation-blind} (it cannot distinguish an operator from its transpose, and therefore cannot detect information-flow direction), with a converse to the blindness theorem bounding any Lipschitz diagnostic's transpose sensitivity by the asymmetry coefficient $G$.
  Pairing this with a closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape for canonical causal architectures, we show that uniform causal attention satisfies an $n$-independent floor $φ\ge 1/5$, while window attention pierces the floor as $O(w/n)$; failure modes are shape-different, not just value-different. This floor is an idealized-architecture benchmark, not an empirical attractor: the fraction of real attention heads that pierce it is itself an architectural signature. The resulting two-axis diagnostic ($φ$ for capacity, $G$ for direction) yields a falsifiable polarity prediction: bottleneck- and diffuse-dominated benchmarks should exhibit opposite polarity. Under length-controlled evaluation, transport features retain interpretable signal (0.62-0.84 LC-AUROC) across the tested decoder-only, encoder-only, and encoder-decoder models, with polarity reversing as predicted between HaluEval and MedHallu.

</details>


### [324] [Understanding Sample Efficiency in Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11911)
*Gaspard Oliviers,Elene Lominadze,Rafal Bogacz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出"目标对齐"（target alignment）指标，量化比较了预测编码（PC）与反向传播（BP）的学习效率。在深度线性网络中推导并验证了目标对齐的解析表达式，证明PC比BP学习更高效，尤其在深层、窄层和预训练网络中更为明显。研究给出了PC最优目标对齐的精确条件，并通过实验验证了线性及非线性模型中的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有实验表明预测编码（PC）在样本效率和有效性上优于反向传播（BP），但对其背后机制缺乏理论理解。为了填补这一空白，本文旨在从理论上量化PC相较于BP的学习效率优势。

Method: 提出"目标对齐"（target alignment）指标来衡量网络输出变化与输出预测误差的对齐程度；在深度线性网络中推导目标对齐的解析表达式；导出PC实现最优目标对齐的精确条件；通过线性与非线性模型的完整训练轨迹进行实验验证。

Result: PC的学习效率高于BP，尤其在深层、窄层和预训练网络中差异显著；推导并实验验证了PC目标对齐的解析表达式及最优条件；即使部分假设被违反，PC的优势在实践中仍然存在。

Conclusion: 本文为PC相较于BP的学习效率优势提供了机制性理解，并可为如何最优配置PC参数以实现高效学习提供指导。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) is an influential account of cortical learning. Much of recent work has focused on comparing PC to Backpropagation (BP) to find whether PC offers any advantages. Small scale experiments show that PC enables learning that is more sample efficient and effective in many contexts, though a thorough theoretical understanding of the phenomena remains elusive. To address this, we quantify the efficiency of learning in BP and PC through a metric called ``target alignment'', which measures how closely the change in the output of the network is aligned to the output prediction error. We then derive and empirically validate analytical expressions for target alignment in Deep Linear Networks. We show that learning in PC is more efficient than BP, which is especially pronounced in deep, narrow and pre-trained networks. We also derive exact conditions for guaranteed optimal target alignment in PC and validate our findings through experiments. We study full training trajectories of linear and non-linear models, and find the predicted benefits of PC persist in practice even when some assumptions are violated. Overall, this work provides a mechanistic understanding of the higher learning efficiency observed for PC over BP in previous works, and can guide how PC should be parametrised to learn most effectively.

</details>


### [325] [TAPIOCA: Why Task- Aware Pruning Improves OOD model Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14738)
*Krish Sharma,Omar Naim,Soumadeep Saha,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Nicholas Asher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了任务感知层剪枝（如TALE方法）在何种条件下提升模型性能及其原因。实验表明，该方法对分布内数据无益，但能稳定提升分布外数据的准确率。通过几何分析发现，分布外输入会扭曲模型的任务适应几何结构，而剪枝消除这些扭曲层，使分布外表示与任务适应几何对齐，从而提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究任务感知层剪枝（如TALE）在什么情况下能提升模型性能，以及其背后的原因机制。

Method: 通过多项式回归和大语言模型上的控制实验，分析分布内和分布外数据下的剪枝效果；利用层间范数和成对距离剖面刻画表示几何，并通过分布偏移控制和残差缩放干预进行因果验证。

Result: 任务感知层剪枝对分布内数据无提升，但能稳定提升分布外准确率；分布外输入会扭曲任务适应几何（表现为层间范数和距离剖面偏离分布内模式），剪枝通过移除造成扭曲的层来修正这种偏离。

Conclusion: 任务感知层剪枝通过消除造成几何扭曲的层，使分布外输入重新对齐到任务适应的表示几何，从而提升分布外性能，且该行为在不同模型规模下一致。

Abstract: Recent work has promoted task-aware layer pruning as a way to improve model performance on particular tasks, as shown by TALE. In this paper, we investigate when such improvements occur and why. We show first that, across controlled polynomial regression tasks and large language models, such pruning yields no benefit on in-distribution (ID) data but consistently improves out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy. We further show empirically that OOD inputs induce layerwise norm and pairwise-distance profiles that deviate from the corresponding ID profiles. This leads to a geometric explanation of task-aware pruning: each task induces a task-adapted geometry, characterized empirically by the representation profiles observed on ID inputs. OOD inputs can introduce a distorted version of the task-adapted geometry. Task-aware pruning identifies layers that create or amplify this distortion; by removing them, it shifts OOD representational norms and pairwise distances toward those observed on the adapted distribution. This realigns OOD inputs with the model's task-adapted geometry and improves performance. We provide causal evidence through controlled distribution shifts and residual-scaling interventions, and demonstrate consistent behavior across model scales.

</details>


### [326] [On the Stability of Growth in Structural Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15435)
*Lute Lillo,Nick Cheney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度学习中网络生长（Grow）与剪枝（Prune）的非对称性，发现新生单元存在"前向激活但反向饥饿"的问题，即在前向传播中参与计算但反向传播中梯度信号显著弱于已有单元。在复杂图像分类任务中，Grow在结构编辑过程中能达到高最终精度，但Prune在平均训练轨迹性能或从头重新训练时表现更强。改善新生单元的整合能提升自适应性能，但不必然产生更好的最终子网络。在持续学习中，Grow仅在新生单元有足够时间整合时才具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示网络生长（Grow）并非剪枝（Prune）的简单逆操作，关键在于新生单元插入已专业化优化的轨迹时面临梯度信号不足的"反向饥饿"问题。

Method: 通过对比Grow与Prune在小型MLP和复杂CNN图像分类任务中的表现，分析插入问题；通过干预优化器状态、插入策略、选择机制和可训练性等手段，研究改善新生单元整合的效果。

Result: Grow在结构编辑过程中可达到高最终精度，但Prune在平均训练轨迹性能和从头重新训练时更强；改善新生单元整合可提升自适应性能但不必然产生更好的最终子网络；在持续学习中，Grow仅在新单元有足够整合时间时才具有竞争力。

Conclusion: Grow不应仅被视为架构搜索算子，而应作为一个对时间敏感的优化过程，其成功取决于插入稳定性。

Abstract: Standard deep-learning pipelines usually choose the network architecture before training and keep it fixed throughout optimization. In contrast, a model can also be adapted by editing its structure during training, for example by pruning existing hidden-neuron units or growing new ones. Although growth is appealing for adaptive and continual systems, we show that it is not simply the inverse of pruning. Pruning selects among units that have participated in training from the start, whereas growth inserts new units into an already specialized optimization trajectory.
