Ultimately, our results show that naively scaling test-time compute is wasteful, and that DIRECT can provide frontier-level embodied planning in robotic systems at a fraction of the cost. Project page can be found at jadee-dao.github.io/direct/.

</details>


### [397] [World Pilot: Steering Vision-Language-Action Models with World-Action Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12403)
*Zefu Lin,Rongxu Cui,Junjia Xu,Xiaojuan Jin,Wenling Li,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: World Pilot提出了一种VLA框架，通过引入World-Action Model的两种先验（Latent Steering和Action Steering），将场景演化潜力和运动轨迹先验注入决策链，显著提升了机器人在分布外操作任务中的泛化能力，在LIBERO-Plus零样本OOD基准上达到84.7%的总成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽从大规模预训练中继承了语义基础，但该基础建立在静态图文对上，无法捕捉操作任务的连续、高接触动力学特性，导致在分布外场景下泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出World Pilot框架，在VLA中引入World-Action Model（WAM）的两条互补路径：Latent Steering通过场景演化潜变量条件化感知层，Action Steering为动作生成器提供预期轨迹作为运动先验，两者共同为VLA提供场景演化预期和轨迹级运动提示。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus零样本OOD基准上达到84.7%的总成功率，SOTA；在四项真实机器人操作任务中全部取得最高成功率，在视角、几何、可变形状态和位姿偏移下优势最为显著。

Conclusion: 通过向VLA注入World-Action Model的场景演化先验和运动轨迹先验，能有效弥补静态预训练与动态操作之间的鸿沟，显著提升操作策略的泛化能力，且场景演化先验即使由未经过动作后训练的视频预训练世界模型提供仍然有效。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models inherit semantic grounding from large-scale pretraining and perform competently across in-distribution manipulation tasks. This grounding, however, is built on static image-text pairs, whereas manipulation is a continuous, contact-rich process whose dynamics this pretraining cannot capture. We present World Pilot, a VLA framework that augments the policy with priors from a World-Action Model (WAM), routed into the decision chain through two complementary pathways. Latent Steering conditions the perception layer on a scene-evolution latent, and Action Steering supplies an anticipated trajectory as a motion prior to the action generator. Together the two priors equip the VLA with an anticipated view of the scene and a trajectory-level motion hint alongside its semantic conditioning, and the scene-evolution prior remains effective even when supplied by a video-pretrained world model that has not been action-post-trained. World Pilot attains a state-of-the-art Total success rate of 84.7% on the LIBERO-Plus zero-shot OOD benchmark and the highest success rate on every real-robot setting across four manipulation tasks, with the largest margins under shifts in viewpoint, geometry, deformable state, and pose. Project Website: https://world-pilot.github.io/

</details>


### [398] [FACTR 2: Learning External Force Sensing for Commodity Robot Arms Improves Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12406)
*Steven Oh,Jason Jingzhou Liu,Tony Tao,Philip Han,Kenneth Shaw,Satoshi Funabashi,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Deepak Pathak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NEXT是一种无需力传感器即可估计外关节扭矩的数据驱动方法，只需10分钟自由运动数据训练1分钟即可达到专用传感器精度；结合FIRST方法（过采样预接触和接触段的行为克隆策略），在5项长期任务中任务进度提升超过17%，实现了低成本机器人的力感知遥操作和策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要力感知能力，但专用力传感器成本高昂，许多机器人手臂缺乏力传感器，亟需低成本、无需额外硬件的力估计方法。

Method: 提出NEXT（神经外部扭矩估计）数据驱动方法，仅需10分钟自由运动数据训练1分钟即可估计外关节扭矩；并提出FIRST（力信息重采样训练），通过过采样预接触和接触段来改进行为克隆中的策略学习。

Result: NEXT的扭矩估计精度可与专用关节扭矩传感器媲美；FIRST在5项长期任务中相比先前力感知策略在任务进度上提升超过17%。

Conclusion: NEXT和FIRST使现成机器人无需额外传感硬件即可实现力感知遥操作和策略学习，显著降低了力感知操作的门槛。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires force sensitivity, but many robot arms lack dedicated force sensors due to their high cost. We present Neural External Torque Estimation (NEXT), a data-driven method that estimates external joint torques without needing any dedicated force sensors. NEXT trains in 1 minute from only 10 minutes of free-motion data, yet achieves estimates comparable to dedicated joint-torque sensors. NEXT enables force-feedback teleoperation on low-cost arms and improves policy learning through Force-Informed Re-Sampling Training (FIRST), which up-samples pre-contact and contact segments during behavior cloning. Across five long-horizon tasks, FIRST outperforms prior force-aware policies by over 17% in task progress. Together, NEXT and FIRST bring force-aware teleoperation and policy learning to off-the-shelf robots without additional sensing hardware. Video results and code are available at https://jasonjzliu.com/factr2

</details>


### [399] [Non-Equilibrium MAV-Capture-MAV via Time-Optimal Planning and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06578)
*Canlun Zheng,Zhanyu Guo,Zikang Yin,Chunyu Wang,Zhikun Wang,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用小型高机动性捕获MAV（微型飞行器）捕捉高机动性目标的问题。研究中采用时间最优规划（TOP）和强化学习（RL）两种方法，TOP提供高机动性和短轨迹，RL在实时适应性和稳定性方面表现更优，并且RL方法在实际场景中成功实现了目标捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有捕获MAV的策略相对简单且受限于平台性能，缺乏应对高机动性目标和不稳定条件下的有效控制策略，本文旨在解决这一不足。

Method: 从较大MAV平台转向专门设计的紧凑型捕获MAV，配备定制发射装置，保持高机动性；分别采用时间最优规划（TOP）和强化学习（RL）两种控制方法进行仿真和实际测试。

Result: 仿真结果表明TOP能生成高机动性的更短轨迹，而RL在实时适应性和稳定性方面更优；RL方法在实际场景中成功实现了不稳定状态下的目标捕获。

Conclusion: 针对高机动性目标和不稳定捕获条件，TOP和RL各有优势，其中RL方法在实际应用中展现了可行性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The capture of flying MAVs (micro aerial vehicles) has garnered increasing research attention due to its intriguing challenges and promising applications. Despite recent advancements, a key limitation of existing work is that capture strategies are often relatively simple and constrained by platform performance. This paper addresses control strategies capable of capturing high-maneuverability targets. The unique challenge of achieving target capture under unstable conditions distinguishes this task from traditional pursuit-evasion and guidance problems. In this study, we transition from larger MAV platforms to a specially designed, compact capture MAV equipped with a custom launching device while maintaining high maneuverability. We explore both time-optimal planning (TOP) and reinforcement learning (RL) methods. Simulations demonstrate that TOP offers highly maneuverable and shorter trajectories, while RL excels in real-time adaptability and stability. Moreover, the RL method has been tested in real-world scenarios, successfully achieving target capture even in unstable states.

</details>


### [400] [iPack: Intuitive Bin Packing with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08445)
*Yannik Blei,Michael Krawez,Adrian Göß,Devadas Vijayan Sheela,Tobias Jülg,Pierre Krack,Florian Walter,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LLM-Pack是一种利用语言和视觉基础模型为杂货打包生成合理顺序的新方法，无需专门训练即可处理新商品，有效防止商品损坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人和自动化技术在物流领域虽有应用，但杂货打包任务尚未得到充分研究。正确的打包顺序对防止商品损坏至关重要（如重物不应放在易碎品上），然而由于商品种类繁多，定义精确的打包顺序标准非常困难。

Method: LLM-Pack方法利用语言和视觉基础模型来识别杂货商品，并生成模仿人类打包策略的打包顺序。该方法无需专门训练即可处理新商品，且其模块化设计允许轻松升级底层基础模型。

Result: 通过广泛实验评估，LLM-Pack展示了良好的性能表现。代码将在论文发表后公开。

Conclusion: LLM-Pack为杂货打包任务提供了一种有效且灵活的新方案，克服了传统方法难以定义打包顺序标准的困难，具有良好的泛化能力和可扩展性。

Abstract: Robotics and automation are increasingly influential in logistics but remain largely confined to traditional warehouses. In grocery retail, advancements such as cashier-less supermarkets exist, yet customers still manually pick and pack groceries. While there has been a substantial focus in robotics on the bin picking problem, the task of packing objects and groceries has remained largely untouched. However, packing grocery items in the right order is crucial for preventing product damage, e.g., heavy objects should not be placed on top of fragile ones. However, the exact criteria for the right packing order are hard to define, in particular given the huge variety of objects typically found in stores. In this paper, we introduce LLM-Pack, a novel approach for grocery packing. LLM-Pack leverages language and vision foundation models for identifying groceries and generating a packing sequence that mimics human packing strategy. LLM-Pack does not require dedicated training to handle new grocery items and its modularity allows easy upgrades of the underlying foundation models. We extensively evaluate our approach to demonstrate its performance. We will make the source code of LLMPack publicly available upon the publication of this manuscript.

</details>


### [401] [SR-LIO++: LiDAR-Inertial Odometry and Quantized Mapping with Caching-Aware Sweep Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22926)
*Zikang Yuan,Ruiye Ming,Chengwei Zhao,Yonghao Tan,Pingcheng Dong,Yuan Ren,Yuzhong Jiao,Xin Yang,Kwang-Ting Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SR-LIO++ 是一种创新的 LiDAR-惯性里程计系统，能够在资源受限平台（如 Raspberry Pi 4B）上实现相对于输入频率两倍的输出频率。该系统通过扫描重建、中间结果缓存机制和基于索引表映射的量化地图点管理，显著提升了计算效率和内存利用率，在保持最先进精度的同时大幅增强效率，并在 Raspberry Pi 4B 上实现了 20 Hz 状态输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 3D LiDAR 固有低采集频率问题，实现高频输出，并克服在资源受限平台上部署频率增强型 LIO 系统所面临的实时性挑战。

Method: 1) 采用扫描重建方法提升 LiDAR 扫描频率，生成高频重建扫描；2) 提出最近分段的中间结果（表面参数）缓存机制，避免相邻重建扫描中公共分段的冗余处理，将处理时间与重建扫描频率的解耦；3) 提出基于索引表映射的量化地图点管理，将全局 3D 点存储从 64 位双精度转换为 8 位字符表示，并将最近邻搜索中的欧氏距离计算从 64 位双精度转换为 16 位短整型和 32 位整数格式。

Result: 在三个不同计算平台和四个公开数据集上的广泛实验表明，SR-LIO++ 在保持最先进精度的同时大幅提升了效率；在 Raspberry Pi 4B 上成功实现了 20 Hz 状态输出。

Conclusion: SR-LIO++ 通过创新的扫描重建、缓存机制和量化地图管理，有效解决了低频率 LiDAR 在资源受限平台上的高频输出问题，实现了精度与效率的平衡。

Abstract: Addressing the inherent low acquisition frequency limitation of 3D LiDAR to achieve high-frequency output has become a critical research focus in the LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (LIO) domain. To ensure real-time performance, frequency-enhanced LIO systems must process each sweep within significantly reduced timeframe, which presents substantial challenges for deployment on resource-constrained platforms. To address these limitations, we introduce SR-LIO++, an innovative LIO system capable of achieving doubled output frequency relative to input frequency on resource-constrained hardware platforms, including the Raspberry Pi 4B. Our system employs the previously proposed sweep reconstruction methodology to enhance LiDAR sweep frequency, generating high-frequency reconstructed sweeps. Building upon this foundation, we propose a caching mechanism for intermediate results (i.e., surface parameters) of the most recent segments, effectively minimizing redundant processing of common segments in adjacent reconstructed sweeps. This method decouples processing time from the traditionally linear dependence on reconstructed sweep frequency. Furthermore, we present a quantized map point management based on index table mapping, significantly reducing memory usage by converting global 3D point storage from 64-bit double precision to 8-bit char representation. This method also converts the computationally intensive Euclidean distance calculations in nearest neighbor searches from 64-bit double precision to 16-bit short and 32-bit integer formats, reducing computational cost. Extensive experimental evaluations across three distinct computing platforms and four public datasets demonstrate that SR-LIO++ maintains state-of-the-art accuracy while substantially enhancing efficiency. Notably, our system successfully achieves 20 Hz state output on Raspberry Pi 4B hardware.

</details>


### [402] [The Unreasonable Effectiveness of Discrete-Time Gaussian Process Mixtures for Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03296)
*Jan Ole von Hartz,Adrian Röfer,Joschka Boedecker,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiDiGap是一种基于离散时间高斯过程混合模型的模仿学习方法，仅需5次示范和摄像头观测即可学习机器人操作任务，在CPU上分钟级完成训练，支持推理时通过碰撞信号和运动学约束进行策略引导，在少样本操作基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人模仿学习方法通常需要大量演示数据或昂贵的计算资源，且难以在长时域任务、高约束运动、动态动作和多模态任务上同时取得良好表现，缺乏灵活的推理时可控性。

Method: 提出Mixture of Discrete-time Gaussian Processes (MiDiGap)，使用离散时间高斯过程混合模型实现策略表示，仅需摄像头观测和少量演示（最少5次），在CPU上线性扩展至大规模数据集；开发推理时引导工具集，利用碰撞信号和机器人运动学约束进行策略调整。

Result: 在约束RLBench任务上，策略成功率提升76个百分点，轨迹代价降低67%；在多模态任务上，策略成功率提升48个百分点，样本效率提升20倍；在跨本体迁移中，策略成功率翻倍以上。

Conclusion: MiDiGap是一种高效、灵活、可扩展的机器人操作模仿学习方法，在少样本设置下实现了强泛化能力和SOTA性能，并支持障碍物规避和跨本体策略迁移等新颖泛化能力。

Abstract: We present Mixture of Discrete-time Gaussian Processes (MiDiGap), a novel approach for flexible policy representation and imitation learning in robot manipulation. MiDiGap enables learning from as few as five demonstrations using only camera observations and generalizes across a wide range of challenging tasks. It excels at long-horizon behaviors such as making coffee, highly constrained motions such as opening doors, dynamic actions such as scooping with a spatula, and multimodal tasks such as hanging a mug. MiDiGap learns these tasks on a CPU in less than a minute and scales linearly to large datasets. We also develop a rich suite of tools for inference-time steering using evidence such as collision signals and robot kinematic constraints. This steering enables novel generalization capabilities, including obstacle avoidance and cross-embodiment policy transfer. MiDiGap achieves state-of-the-art performance on diverse few-shot manipulation benchmarks. On constrained RLBench tasks, it improves policy success by 76 percentage points and reduces trajectory cost by 67%. On multimodal tasks, it improves policy success by 48 percentage points and increases sample efficiency by a factor of 20. In cross-embodiment transfer, it more than doubles policy success. We make the code publicly available at https://midigap.cs.uni-freiburg.de.

</details>


### [403] [LEMON-Mapping: Loop-Enhanced Large-Scale Multi-Session Point Cloud Merging and Optimization for Globally Consistent Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10018)
*Lijie Wang,Xiaoyi Zhong,Ziyi Xu,Kaixin Chai,Anke Zhao,Tianyu Zhao,Changjian Jiang,Qianhao Wang,Xieyuanli Chen,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LEMON-Mapping框架，通过创新的回环处理、空间光束法平差和位姿图优化，解决多机器人建图中重叠区域的发散与模糊问题，实现高精度全局一致的大规模点云融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多机器人位姿图优化方法忽略地图几何结构，仅将回环作为位姿节点间的约束，导致重叠区域出现发散和模糊，缺乏全局一致的高精度地图构建能力。

Method: 1) 鲁棒回环处理机制（异常值剔除+回环召回策略）；2) 面向多机器人地图的空间光束法平差（减少发散、消除重叠模糊）；3) 基于位姿图优化的方法，利用精化后的光束法平差约束将局部精度传播至全局地图。

Result: 在多个公开数据集及自采集数据集上，相比传统融合方法，LEMON-Mapping实现了更优的建图精度和全局一致性；可扩展性实验证明其能有效处理包含大量机器人的场景。

Conclusion: LEMON-Mapping通过重新审视回环在多机器人建图中的角色，结合空间光束法平差与位姿图优化，显著提升了大规模多机器人点云融合的精度和一致性。

Abstract: Multi-robot collaboration is becoming increasingly critical and presents significant challenges in modern robotics, especially for building a globally consistent, accurate map. Traditional multi-robot pose graph optimization (PGO) methods ensure basic global consistency but ignore the geometric structure of the map, and only use loop closures as constraints between pose nodes, leading to divergence and blurring in overlapping regions. To address this issue, we propose LEMON-Mapping, a loop-enhanced framework for large-scale, multi-session point cloud fusion and optimization. We re-examine the role of loops for multi-robot mapping and introduce three key innovations. First, we develop a robust loop processing mechanism that rejects outliers and a loop recall strategy to recover mistakenly removed but valid loops. Second, we introduce spatial bundle adjustment for multi-robot maps, reducing divergence and eliminating blurring in overlaps. Third, we design a PGO-based approach that leverages refined bundle adjustment constraints to propagate local accuracy to the entire map. We validate LEMON-Mapping on several public datasets and a self-collected dataset. The experimental results show superior mapping accuracy and global consistency of our framework compared to traditional merging methods. Scalability experiments also demonstrate its strong capability to handle scenarios involving numerous robots.

</details>


### [404] [CU-Multi: A Dataset for Multi-Robot Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19463)
*Doncey Albin,Daniel McGann,Miles Mena,Annika Thomas,Harel Biggie,Xuefei Sun,Steve McGuire,Jonathan P. How,Christoffer Heckman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CU-Multi是一个多日、多地点收集的多机器人协作数据集，包含同步RGB-D、RTK GPS、语义LiDAR和精确地面实况里程计，专为可重复的多机器人协作感知评估而设计，克服了现有数据集轨迹短、重叠少、闭环稀疏的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作SLAM评估缺乏专用多机器人数据集，常依赖单机器人轨迹分割，缺乏标准化且难以跨研究比较；现有多机器人数据集存在轨迹短、机器人间重叠有限、机器人内闭环稀疏等问题。

Method: 在科罗拉多大学博尔德校区两个大型户外场地收集多天数据，包含四次同步运行（对齐起始时间、控制轨迹重叠），提供RGB-D、RTK GPS、语义LiDAR和精炼地面实况里程计，并包含密集语义标注。

Result: CU-Multi数据集提供了重叠变化与密集语义标注相结合的标准化多机器人协作感知评估基础。

Conclusion: CU-Multi为多机器人协作感知任务的可重复评估提供了强有力基础，弥补了现有数据集在轨迹长度、重叠程度和标准化方面的不足。

Abstract: A central challenge for multi-robot systems is fusing independently gathered perception data into a unified representation. Despite progress in Collaborative SLAM (C-SLAM), benchmarking remains hindered by the scarcity of dedicated multi-robot datasets. Many evaluations instead partition single-robot trajectories, a practice that may only partially reflect true multi-robot operations and, more critically, lacks standardization, leading to results that are difficult to interpret or compare across studies. While several multi-robot datasets have recently been introduced, they mostly contain short trajectories with limited inter-robot overlap and sparse intra-robot loop closures. To overcome these limitations, we introduce CU-Multi, a dataset collected over multiple days at two large outdoor sites on the University of Colorado Boulder campus. CU-Multi comprises four synchronized runs with aligned start times and controlled trajectory overlap, replicating the distinct perspectives of a robot team. It includes RGB-D sensing, RTK GPS, semantic LiDAR, and refined ground-truth odometry. By combining overlap variation with dense semantic annotations, CU-Multi provides a strong foundation for reproducible evaluation in multi-robot collaborative perception tasks.

</details>


### [405] [Learning Ordinal Response Policies in Rank-Based Stochastic Prize-Collecting Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24515)
*Malintha Fernando,Petter Ögren,Silun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出随机带奖励收集定向游戏（SPCOG），将团队定向问题（TOP）扩展至自利智能体竞争场景。理论证明完全图和星图下存在唯一纯纳什均衡，并提出序数排名（OR）作为局部信息表征。实验表明OR条件化策略在参数共享和泛化上优于全局排名策略，同时提出虚构序数响应学习（FORL）算法实现独立学习场景下的收敛策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TOP方法假设所有智能体合作追求单一目标，无法扩展到智能体在稀缺奖励环境中相互竞争的场景，需要一种能处理自利智能体在图上规划的新框架。

Method: 1) 提出SPCOG框架，扩展TOP至自利智能体、能量约束和随机转移场景；2) 理论上证明完全图和星图下存在唯一纯纳什均衡；3) 提出序数排名（OR）概念作为局部拓扑邻域内的全局排名表征；4) 提出FORL算法用于独立学习场景下的策略收敛。

Result: 在真实路网图上，动态和静态奖励分布下：OR条件化局部信息策略优于全局信息策略；OR条件化策略在大量智能体场景下泛化能力更强。

Conclusion: OR作为多智能体图上博弈的强归纳偏置，能有效提升策略性能与泛化能力；FORL算法可解决独立学习场景中的收敛问题。

Abstract: The Team Orienteering Problem (TOP) generalizes many real-world multi-agent scheduling and routing tasks that occur in autonomous mobility, aerial logistics, and surveillance applications. While many flavors of the TOP exist for planning in multi-agent systems, they assume that all the agents cooperate toward a single objective; therefore, they do not extend to settings when they compete in reward-scarce environments. We propose Stochastic Prize-Collecting Orienteering Games (SPCOG) as an extension of the TOP to plan in the presence of self-interested agents operating on a graph, under energy constraints and stochastic transitions. A theoretical discussion on complete and star graphs establishes that there is a unique pure Nash equilibrium in SPCOGs that coincides with the optimal routing solution of an equivalent TOP under rank-based conflict resolution. We propose the concept of Ordinal Rank (OR) as a concise representation of an agents' global rank and its location within a topological, well-defined neighborhood. Empirical evaluations conducted on real-world, road-network graphs under both dynamic and stationary prize distributions show that in parameter-sharing settings, the policies that leverage local information can outperform those policies leverage global information when the former is conditioned on the OR rather than the global rank, indicating that the OR acts as a strong inductive bias in multi-agent games on graphs. The OR-conditioned policies also generalize much better to games with large number of agents compared to global-rank conditioned policies. Finally, we also propose we propose Fictitious Ordinal Response Learning (FORL) as an entropy-regulated algorithm to obtain convergent policies in independent-learning settings in prize-collecting games on graphs.

</details>


### [406] [SIL: Symbiotic Interactive Learning for Language-Conditioned Human-Agent Co-Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05203)
*Linus Nwankwo,Bjoern Ellensohn,Christian Rauch,Elmar Rueckert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出共生交互学习（SIL）框架，通过共享潜在任务空间中的双向共适应机制，使人类与具身智能体实现协同推理与规划，在指令跟随、信息检索等任务中达到90.4%完成率，信念对齐评分约0.83，较最佳消融实验提升约20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机交互框架大多采用单向的主从模式，智能体被动执行指令，缺乏人类日常交互中存在的共适应、多轮交互特性，无法实现双向学习和任务协作。

Method: 提出SIL框架，在共享潜在任务空间中实现双向共适应：1) 利用基础模型进行空间感知与推理；2) 采用三元组损失训练的神经编码器将FM输出映射为任务特定潜在表示；3) 引入情景记忆和语义记忆架构，通过弹性权重巩固（EWC）正则化缓解灾难性遗忘；4) 人类与智能体维护随交互历史演化的联合信念状态，支持主动澄清、适应性建议和共享计划优化。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的具身任务（指令跟随、信息检索、查询导向推理、交互式对话）中，任务完成率达90.4%，信念对齐评分ρ≈0.83，较最佳消融实验绝对提升约20个百分点。

Conclusion: SIL框架通过双向共适应机制有效弥合了当前人机交互与人类日常交互之间的鸿沟，显著提升了具身智能体的协作能力和任务完成质量。

Abstract: Today's autonomous agents, largely driven by foundation models (FMs), can understand natural language instructions and solve long-horizon tasks with human-like reasoning. However, current human-robot interaction frameworks largely follow a one-way master-apprentice technique where the embodied agent passively executes commands without reciprocal learning. This neglects the co-adaptive, multi-turn nature of everyday human-to-human interactions. We introduce symbiotic interactive learning (SIL), a bidirectional co-adaptation framework in a shared latent task space, where both the human and the agent maintain joint belief states that evolve with the interaction history. This enables proactive clarification, adaptive suggestions, and shared plan refinement. SIL leverages FMs for spatial perception and reasoning, together with a triplet-loss-trained neural encoder that grounds the FMs' outputs into task-specific latent representations. To support long-term stability as tasks evolve, SIL utilises episodic and semantic memory architectures, regularised via elastic weight consolidation to mitigate catastrophic forgetting. We evaluate SIL on simulated and real-world embodied tasks, including instruction following, information retrieval, query-oriented reasoning, and interactive dialogue, achieving a $90.4\%$ task completion rate and a belief alignment score of $ρ\approx 0.83$, an absolute improvement of about $20$ percentage points over the best ablations. Demos and resources: https://linusnep.github.io/SIL/.

</details>


### [407] [Phase-Based Multi-Gait Learning for a Salamander-Like Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.08299)
*Zhiang Liu,Yang Liu,Yongchun Fang,Xian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于相位变量的学习框架，使蝾螈机器人无需参考运动即可自主习得22种多样化步态，通过相位覆盖奖励和形态对称数据增强实现高效学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制器无法充分利用蝾螈机器人的形态特征，依赖预定义模式或关节轨迹，导致步态多样性不足，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出基于相位变量的学习框架：每个身体部位由可前向/反向演化的相位变量控制；引入相位覆盖奖励促进腿部相位空间探索；通过数据增强融入形态对称性，提升样本效率并强制运动级和任务级对称。

Result: 机器人成功习得22种代表性步态，展现出动态性和对称性运动。

Conclusion: 该相位学习框架能有效利用形态特征，无需参考运动即可生成多样灵活步态，证明了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Salamander-like robots are designed inspired by the skeletal structure of their biological counterparts. However, existing controllers cannot fully exploit these morphological features and largely rely on predefined patterns or joint trajectories, which prevents the generation of diverse and flexible gaits and limits their applicability in real-world scenarios. In this paper, we propose a phase-based learning framework that enables the robot to acquire a diverse repertoire of gaits without using reference motions. Each body part is controlled by a phase variable capable of forward and backward evolution, with a phase coverage reward to promote the exploration of the leg phase space. Additionally, morphological symmetry of the robot is incorporated via data augmentation, improving sample efficiency and enforcing both motion-level and task-level symmetry in learned behaviors. Extensive experiments show that the robot successfully acquires 22 representative gaits exhibiting both dynamic and symmetric movements, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed learning framework.

</details>


### [408] [PIGEON: VLM-Driven Object Navigation via Points of Interest Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13207)
*Cheng Peng,Zhenzhe Zhang,Xiaobao Wei,Yanhao Zhang,Heng Wang,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Cheng Chi,Shanghang Zhang,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PIGEON是一个基于VLM的零样本目标导航框架，通过稀疏视觉决策单元（PoI）将高层语义推理与原始视觉观测相结合，在Habitat ObjectNav基准上达到SOTA，并成功迁移到真实机器人和Active EQA任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM导航方法面临两难：密集VLM推理开销大，而将环境抽象为符号记忆则割裂了高层推理与原始视觉证据的联系。

Method: 提出PoI（兴趣点）作为稀疏视觉决策单元，将几何上可执行的航路点与原始自我中心观测耦合；VLM仅在任务关键PoI（探索前沿、疑似目标、楼梯、楼层摘要）间进行选择，低层级规划器执行连续运动；并引入RLVR流水线，无需人工CoT标注即可改进本地VLM。

Result: 在Habitat ObjectNav基准上达到零样本SOTA；随基础模型容量一致扩展；仅修改prompt即可迁移至Active EQA；物理机器人真实部署验证了鲁棒性和效率。

Conclusion: PoI接口使高层导航决策可验证，RLVR流水线能有效提升本地VLM性能，证明了稀疏化VLM推理在具身导航中的有效性。

Abstract: Object navigation in unseen indoor environments requires agents to perform semantic search under partial observability. Vision-language models (VLMs) provide strong semantic-spatial priors for this task, but how to interface them with robot navigation remains challenging: dense VLM inference is expensive, while abstracting environments into symbolic memories often separates high-level reasoning from the raw visual evidence that supports it. We propose we propose PIGEON (Point of Interest Guided Exploration for Object Navigation), a VLM-driven framework that formulates object navigation as raw-observation-grounded sparse decision problem. PIGEON introduces Points of Interest (PoIs) as sparse visual decision units that couple geometrically executable waypoints with raw egocentric observations. Rather than using VLMs as dense controllers or restricting them to frontier ranking, PIGEON enables VLMs to select among task-critical PoIs, including exploration frontiers, suspected target objects, traversable stairs, and floor-level summaries, while low-level planners execute continuous motion between them. This PoI interface further makes high-level navigation decisions verifiable, allowing us to develop an RLVR pipeline that improves local VLMs without manual Chain-of-Thought annotations. Extensive experiments on Habitat ObjectNav benchmarks show that PIGEON achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance, scales consistently with foundation model capacity, and transfers to Active Embodied Question Answering with only prompt modifications. Real-world deployments on physical robots further demonstrate its robustness and efficiency.

</details>


### [409] [Self-Supervised Multisensory Pretraining for Contact-Rich Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14427)
*Rickmer Krohn,Vignesh Prasad,Gabriele Tiboni,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiSensory Dynamic Pretraining (MSDP)框架，通过掩码自编码和跨模态预测学习多感官表示，并利用非对称actor-critic架构实现鲁棒的下游策略学习，在接触式机器人操作任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触式机器人操作需要协同利用视觉、力和本体感觉等多模态感知信息，但强化学习智能体在多感官环境中难以有效学习，尤其是在存在传感器噪声和动态变化的情况下。

Method: MSDP基于掩码自编码，训练Transformer编码器从部分传感器嵌入重构完整多感官观测，实现跨模态预测与传感器融合。下游策略采用非对称架构：critic通过交叉注意力从冻结嵌入中提取动态任务特征，actor使用稳定的池化表示指导动作。

Result: 在多个接触式机器人操作任务中，MSDP展现出加速学习和鲁棒性能，在真实机器人上仅需6000次在线交互即可达到高成功率，对传感器噪声和目标动力学变化具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MSDP为复杂多感官机器人控制提供了一种简单而强大的解决方案，有效实现了多模态表示学习与任务导向策略学习的结合。

Abstract: Effective contact-rich manipulation requires robots to synergistically leverage vision, force, and proprioception. However, Reinforcement Learning agents struggle to learn in such multisensory settings, especially amidst sensory noise and dynamic changes. We propose MultiSensory Dynamic Pretraining (MSDP), a novel framework for learning expressive multisensory representations tailored for task-oriented policy learning. MSDP is based on masked autoencoding and trains a transformer-based encoder by reconstructing multisensory observations from only a subset of sensor embeddings, leading to cross-modal prediction and sensor fusion. For downstream policy learning, we introduce a novel asymmetric architecture, where a cross-attention mechanism allows the critic to extract dynamic, task-specific features from the frozen embeddings, while the actor receives a stable pooled representation to guide its actions. Our method demonstrates accelerated learning and robust performance under diverse perturbations, including sensor noise, and changes in object dynamics. Evaluations in multiple challenging, contact-rich robot manipulation tasks in simulation and the real world showcase the effectiveness of MSDP. Our approach exhibits strong robustness to perturbations and achieves high success rates on the real robot with as few as 6,000 online interactions, offering a simple yet powerful solution for complex multisensory robotic control. Website: https://msdp-pearl.github.io/

</details>


### [410] [Adaptive Sliding Mode Control for Vehicle Platoons with State-Dependent Friction Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10724)
*Rishabh Dev Yadav*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对轮式移动机器人车队的新型自适应滑模控制器，无需摩擦力的先验参数和结构知识，即可处理未知且复杂的摩擦力行为，通过运动学与动力学分离的两阶段控制策略，实现车队速度调节和机器人间安全距离保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人编队控制面临外部扰动和系统参数不确定性等挑战，尤其是车轮与地面之间摩擦力未知/不确定且随路面、轮胎磨损和车速变化，现有自适应控制器需先验边界假设，无法准确建模和辨识状态相关的摩擦力。

Method: 采用自适应滑模控制技术，分两阶段进行：(1) 运动学控制器根据期望轨迹计算期望速度；(2) 动力学模型生成实现期望运动的指令。通过分离运动学和动力学简化控制问题，无需摩擦力参数和结构的先验知识。

Result: 该控制器能够在外部扰动和系统参数不确定的情况下，有效调节车队速度并维持预定义的机器人间安全距离，尤其适用于未知摩擦力环境。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应滑模控制器能够在无摩擦力先验知识的前提下处理其未知复杂行为，相较于传统方法在鲁棒性和适应性上具有优势，为轮式移动机器人车队编队控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot formation control has various applications in domains such as vehicle troops, platoons, payload transportation, and surveillance. Maintaining formation in a vehicle platoon requires designing a suitable control scheme that can tackle external disturbances and uncertain system parameters while maintaining a predefined safe distance between the robots. A crucial challenge in this context is dealing with the unknown/uncertain friction forces between wheels and the ground, which vary with changes in road surface, wear in tires, and speed of the vehicle. Although state-of-the-art adaptive controllers can handle a priori bounded uncertainties, they struggle with accurately modeling and identifying frictional forces, which are often state-dependent and cannot be a priori bounded.
  This thesis proposes a new adaptive sliding mode controller for wheeled mobile robot-based vehicle platoons that can handle the unknown and complex behavior of frictional forces without prior knowledge of their parameters and structures. The controller uses the adaptive sliding mode control techniques to regulate the platoon's speed and maintain a predefined inter-robot distance, even in the presence of external disturbances and uncertain system parameters. This approach involves a two-stage process: first, the kinematic controller calculates the desired velocities based on the desired trajectory; and second, the dynamics model generates the commands to achieve the desired motion. By separating the kinematics and dynamics of the robot, this approach can simplify the control problem and allow for more efficient and robust control of the wheeled mobile robot.

</details>


### [411] [Consensus-based optimization (CBO): Towards Global Optimality in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06868)
*Xudong Sun,Armand Jordana,Massimo Fornasier,Jalal Etesami,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次将共识优化（CBO）引入机器人学，用于全局轨迹优化。CBO在温和假设下保证收敛到全局最优，与MPPI、CEM等局部方法本质不同。在三个具有挑战性的轨迹优化场景（长时域、欠驱动动态平衡、高维终端成本）中，CBO均取得了比现有方法更低的代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶优化方法（如MPPI、CEM、CMA-ES）本质上都是局部方法，依赖梯度估计，容易陷入局部最优。机器人领域需要一种能保证全局收敛的优化方法。

Method: 将共识优化（CBO）引入机器人轨迹优化。CBO是一种基于粒子群的全局优化算法，通过粒子间的共识机制实现全局收敛，不依赖梯度估计。本文提供了理论分析证明其收敛性，并通过三个轨迹优化场景验证其可扩展性。

Result: 在三个挑战性场景（长时域简单系统、高度欠驱动系统的动态平衡、高维终端成本问题）中，CBO均取得了比现有方法更低的代价。

Conclusion: CBO为机器人全局轨迹优化提供了一个新的理论框架，有望替代现有的局部优化方法。

Abstract: Zero-order optimization has recently received significant attention for designing optimal trajectories and policies for robotic systems. However, most existing methods (e.g., MPPI, CEM, and CMA-ES) are local in nature, as they rely on gradient estimation. In this paper, we introduce consensus-based optimization (CBO) to robotics, which is guaranteed to converge to a global optimum under mild assumptions. We provide theoretical analysis and illustrative examples that give intuition into the fundamental differences between CBO and existing methods. To demonstrate the scalability of CBO for robotics problems, we consider three challenging trajectory optimization scenarios: (1) a long-horizon problem for a simple system, (2) a dynamic balance problem for a highly underactuated system, and (3) a high-dimensional problem with only a terminal cost. Our results show that CBO is able to achieve lower costs with respect to existing methods on all three challenging settings. This opens a new framework to study global trajectory optimization in robotics.

</details>


### [412] [EKF-Based Depth Camera and Deep Learning Fusion for UAV-Person Distance Estimation and Following in SAR Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20958)
*Luka Šiktar,Branimir Ćaran,Bojan Šekoranja,Marko Švaco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于EKF融合深度相机与单目相机距离估计的无人机视觉系统，用于提高人体跟踪跟随中的距离估计精度，在三个场景下将RMSE等误差降低了15.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机跟随人时需要准确估计距离以保持安全，而单一传感器（深度相机或单目相机）在真实条件下存在局限，因此需要融合多模态图像信息来提高距离估计的鲁棒性和精度。

Method: 利用YOLO-pose深度学习模型实现深度相机数据滤波和单目相机的人体到相机距离估计，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）算法对两种距离信息进行实时融合，输出最终的距离估计值。

Result: 在室内真实场景下三个测试案例中，系统将距离估计的平均误差、RMSE和标准差均降低了15.3%，在深度相机最佳工作范围之外也能有效减少误差，并在反射、低可见度等挑战条件下表现出更好的鲁棒性和精度。

Conclusion: EKF融合方法能够有效扩展深度检测范围，提升复杂环境下的距离估计精度和鲁棒性，适用于搜索救援（SAR）等实际任务。

Abstract: Vision-based Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) frameworks aid human search tasks by detecting and recognizing specific individuals, then tracking and following them while maintaining a safe distance. A key safety requirement for UAV following is the accurate estimation of the distance between camera and target object under real-world conditions, achieved by fusing multiple image modalities. As part of the system for automatic people detection and face recognition using deep learning, in this paper we present the fusion of depth camera measurements and monocular camera-to-body distance estimation for robust tracking and following. Deep learning based filtering of depth camera data and estimation of camera-to-body distance from a monocular camera are achieved with YOLO-pose, enabling real-time fusion of depth information using the Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) algorithm. The proposed subsystem, designed for use in drones, estimates and measures the distance between the depth camera and the human body keypoints, to maintain the safe distance between the drone and the human target. Our system provides an accurate estimated distance, which has been validated against motion capture ground truth data. The system has been tested in real time indoors, where it reduces the average errors, RMSE and standard deviations of distance estimation up to 15,3% in three tested scenarios. Based on the test results, the EKF fusion-based approach increases the depth detection range by reducing the errors outside the optimal depth camera working range. It also shows improved robustness and precision in challenging conditions, such as reflections and poor visibility, making it suitable for SAR.

</details>


### [413] [Harnessing Embodied Agents: Runtime Governance for Policy-Constrained Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07833)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种运行时治理框架，将具身智能体的认知执行与执行监督分离，通过外部化策略检查、能力准入、执行监控、回滚和人工干预等机制，实现对智能体行为的可治理控制。在1000次随机试验中，该框架拦截了96.2%的未授权动作，将运行时漂移下的不安全延续从100%降至22.2%，恢复成功率达90.7%，相较现有方法显著提升了运行时检测与结构化恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体从被动推理系统发展为主动执行器后，核心挑战从"如何让它行动"转变为"如何在运行时保持其行为可治理"。现有方法将安全、恢复和决策约束嵌入智能体循环内部，导致执行控制难以标准化、审计和跨环境适配，亟需一种将治理外部化的框架。

Method: 提出运行时治理框架，将治理外部化为专用运行时层，包含策略检查、能力准入、执行监控、回滚和人工覆盖机制。形式化定义了持久化具身智能体、模块化能力包与治理层之间的控制边界，并构建了策略约束下的执行流水线，在受控仿真环境中进行评估。

Result: 1000次随机试验中：未授权动作拦截率96.2%±2.7%；运行时漂移下不安全延续从100%降至22.2%±3.1%；恢复成功率90.7%±3.0%且完全符合策略。与AutoRT、RoboGuard等五种基线对比，只有本框架提供连续运行时检测（RVDR=61.3% vs 0%）和结构化恢复（均p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 未来的具身系统应当设计为可治理执行，而非仅关注感知与决策能力。该工作为具身智能体的安全可控执行提供了一种标准化的治理范式。

Abstract: Embodied Agents are evolving from passive reasoning systems into active executors that interact with tools, robots, and physical environments. Once an agent gains execution authority, the central challenge shifts from how to make it act to how to keep its actions governable at runtime. Existing approaches embed safety, recovery, and decision constraints inside the agent loop, making execution control difficult to standardize, audit, and adapt across environments. We propose a runtime governance framework for policy-constrained execution that separates agent cognition from execution oversight. Governance is externalized into a dedicated runtime layer performing policy checking, capability admission, execution monitoring, rollback, and human override. We formalize the control boundary among a persistent Embodied Agent, modular Capability Packages, and the governance layer, and define a policy-constrained execution pipeline evaluated under controlled simulation. Over 1000 randomized trials, the framework achieves 96.2%+/-2.7% interception of unauthorized actions, reduces unsafe continuation from 100% to 22.2%+/-3.1% under runtime drift, and attains 90.7%+/-3.0% recovery success with full policy compliance. Comparison with five baselines, including AutoRT-style constitution filtering and RoboGuard-style two-stage guardrails, shows that pre-execution filtering is equally effective across governance-aware methods, while only the proposed framework provides continuous runtime detection (RVDR = 61.3% vs. 0%) and structured recovery (all p<0.001). A sensitivity sweep across the full detection range confirms a genuine detection-continuation trade-off. This work argues future embodied systems should be designed for governable execution.

</details>


### [414] [Bimanual Robot Manipulation via Multi-Agent In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20348)
*Alessio Palma,Indro Spinelli,Vignesh Prasad,Luca Scofano,Yufeng Jin,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Fabio Galasso*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiCICLe是首个无需微调即可让标准LLM通过上下文学习完成双手臂操作的框架，采用多智能体领导者-跟随者方法解耦动作空间，在13个任务上达到70.5%平均成功率，超越无训练基线和多数监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM通过上下文学习可泛化执行机器人任务，但双手臂操作面临高维联合动作空间和严苛的臂间协调约束，标准上下文窗口难以处理。

Method: 将双手臂控制建模为多智能体领导者-跟随者问题，将联合动作空间解耦为顺序的、条件化的单手臂预测，使标准LLM能以少样本方式执行。

Result: 在TWIN基准的13个任务上平均成功率70.5%，超越最佳无训练基线6.1个百分点，超越大多数监督方法；在3个真实任务上表现优越且无需硬件特定重训练。

Conclusion: BiCICLe首次证明了无需微调的标准LLM也能通过上下文学习有效处理双手臂协调操作，为训练-free的复杂具身控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful reasoning engines for embodied control. In particular, In-Context Learning (ICL) enables off-the-shelf, text-only LLMs to predict robot actions without any task-specific training while preserving their generalization capabilities. Applying ICL to bimanual manipulation remains challenging as the high-dimensional joint action space and tight inter-arm coordination constraints rapidly overwhelm standard context windows. To address this, we introduce BiCICLe (Bimanual Coordinated In-Context Learning), the first framework that enables standard LLMs to perform few-shot bimanual manipulation without fine-tuning. BiCICLe frames bimanual control as a multi-agent leader-follower problem, decoupling the action space into sequential, conditioned single-arm predictions. Evaluated on 13 tasks from the TWIN benchmark, BiCICLe achieves 70.5% average success rate, outperforming the best training-free baseline by 6.1 percentage points and surpassing most supervised methods. We also demonstrate superior real-world performance on 3 tasks without hardware-specific retraining.

</details>


### [415] [Embodied Interpretability: Linking Causal Understanding to Generalization in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00321)
*Hanxin Zhang,Mingshuo Xu,Abdulqader Dhafer,Shigang Yue,Hongbiao Dong,Zhou Daniel Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出干预显著性评分（ISS）和干扰质量比（NMR）两种度量，用于评估视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中视觉区域对动作预测的因果影响，并识别策略是否依赖虚假相关性而非任务相关特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在分布偏移下常因依赖虚假视觉相关性而非任务相关原因产生失败，现有方法缺乏对视觉区域因果影响的严格量化手段。

Method: 将视觉-动作归因形式化为干预估计问题，提出ISS（干预掩蔽程序估计视觉区域对动作预测的因果影响）和NMR（量化对任务无关特征的归因程度），分析ISS的无偏估计性质及动作预测误差作为因果影响代理变量的有效条件。

Result: 在多种操作任务上，NMR能预测泛化行为，ISS相比现有可解释性方法产生更忠实的解释。

Conclusion: 干预归因为识别具身策略中的因果错位提供了简单有效的诊断方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies often fail under distribution shift, suggesting that decisions may depend on spurious visual correlations rather than task-relevant causes. We formulate visual-action attribution as an interventional estimation problem. Accordingly, we introduce the Interventional Significance Score (ISS), an interventional masking procedure for estimating the causal influence of visual regions on action predictions, and the Nuisance Mass Ratio (NMR), a scalar measure of attribution to task-irrelevant features. We analyze the statistical properties of ISS and show that it admits unbiased estimation, and we characterize conditions under which action prediction error provides a valid proxy for causal influence. Experiments across diverse manipulation tasks indicate that NMR predicts generalization behavior and that ISS yields more faithful explanations than existing interpretability methods. These results suggest that interventional attribution provides a simple diagnostic approach for identifying causal misalignment in embodied policies.

</details>


### [416] [Closing the Motion Execution Gap: From Semantic Motion Task Constraints to Kinematic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12053)
*Simon Stelter,Vanessa Hassouna,Malte Huerkamp,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Giskard框架，通过运动状态图解决运动执行鸿沟，实现跨平台可迁移的复杂机器人运动执行，并在8种机器人平台上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高层级符号任务描述（使用语义约束）与可执行机器人运动之间的断开问题（即运动执行鸿沟）。

Method: 引入运动状态图作为可执行的符号化运动表征，支持运动约束、监视器或嵌套状态图的任意并行与序列组合；利用统一的、可微分的运动学世界模型实现以世界为中心的运动规范化和跨本体泛化；通过基于极限模型预测控制的任务函数方法实现运动执行，任务切换时利用加加速度边界确保平滑过渡。

Result: 在8种不同机器人平台上成功部署，验证了跨平台可迁移性。

Conclusion: 提出的Giskard框架可有效弥合符号任务规划与底层运动执行之间的鸿沟，且具备良好的跨平台通用性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the Motion Execution Gap, the disconnect between high-level symbolic task descriptions using semantic constraints and executable robot motions. Motion Statecharts are introduced as an executable symbolic representation for complex motions. They allow the arbitrary arrangement of motion constraints, monitors or nested statecharts in parallel and sequence. World-centric motion specification and generalization across embodiments are enabled through the use of a unified differentiable kinematic world model of both, robots and environments. Motion execution is realized through a lMPC-based implementation of the task-function approach, in which smooth transitions during task switches are ensured using jerk bounds. Cross-platform transferability was demonstrated by deploying the method on eight robot platforms, operating in diverse environments. The proposed framework is called Giskard and is available open source: https://github.com/cram2/cognitive_robot_abstract_machine.

</details>


### [417] [SafeManip: A Property-Driven Benchmark for Temporal Safety Evaluation in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12386)
*Chengyue Huang,Khang Vo Huynh,Sebastian Elbaum,Zsolt Kira,Lu Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeManip是一个基于LTLf时序逻辑的机器人操作安全基准，通过可复用的安全模板评估操作过程中的时间安全性，发现即使任务成功完成，许多执行过程仍然存在安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作评估主要关注任务完成率或单步约束违反，忽略了时间维度上的安全属性（如污染后接触、未完全放入即松开等），缺乏系统评估时序安全的方法。

Method: SafeManip使用有限迹线性时序逻辑（LTLf）定义可复用的安全模板，将机器人执行轨迹映射为符号谓词迹，通过LTLf监控器进行时序安全评估，覆盖碰撞、抓取稳定、释放、交叉污染等8类安全类别。

Result: 在6种视觉-语言-动作策略、50个RoboCasa365家庭任务上评估，发现即使强模型也常表现出不安全行为；任务成功率的提升并不可靠地转化为更安全的执行，许多成功轨迹仍不安全。

Conclusion: SafeManip提供了一个可复用的评估层，用于诊断时序安全故障并在任务完成之外衡量安全成功，揭示了现有模型在时序安全方面的不足。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation is typically evaluated by task success, but successful completion does not guarantee safe execution. Many safety failures are temporal: a robot may touch a clean surface after contamination or release an object before it is fully inside an enclosure. We introduce SafeManip, a property-driven benchmark to explicitly evaluate temporal safety properties in robotic manipulation, moving beyond prior evaluations that largely focus on task completion or per-state constraint violations. SafeManip defines reusable safety templates over finite executions using Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces (LTLf). It maps observed rollouts to symbolic predicate traces and evaluates them with LTLf-based monitors. Its property suite covers eight manipulation safety categories: collision and contact safety, grasp stability, release stability, cross-contamination, action onset, mechanism recovery, object containment, and enclosure access. Templates can be instantiated with task-specific objects, fixtures, regions, or skills, allowing the same safety specifications to generalize across tasks and environments. We evaluate SafeManip on six vision-language-action policies, including $π_0$, $π_{0.5}$, GR00T, and their training variants, across 50 RoboCasa365 household tasks. Results show that even strong models often behave unsafely. Task-success gains do not reliably translate into safer execution: many successful rollouts remain unsafe, while longer-horizon or more complex tasks expose more violations. SafeManip provides a reusable evaluation layer for diagnosing temporal safety failures and measuring safe success beyond task completion.

</details>


### [418] [ActionMap: Robot Policy Learning via Voxel Action Heatmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06904)
*Pei Yang,Hai Ci,Yanzhe Chen,Qi Lv,Han Cai,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ActionMap，一种基于体素热图的动作解码头，替代现有VLA模型中的单点预测器，将动作空间建模为结构化体素热图。在LIBERO仿真和真实Franka机械臂实验中，该方法在两个不同架构骨干网络上均显著优于L1回归等传统方法（如LIBERO四套件平均+8.2%），且数据效率更高、收敛速度相当或更快，证明了动作表示是提升VLA性能的关键杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的动作解码器几乎未得到改进，仍采用单点预测方式（自回归token bins、L1回归或流匹配去噪），将动作空间视为非结构化数据，未能利用邻近动作之间的几何相似性进行训练。

Method: 提出ActionMap动作头，替代VLA中原生的动作解码器。对每个新动作，预测一个覆盖动作空间的体素热图，每个体素直接存储对应动作的概率。

Result: 在LIBERO仿真和真实Franka机械臂操作任务中，基于两种不同架构的骨干网络，ActionMap在相同训练步数下显著优于基线（如OpenVLA-OFT的L1回归头在LIBERO四套件平均提升8.2%），收敛速度相当或更快，且在低数据量下数据效率明显更高。

Conclusion: 动作表示是独立于骨干网络扩展和训练配方缩放之外的、真正影响VLA性能的关键因素，跨骨干网络的一致性表现证实了动作解码器改进的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced rapidly across backbones, training recipes, and data scale, yet the action decoder, which converts the backbone's hidden state into a continuous control signal, has barely changed and remains a single-point predictor across the majority of current VLAs. Whether implemented via autoregressive token bins, L1 regression, or flow-matching denoising, the resulting decoder treats the action space as unstructured, leaving the geometric proximity of neighboring actions unexploited during training. To advance this, we introduce ActionMap, a voxel heatmap action head that drops into an existing VLA in place of its native action decoder. For each new action, the head predicts a voxel heatmap over the action space, where each voxel directly stores the probability of the corresponding action. Across LIBERO simulation and real-world Franka manipulation, our heatmap head surpasses two architecturally distinct backbones at matched training steps (e.g., +8.2% over OpenVLA-OFT's L1 regression head on the LIBERO four-suite average), converges at comparable or faster rates on both backbones, and remains markedly more data-efficient at low training data. The cross-backbone consistency indicates that action representation is a real lever for VLA performance, distinct from further backbone or recipe scaling. Project Page: https://showlab.github.io/ActionMap/.

</details>


### [419] [Continual Quadruped Robots Coordination via Semantic Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08102)
*Daoqing Wang,Yuchen Xiao,Weixuan Huang,Zhilong Zhang,Shenghua Wan,Meng Li,Lei Yuan,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Conquer框架，一种基于语义技能库的持续学习方法，用于解决多四足机器人在开放任务序列中的协调问题。通过检索-适应-更新流程，Conquer在模拟中达到95.6%的平均成功率，并在真实Unitree Go2机器人上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体强化学习的多四足协调方法局限于预定义或封闭任务族，无法在开放持续学习场景中顺序学习新任务并复用已有技能，存在灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出Conquer框架：(1) 设计团队结构化的Self-Allies-Goal (SAG)骨干网络，支持变规模机器人团队；(2) 对每个新任务构建任务级语义描述符，从技能库中检索并适应相关技能；(3) 执行后提取轨迹级语义描述符，按语义距离组织更新技能库，实现持续技能积累与跨任务知识迁移。

Result: 模拟实验中最终平均成功率达95.6%，展示强正向迁移和可忽略的灾难性遗忘；在Unitree Go2机器人上进行了真实世界部署验证。

Conclusion: Conquer框架有效解决了多四足机器人在开放持续学习场景中的协调问题，实现了跨任务知识复用和技能积累，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Multi-quadruped coordination has attracted increasing attention due to its enhanced payload capacity, broader contact coverage, and improved adaptability to challenging tasks. Existing methods for multi-quadruped manipulation typically focus on predefined or closed task families, often relying on multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) to train task-specific coordination policies. However, such methods struggle in open-ended continual learning settings, where tasks arrive sequentially and robots are expected to acquire new coordination skills while reusing previously learned ones without catastrophic forgetting. To address this challenge, we propose Conquer, a semantic skill-library framework that formulates continual multi-quadruped coordination as a retrieve-adapt-update process. First, to accommodate varying team sizes across tasks, we design a team-structured Self-Allies-Goal (SAG) backbone that supports variable-cardinality robot teams by explicitly modeling each robot's own state, teammate context, and task goal. For each incoming task, Conquer constructs a task-level semantic descriptor from pre-execution information and retrieves a relevant skill from the library for adaptation. After successful execution, Conquer updates the skill library by extracting trajectory-level semantic descriptors and organizing them according to semantic distance, thereby enabling continual skill accumulation and cross-task knowledge transfer. Simulation experiments show that Conquer achieves a final average success rate of 95.6%, demonstrating strong forward transfer and negligible catastrophic forgetting. Real-world rollouts on Unitree Go2 teams further validate the deployment feasibility of Conquer for practical multi-quadruped coordination. Simulation and real-robot demonstration videos are available at: https://conquer-project.pages.dev/.

</details>


### [420] [GEAR-VLA: Learning Geometry-Aware Action Representations for Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08530)
*Yuan Zhang,Shiqi Zhang,Yedong Shen,Shuai Dong,Jiajun Deng,Xin Zhang,Yuxuan Gao,Jiajia Wu,Xin Nie,Zhiyuan Cheng,Jianmin Ji,Yanyong Zhang,Xingyi Zhang,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GEAR-VLA提出了一种统一几何感知动作表征的VLA框架，通过粗到细的动作学习、语义对齐的3D融合和具身规范化，实现了对未见物体、背景变化和不同机器人本体的强泛化能力，在多个基准测试中取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在真实部署中面对未见物体、背景变化和不同机器人本体时表现不佳，原因在于缺乏统一的几何感知操作表征，导致对低级轨迹监督脆弱、3D特征未对齐以及无法处理本体差异。

Method: 1) 粗到细动作学习：多源具身预训练赋予VLM具身推理和离散动作理解能力，潜动作令牌将动作语义连接到梯度解耦的DiT连续动作专家；2) 语义对齐3D融合：对齐可训练的3D空间骨干与VLA表征，同时冻结原始VLM对齐的视觉通路；3) 具身规范化：具身感知状态和具身不变动作将机器人差异限制在低级接口层面。

Result: 在LIBERO、零样本LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0上达到最先进性能；AgileX上85.9%成功率，预训练未见LDT-01本体上81.0%成功率；在包含212个未见过物体的6,360次试验通用抓取基准上达到90.1%成功率。

Conclusion: GEAR-VLA通过学习统一几何感知动作表征，有效解决了VLA模型在真实世界部署中的泛化问题，验证了跨本体、跨场景和跨物体的强大泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong benchmark performance but still struggle in real-world deployment with unseen objects, background shifts, and different robot embodiments. We argue that this stems from the lack of a unified geometry-aware manipulation representation, leaving existing VLAs vulnerable to low-level trajectory supervision, misaligned 3D features, and embodiment differences. To address this, we propose GEAR-VLA, a VLA framework for learning unified geometry-aware action representations for generalizable robotic manipulation. GEAR-VLA adopts coarse-to-fine action learning, where multi-source embodied pretraining equips the VLM with embodied reasoning and discrete action understanding before latent action tokens connect action semantics to a gradient-decoupled DiT continuous action expert. It further performs semantic-aligned 3D integration by aligning a trainable 3D spatial backbone with the VLA representation while freezing the original VLM-aligned visual pathway. To share this representation across robots, GEAR-VLA uses embodiment canonicalization, where embodiment-aware states and embodiment-invariant actions confine robot differences to the low-level interface. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate strong generalization: GEAR-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance on LIBERO, zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin 2.0, reaches 85.9% success on AgileX and 81.0% on the pretraining-unseen LDT-01 embodiment, and obtains 90.1% success on a 6,360-trial universal grasping benchmark with 212 unseen objects. Code and models will be released at https://github.com/babynabeauty/GEAR-VLA.

</details>


### [421] [TORL-VLA: Tactile Guided Online Reinforcement Learning for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09337)
*Huaihang Zheng,Yi Yang,Kai Ma,Shenglin Xu,Tian Xie,Guozheng Li,Xiangyu Wang,Yiren Ma,Si Liu,Yinian Mao,Baoxu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TORL-VLA提出了一种触觉引导的在线强化学习框架，将触觉反馈与策略细化相结合，通过触觉感知的力矩感知VLA预测参考动作和未来力矩序列，并利用轻量级在线RL模块优化动作，引入干预删减评论家解决离线策略的在线适应问题，在接触密集型机器人操作任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在接触密集型任务中引入了触觉或力反馈，但通常作为离线策略部署。当接触条件偏离训练分布时，策略无法进行在线适应，导致接触力不当和重试效率低下等问题。

Method: 提出TORL-VLA框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 触觉衍生的力矩感知VLA，用于预测参考动作和未来力矩序列；(2) 轻量级在线强化学习模块，用于优化参考动作。同时引入干预删减评论家（intervention-censored critic），防止干预后的成功被错误归因于干预前的策略生成动作，稳定混合探索策略和人类干预数据的学习过程。

Result: 在长时域接触密集型任务（门闩操作、咖啡杯放置、鸡蛋处理）的真实机器人实验中，TORL-VLA在子任务和全任务层面的成功率以及时间约束下的执行效率均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 触觉引导的在线强化学习框架能够有效解决VLA模型在接触条件变化时的在线适应问题，提升接触密集型机器人操作的成功率和执行效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a powerful framework for robotic manipulation, and recent studies have introduced tactile or force feedback into VLAs to address contact-rich tasks. However, these models are typically deployed as offline policies. When contact conditions shift from the training distribution, the policy cannot perform online adaptation, leading to problems such as inappropriate contact forces and inefficient retries. Therefore, we propose TORL-VLA, a tactile-guided online reinforcement learning framework that couples tactile feedback with policy refinement for contact-rich manipulation. Our method introduces a tactile-derived wrench-aware VLA to predict reference actions and future wrench sequences, while a lightweight online RL module is used to refine the reference actions. To stabilize learning from mixed exploratory policy-generated and human-intervention data, we introduce an intervention-censored critic that prevents post-intervention success from being wrongly credited to policy-generated actions preceding intervention. Real-robot experiments on long-horizon contact-rich tasks, including latch manipulation, coffee-cup placement, and egg handling, show that TORL-VLA improves success rates at both subtask and full-task levels, as well as time-bounded execution efficiency over strong baselines.

</details>


### [422] [Efficient-WAM: A 1B-Parameter World-Action Model with Low-Cost Future Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10040)
*Jiajun Li,Tiecheng Guo,Yifan Ye,Rongyu Zhang,Xiaowei Chi,Qianpu Sun,Ying Li,Yunfan Lou,Yan Huang,Zhihe Lu,Meng Guo,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Efficient-WAM通过紧凑视频专家、令牌稀疏视频潜变量和非对称视频-动作去噪，将未来视频预测作为动作生成的紧凑引导信号，在保持控制性能的同时大幅降低推理延迟（约100ms，相比现有WAM加速30倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）依赖逼真的未来预测，导致高推理延迟，难以满足机器人实时部署需求。因此需要设计更高效的WAM，在保留未来视觉预测控制收益的同时降低推理成本。

Method: 1) 从WAN-2.2-5B迁移紧凑视频专家；2) 使用令牌稀疏视频潜变量；3) 非对称视频-动作去噪（为视频分配更少的采样步数）；4) 将未来视频预测视为动作生成的紧凑引导信号，而非追求视觉保真度。

Result: 1B参数模型在RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界操作任务中保持了强动作性能，尽管未来预测视觉上粗糙；物理部署时每chunk延迟降至约100ms，相比现有WAM实现30倍加速。

Conclusion: Efficient-WAM验证了在保留控制性能的前提下，通过牺牲视觉预测保真度换取推理效率的可行性，为实时机器人部署提供了高效的世界-动作模型设计方案。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for embodied control by coupling future visual prediction with action generation. However, most existing WAMs rely on photorealistic future prediction, which incurs high inference latency and makes real-time robot deployment difficult. This motivates a more efficient WAM design that preserves the control benefits of future visual prediction while reducing its inference cost. We introduce Efficient-WAM, a World-Action Model that reduces the cost of future imagination while preserving its control benefit. Efficient-WAM improves inference efficiency via a compact video expert transferred from WAN-2.2-5B, token-sparse video latents, and asymmetric video-action denoising that allocates fewer sampling steps to video than to actions. Instead of optimizing the future branch for visual fidelity, Efficient-WAM treats future video prediction as a compact guidance signal for action generation. Comprehensive experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 and real-world manipulation tasks show that Efficient-WAM maintains strong action performance despite visibly coarse future predictions. While maintaining competitive control capabilities, our 1B-parameter model can reduce per-chunk latency to around 100 ms during physical deployment, achieving a 30x speedup over existing WAMs.

</details>


### [423] [Planar-Sector LOS Guidance for Interception of Agile Targets with Lifting-Wing Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10639)
*Linkai Liu,Kun Yang,Han Zou,Chen Min,Shuli Lv,Shuai Wang,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种平面扇形视线（PS-LOS）制导框架，利用升力翼四旋翼和单目相机实现敏捷空中目标的自主视觉拦截。与传统圆锥LOS约束不同，PS-LOS在严格约束侧向图像误差的同时放松纵向误差，在保持目标可见性的同时释放更多机动性，在LOS方向提供近50%的额外可用推力。结合延迟补偿状态估计和非线性制导控制架构，在138米范围内实现了对大幅、高频、不可预测运动目标的成功拦截。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 strapdown 相机拦截方法采用圆锥LOS约束将目标保持在图像中心附近，这种对称约束虽然安全，但不必要地限制了机动性，显著减少了可用于追击的推力。受FPV飞手不会在所有图像方向保持相等可见性边界的观察启发，希望设计一种在保持可见性的同时释放机动性的制导框架。

Method: 1) 提出平面扇形视线（PS-LOS）制导框架，严格约束侧向图像误差，放松纵向图像误差；2) 基于升力翼四旋翼模型进行分析；3) 开发延迟补偿状态估计框架和非线性制导控制架构，实现仅依赖LOS的拦截（无需直接深度测量）。

Result: PS-LOS在LOS方向比传统圆锥LOS约束提供近50%的额外可用推力；在真实风扰下实现对大幅、高频、不可预测运动的敏捷目标自主拦截；成功拦截距离达138米，全程保持连续视觉跟踪。

Conclusion: PS-LOS是一种兼顾可见性保持与机动性感知的制导框架，能够有效实现远程视觉拦截敏捷空中目标，突破了传统对称约束对机动性的限制。

Abstract: Autonomous visual interception of agile aerial targets is challenging due to unpredictable target motion, limited sensing, and the strong coupling between target visibility and interceptor maneuverability. Most existing strapdown-camera interception methods preserve visibility using conic line-of-sight (LOS) constraints that keep the target near the image center. While safe, such symmetric constraints unnecessarily restrict maneuverability and can significantly reduce the usable thrust for pursuit. Motivated by the observation that aggressive FPV pilots do not maintain equal visibility margins in all image directions, this paper proposes a Planar-Sector Line-of-Sight (PS-LOS) guidance framework for autonomous interception using a lifting-wing quadcopter equipped with only a strapdown monocular camera. PS-LOS tightly constrains lateral image error while relaxing longitudinal image error within a safe field-of-view margin, preserving visibility while releasing maneuverability for acceleration-intensive pursuit. Under the lifting-wing quadcopter model, PS-LOS provides nearly 50% more available thrust near the LOS direction than conventional conic LOS constraints. To realize LOS-only interception without direct depth measurements, a delay-compensated state-estimation framework and a nonlinear guidance-and-control architecture are developed for lifting-wing quadcopters. Extensive outdoor flight experiments demonstrate autonomous interception of agile targets exhibiting large-amplitude, high-frequency, and unpredictable motion under real wind disturbances. The proposed system achieves successful interceptions at ranges up to 138 m while maintaining continuous visual tracking throughout the engagement. The results validate PS-LOS as a visibility-preserving, maneuverability-aware guidance framework for long-range visual interception of agile aerial targets.

</details>


### [424] [RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11092)
*Yichao Zhong,Yidan Lu,Yuhang Lu,Tianyang Tang,Haoguang Mai,Yixuan Pan,Tianyu Li,Li Chen,Jingbo Wang,Zhongyu Li,Peng Lu,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboNaldo，一种三阶段运动引导课程强化学习框架，使仿人机器人能够完成高冲击力的足球射门动作。通过单一人体踢球参考作为脚手架，逐步从学习全身稳定踢球先验，过渡到定点任意位置射门，最终扩展到运动球射门。在仿真中自由球射门误差降低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍；在真实Unitree G1机器人上，3米距离定点/运动球平均射门误差分别为0.73m和0.86m，球速达13.10m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，运动跟踪驱动的强化学习虽能提供全身运动协调的稳定性，但固定参考难以适应变化的球位和击球时机；而任务奖励驱动的强化学习则难以从零开始探索和发现有效的踢球动作。两者均无法很好地实现高冲击力的仿人机器人足球射门。

Method: 提出三阶段运动引导课程强化学习框架RoboNaldo：第一阶段学习全身稳定踢球先验（基于单一人体踢球参考）；第二阶段将踢球动作适配到自由球场景（球静止于随机位置）；第三阶段通过运动命令与踢球触发接口扩展到运动球射门。训练时高层启发式规划器控制接口，推理时可由其他高层控制器驱动相同底层策略。

Result: 仿真中自由球射门误差比基线低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍。真实Unitree G1机器人上，3米距离定点射门平均误差0.73m，运动球射门平均误差0.86m，触球后球速达13.10m/s，达到职业球员公开射门速度的59-71%。

Conclusion: RoboNaldo通过运动引导的课程学习框架，有效结合了运动跟踪的稳定性和任务奖励的适应性，使仿人机器人能够完成高冲击力、高精度的足球射门，且单一参考即可泛化到多种射门场景。

Abstract: Elite humanoid soccer shooting requires whole-body stability, high-impulse whole-body interactions, and accuracy to targets. Motion tracking-driven reinforcement learning (RL) provides stability in whole-body movement coordination, but a fixed reference makes it hard to adapt to varied ball positions and strike timings; in contrast, task reward-driven RL struggles to explore and discover valid kicks from scratch. We therefore introduce RoboNaldo, a three-stage motion-guided curriculum RL framework for high-impulse humanoid interaction. A single human-kick reference is used as a scaffold and progressively shifts optimization towards shooting performance. The curriculum first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts the kick to free-kick settings where the ball is stationary at random positions, and finally extends it to moving-ball shooting through a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface. A high-level heuristic planner controls this interface during training, while alternative high-level controllers can drive the same low-level policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo demonstrates free-kick shot error 48.6% lower and shoot velocity 2.96x than prior work baselines. In real world on a Unitree G1 with onboard perception, RoboNaldo attains 0.73 m and 0.86 m average target shooting error from 3 m away in free-kick and moving-ball cases, accordingly. And the post-contact ball velocity reaches 13.10 m/s, which is 59-71% of reported professional open-play shot speed. Project page: https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [425] [From Consumption to Reflection: Designing Human-AI Relations for Stable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11195)
*Rikard Rosenbacke,Carl Rosenbacke,Victor Rosenbacke,Martin McKee*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出关系反思智能（RRI），一种推理时治理层，通过在人类与LLM交互中嵌入可审计的推理循环来解决混合认知偏差导致的"关系漂移"问题，将AI安全重新定义为认知架构问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在信息获取上效率极高，但加速了信息消费而非理性推理。人类与LLM共享类似的认知脆弱性（直觉捷径、表征-现实混淆、偏好一致性而非证伪），当两者交互时错误会相互放大，产生"关系漂移"——一种源于交互本身而非单一模型的失败。现有方法缺乏在推理过程中嵌入结构化反思的机制。

Method: RRI包含三个组件：(1) Rose-Frame：识别推理中可能发生的认知崩溃点；(2) Architect's Pen：在关键时刻引入针对性反思步骤；(3) 推理时工作流：在不重新训练模型的前提下将反思步骤嵌入交互过程。这三个组件将人机交互转化为带有显式检查点、冲突暴露和可审计假设轨迹的联合推理系统。

Result: RRI在模型外部（而非内部）构建了一个实用的、结构化的推理治理层，实现了稳定且可审计的人类-LLM协同推理，使双方能够补偿彼此的限制。

Conclusion: RRI将AI安全重新定义为认知架构问题——可靠决策取决于将反思直接嵌入交互过程之中，而非让机器像人类一样思考或强迫人类像机器一样推理。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have transformed how humans access information, but not how we reason with it. Their fluency accelerates consumption while bypassing the slow, reflective processes that underpin sound judgment. This paper introduces Relational Reflective Intelligence (RRI), an inference-time governance layer that operationalizes reflection through auditable reasoning loops. RRI operates not inside the model but around it, providing a practical structure for stable, auditable reasoning between humans and LLMs.
  The core premise is that LLMs inherit cognitive vulnerabilities similar to those that shape human thought: reliance on intuitive shortcuts, confusion between representation and reality, and a preference for coherence over falsification. When humans and models share these tendencies, their errors compound. We refer to this as relational drift, a failure that arises from interaction rather than from the model alone. Addressing this requires a shift from modeling relations between words to structuring relations between model outputs and human reasoning.
  RRI provides this missing layer through three components: the Rose-Frame, which identifies likely breakdowns in reasoning; the Architect's Pen, which introduces targeted reflection steps at critical moments; and an inference-time workflow that embeds these steps without retraining the model. Together, these elements transform human-AI interaction into a joint reasoning system with explicit checkpoints, conflict surfacing, and an auditable trail of assumptions.
  Rather than making machines think like humans or forcing humans to reason like machines, RRI creates a structured interaction in which both compensate for each other's limitations. It reframes AI safety as a cognitive architecture problem, where reliable decisions depend on embedding reflection directly into the interaction process.

</details>


### [426] [From Awareness to Action: Understanding and Overcoming the Research-Practice Gap in Algorithmic Fairness for Public Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11214)
*Sara Altamirano,Tijs Portegies,Sennay Ghebreab*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法研究（专家访谈、在线调查、系统映射）揭示了ML驱动公共卫生研究中算法公平性的“意识-行动差距”，并提出了整合方法、组织和系统三个维度的Fairness-to-Action框架，指出公平性制度化薄弱、转化机制外部驱动、系统层面重准确率轻公平性等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法公平性对负责任的ML驱动公共卫生研究至关重要，但实际实施仍然有限，存在意识与行动之间的差距，需要系统性理解这一差距的成因和解决方法。

Method: 采用序贯混合方法：先进行专家访谈，以此设计在线调查，最后进行系统映射，并将结果映射到三个研究-实践差距视角（知识-实践差距、知识-行动循环、知行差距）上进行分析。

Result: 发现公平性定义碎片化、培训与指导有限、依赖外部资源、缺乏正式评估/缓解/监测机制；公平性制度化薄弱、转化机制外部驱动、系统层面优先考虑准确率而非公平性。

Conclusion: 提出了Fairness-to-Action框架，整合方法、组织和系统三个维度识别知识转化瓶颈，指出了推进安全、公平、伦理的ML驱动公共卫生研究实践的关键杠杆点。

Abstract: Algorithmic fairness is essential for responsible ML-driven public health research, yet its practical implementation remains limited. To investigate this awareness-action gap, we conducted a sequential mixed-methods study comprising expert interviews, an online survey, and systematic mapping. The expert interviews informed the design of the survey, which in turn revealed fragmented definitions of fairness, limited training and guidance, reliance on external sources, and rare use of formal assessment, mitigation, or monitoring. These findings were subsequently mapped onto three established research-practice gap lenses: the Knowledge-Practice Gap, the Knowledge-to-Action Cycle, and the Knowing-Doing Gap, each offering complementary perspectives. Building on this synthesis, we introduce the Fairness-to-Action framework, which integrates methodological, organizational, and systemic dimensions to identify where translation of algorithmic fairness knowledge stalls. Our analysis shows that fairness remains weakly institutionalized, translation mechanisms are externally driven, and system-level priorities continue to emphasize accuracy over fairness. These insights suggest critical leverage points for advancing safe, fair, and ethical ML-driven public health research practice.

</details>


### [427] [The Environmental Cost of LLMs in AIED: Reporting and Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11215)
*Sabrina C. Eimler,Lukas Erle,Daniel Flood,Aditi Haiman,Luca Häckert,André Helgert,Lachlan McGinness,Büsra Yapici*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对AIED领域使用LLM时缺乏计算成本和环境影响报告标准的问题，提出了一个开源方法来系统测量和报告LLM的计算开销及碳足迹，旨在推动更透明的成本报告实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AIED社区广泛使用LLM，但缺乏标准化的流程来测量和报告其计算成本和环境影响，这些隐藏成本未被充分认识。通过对AIED 2025会议论文的文献综述，发现大多数项目使用LLM但很少报告计算资源使用情况，几乎无人讨论环境影响作为伦理问题。

Method: 首先对AIED 2025会议论文集进行文献综述，评估LLM计算/环境成本的报告情况。然后提出一个开源方法，包括：1) 测量本地和云端硬件碳足迹的软件解决方案；2) 一个简便公式用于计算前沿LLM的计算开销（即使未知确切参数数量）。

Result: 文献综述发现大多数项目使用LLM，但很少报告计算资源，几乎不讨论环境影响。提出的开源方法提供了测量碳足迹的软件工具和计算LLM开销的公式。

Conclusion: 本文希望推动AIED社区采用所提出的方法，实现使用LLM时隐藏成本的更透明报告。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) usage in recent years has become increasingly widespread in the Artificial Intelligence in Education (AIED) community. While LLMs offer unique avenues for learners and educators, using LLMs comes with computational and environmental costs. These costs are mostly hidden due to a lack of standardised procedures to measure and report these impacts. To address this gap, we first conducted a literature review of all papers published as part of the AIED 2025 conference proceedings, determining if and how computational or environmental costs of LLMs are reported. Most projects use LLMs, but few report computational resources used and almost none discuss environmental impacts of LLMs as an ethical concern.
  To address this lack of standardised reporting practices, we propose an open-source method for systematically measuring and reporting the computational expense of LLMs and environmental impact of running Machine Learning (ML) AIED systems. We provide software solutions to measure the carbon footprint for both local and cloud based hardware. We also provide an easy-to-use formula to calculate the computational expense of frontier LLMs even when the exact number of parameters is not known. Overall, we hope to motivate colleagues to use our method to strive for more transparent reporting of hidden costs of using LLMs in the AIED community.

</details>


### [428] [Great Disappearance Acts Generative Search and Shadow Banning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11216)
*Danny Friedmann*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了生成式搜索（基于RAG技术）和影子封禁这两种不透明实践对互联网去中心化公共领域的侵蚀，分析了其法律与监管层面的影响，指出中国《算法推荐管理规定》和欧盟《人工智能法案》虽提供了部分解决方案，但未能充分保障公平、透明和救济机制，呼吁通过监管干预和算法透明度来维护互联网作为民主化公共空间的特质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网正被生成式搜索和影子封禁等不透明实践所侵蚀，这些实践通过流量分流和可见性压制，威胁独立内容创作者、小型企业和开放网络的可持续性，亟需从法律与监管角度进行系统性分析。

Method: 采用法律与监管视角的分析方法，分别考察生成式搜索的技术与法律影响（包括版权侵权、不正当竞争、不当得利）以及影子封禁中的算法劝阻、降级排名和流量减少，并重点评估中国《算法推荐管理规定》和欧盟《人工智能法案》的规制效果。

Result: 现有监管框架（中国的RAR和欧盟的AIA）虽提供了部分解决方案，但在确保公平性、透明度和救济机制方面存在不足；主导平台向集中化控制的转向优先考虑利润和风险管理，而非创新、公平和多样性。

Conclusion: 为维护互联网作为自由表达和创新的民主化公共空间，必须采取监管干预、算法透明度和公平框架等关键措施；否则，互联网将面临失去其去中心化公共领域特征的风险。

Abstract: The internet, once celebrated as a decentralized public sphere, is increasingly undermined by practices such as generative search and shadow banning, which divert traffic and suppress visibility. Generative search, powered by Retrieval Augmented Generation RAG, synthesizes content into direct answers, bypassing websites and depriving them of traffic and revenue. This threatens the sustainability of independent content creators, small enterprises, and the open web ecosystem. Shadow banning, a practice that intentionally reduces the visibility of social media posts through algorithmic moderation, exacerbates these issues by chilling free expression and limiting transparency and accountability.
  This article explores these opaque practices through a legal and regulatory lens. The first part examines the rise of generative search, analyzing its technological and legal implications, including copyright infringement, unfair competition, and unjust enrichment. It also evaluates potential solutions such as licensing agreements and agentic AI. The second part focuses on shadow banning: algorithmic dissuasion, de-ranking, and the reduction of traffic, with specific attention to Chinas Regulation on Algorithmic Recommendations RAR and the EUs Artificial Intelligence Act AIA. Both frameworks offer partial solutions but fall short of ensuring fairness, transparency, and redress mechanisms.
  Ultimately, the shift toward centralized control by dominant platforms prioritizes profit and risk management over innovation, fairness, and diversity in online expression. To counteract these trends, regulatory interventions, algorithmic transparency, and equitable frameworks are essential. Without such measures, the internet risks losing its character as a democratized public sphere for free expression and innovation.

</details>


### [429] [Preregistration for Experiments with AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11217)
*Michelle Vaccaro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文主张将预注册实践扩展到基于AI智能体的"in silico"行为实验中，以应对该范式因模型选择、提示措辞等研究者自由度带来的方法论漏洞，并提出了一份针对AI智能体实验的预注册模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型和自主AI智能体越来越多地作为人类代理参与认知、决策和社会动态研究，理解其行为成为重要研究课题。然而，这类实验在继承人类被试研究方法论漏洞的同时，因低迭代成本和无报告规范而放大了这些漏洞，亟需建立预注册机制以提升研究可信度。

Method: 系统梳理AI智能体实验中研究者自由度的来源（模型选择、提示措辞、参数设置、基于结果重新设计等），设计针对AI智能体实验的预注册模板，并呼吁会议、期刊和资助机构将其标准化。

Result: 识别出AI智能体实验特有的方法论脆弱性，即低迭代成本和缺乏报告规范使研究者可轻易利用自由度操纵结果且难以检测；提出了一套定制化的预注册模板以约束这些自由度。

Conclusion: 预注册应成为AI智能体行为实验的标准实践，以提升该新兴研究范式的可信度和可重复性。

Abstract: The proliferation of large language models (LLMs) and autonomous AI agents has given rise to a rapidly growing methodological paradigm: "in silico" behavioral experiments. Originally conceived as a way to use AI agents as proxies for human participants in studies of cognition, decision-making, and social dynamics, this approach has taken on new significance -- as AI agents increasingly negotiate, transact, and make consequential decisions on behalf of people and organizations, understanding their behavior has become a research priority in its own right. While these experiments with AI agents offer unprecedented advantages in terms of scalability, cost efficiency, and experimental control, they also inherit, and in some cases amplify, methodological vulnerabilities that have long plagued human subjects research. To address these issues, this paper argues that preregistration practices -- central to improving the credibility of human subjects experiments -- should now be extended to experiments with AI agents. We systematically catalog the researcher degrees of freedom that experiments with AI agents introduce -- model selection, prompt wording, settings, and outcome-contingent redesign, for example -- and show how the low cost of iteration and lack of reporting norms make these choices both easy to exploit and difficult to detect. We propose a preregistration template tailored to experiments with AI agents and call on conferences, journals, and funding agencies to make preregistration standard practice for this emerging research paradigm.

</details>


### [430] [An Ethical eValuation Agent (EeVA): Results of a Proof-of-Concept Test on a Prototype Agentic-like Workflow to Assist Ethical Deliberations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11218)
*Stephen Milford,B. Zara Malgir,Miguel Vazquez*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了EeVA，一种基于LLM的代理式工作流，旨在支持比较性伦理反思而非给出确定性伦理答案。EeVA通过n8n平台构建，包含三个相互关联的工作流（启动器、工作者、发射器），对上传的用例基于10种伦理框架进行评估。在三个公开案例（城市出行、点对点能源交易、社会服务资源分配）上的概念验证测试表明，EeVA能够生成结构化的框架特定评估和综合报告，区分不同框架，识别趋同与分歧，推荐调整方案，并突出持续的伦理紧张关系。研究结果表明，LLM可以被组织成可用的工作流，保留伦理多元性，同时帮助弥合伦理学家与非伦理专业人员之间的沟通鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伦理讨论常被误解为寻找单一对错答案，这使得缺乏伦理训练的人员难以应对复杂的伦理挑战。现有方法要么过于简化，要么需要专业伦理学家参与，缺乏可扩展的工具来支持非专家的结构化伦理反思。

Method: 在n8n平台上使用三个互连工作流（starter、worker、emitter）构建EeVA系统。系统通过评估提示和综合提示，对上传的用例依据10种伦理框架进行评价。使用三个已发表的案例（城市出行、点对点能源交易、社会服务资源分配）进行概念验证测试。

Result: EeVA在所有案例中均生成了一致结构化的框架特定评价和综合报告。输出能够区分不同框架，识别趋同与分歧，推荐增加一致性的修改方案，并突显持续的伦理张力。综合报告对非专业人员可读，将焦点从简单答案转向设计条件、保障措施和跨框架难以达成一致的领域。

Conclusion: LLM可以被组织成可用的工作流，在保留伦理多元性的同时帮助弥合伦理学家与非伦理人员之间的沟通鸿沟。EeVA的价值不在于取代伦理学家或解决道德分歧，而在于为结构化伦理反思提供支架。EeVA为在缺乏伦理专家资源的场景中支持伦理反思提供了有前景的概念验证，但在成为成熟工具前还需进一步研究可重复性、人工评估、用户测试和效率优化。

Abstract: Ethical deliberation is often misunderstood as a search for single right or wrong answers, creating difficulties for non-ethically trained personnel who must address ethically laden challenges. We developed EeVA, an agentic-like LLM-based workflow designed to support comparative ethical reflection rather than deliver definitive ethical answers. EeVA was programmed in n8n using three interconnected workflows: starter, worker, and emitter. It evaluated uploaded use cases against 10 ethical frameworks through evaluator and synthesis prompts. Proof-of-concept testing used three published cases from urban mobility, peer-to-peer energy trading, and social-service resource allocation. Across all cases, EeVA produced consistently structured framework-specific evaluations and integrated syntheses. Outputs differentiated between frameworks, identified convergences and divergences, recommended modifications to increase alignment, and highlighted persistent ethical tensions. Syntheses were readable for non-specialists and shifted attention away from simplistic answers toward design conditions, safeguards, and areas where full cross-framework agreement was unlikely. The findings suggest that LLMs can be organised into usable workflows that preserve ethical plurality while helping bridge the communicative gap between ethicists and non-ethically trained personnel. EeVA's value lies not in replacing ethicists or resolving moral disagreement, but in scaffolding structured ethical deliberation. EeVA offers a promising proof of concept for supporting ethical reflection where access to ethics expertise is limited. Further work is needed on reproducibility, human evaluation, user testing, and efficiency before it can be considered a mature tool.

</details>


### [431] [Investigating Gender Bias in Touch Biometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11457)
*Joshua Lee,Ben Khant,Rajesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于滑动手势的行为生物特征认证中的性别偏差问题，使用BBMAS和ANTAL两个数据集，采用XGBoost和DenseNet分类器进行实验。结果表明，两种分类器在男女用户间的认证错误率（FAR和FRR）无显著统计学差异，说明滑动认证在保持高准确率的同时具有性别公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为生物特征认证虽然展现出连续认证的潜力，但其在不同人口群体间的公平性问题尚未得到充分探索，尤其缺乏对性别偏差的系统性研究。

Method: 使用BBMAS（117名用户）和ANTAL（71名用户）两个公开数据集，采用XGBoost和DenseNet两种分类器进行滑动手势认证，以FAR和FRR作为评估指标，并运用Kolmogorov-Smirnov、Mann-Whitney和Wasserstein排列检验三种统计方法检测性别差异的显著性。

Result: XGBoost在BBMAS和ANTAL数据集上分别取得了92%和94%的认证准确率；除极少数实验设置外，三种统计检验均未发现男女用户在认证错误率上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 基于滑动手势的行为生物特征认证不仅能实现高准确率，而且在男女用户间保持了可比的性能表现，支持其作为一种公平且可靠的行为生物特征模态的潜力。

Abstract: Behavioral biometrics offer a promising approach for continuous authentication, but their fairness across demographic groups remains largely unexplored. This paper investigates gender bias in swipe-based authentication using the BBMAS (117 users) and ANTAL (71 users) datasets and evaluates XGBoost and DenseNet classifiers through False Acceptance Rate (FAR) and False Rejection Rate (FRR). XGBoost achieved authentication accuracies of 92% and 94% on the BBMAS and ANTAL datasets, respectively, while statistical tests (Kolmogorov-Smirnov, Mann-Whitney, and Wasserstein permutation) found no significant gender differences in authentication error rates across almost all experimental settings. These findings suggest that swipe-based authentication can achieve high accuracy while maintaining comparable performance for male and female users, supporting its potential as a fair and reliable behavioral biometric modality.

</details>


### [432] [AI Researchers Must Help Lead Arms Control to Mitigate Military AI Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11533)
*Ted Fujimoto,Jacob Benz*

Main category: cs.CY
